Only the current files were copied, not the `.bak-*` versions. Every file here is a byte-for-byte copy of its source, with nothing summarized, trimmed or edited.

**Status: reference copy only.**
- **Our own notes win on any conflict.** That covers `[local path]`, `verdicts.md`, the Suspect Insight debate notes and anything else we wrote. Where this folder disagrees with them, ours stands until we check the primary text ourselves.
- **Its verdicts count as opinion, not feats.** The "Verdict-relevant takeaways", "Quick ladder", "Scaling" lines, tier placements and confidence levels are the other agent's judgments. Treat them like a forum opinion. The feat and statement logs cite novel line numbers and quote the text, but we have not re-checked them against the books. They are secondhand reads and do not count toward our own MASTERY tier for any character.
- Paths inside the notes (`[local path]`, `[local path]`, `[local path]`) belong to the other agent's box. They may not exist here.

# Legacy of the Force 1: Betrayal (novel): feats, scaling and story notes

<!-- read up to: L8276, END OF BOOK; fights/power/arc scenes in full, rest skimmed -->

- **Book:** *Star Wars: Legacy of the Force: Betrayal* by Aaron Allston (2006). First of nine LotF novels. Legends, 40 ABY.
- **Source file:** `[local path]` (8,276 lines, 4,138 non-blank; working copy `[local path]`, `\r` stripped, same line numbers). OCR noise kept verbatim.
- **Method (what was read in full vs skimmed):** Read IN FULL (every line of the passage): the Adumar factory raid (Ch1–2, L135–L431), Centerpoint vs Thrackan and the sonic trap (Ch10, L1951–L1999; Ch11, L2247–L2285), Jacen vs Thrackan's decoys (Ch15, L3213–L3299), the Toryaz Station assassination raid (Ch19–20, L4125–L4199), the phantom attacks on Luke and Mara (Ch31, L7215–L7247), Ben/Nelani in the caverns (Ch30, L7103–L7177), Luke vs not-Jacen, Ben vs Sith-Mara and Jacen vs phantom-Luke (Ch31–32, L7440–L7527), the Vectivus house, Lumiya's reveal, Nelani vs Lumiya and Nelani's murder (Ch32–33, L7676–L8129), and Ch34 to the end (L8131–L8276). Opening and Luke's dream (L81–L113) also in full. SKIMMED: every chapter's opening paragraphs, plus a keyword pass (lightsaber/Force/lightning/phantom/Brisha etc., first ~130–150 characters of each matching paragraph) over L1–L7670. Politics, fleet battles (Klauskin, Syal/Zueb, Tralus air war), Han/Wedge/Leia Corellia intrigue and the Jaina/Zekk/Kolir/Thann kidnap mission (Ch9, Ch14, Ch16) were skimmed, not read line by line. `L####` = line number in the source.
- **Chapter heading lines:** Ch1 L77, Ch2 L317, Ch3 L511, Ch4 L669, Ch5 L901, Ch6 L1117, Ch7 L1309, Ch8 L1517, Ch9 L1713, Ch10 L1905, Ch11 L2111, Ch12 L2375, Ch13 L2611, Ch14 L2795, Ch15 L3003, Ch16 L3319, Ch17 L3613, Ch18 L3807, Ch19 L4005, Ch20 L4163, Ch21 L4451, Ch22 L4641, Ch23 L4899, Ch24 L5207, Ch25 L5557, Ch26 L5811, Ch27 L6055, Ch28 L6349, Ch29 L6641, Ch30 L6885, Ch31 L7213, Ch32 L7489, Ch33 L7855, Ch34 L8131.
- **Headline:** the **Jacen-turns** book. Jacen's commando raids (Adumar, Centerpoint) show a cold, efficient Knight. Lumiya, disguised as "Brisha Syo", lures him to the Vectivus asteroid near Bimmiel, tests him with Force phantoms (a phantom Luke nearly beats him), reveals herself, and argues that the Sith can rule without corruption. Jacen foresees that he kills Luke in every future where Nelani reports what she saw, so he cripples and kills Nelani, edits Ben's memory, and lets Lumiya go. Lumiya's closing scene confirms Luke's dream figure is the Sith Jacen will become.

---

## 1. Plot and themes

### Plot (in order)
- **Ch1–2:** Luke dreams of a shadowed, Anakin-like figure who «brings great pain to the galaxy» (L93). Jacen and Ben raid an Adumari munitions factory and escape a missile-armed airspeeder.
- **Ch3–6:** Solo–Skywalker family dinner; Han resents the GA's pressure on Corellia. The GA plans a show of force; Jacen is sent to sabotage Centerpoint Station.
- **Ch7–16:** Klauskin's fleet arrives off Corellia. Jaina, Zekk, Kolir and Thann fail to kidnap Prime Minister Saxan's convoy party (Ch9, Ch14). At Centerpoint, Thrackan Sal-Solo floors Jacen with a sonic weapon; Jacen fights through, then is tricked twice by Thrackan's recordings (Ch10–15). Ben, separately, faces the "Anakin Solo" droid (Ch15). Centerpoint's self-destruct is triggered; Thrackan escapes.
- **Ch17–21:** Peace talks at Toryaz Station. A commando raid hits every delegation at once; Luke, Mara, Jacen, Jaina, Zekk, Han and Leia repel it (Ch19–20). Investigation follows.
- **Ch22–28:** Luke and Corran in Coronet; Han throws in with Corellia; Jacen and Ben, with Nelani Dinn, track Jedi-linked "phantom" incidents on Lorrd and meet "Brisha Syo".
- **Ch29–33:** The Bimmiel-area asteroid. Phantoms separate Ben and Nelani; a phantom Luke tests Jacen. Simultaneously phantom Jacen and phantom Ben attack Luke and Mara on Coruscant. Brisha reveals she is Lumiya; Nelani fights her; Jacen's foresight shows Luke dying by his hand along every path where Nelani lives; he kills Nelani.
- **Ch34:** The Tralus battle ends; Leia renounces doing evil to protect family. Lumiya, alone, talks with a phantom of the Sith Jacen will become. Jacen lies to Ben. Luke senses «He exists» (L8269).

### Themes
- **The reasonable road to the dark side.** Lumiya never uses a mind trick on Jacen; Nelani herself confirms «Lumiya isn't using any Force techniques against you» (L7917). Jacen falls to an argument plus foresight.
- **Sacrifice of one to save many** (Lumiya: «sometimes this means sacrificing one so that twenty may live», L7901), the same logic Leia rejects in Ch34 («she would not give up a life she did not have the right to sacrifice», L8183).
- **Attachment.** Leia's Ch34 meditation mirrors Lumiya's Sith «balance» speech (L8203, L8237).
- **Luke's denial.** Lumiya: Luke can't see the dream figure's face because he won't accept «your face where the next Lord of the Sith stands» (L7905).

## 5. Scaling connections
- **Luke vs Jacen (Betrayal-era):** S3 (Jacen concedes), D10/S8–S10 (a phantom at roughly Luke's level, boosted by the place's dark side energy, beats Jacen; Jacen judges it «a fair match for Luke»). Indirect but consistent: **Luke > Jacen at 40 ABY start**, matching `lotf-2..4` and the high-confidence Luke > Caedus verdict.
- **Jacen's growth line:** Betrayal Jacen = elite Knight (bolt-catching F11, decisive TK F10, perfect foresight F33, kills Nelani without effort). Already a top duelist below Luke, far below his Sacrifice–Invincible Caedus peak.
- **Lumiya:** superior technique vs a Knight (D11), multi-phantom coordination (F27), even TK push-of-war with Nelani while talking (F31, she is visibly straining, L7897). Fits the existing `verdicts.md` entries Mara ≥ Lumiya and Luke > Lumiya.
- **Mara:** routs a phantom Ben (D8) and kills an armored commando (F17) in the opening second of a raid.
- **Ben (13–14):** competent apprentice, KO'd by phantom Mara (D9, S20).
- Links: `lotf-2-bloodlines-notes.md` … `lotf-9-invincible-notes.md`; `caedus-legends-quotes.txt`, `luke-legends-quotes.txt`, `mara-jade-quotes.txt`.

## Character analyses (10 characters)

### 1. Jacen Solo (future Darth Caedus)
- **Role/arc:** a disillusioned Knight whose «life of the Jedi hadn't given him the sense of purpose it seemed to have provided his uncle» (L7785). Persuaded by argument, not manipulation (S15), he frees Lumiya and kills Nelani to avert the future where he kills Luke (L7983).
- **Key feats:** bolt redirection and saber deflection (F1–F2, F8); pain-mastery recovery from the sonic trap (L2263: «A sudden infliction of pain could surprise him, surely. But it couldn't keep him down.»); bare-handed bolt catch (F11); TK disarm and wall-slam of Thrackan (F10, F12); thrown-saber kill (F20); long-range foresight across «Scores of time lines» (F33, L7955); memory editing (F36).
- **Statements about him:** Thrackan, impressed (S4). Lumiya: «the first one in centuries with the strength to use the Sith techniques to help others» (S13, pitch). Luke's dream: Anakin-like features (S2).
- **Quote:** «The law is what we make of it.» (L7933).
- **Fights/results:** wins D1–D3 (with Thrackan's tricks costing him twice), D12 (kills Nelani). Loses D10 to phantom Luke.
- **Scaling:** high Knight / near-Master; clearly below Luke here (S3, D10). LotF 1 adds no new peak to Caedus's tier (verdicts.md unchanged on tier).

### 2. Luke Skywalker
- **Role/arc:** Grand Master haunted by a dream (S1–S2); politically sidelined; at the end he knows the figure «finally exists» (S21).
- **Key feats:** reverses a blast-propelled door (F13); simultaneous Force sweeps (F14); tears down a ceiling on attackers (F15); rips a Jedi-trap net (F16); fends off phantom Jacen «with little effort» (F21, F23).
- **Statements:** Jacen: «No, just educated in different directions» when asked if he's stronger than Luke (S3). The phantom built from him nearly kills Jacen (S9–S10).
- **Quote:** his waking words open the book (L81): he doesn't exist (printed in capitals in the source).
- **Fights:** D5, D7 (wins).
- **Scaling:** top of the LotF ladder; supports Luke > Jacen/Caedus (high).

### 3. Ben Skywalker
- **Role/arc:** Jacen's apprentice; capable but young; his memories of Brisha erased (F36).
- **Feats:** helps Jacen through the factory and turbolift (F3); cuts the Adumari airspeeder (L417–L421); outmaneuvers phantom Mara's TK (F24).
- **Statements:** Lumiya: «not yet strong enough to face a Sith» (S5). Ben: «She kicked the stuffing out of me.» (S20).
- **Fights:** D4 (stopped by shock tech), D9 (KO'd by phantom Mara).
- **Scaling:** apprentice tier; below every Knight in the book.

### 4. Mara Jade Skywalker
- **Role/arc:** Luke's partner; protective of Ben («is more open. More trusting.», L673).
- **Feats:** one-motion kill through rifle and neck (F17); Force-throws phantom Ben (F22); Force-pulls her saber from sleep (L7229).
- **Statements:** Mara: «We'll find him,» … «And we'll beat him.» (L8275).
- **Fights:** D5, D8 (wins).
- **Scaling:** Master-level combatant; nothing here changes Mara ≥ Lumiya.

### 5. Lumiya (Shira Brie / "Brisha Syo")
- **Role/arc:** the architect. Trained by Vader (L7687), cyborg (L7715), she claims she can never be a true Master (S11, L7735), and wins: «I've won» (L8217).
- **Key feats:** coordinates multiple phantoms at once on Coruscant and the asteroid (F27–F28); lightwhip superiority over Nelani (F29–F30); TK stalemate with Nelani while debating (F31).
- **Statements:** «If it had been an assault, I would have let the Luke phantom kill you.» (L7521). Her own lore claim that cyborgs can't reach «true Mastery» (S11, L7735) also downgrades Vader (S12); treat as self-serving.
- **Fights:** D11 (ahead of Nelani); never fights Jacen.
- **Scaling:** strong dark-sider with exceptional illusion range; below Luke and Mara in direct combat (other LotF books).

### 6. Han Solo
- **Role/arc:** Corellian loyalist drifting from the GA; flies with Wedge at Tralus; sees Jaina «didn't need him» (L8019).
- **Feats (non-Force):** shoots two raiders before Leia lands (L4171–L4173); hits a raider's armor to set up Jacen's thrown saber (L4191–L4193).
- **Statement:** «C'mon, kid» (L4187), to Jacen as they run to the Corellian suite.
- **Scaling:** non-Force; useful as setup man in D6.

### 7. Leia Organa Solo
- **Role/arc:** Jedi Knight and diplomat who, at Tralus, manipulates and lies to protect Han and Jaina, then rejects that path (L8183).
- **Feats:** Force-assisted leap to the ceiling with saber lit (F19); kills/disarms raiders (D6, L4181).
- **Quote:** «Both sides . . . are us.» (L8163).
- **Scaling:** Knight; below Luke/Mara/Jacen.

### 8. Jaina Solo
- **Role/arc:** Knight and pilot on Team Purella; dominant in the Tralus air war (Han's POV, L8021).
- **Feats:** destroys YVH droids in the failed kidnap (L2033–L2041); emerges in the Toryaz raid with saber lit (L4161). Han wonders whether she downed missiles «with a flip of her hand and a Force technique» (L8015).
- **Quote (about her):** «Part Han, part Leia, and all herself, she dominated the air around her.» (L8021).
- **Scaling:** top Knight / ace; no saber duel in this book.

### 9. Nelani Dinn
- **Role/arc:** Jedi Knight from Lorrd; the only witness to Jacen's turn; killed for it.
- **Feats:** TK bust push-of-war with Lumiya (F31); attacks Lumiya first (L7853).
- **Statements:** «Like any dark-sider, she mixes truth with lies until you can't separate them.» (L7793). «If you do this, you'll become something bad. Something destructive.» (L8109).
- **Fights:** D11 (losing to Lumiya), D12 (killed by Jacen).
- **Scaling:** low-mid Knight benchmark.

### 10. Thrackan Sal-Solo
- **Role/arc:** Corellian Chief of State; Centerpoint's restorer; Lumiya names him behind the Toryaz attack (L7893).
- **Feats (non-Force):** a sonic weapon that floors a Jedi (F5); voice-recording decoys that beat Jacen twice (L3239, L3295).
- **Quote:** Jacen: «You can never prepare against Jedi.» (L1967). Thrackan: «Yes, you can.» (L1969).
- **Scaling:** non-Force; shows anti-Jedi tech can neutralize even Jacen briefly.

### 11. Zekk, Kolir Hu'lya, Thann Mithric (Team Purella)
- **Feats:** Kolir «could telekinetically lift tons» (F4, L1761) and kills YVH droids; Zekk cuts into an inverted speeder (L1743).
- **Scaling:** Knight calibration below Jaina.

*Character count: 10 numbered analyses + 1 group entry.*

---

## 6. Verdict-relevant takeaways
1. **Luke > Jacen (40 ABY start)**: S3, D10, S8–S10. Confidence high.
2. **Jacen's Betrayal tier:** elite Knight, below Luke; this book cannot raise Caedus's peak (it can only fill in his early arc). **Caedus's final tier is unchanged** (top of tier 5, below Luke); LotF 1 was the last gap.
3. **Lumiya** is a skilled illusionist who beats a Knight on technique (D11), consistent with the existing Mara ≥ Lumiya entry.

---

<!-- 021-legacy-of-the-force/lotf-2-bloodlines-notes.md (verbatim copy) -->

# Legacy of the Force 2: Bloodlines (novel), feats and scaling notes

**read up to: COMPLETE (Prologue to Ch23, L1–L8684)**

- **Book:** *Star Wars: Legacy of the Force: Bloodlines* by Karen Traviss (2006). Book 2 of 9 in Legacy of the Force (LotF).
- **Continuity:** Legends. The time is 40 ABY: «ten standard years after the Yuuzhan Vong war» (L63), and the main story opens «SIXTEEN DAYS AFTER THE RAID ON CENTERPOINT STATION» (L143), which was the climax of *Betrayal*.
- **Source file:** `[local path]` (8,684 lines). A line-identical working copy is at `[local path]`.
- **Method:** I read every line of the file in order, in chunks of about 700–1,200 lines. `L####` is the line number in that file. Quotes inside «…» are copied exactly from the file, including its OCR typos (for example "be fell" at L1863, "he back" at L8049, "Fat" for Fett at L8359). Where a quote leaves out words, it is split into separate «…» fragments or marked with "…". A script checked every quote against the line(s) cited for it (see the end of the file).
- **Structure:** Prologue (Fett, first person), then Chapters 1–23. Each chapter opens with an in-universe epigraph (news reports, memoirs, messages). The POVs alternate between Jacen, Luke, Ben, Han/Leia, and Boba Fett. Karen Traviss gives Fett and the Mandalorians about a third of the book.
- **Feat vs. statement:** Section 2 lists things the narration shows happening (**FEAT**). Section 3 lists what characters say or think about power or skill (**STATEMENT**, not a feat). POV narration that reports what a character believes is marked *(POV)*.
- **Headline:** this book has **no lightsaber duel between major Force users**. No Jacen vs. Luke, no Mara vs. Lumiya, and Alema Rar does not appear. Its scaling value comes from Jacen's stealth, illusion, and mind feats against Luke, from narration that directly compares Jacen with Luke and with Anakin/Vader, and from the Jacen–Lumiya power relationship.

---

## 1. Cast who fight or use the Force

| Character | Role in this book | Force user? |
|---|---|---|
| Jacen Solo | Colonel, head of the new Galactic Alliance Guard (GAG); Lumiya's Sith apprentice | Yes |
| Lumiya ("Shira") | Sith, Jacen's teacher; comes to Coruscant on his summons | Yes |
| Luke Skywalker | Grand Master; senses darkness and identifies Lumiya | Yes |
| Mara Jade Skywalker | Jedi Master; defends Jacen to Luke. **No combat feats here.** | Yes |
| Ben Skywalker | 13 (L145), GAG member under Jacen; makes his first kills | Yes |
| Jaina Solo | Fighter pilot (31, L2867); refuses Jacen's order | Yes |
| Leia Organa Solo | In hiding on Corellia with Han; plots against Thrackan | Yes |
| Han Solo | About 60 (L2059); plots to kill Thrackan | No |
| Boba Fett | Mand'alor, 71 (L503), dying of clone degeneration | No |
| Mirta Gev | Bounty hunter, revealed as Fett's granddaughter | No |
| Ailyn Vel / "Habuur" | Fett's daughter, hired to kill the Solos; dies under Jacen's interrogation | No |
| Kyp Durron, Corran Horn, Kyle Katarn, Cilghal | Council members. **Talk only, no feats.** | Yes |
| Tenel Ka / Allana | Mentioned only; Jacen touches them through the Force (L867) | — |
| Thrackan Sal-Solo | Corellian President, assassinated in Ch17 | No |

---

## 2. Combat and Force feats, in chapter order

### Prologue / Ch1
1. **Prologue · L71–79, L111 · Fett (FEAT, ship/gear)**: runs down H'buk's Z-95 in *Slave I*, then «crack open its canopy seal with the laser housed in my wrist gauntlet» (L111). *Context:* he is ill: «It hurts deep in my stomach. I don't let anyone see that.» (L113).

### Ch2
2. **Ch2 · L873 · Jacen (FEAT, minor)**: levitates while meditating and touches Tenel Ka through the Force across the galaxy (L867).
3. **Ch2 · L983–989 · Jacen (FEAT, KEY)**: the hotel bomb blast. His «instinct was to fling himself on Niathal and wrap the taxi tight in a Force shield», and «It hammered the hull as Jacen fought to hold the taxi steady, oblivious of the pilot's efforts.» The taxi survives «without Jacen's unseen assistance» … (L989: the pilot thinks it was his own doing). At L1009 he Force-pushes logjammed speeders apart. *Context:* surprise attack, reaction speed, shield plus telekinesis.

### Ch3
4. **Ch3 · L1157 · Jacen vs. Luke and Mara (FEAT, concealment)**: «He hadn't even felt Jacen enter the room. The fact that Jacen could startle him was disturbing.» (Luke POV). Jacen then floods the room with his presence: «So he wants to show me how powerful he is.» *Context:* Luke and Mara were not hunting for him, but neither felt him. Earlier, Mara feels him «just blinked out» (L1049).

### Ch4
5. **Ch4 · L1777 · Jacen (FEAT plus a statement of capability)**: senses Lumiya's emotions from Coruscant at her asteroid near Bimmiel. In the same passage: «She might be able to project totally false emotions, too, because he could do much the same himself and fool even other Jedi Masters into believing them.»
6. **Ch4 · L1813–1869 · Jacen (FEAT, flow-walking)**: «Jacen could time-drift.» (L1813). He watches Anakin and the clone troopers in the Temple during Order 66 and reads Anakin's emotions: «Anakin had been driven to the dark side by agonized love.» (L1829).
7. **Ch4 · L1879–1883 · Jacen (FEAT, long-range command)**: reaches out «not as an open hand but as a commanding fist» and summons Lumiya from the Bimmiel system to Coruscant. She comes (Ch5).

### Ch5
8. **Ch5 · L2051 · Leia (FEAT, minor)**: kills the ambusher at the Falcon: «A lightsaber cut an arc through the air, once, twice, three times.» Han wounded him first with continuous fire (L2045).
9. **Ch5 · L2311 · Lumiya (FEAT, Force concealment)**: in the Jedi Temple, «it felt as if she had cloaked herself completely». No Jedi reacts to her inside the Temple (L2345).
10. **Ch5 · L2371–2389 · Jacen (+ Lumiya) vs. Luke (FEAT, KEY)**: «Jacen cloaked Lumiya in a Force illusion and projected his own unconcerned calm as Luke approached. It was an exhausting maneuver, nothing beyond him when dealing with ordinary people, but something that took all his strength when deceiving a Jedi Master of Luke's stature.» (L2379). «Luke was sensing Lumiya, but very faintly. Jacen knew it.» (L2389). *Context:* this is two people's concealment stacked (Lumiya's own cloak plus Jacen's illusion). Luke is rushing out of a Council meeting and suspects nothing. It costs Jacen «all his strength». Luke «seemed baffled» (L2385), so he still half-caught her. Read it as a partial win under favorable conditions, not proof that Jacen outclasses Luke.
11. **Ch5 · L2535 · Ben (FEAT)**: half-blinded by gas and being hauled up from the ground, he «just pulled out his lightsaber with his free hand and saw the bright blue blade collide with a stream of white energy, deflecting it.» This saves a CSF officer from Barit Saiy's shot. The officer: «You're fast, kid.» (L2541).

### Ch6
12. **Ch6 · L2569–2609 · Jacen + Leia (FEAT, KEY, shared)**: «Jacen had mastered a Theran technique that let him use the Force to hear remotely.» (L2569). From the Temple he listens to the Falcon's cockpit as it re-enters. Leia is holding the ship's cracking drive housing together with the Force («It was a massive task.», L2593). Jacen channels his telekinesis through her: «Use the Force I'm channeling through you.» (L2599–2603). «He had drowned out her presence in his own mind with the sheer strength of the telekinesis he was projecting.» (L2601, about Jaina). Result: «the hull around her drive assembly compressed instead of expanding catastrophically» (L2605). Afterward: Han, «If Jacen hadn't projected the Force through Leia, you'd have needed a shovel to pick us up at the spaceport.» (L2985). Luke: «You've developed some impressive skills lately.» (L3033).

### Ch7
13. **Ch7 · L3039–3045 · Luke (FEAT, sensing)**: catches «a very faint touch of something familiar» and works out who the hooded man from his vision is: «I know who the hooded man is. I know now, and it's not a man at all.» (L3045). This partly undoes feat #10. Luke did register Lumiya.

### Ch8
14. **Ch8 · L3495–3519 · Jacen vs. Luke, second time (FEAT)**: he «projected a Force illusion around Lumiya to bolster her own cloaking» (L3495), with a «ball of heat building in his chest». Luke walks past, and Jacen holds it for ten more minutes (L3517). Lumiya: «I'm impressed by your ability to deceive Luke» (L3519). *Context:* stacked concealment again. Luke was distracted after the Niathal meeting.
15. **Ch8 · L3481 · Jacen (statement of ability)**: he could «snatch the sounds out of the air from behind closed doors at the far end of the floor».

### Ch9
16. **Ch9 · L3713–3721 · Jacen (FEAT, precision)**: Jabi Town raid. «He sent a Force jolt through all three targets simultaneously, paralyzing them, and a second later the doors blew open not with the punishing shock wave of a conventional blast but the contained violence of the Force.» The targets are out of sight in a back room. «Just a shock to the spinal cord.» (L3721). Troopers call it «REBJ» (Rapid Entry By Jedi, L3727).
17. **Ch9 · L3759 · Ben (FEAT)**: Force-wrenches a set of doors apart. He senses unassembled explosives at L3699.

### Ch10–11
18. **Ch10–11 · L3881, L3909, L4261–4311 · Fett (FEAT, infiltration)**: on Vohai he jetpacks to a roof in under 3 s (L3881), uses his helmet's terahertz radar that «could see through walls» (L3909), loops the fence sensor (L4273–4277), and crawls a roof for 3+ hours to find Taun We. *Context:* he is in pain throughout: «This is really hurting my hips. And my chest.» (L4285).
19. **Ch11 · L4561–4577 · Jaina vs. Luke, training (FEAT, piloting)**: «Even for him, Jaina Solo was a serious challenge in aerial combat.» (L4561). Luke «could have taken a serious shot and he still might not have hit her» (L4577). *Context:* a live-fire exercise. Luke wonders «Or maybe I'm slowing down.» (L4563).
20. **Ch11 · L4621 · Jaina (FEAT, surprise)**: «Luke, caught off-guard by his own shame at Ben's appearance, failed to block Jaina's Force push as the captain hit the wall of the mess». *Context:* Luke was distracted and not opposing her as an enemy.
21. **Ch11 · L4771–4777 · Jacen (FEAT, multitasking)**: «The doors shot apart» from a meter away; his «lightsaber seemed to have a life of its own as he deflected red blaster bolts»; «the blasters flew from their hands as if snatched by an unseen hand» (L4773); «Jacen slammed her flat against the wall with the Force and pinned her there.» (L4775, Ailyn). Ben «fended off a stray blaster bolt» (L4771). Shevu: «Can't quite keep up with your box of tricks.» (L4777).

### Ch13–14
22. **Ch13 · L5239 · Jacen (FEAT, self-cloaking)**: «he had withdrawn from the Force, cloaking himself against detection». He does this to hide Tenel Ka and Allana from Lumiya, because «Lumiya's Force skills were still not to be taken for granted».
23. **Ch14 · L5637–5663 · Jacen, Jaina, Zekk vs. 3 Corellian assault fighters (FEAT, piloting)**: «Corellians were excellent pilots, but they weren't Jedi.» (L5641). Jacen dodges a missile with chaff (L5651), then makes a deliberate choice: he «could have sent the Corellian spiraling harmlessly away by using the Force to tip his wings» (L5659), but kills him with lasers instead. Jaina: «You fired the opening shot of the real war.» (L5671).
24. **Ch14 · L5699–5703 · Fett (FEAT, piloting)**: a hyperspace micro-jump through the blockade at «half the speed of light», with a near-catastrophic reentry over Coronet.

### Ch15
25. **Ch15 · L5797–5805 · Jacen (FEAT, KEY, crowd)**: about half a million protesters (L5769). He takes «the precaution of throwing up a Force-shield around them» (L5797). «All of it bounced back from the Force-barrier» (L5799). He pushes the crowd back while keeping restraint: «Nothing violent. Mustn't cause a crush» (L5803).
26. **Ch15 · L5869 · Jacen vs. Luke and Mara (FEAT, emotional masking)**: he «suppressed his detectable feelings further so that he presented quiet bewilderment to Luke and Mara». This works, though Luke had felt him «flinch as if he'd had an electric shock» (L5857) at a touch.
27. **Ch15 · L5969–6023 · Jacen kills Ailyn Vel (FEAT, KEY, dark)**: «Suddenly Habuur's head slammed down on the table without warning … Jacen hadn't lifted a finger.» (L5969). After two hours of Force interrogation she dies. «"I've entered people's minds before and they've always been fine afterward," said Jacen. He seemed surprised that his Force technique had caused so much damage to Habuur, but not sorry.» (L6023). *Context:* a bound, non-Force-sensitive prisoner whom Girdun had already beaten. Jacen later says: «She died of an aneurysm.» (L8093).

### Ch16
28. **Ch16 · L6243, L6305 · Lumiya illusion vs. Jacen dispel (FEAT, KEY for the master–apprentice scale)**: Lumiya fills the room with an underwater illusion. Jacen's view: «her power and energy could only find an expression in illusion» and «She could change nothing permanently. He could, though.» (L6243, POV). He ends it: he «clapped his hands together once in a burst of Force energy. The beautiful blue underwater illusion vanished like shattering ice» (L6305). *Context:* Lumiya was not resisting. It was a lesson, not a contest.
29. **Ch16 · L6441, L6525–6527 · Fett (+ Mirta) vs. Han (FIGHT)**: «Fett brought Han down with a forearm smash under the chin and sent him sprawling.» (L6441). Han charges Mirta, Fett hits him with a blaster stock and Mirta knees him: «Don't take on two bounty hunters at once.» (L6527). *Context:* Han is about 60, Fett is 71 and dying, and it is 2v1 at the end.

### Ch17
30. **Ch17 · L6595–6661 · Jacen (FEAT, KEY, triple technique)**: «he had to make himself invisible at the same time as shutting down his Force presence and flow-walking back in time. He doubted he could hold all three elements together for long.» (L6595). He «used the Fallanassi skills he had learned to project an illusion of being nothing» and «drew his Force presence so far inside himself that he vanished to all Jedi senses.» (L6597). «The effort of sustaining all the techniques at once was making him sweat.» (L6631). Afterward he «mind-rubbed the memory from the man» (L6661), a maintenance worker.
31. **Ch17 · L6817–6819 · Jaina vs. Jacen (FEAT, mental)**: she shoves him out of her mind: «Get out of my head».
32. **Ch17 · L6913 · Jacen (FEAT, ruthless)**: he «sent a spread of five torpedoes into the freighter» after Jaina refuses («Colonel Solo, I'm refusing that order.», L6909).
33. **Ch17 · L6971–7007 · Thrackan killed (FIGHT)**: Han tackles and head-butts him, and Fett pins him. Mirta «rolled across the desk to fire three bolts into Sal-Solo's forehead» (L6997). Fett: «You should learn to shoot first, Solo» (L7001).

### Ch18
34. **Ch18 · L7141–7169 · Fett (FEAT, gear)**: uses a flamethrower in the escape tunnel and a jetpack missile («You know how much these MM-nines cost?», L7165) to collapse the tunnel behind them.
35. **Ch18 · L7351–7391 · Ben (FEAT, KEY for Ben)**: Force-assisted drop from about 10 m (L7351) and a Force push that opens doors (L7357). «Ben sent a surge of energy ahead of him in a shock wave to flatten anyone inside.» (L7389). He kills two men with his lightsaber and deflects a bolt «without thinking» (L7391). These are his first kills. One man may have been unarmed (L7411–7413).

### Ch19–23
36. **Ch19–20 · L7683–7693 · Leia (FEAT, reaction)**: Mirta shoots at Fett, and «Leia Solo reaching out both hands as if she could seize the very energy with them.» (L7683). «Mirta's blaster flew high in the air» (L7685). Leia then pins Mirta against the wall with the Force (L7693).
37. **Ch19 · L7571–7581 · Luke (FEAT, sensing, failed)**: Luke feels Ben's pain for a moment, then it is «snatched back under cover». He guesses Jacen «could mask the presence of others» (L7581).
38. **Ch23 · L8541 · Jacen vs. Lumiya (FEAT, masking)**: he «projected a sense of bewilderment to mask the dread and fear churning his stomach, and it took almost all his strength.» Lumiya does not detect his secret (Tenel Ka and Allana). At L8563 he holds his hand in a candle flame until the flesh chars.

---

## 3. Statements and hype (STATEMENT: not feats)

- **S1 · L295 · Narration on Ben**: Ben's Force senses are «a fraction of Jacen's». *Reliable* (narration).
- **S2 · L837 · Mara on Jacen**: «No, I feel that Jacen's developing powers way beyond mine, and that he's good for Ben». *Sincere.* Mara is biased toward Jacen at this point, but this is still a Jedi Master putting Jacen above herself. Important for Caedus vs. Mara (Sacrifice).
- **S3 · L1777 · Jacen (POV)**: he can project false emotions and «fool even other Jedi Masters». *Supported* by feats #10, #14, #26, #38.
- **S4 · L2275 · Jacen (POV)**: «there was little-if anything-that could present a serious threat to him now». *Self-assessment.* The book frames it as hubris.
- **S5 · L2299 · Lumiya**: «I can hide indefinitely-anywhere.» *Partly supported* (feat #9), but Luke senses her anyway (feat #13).
- **S6 · L3027, L3033 · Luke**: Jacen's skills are «getting stronger and more subtle every day»; «You've developed some impressive skills lately.» *Reliable.*
- **S7 · L3035 · Luke (POV)**: «Jacen was the only other Jedi Luke knew who could defeat Lomi Plo's illusion of invisibility.» *Reliable.* This connects to the *Dark Nest* events.
- **S8 · L5127 · Kyp**: «It's time he became a Master. We all know what he can do.» *The Council's consensus on Jacen's power.*
- **S9 · L5179 · Luke (POV)**: «Jacen would still be a massively powerful Force-user, and nothing could take that from him.»
- **S10 · L6243 · Jacen (POV) on Lumiya**: «She could change nothing permanently. He could, though.» *Reliable as a description of their power styles.* Feat #28 supports it.
- **S11 · L6265 · Lumiya on Anakin**: «He also lacked your breadth of education in the Force.» *Self-serving.* She is grooming Jacen, but the book supports the claim that Jacen is unusually broadly trained (Fallanassi, Theran, Aing-Tii, Vergere).
- **S12 · L6653–6655 · Narration (Jacen POV), KEY**: «Jacen was more powerful than any of them except Luke. And he was growing closer to Luke's strength by the day.» Then «When he achieved Sith Mastery, he would surpass him.» *The first sentence reads as reliable narration about 40 ABY: Luke > Jacen > everyone else. The second is Jacen's expectation.* "Them" is the Jedi Order (the context is the Council chamber).
- **S13 · L6643 · Jacen (POV) on Anakin**: Anakin was «largely untrained», and «Had he received the training that Jacen had» things would have gone differently. *Jacen's opinion.*
- **S14 · L7063 · Lumiya**: Anakin «started his training too late and was still inexperienced when he was exploited by a man who wanted power». *Lumiya's manipulative framing.*
- **S15 · L7075 · Lumiya**: «weak men like Palpatine saw only power, and why they were defeated». *Lumiya's ideology.* It is not a power claim about Sidious.
- **S16 · L7533 · Luke**: «I know Jacen is turning to the dark side, and that it's beyond anything that you or I did when we ventured there.» *Reliable.*
- **S17 · L7853 · Jacen (POV) on Fett**: «He has a Mandalorian army that could take on the Yuuzhan Vong. Avoid him for as long as you can.» *Jacen's caution reflects Fett's resources, not personal combat.*
- **S18 · L8013 · Fett on Vader**: «Vader had been the master of causing pain and death without so much as a touch of his finger.» Fett connects this to Jacen's Force-killing of Ailyn.
- **S19 · L8369 · Han**: «If you're thinking of going after Jacen, he's too tough a quarry, even for you.» *A father's opinion.*
- **S20 · L8543 · Lumiya**: «I can't teach you any more skills.» Also L6233: «Technique is for apprentices. You know all you need to know.» *By the end of Bloodlines, the student has outgrown Lumiya's teaching. Jacen only needs the "final trial."*
- **S21 · L8679 · Narration (Fett POV)**: «Jacen Solo was becoming a pale imitation of his grandfather, Lord Vader, and would take on more than he had ever bargained for.» *The closing line, and the author's framing of Jacen as a lesser Vader.*

---

## 4. Duels and fights

There are **no saber duels between Force users**. The fights:
- **A. Jacen vs. Ailyn Vel (Ch11 capture, L4771–4777; Ch15 death, L5969–6023).** She is a non-Force-sensitive bounty hunter. He wins easily: deflection, disarm, and pin. Her later death in custody is a torture-by-Force kill. The point is character, not power.
- **B. Jacen/Jaina/Zekk vs. 3 Corellian fighters (Ch14).** The Jedi win. Jacen chooses to kill even though he could have disabled.
- **C. Fett + Mirta vs. Han (Ch16, L6441–6527).** Fett wins easily, 2v1 at the end. Fett is old and dying.
- **D. Thrackan's assassination (Ch17).** Han, Fett, and Mirta against Thrackan. It is an execution.
- **E. Ben at H-90 (Ch18).** Ben, 13, against armed rioters: two kills with the saber and a shock-wave entry.
- **F. Contests of will rather than fights:** Jacen's illusions vs. Luke (Ch5, Ch8, partly successful). Jacen vs. Jaina's mind (Ch17, Jaina pushes him out). Jacen's dispel vs. Lumiya's illusion (Ch16). Jacen's masking vs. Lumiya's reading (Ch23, Jacen wins).

---

## 5. Scaling connections

### A. Luke vs. Jacen/Caedus, status at the end of Bloodlines
- Narration ranks Luke above Jacen, with Jacen closing in: «Jacen was more powerful than any of them except Luke. And he was growing closer to Luke's strength by the day.» (L6653).
- Jacen's stealth wins over Luke all come with conditions: concealment stacked with Lumiya's, Luke distracted, and Jacen using «all his strength» (L2379). Luke still half-senses Lumiya (L2389) and later identifies her (L3045).
- **Reading:** Luke > Jacen in overall power. Jacen ≥ Luke at hiding and deception. This sets up Exile and Sacrifice. See `[local path]` and `luke-legends-quotes.txt` for the later endpoint (Invincible).

### B. Jacen vs. Lumiya
- Jacen's raw, physical Force exceeds Lumiya's. She is an illusion and stealth specialist (S10, feat #28). She tells him she has nothing left to teach (S20). He hides a secret from her at full strain (feat #38). **Jacen > Lumiya** in power by the end of Book 2, while she keeps the edge in Sith lore and manipulation.

### C. Jacen vs. Mara (pre-Sacrifice)
- Mara: Jacen's «developing powers way beyond mine» (L837). Mara is «far more attuned to Jacen than Luke was» (L1051), which foreshadows her being the one to find him out. No fight happens in this book.

### D. Caedus vs. Vader / Anakin
- The book draws the parallel constantly: Order 66 flow-walk (L1813–1869), Anakin's Mastery debate (L6609–6655), Lekauf's grandfather (L4031), Omas's memoir (L3539), Beviin's «he fancies himself as the new Vader» (L7987), and Fett's «pale imitation» (L8679).
- **Power claims:** S11, S13, and S14 say Jacen is better trained than Anakin was. Those claims come from Lumiya and from Jacen himself. The authorial verdict (S21) says Jacen is a *lesser* Vader. Treat Bloodlines as framing that favors Vader on stature, with no head-to-head data.

### E. Sidious and Krayt
- Nothing direct. Lumiya's dismissal of Palpatine (S15) is ideology. For existing comparisons, see `debates-sidious-plagueis-tenebrous-vs-krayt-caedus-valkorion.md`.

### F. Common opponents and other links
- Lomi Plo (S7): Luke and Jacen both beat her invisibility. That links to Dark Nest feats.
- Ben's level: deflects bolts at 13 and can clear a room with a shock wave. He is well below Jacen (S1).
- Jaina: pushes Jacen out of her mind (feat #31) and challenges Luke in the air (feat #19). This foreshadows her role in Invincible.

### Quick ladder (this novel only)
Luke > Jacen (closing fast; best in the book at stealth and mind work) > Lumiya (illusion specialist) ≈ Mara (no feats; statement places Jacen above her) > Jaina > Leia > Ben. Fett is the top non-Force fighter, though dying.

---

## 6. Verdict-relevant takeaways
1. At 40 ABY (Bloodlines), **Luke is still above Jacen** in reliable narration (L6653). Jacen's wins over Luke are stealth-only and costly.
2. **Jacen > Lumiya** in raw power by the end of Book 2 (L6243, L6305, L8543).
3. Jacen's toolkit is the broadest of the era: Fallanassi illusion, Theran remote hearing, flow-walking, presence-erasure, memory wipes, Force interrogation, and channeling telekinesis through another person.
4. The author frames Jacen as a «pale imitation» of Vader (L8679). Use this against "Caedus > Vader" claims that rest on breadth of training.
5. No Mara, Alema, Kyp, Corran, or Tenel Ka combat data in this book.

---

## Story and prose notes

### SP1. Plot summary, in order
- **Prologue–Ch3 (L61–1626):** Fett is told he is dying and has a «standard year or two» (L359). Thrackan puts a contract on the Solo family, and Fett's estranged daughter Ailyn takes it. A Corellian bombing hits Coruscant (the Elite Hotel). Jacen shields Niathal. Gejjen recruits Han against Thrackan.
- **Ch4–Ch8 (L1627–3536):** Jacen flow-walks to Order 66 and summons Lumiya, who arrives disguised as «Shira». Jacen hides her from Luke twice. Jacen channels his telekinesis through Leia to save the Falcon. Luke works out that the hooded man is Lumiya. Jacen becomes a colonel heading the Galactic Alliance Guard (GAG), and Pellaeon resigns.
- **Ch9–Ch13 (L3537–5366):** The GAG raids Corellian neighborhoods. Fett hunts Taun We and Ko Sai's anti-aging data and learns of a gray-armored "Skirata" clone. Ben betrays Barit Saiy's family. Jacen captures Ailyn. The blockade of Corellia begins.
- **Ch14–Ch18 (L5367–7478):** The Mandalorians and Kad'ika come into the story. Jacen fires the «opening shot of the real war». Jacen kills Ailyn by Force interrogation. Fett takes contracts from both Thrackan and Gejjen. Lumiya teaches the "breaking of limits." Jacen sees Anakin denied the rank of Master. Jaina refuses Jacen's order and is suspended. Thrackan is assassinated. Ben makes his first kills.
- **Ch19–Ch23 (L7479–8684):** Mirta is revealed as Fett's granddaughter. Fett learns Jacen killed Ailyn and chooses to let the Jedi tear themselves apart. Han disowns Jacen. The GA declares war on Corellia. Lumiya gives Jacen the final trial: «You have to kill what you love.» (L8535). Jacen concludes, wrongly at this point, that it means Tenel Ka and Allana.

### SP2. Character profiles
- **Jacen:** a utilitarian who believes he is choosing the Sith path for the galaxy's sake. «I did it for the galaxy, not for my own selfish love of a woman.» (L8529). He gets colder in stages: the shield (Ch2), the kill (Ch14), the torture death (Ch15), the torpedoes (Ch17), and the heart-break acceptance (Ch23). Early on he still doubts himself in his deleted journal: «Am I making the same mistake as Grandfather?» (L565).
- **Lumiya:** a patient, intellectual Sith. «I don't need to create chaos, Jacen». She says people create it themselves (L2365). She calls the Sith way «acceptance» (L7055). Vader saved her after Luke shot her down (L7035).
- **Luke:** ages and hesitates. He is constrained by the politics of his position and is reluctant to force Ben. «I'm not a great student of history … But I think we've been here before somehow.» (L7887).
- **Mara:** defends Jacen out of loyalty and because of her own past as the Emperor's Hand (L833).
- **Ben:** loses his innocence. «Ben knew at that moment that he would never be a child again.» (L5365). «I killed someone … And I feel really bad about it.» (L8217).
- **Fett:** a Traviss focus. He is dying, Mand'alor without any interest in the role, and finds family. «Jedi always take everything from me.» (L8419).
- **Han and Leia:** «I don't know who you are, but you aren't my son anymore.» (L8129).

### SP3. Themes
- **Fathers and legacies:** Jango/Boba/Ailyn/Mirta mirror Anakin/Jacen and Luke/Ben. The epigraph at L5371 reads «Train your sons to be strong but your daughters to be stronger.»
- **The road to authoritarianism:** the GAG as secret police (Mara, L1741), protesters chanting «The-Empire's-back!» (L5773), emergency powers (L7881–7883).
- **Sacrifice as self-justification:** «Jacen was prepared to be hated in pursuit of a greater good.» (L3535). The Sith prophecy reads «He will immortalize his love.» (L8469).
- **Force-blindness to family:** Ben asks, «I thought you could do anything in the Force, Jacen. Why did you miss that?» (L6067–6071).

### SP4. Notable prose passages and dialogue
- L245 Jacen: «I can tell you, with the certainty of the Force, that failing to stamp out dissent completely now will result in the deaths of billions in the coming years.»
- L247 Omas: «I remember the Empire, Jacen. I lived through it.»
- L1829 «Anakin had been driven to the dark side by agonized love.»
- L5045 Fett: «Maybe I'd have grown up a nice guy if a Jedi hadn't cut my dad's head off in front of me.»
- L6645 «I'm the second chance.»
- L8535 Lumiya: «You have to kill what you love.»
- L8591 «Jacen accepted the burden in its entirety, and his heart-irrelevant, fragile, expendable-broke.»
- L8683 «Good night, Dad. We're going home.»

### SP5. Worldbuilding and lore stated in the text
- Galactic Alliance Guard: Jacen as colonel, Shevu, Girdun (ex-NRI), the 967 Commando (L3127–3159, L3665).
- Flow-walking depends on location: Jacen «can only see what happened to Lord Vader on Coruscant» (L8505).
- Fallanassi illusion of being nothing (L6597). Theran remote hearing (L2569).
- Ko Sai's anti-aging research. Kal Skirata and the Nulls are implied to be alive (L4423, L4447, L3379–3403).
- Mandalorian terms: Mand'alor, Ba'buir, verd'goten, dar'manda, «Verd ori'shya beskar'gam» (L1305), Kad'ika (L5511–5525).
- A Sith prophecy tassel with three lines (L8465–8469).

### SP6. Timeline placement and connections
- **From the text:** 10 years after the Yuuzhan Vong War (L63). Sixteen days after Centerpoint (L143). Anakin's Order 66 was «fifty-nine years» earlier (L1809; Jacen POV). Fett is 71 (L503). Allana is 4 (L869).
- **Outside context:** 40 ABY. LotF 2 of 9: after *Betrayal* (Aaron Allston), before *Tempest* (Troy Denning). Book 1 events referenced here: Centerpoint, the killing of Nelani, and Ben's lost memory (L325, L8185–8195).

## Character analyses (added 2026-10-17 under the 7+ characters rule)

*Method:* every entry below was built from the verified feat/statement log above (Sections 2–5) and checked again against the source file. `L####` = line in `838ba53a…txt` (chapter given alongside). Quotes in «…» are verbatim and were re-checked by script (`[local path]`) after writing. Nothing here comes from a wiki unless marked **[wiki summary]**. Before this pass the file had 7 short story profiles (SP2) but **no** per-character analyses with feats, fights and scaling; this section adds **11**.

### 1. Jacen Solo
- **Role/arc:** GAG colonel and Lumiya's apprentice. He goes from shielding a taxi (Ch2) to his first deliberate kill (Ch14), the Force-interrogation death of Ailyn Vel (Ch15), torpedoing a freighter (Ch17), and accepting that his Sith trial means killing someone he loves (Ch23).
- **Key feats:** Force shield around Niathal's taxi in a bomb blast (Ch2, L989). Summons Lumiya from the Bimmiel system «as a commanding fist» (Ch4, L1879–1883). Hides Lumiya from Luke twice by stacking illusion on her cloak, at «all his strength» (Ch5, L2379; Ch8, L3495). Channels telekinesis through Leia to keep the *Falcon*'s drive housing together (Ch6, L2599–2605). Paralyzes three unseen targets at once (Ch9, L3713). Shields a crowd of about half a million (Ch15, L5797–5799). Holds invisibility, presence-erasure and flow-walking at once (Ch17, L6595–6631). Dispels Lumiya's illusion with a clap (Ch16, L6305).
- **Statements about him:** Mara: «No, I feel that Jacen's developing powers way beyond mine» (Ch2, L837). Narration: «Jacen was more powerful than any of them except Luke. And he was growing closer to Luke's strength by the day.» (Ch17, L6653). Luke: «You've developed some impressive skills lately.» (Ch6, L3033). Fett's closing line calls him «a pale imitation of his grandfather, Lord Vader» (Ch23, L8679).
- **Notable quotes:** «I did it for the galaxy, not for my own selfish love of a woman.» (Ch23, L8529). «Am I making the same mistake as Grandfather?» (Ch2, L565).
- **Fights/results:** beats Ailyn Vel easily (Ch11, L4771–4775). With Jaina and Zekk, beats three Corellian fighters and kills one he could have spared (Ch14, L5659). Jaina pushes him out of her mind (Ch17, L6817–6819).
- **Scaling:** Luke > Jacen in overall power, but Jacen is the best in the book at stealth, illusion and mind work, with the widest toolkit of the era. Jacen > Lumiya in raw power by the end of the book.

### 2. Lumiya
- **Role/arc:** a Sith teacher who comes to Coruscant when Jacen summons her and admits by the end that she has nothing left to teach him.
- **Key feats:** cloaks herself inside the Jedi Temple and no Jedi reacts (Ch5, L2311, L2345). Fills a room with an underwater illusion (Ch16, L6243). Long-range emotional projection is implied by Jacen (Ch4, L1777).
- **Statements:** «I can hide indefinitely-anywhere.» (Ch5, L2299). Jacen's POV: «She could change nothing permanently. He could, though.» (Ch16, L6243). Her own: «I can't teach you any more skills.» (Ch23, L8543); «Technique is for apprentices.» (Ch16, L6233).
- **Notable quotes:** «I don't need to create chaos, Jacen» (Ch5, L2365). On Anakin: «He also lacked your breadth of education in the Force.» (Ch16, L6265).
- **Fights/results:** none. Jacen breaks her illusion (Ch16, L6305, a lesson rather than a contest), and she fails to read his hidden secret (Ch23, L8541).
- **Scaling:** an illusion and stealth specialist, below Jacen in power. Luke still identifies her (Ch7, L3045), so Luke > Lumiya at sensing. This lines up with Luke vs. Lumiya in Exile.

### 3. Luke Skywalker
- **Role/arc:** a Grand Master held back by politics and by his reluctance to push Ben. He senses a darkness and works out that the hooded figure is Lumiya.
- **Key feats:** half-senses Lumiya through two layers of concealment (Ch5, L2385–2389) and then identifies her: «I know who the hooded man is. I know now, and it's not a man at all.» (Ch7, L3045). Briefly feels Ben's masked pain (Ch19, L7571–7581).
- **Statements:** narration still ranks him first (L6653, above). He admits to Jaina's flying, «Or maybe I'm slowing down.» (Ch11, L4563). Luke on Jacen: «I know Jacen is turning to the dark side, and that it's beyond anything that you or I did when we ventured there.» (Ch19, L7533).
- **Notable quote:** «I'm not a great student of history … But I think we've been here before somehow.» (L7887).
- **Fights/results:** a training dogfight in which Jaina is «a serious challenge» (Ch11, L4561). Jaina's Force push lands while he is distracted (Ch11, L4621). No real duel.
- **Scaling:** the top of this book on narration alone. Losing to Jacen's illusions came with conditions (distracted, double concealment), so it does not show Jacen > Luke.

### 4. Mara Jade Skywalker
- **Role/arc:** a Jedi Master who defends Jacen to Luke out of loyalty, though she is the one most «attuned» to him (L1051), which sets up Sacrifice.
- **Key feats:** none in combat. She senses Jacen «just blinked out» (Ch2, L1049).
- **Statements:** her own, Jacen's «developing powers way beyond mine» (L837). Narration: Mara is «far more attuned to Jacen than Luke was» (L1051).
- **Fights/results:** none.
- **Scaling:** her own statement puts Jacen above her. That is an early data point for Caedus > Mara (Sacrifice), but it is a statement, not a feat.

### 5. Ben Skywalker
- **Role/arc:** 13 years old (L145), a GAG recruit under Jacen. He makes his first kills and loses his innocence.
- **Key feats:** deflects a shot while half-blinded and being hauled off the ground (Ch5, L2535). The officer says «You're fast, kid.» (L2541). Force-wrenches doors apart (Ch9, L3759). At H-90 he takes a Force-assisted 10 m drop and «sent a surge of energy ahead of him in a shock wave to flatten anyone inside» (Ch18, L7351, L7389), then kills two men.
- **Statements:** narration says his senses are «a fraction of Jacen's» (L295).
- **Notable quotes:** «Ben knew at that moment that he would never be a child again.» (L5365). «I killed someone … And I feel really bad about it.» (L8217).
- **Fights/results:** beats armed rioters (Ch18).
- **Scaling:** a gifted teenager, far below Jacen. This is his baseline for his growth through Revelation and Invincible.

### 6. Jaina Solo
- **Role/arc:** a 31-year-old fighter pilot (L2867) who refuses Jacen's order to fire, an early sign of her role as the Sword.
- **Key feats:** in training, Luke «could have taken a serious shot and he still might not have hit her» (Ch11, L4577). She lands a Force push on Luke (Ch11, L4621). She expels Jacen from her mind: «Get out of my head» (Ch17, L6817–6819).
- **Notable quote:** «Colonel Solo, I'm refusing that order.» (Ch17, L6909). To Jacen: «You fired the opening shot of the real war.» (Ch14, L5671).
- **Fights/results:** with Jacen and Zekk, beats three Corellian fighters (Ch14).
- **Scaling:** an elite pilot, close to Luke in a fighter. Her mental resistance to Jacen foreshadows the Invincible duel.

### 7. Leia Organa Solo
- **Role/arc:** hiding on Corellia with Han. She chooses family over the GA.
- **Key feats:** kills an ambusher with three saber strokes (Ch5, L2051). Holds the *Falcon*'s cracking drive housing together with the Force, «a massive task» (Ch6, L2593), with Jacen channeling through her. Disarms Mirta mid-shot and pins her (Ch19–20, L7683–7693).
- **Statements:** Han: «If Jacen hadn't projected the Force through Leia, you'd have needed a shovel to pick us up at the spaceport.» (L2985).
- **Notable quote** (with Han, to Jacen): «I don't know who you are, but you aren't my son anymore.» (L8129).
- **Scaling:** a competent Knight-level user with fast reactions against non-Force threats. She sits below Jaina and Jacen.

### 8. Han Solo
- **Role/arc:** about 60 (L2059). He plots and carries out Thrackan's death and breaks with Jacen.
- **Fights/results:** Fett and Mirta beat him 2v1 (Ch16): «Fett brought Han down with a forearm smash under the chin and sent him sprawling.» (L6441). Mirta: «Don't take on two bounty hunters at once.» (L6527). He tackles and head-butts Thrackan (Ch17, L6971–7007).
- **Statements:** to Fett about Jacen: «If you're thinking of going after Jacen, he's too tough a quarry, even for you.» (L8369).
- **Scaling:** a non-Force veteran and an elite pilot. In melee he is below Fett even with Fett ill.

### 9. Boba Fett
- **Role/arc:** a reluctant, dying Mand'alor (71, L503) who finds a granddaughter.
- **Key feats:** cuts open a Z-95 canopy with his gauntlet laser (Prologue, L111). Roof infiltration with terahertz radar that «could see through walls» (Ch10–11, L3909). Hyperspace micro-jump through the blockade at «half the speed of light» (Ch14, L5699–5703). Collapses an escape tunnel with a jetpack missile (Ch18, L7165).
- **Statements:** Jacen's POV: «He has a Mandalorian army that could take on the Yuuzhan Vong. Avoid him for as long as you can.» (L7853). Fett on Vader: «Vader had been the master of causing pain and death without so much as a touch of his finger.» (L8013).
- **Notable quotes:** «It hurts deep in my stomach. I don't let anyone see that.» (L113). «Jedi always take everything from me.» (L8419). «You should learn to shoot first, Solo» (L7001).
- **Fights/results:** beats Han (Ch16). Takes part in Thrackan's execution (Ch17).
- **Scaling:** the top non-Force fighter in the book, though dying. His threat to Jacen is his army, not personal combat.

### 10. Mirta Gev
- **Role/arc:** a bounty hunter who turns out to be Fett's granddaughter. She shoots at him, then joins him.
- **Feats:** finishes Thrackan, «rolled across the desk to fire three bolts into Sal-Solo's forehead» (Ch17, L6997). Leia disarms her when she shoots at Fett (L7683–7685).
- **Scaling:** a skilled non-Force fighter, below Fett and Leia.

### 11. Ailyn Vel ("Habuur")
- **Role/arc:** Fett's estranged daughter, hired against the Solos. Jacen captures her and she dies under his interrogation.
- **Fights/results:** loses to Jacen at once: «Jacen slammed her flat against the wall with the Force and pinned her there.» (Ch11, L4775). Death: «Suddenly Habuur's head slammed down on the table without warning … Jacen hadn't lifted a finger.» (Ch15, L5969). Jacen: «She died of an aneurysm.» (L8093).
- **Scaling:** a character beat, not a power marker. It shows that Jacen can kill a non-sensitive through mind-probing, the parallel to Vader that Fett draws (L8013).

<!-- 021-legacy-of-the-force/lotf-3-tempest-notes.md (verbatim copy) -->

# Legacy of the Force 3: Tempest (novel), feats and scaling notes

**read up to: COMPLETE (Prologue to Epilogue, L1–L7066)**

- **Book:** *Star Wars: Legacy of the Force: Tempest* by Troy Denning (2006). Book 3 of 9 in Legacy of the Force (LotF).
- **Continuity:** Legends, 40 ABY. It follows *Bloodlines* directly: Thrackan Sal-Solo is dead and Wedge was appointed «some ten standard days earlier-the day after Thrackan Sal-Solo's assassination» (L418), the GAG and the Corellian war are under way, and Leia has been a Jedi Knight for «four years» under Saba (L428, L1460).
- **Source file:** `[local path]` (7,065 lines). A line-identical working copy is at `[local path]`.
- **Method:** I read every line in order, in chunks of about 600–1,000 lines. `L####` is the line number in that file. «…» quotes are copied exactly, including OCR noise. Where a quote leaves out words, it is split into separate «…» fragments. Typos are cited in plain "quotes" instead. A script checked every «…» against the cited lines (see the footer).
- **Structure:** Prologue (Alema POV), Chapters 1–24, and an Epilogue (Tenel Ka POV). The POVs rotate between Jacen, Luke/Mara, Han/Leia, Jaina/Zekk, Ben, Alema, and Lumiya. Setting: the Hapes Consortium coup (the Heritage Council, the AlGray/Galney sisters) and Corellia's secret fleet.
- **Feat vs. statement:** Section 2 covers what the narration shows happening (**FEAT**). Section 3 covers claims, beliefs, and hype (**STATEMENT**). *(POV)* marks a character's narrated belief.
- **Headline:** this is the **combat book** of the first LotF trilogy. It contains: **Tresina Lobi vs. Lumiya + Alema** (Ch6), **Jacen vs. Aurra Sing** (Ch19), **Luke + Mara vs. Lumiya + Alema** at Roqoo Depot (Ch21), and Jacen ordering the Anakin Solo to fire on the Falcon (Ch23).

---

## 1. Cast who fight or use the Force

| Character | Role in this book | Force user? |
|---|---|---|
| Jacen Solo | GAG colonel, commands the Star Destroyer *Anakin Solo*; Lumiya's apprentice; secret father of Allana | Yes |
| Lumiya | Sith Master, wields a lightwhip, works with Alema; wounded at Roqoo; fate left open | Yes |
| Alema Rar | Dark Twi'lek, the last of the Gorog; crippled (half foot, one lekku, atrophied arm); Lomi Plo-style "slippery presence" | Yes |
| Luke Skywalker | Grand Master; hunts Lumiya with a shoto in Jar'Kai style; loses his cybernetic hand at Roqoo | Yes |
| Mara Jade Skywalker | Jedi Master; beats Alema at Roqoo | Yes |
| Ben Skywalker | 13; GAG; spars with Luke; tracks Jaina; accidentally wounds Zekk | Yes |
| Jaina Solo / Zekk | Discharged pilot turned Jedi with a battle-meld; fight YVH droids | Yes |
| Leia Organa Solo | Jedi Knight; flies the *Falcon*; fights assassins at the Hapan palace | Yes |
| Han Solo | Corellian envoy, then turns on Corellia to save Tenel Ka | No |
| Tenel Ka | Hapan Queen Mother; still has her «Force skills» (L2032) | Yes |
| Aurra Sing ("Nashtah") | Jedi hunter, about 80, Force-sensitive, lightsaber user | Yes (minor) |
| Tresina Lobi | Jedi Master and Council member, killed in Ch6 | Yes |
| Jagged Fel | Exiled bounty hunter, chasing Alema | No |
| Boba Fett, Kyp, Corran | **Absent / no feats** | — |

---

## 2. Combat and Force feats, in chapter order

### Prologue: Alema stalks Jacen at the World Brain (L70–379)
1. **Prologue · L180 · Alema (FEAT, TK):** «Still calling on the Force, she lifted him off his feet and squeezed until she heard the happy crackle of crushed cartilage.» *Opponent:* a Nikto thug. Also Force-pins and hurls boys (L96–114) and turns Quarren blasters aside (L182–194).
2. **Prologue · L224 · Alema (FEAT, stealth ability):** «Lomi Plo-had helped her develop a slippery presence in the Force. Now, as soon as Alema vanished from someone's sight, she also vanished from memory.» This is her signature tool, and it explains how she gets the drop on Luke and Mara later.
3. **Prologue · L232 · Lumiya (FEAT, lightwhip):** «whoever was following Alema was using some sort of strange lightsaber technology- and using it well.» She cuts down Quarren guards.
4. **Prologue · L320–326 · Lumiya saves Jacen (FEAT):** Alema's Force-hidden dart is incoming, and Jacen's blade «was not rising to block» (L320). Then «Jacen cried out and flew backward, as though hurled by an invisible hand» (L322). *Context:* **Lumiya sensed Alema and Jacen did not.** Jacen was distracted talking to the World Brain.
5. **Prologue · L332–336 · Force lightning hits Alema** (the caster is ambiguous, L334). Jacen is «burning with a rage that Alema had not thought possible for him» (L336). Alema retreats: she «knew she lacked the power to kill Jacen with one attack-and that she would not have time for two» (L340).
6. **Prologue · L342 · Lumiya (FEAT, acrobatics):** «dancing past the thrashing tentacles as only someone trained in Force acrobatics could.»

### Ch2–Ch5
7. **Ch2 · L720–722 · Leia (FEAT, sensing):** she senses Jaina and Zekk through their meld at long range. Jaina is «astonished by her mother's Force sensitivity».
8. **Ch3 · L834–962 · Luke vs. Ben, sparring (FEAT, holding back heavily).** Luke never ignites his saber; it has no power cell (L962). He uses a leg sweep (L840) and a nerve-kick disarm (L842–844), and «Luke somersaulted three meters up to land atop a balance beam.» (L844). Ben scores once by Force-shoving a wobble ball into Luke's chest and knocking him off the beam (L866). At L942 Luke, eyes closed, holds Ben's saber hilt motionless «two meters away» through the Force (the text has the OCR typos "thi hilt" and "mo tionless"). At L948 «Luke did not budge». Luke: «A Jedi's greatest weapon is his mind.» (L964). *Use:* this is a teaching spar and gives no ceiling for Luke.
9. **Ch5 · L1460 · Leia (FEAT, strength):** «After four years of Saha-style Jedi training, she was just that strong.» ("Saha" is an OCR error for Saba.) She drags a bound guard one-handed and earlier Force-jerks a sentry through a door (L1450).

### Ch6: Tresina Lobi vs. Alema + Lumiya (L1474–1707)
10. **Ch6 · L1632–1640 · Lobi vs. Alema (FEAT):** Lobi counters Alema's Force-leap stomp by «smashing the back of an elbow into Alema's knee» (L1632). «Lobi had never been a flashy fighter, but she was powerful and effective-and clearly deserving of her rank.» (L1634).
11. **Ch6 · L1656 · Alema (FEAT, lightning):** her lightning «caught her target square between the shoulder blades-and drove her to the ground». Lobi gets up, and Alema cannot redirect Lobi's thrown urn: «Lobi's Force grasp was too secure» (L1666).
12. **Ch6 · L1668–1674 · Lobi vs. Lumiya (FEAT, key):** Lobi was «slowly forcing Lumiya back» (L1668). She Force-pulls Alema into the whip's path (L1672), and «Lumiya's weapon arm-one of her many cybernetic parts- fell to the ground» (L1674). *Context:* this was **1v1 vs. Lumiya while Alema was recovering**. A Jedi Master was out-fighting Lumiya.
13. **Ch6 · L1688–1700 · Alema + Lumiya kill Lobi (2v1):** Alema's Force whisper: «Even you cannot defeat two of us.» (L1688). Lumiya's whip takes Lobi's legs (L1696) and Alema beheads her (L1700). *Context:* 2v1, and Lumiya fought on after losing a cybernetic arm.

### Ch7–Ch15
14. **Ch7 · L1832 · Leia (FEAT, combat):** «cloaking herself behind a basket of sapphire light and batting blaster bolts back». She kills three assassins and «used the Force to crush two more beneath a flying suit of heavy plexoid armor».
15. **Ch10 · L2388–2400 · Luke and Mara (FEAT, danger sense):** they detect a booby-trapped detonator on Lumiya's door keypad. Luke also spots the «Force-flash» used to blank cameras (L2338).
16. **Ch11 · L2922–2926 · Tenel Ka vs. Jacen (FEAT, TK contest):** Tenel Ka Force-pulls Jacen's hands off a keyboard, and he «pulled his hands free of her Force grasp» (L2926). Jacen wins easily.
17. **Ch13 · L3360–3420 · Jaina and Zekk vs. YVH battle droids (FEAT):** the narration says «Even Jedi were a poor match for the accuracy and ferocious firepower of YVH battle droids.» (L3360). Jaina Force-flips past two cannons (L3370–3372), hides by controlling her heartbeat (L3396–3410), and slide-cuts a YVH at the knee (L3416–3418). She takes shrapnel (L3420).
18. **Ch14 · L3756–3792 · Aurra Sing (FEAT):** clears a Renatyl drug with a Force trance (L3756) and kills with head shots (L3768, L3786, L3792). Leia deflects full-power bolts upward so she doesn't kill anyone (L3776).
19. **Ch16 · L4256 · Jacen (FEAT, concealment):** Luke cannot sense Jacen while Jacen is standing in front of him. See also L4352 and L4472 «Jacen was keeping his Force presence buried and unreadable».
20. **Ch18 · L4936–4942 · Ben (FEAT, concealment):** Ben «drew a veneer of calm over his Force presence so Jaina and Zekk would not sense his» lie (the text has the OCR typo "He" for "lie"). At L4942: «not even Jacen could always tell when he was lying».

### Ch19: Jacen vs. Aurra Sing aboard the *Anakin Solo* (L5046–5249)
21. **Ch19 · L5146–5150 · Jacen (FEAT, mind domination):** «expanding his Force presence into Sing's mind» (L5146), and «Sing fought back» but «Jacen had taken her by surprise. He had the power of his anger and his fear and his hatred behind him, and she simply was not strong enough.» (L5150). *Context:* he surprised her, and she is a minor Force-sensitive. Sing: «Don't ever touch me like that again.» (L5164).
22. **Ch19 · L5172–5214 · Jacen vs. Sing, melee (FEAT):** she breaks free when arming a detonator (L5172). Jacen summons the detonator mid-throw (L5174), takes knee strikes and toe-knife kicks (L5178–5192), and blocks her saber thrust toward his eye in a blade lock (L5180–5182). «But pain was an old friend of Jacen's» (L5194). He Force-pushes her, but she disarms him as she flies (L5198). He readies lightning, «his body began to crackle and burn with dark energy» (L5202), then holds it because Allana is watching (L5206). He cartwheels over her blade and dislocates her knee (L5210–5214). Allana's «safety stick» sedates her (L5228–5236). *Context:* a solo assassin, about 80 years old. Jacen was surprised, guarding his daughter, holding back on lightning, and took real damage. **Not a strong feat for the Caedus of later books.**

### Ch21: Luke + Mara vs. Lumiya + Alema at the Roqoo Depot cantina (L5658–5915)
23. **Ch21 · L5718 · Luke (FEAT):** Force-hurls Alema's grenade back. Alema is in Force shadow and Luke cannot feel her.
24. **Ch21 · L5736–5756 · Lumiya (FEAT):** the lightwhip butchers cantina patrons, and she uses the Force to turn the crowd's fear and anger on Luke (L5754).
25. **Ch21 · L5766–5776 · Luke vs. Lumiya, exchange (FEAT):** «Lumiya's counter was, of course, masterful.» (L5766; Alema POV). Luke cuts away a strand of the whip (L5768). L5776: Luke «made an unbelievable parry across his body with his short blade, then spun into a slashing, whirling advance», and Lumiya retreats behind bystanders (L5780).
26. **Ch21 · L5786–5790 · Lumiya + Alema maim Luke (FEAT, 2v1 + distraction):** Alema projects her anguish into Luke. «A bolt of surprise shot through the Force», and the whip wraps him «in a fiery cage». «The short blade went flying, taking along the hand that had been holding it» (his cybernetic hand), and his torso is lacerated. *Context:* 2v1, a surprise psychic attack, and Luke protecting bystanders.
27. **Ch21 · L5792–5840 · Mara vs. Alema (FEAT):** Mara dodges the dart on Luke's warning (L5792). Alema stabs through an Arcona at her (L5808), and Mara grazes Alema's lekku (L5810). «Alema was not much of a challenge» (L5832; Mara POV). Then «As overconfidence always does, Mara's proved costly» (L5834): Alema's talons pierce Mara's throat, and her lightning misses narrowly (L5836–5838). Mara's scissor takedown knocks Alema out (L5838–5840). Mara spares her and disarms Alema's dead-man vest (L5846–5848). **Result: Mara wins.**
28. **Ch21 · L5850–5862 · Luke vs. Lumiya, wounded (FEAT):** «He was calling on the Force to keep fighting» … «literally boiling his own cells. At most, he had another minute of fight in him» (L5850). He refuses to cut through a hostage (L5854), Force-shoves Lumiya into a spin (L5856), and wrenches the hostage free (L5858). His saber slips from his weakening grip (L5862).
29. **Ch21 · L5892–5900 · Luke beats Lumiya (FEAT):** falling back under the whip, Luke fires a dead spacer's blaster, «allowing the Force to guide his hand» (L5892). Lumiya goes down with her life-support girdle sparking (L5900). Mara: «You can't kill her!» (L5898), because of the dead-man vest. Mara: «I think we'd better run.» (L5912). **Result: Luke wins** while one-handed and bleeding out, against a 2v1 that was handled by splitting the pair.

### Ch22–Ch24
30. **Ch22 · L5988–5994, L6092 · Leia (FEAT, piloting + danger sense):** she flies the *Falcon* through a partial Reverse Slingshot around Megos.
31. **Ch23 · L6314–6318 · Jacen (FEAT, Force choke):** «Jacen made a pinching motion with his fingers, and Espara was suddenly too busy gasping for breath» (L6314). «Jacen froze them with a glance» (her aides, L6318). This is his first on-page Vader-style choke of an ally.
32. **Ch23 · L6366, L6450 · Jacen:** grabs Alema in the Force (L6366). He projects reassurance to Ben and Zekk across the battle zone (L6450).
33. **Ch24 · L6712–6722 · Ben wounds Zekk:** Ben ignites on Jaina; Zekk grabs him, a turbolaser shock jolts the ship, and the blade slashes Zekk. Jaina Force-lifts Zekk (L6748).

## 3. Statements and hype (STATEMENT: not feats)
- **S1 · L234 · Alema (POV):** «Alema could not best Jacen Solo in a straight duel-not with one half-useless arm and one clumsy half foot.» *Reliable as her self-assessment.*
- **S2 · L138 · Alema (POV):** «had it been Jacen lurking behind the door, she would have sensed nothing at all.» *Reliable on Jacen's stealth.*
- **S3 · L350, L362 · Lumiya → Jacen:** «Lumiya's tone was one of command-a Master to a student.» / «You are a servant to your emotions, not a master to them.» *Shows the hierarchy.*
- **S4 · L810 · Ben on Luke:** «The greatest sword handler in the galaxy». *Biased (his son), but consistent with the era.*
- **S5 · L826 · Ben:** «Jacen says I'm stronger in the Force than any of the academy's apprentices.» *Jacen flattering Ben; low reliability.*
- **S6 · L1008 · Luke:** «Jacen isn't ready to be a Master . . . and I don't think he ever will be.» Mara: «You can't dispute Jacen's Force knowledge.» (L1006). *Opinion.*
- **S7 · L1044 · narration:** the shoto was «built after nearly losing his life the first time he encountered Lumiya's lightwhip» (Jar'Kai). *Reliable history: Lumiya's whip once nearly killed Luke.*
- **S8 · L1496 · Jacen → Ben:** «Many Jedi Knights aren't strong enough to use the whole Force, but some are.» *Sith-recruiting rhetoric.*
- **S9 · L1556 · Lumiya:** «Tahiri is not a descendant of Anakin Skywalker» (on wanting Ben as an apprentice).
- **S10 · L2692 · Lumiya:** «he cared more about personal power than his responsibility to the galaxy.» (about Sidious). *Ideology.*
- **S11 · L2708 · Lumiya:** «Kyp Durron is too stubborn and unpredictable, Mara too committed to her attachments, your sister too ruled by emotion». Luke is «too bound by Jedi dogma» (L2712). *She names the Jedi she considered as candidates.*
- **S12 · L2716–2720 · Lumiya (key):** «I'm as much machine as human, and you know how that limits me.» Jacen replies that her powers «don't seem all that limited». Lumiya: «Neither did your grandfather's-except to the Emperor, whose power had no limit». She adds: «You have the potential to succeed. I don't.» *An in-universe Sith claim that cybernetics capped both her and Vader, and that Sidious had no limit. Useful for Vader and Caedus scaling.*
- **S13 · L2728 · Lumiya:** «Your defeat of Tsavong Lah in personal combat proved you were not afraid to use great power when necessary.»
- **S14 · L2732:** Vergere helped the Yuuzhan Vong capture Jacen to isolate him from Luke. *A retcon, from Lumiya.*
- **S15 · L2626 · Jacen (POV):** his Force-visions are «a sign of his growing power».
- **S16 · L3046 · Aurra Sing:** «I specialize in Jedi». Also L1924: «There is nothing I love more than killing Jedi.» *Hype; Jacen handles her.*
- **S17 · L4220 · Mara (key):** «If you're right-if Jacen has been making a fool of me-he won't be easy to handle. It'll take both of us.» *Mara's own judgment: she and Luke would need to team up against Jacen. Strong framing for Mara < Jacen.*
- **S18 · L4228–4242 · Mara/Luke:** «We'll have to end it.» (L4228), Luke: «Lumiya is going down.» (L4242).
- **S19 · L4326 · Luke (POV):** «Jacen Solo had fallen not because he was selfish, Luke realized, but because he was selfless». *Authorial thesis.*
- **S20 · L4574 · Lumiya:** «He's arranging a fight between me and Luke.» … «If I kill Luke, it creates an opening for Jacen to take over leadership of the Jedi order.»
- **S21 · L4594–4638 · Alema/Lumiya (key):** Alema: «Master Skywalker is not someone to trifle with» (L4594). Lumiya: «I have fought him before, you know.» (L4596) and «being killed is a more likely outcome than I would prefer» (L4638). *Lumiya expects to lose to Luke. High reliability; it comes from the Sith's own POV.*
- **S22 · L4578 · Lumiya on Jacen:** «a thernbee-sly, ruthless, and deadly. I couldn't be more proud.»
- **S23 · L4670 · Lumiya:** «There is a plan-a plan that will be carried out whether or not I survive.» *Points ahead to the One Sith and to Exile.*
- **S24 · L5696 · Mara:** «Jacen is too powerful already».
- **S25 · L5700 · narration:** Luke had captured Raynar «in personal combat».
- **S26 · L6248 · Jacen (POV):** «Lumiya needed him more than he needed her.»
- **S27 · L6466 · Alema to Jacen:** "You have already proved that you are too powerful and smart for us" (the text has the OCR typo "MS" for "us"). *She is flattering him, but it matches S1.*
- **S28 · L7002 · Ben:** «No one's as strong in the Force...» (about Jacen; cut off). *Biased.*
- **S29 · L7010–7014 · Luke/Mara:** the bomb went off «after we left the cantina» (L7010). Mara: «It took nearly two minutes for that bomb to detonate.» (L7012). Luke won't believe Lumiya is dead «until» he cremates her body (L7014; the text prints "1 won't believe" with OCR digit "1"). *Lumiya's survival is left open for Exile.*
