## 5. Scaling connections

### A. Luke vs. Lumiya
- Lumiya herself expects to lose (S21). Luke out-parries her in an even exchange (feat 25). She only lands the maiming blow because Alema projects distress into Luke (feat 26), and Luke still finishes her one-handed (feats 28–29).
- History: her whip once nearly killed him (S7). **Luke > Lumiya** clearly. Compare Lobi, a Council Master below Luke, who was already beating Lumiya 1v1 (feat 12).

### B. Mara vs. Alema
- Mara rates her as «not much of a challenge» (L5832), loses ground only to overconfidence, and wins decisively (feat 27). **Mara > Alema.** Alema's equalizers are stealth (feat 2) and dirty tactics.

### C. Jacen vs. Lumiya
- Lumiya is still the "Master" in tone (S3) and out-senses Jacen in the Prologue (feat 4). But she says cybernetics cap her and that Jacen has potential she lacks (S12), and Jacen thinks she needs him more (S26). Taken with Bloodlines (L6243, L6305, L8543): **Jacen > Lumiya in power; Lumiya > Jacen in situational sensing and craft at this point.**

### D. Jacen vs. Luke and Mara
- Jacen's concealment beats Luke's senses (feat 19). Mara says it would take both of them (S17) and «Jacen is too powerful already» (S24, L5696). No direct fight. This confirms the Bloodlines picture (Luke > Jacen in power, Jacen ≥ Luke at hiding) and points to *Sacrifice*. See `[local path]` §5A, `caedus-legends-quotes.txt`, and `mara-jade-quotes.txt`.

### E. Caedus vs. Vader, Sidious, Krayt
- S12 is a useful in-universe line: cybernetics limited Vader (and Lumiya), and Sidious's power «had no limit» (L2720). Jacen's choke of Espara (feat 31) and firing on his own parents (Ch23) are explicit Vader echoes. No direct power comparison. See `vader-legends-quotes.txt`, `sidious-legends-quotes.txt`, and `debates-sidious-plagueis-tenebrous-vs-krayt-caedus-valkorion.md`.

### F. Common opponents
- **Aurra Sing:** prequel-era Jedi hunter (she saw Anakin's Boonta Eve race, L3070). Jacen beats her while surprised and holding back (D6). A modest data point.
- **Tsavong Lah:** Jacen defeated him «in personal combat» (S13, L2728).
- **Tresina Lobi:** a calibration Master who was beating Lumiya 1v1 (D3).

### Quick ladder (this novel only)
Luke > Jacen (raw power behind Luke; best stealth) ≥ Mara > Lumiya (lightwhip, crowd manipulation; cyber-limited) ≈ Lobi > Alema (stealth specialist, crippled) > Jaina ≈ Zekk > Leia > Aurra Sing > Ben.

---

## 6. Verdict-relevant takeaways
1. **Luke beats Lumiya** at 40 ABY, even in a 2v1 ambush with hostages, after losing his hand. Lumiya predicted that she would probably lose (L4638).
2. **Mara beats Alema** decisively; her only scare came from overconfidence (L5834).
3. **Lumiya was being out-fought by a Council-level Master** (Lobi) 1v1 (L1668–1674). She is a manipulator and ambusher, not a top-tier duelist.
4. **Mara frames Jacen as beyond her alone** (L4220, L5696). That supports Caedus > Mara before *Sacrifice*.
5. Jacen's combat showing here (vs. Sing) is unremarkable. His strengths are mind domination, concealment, and willingness to use dark techniques: the choke, and firing on the *Falcon*.
6. No Kyp, Corran, or Boba Fett data.

---

## Story and prose notes

### SP1. Plot summary, in order
- **Prologue–Ch5 (L70–1473):** Alema tries to assassinate Jacen on Tenupe and discovers he is Lumiya's apprentice. Wedge commands Corellia's secret fleet at Kiris. Han and Leia go to Hapes as Corellian envoys. Luke spars with Ben. Jaina, now discharged, joins Bwua'tu's plan. A Hapan noble plot (the Heritage Council and the Gejjen deal) frames the Solos at the Queen's Pageant.
- **Ch6–Ch10 (L1474–2609):** Alema and Lumiya kill Master Lobi. Assassins strike at the Hapan palace and "Nashtah" appears. Luke and Mara trace Lumiya to GAG through Astrotours.
- **Ch11–Ch15 (L2610–4115):** Jacen's «Mine.» vision. Lumiya on sacrifice. Jacen issues a warrant for his parents. Jaina and Zekk fight YVH droids at the Galney villa. On Telkur Station, Jag Fel is hunting Alema and Nashtah is revealed as Aurra Sing. Han and Leia choose Tenel Ka over Corellia.
- **Ch16–Ch18 (L4116–5044):** Luke confronts Jacen aboard the *Anakin Solo* and orders Ben out of GAG. Jacen sets up the Roqoo Depot trap, sending Lumiya against the Skywalkers. Lumiya knowingly accepts, wearing a dead-man vest. Ben and the Rover crew divert to Hapes.
- **Ch19–Ch21 (L5046–5915):** Sing attacks Allana aboard the *Anakin Solo* and Jacen defeats her. Han betrays the usurper fleet's coordinates. Roqoo Depot: the Skywalkers beat Lumiya and Alema.
- **Ch22–Epilogue (L5916–7066):** The Battle of Hapes. Bwua'tu arrives late, and the Rover crew sacrifice themselves to warn Tenel Ka. Alema reports to Jacen. Jacen concludes that «Lumiya had been right» (L6510) and orders the *Falcon* fired on (L6516–6524). The Noghri die (L6602). Ben wounds Zekk. In the Epilogue, Jacen is hailed as a hero, refuses to lift the warrant, and lies that he has «never even met» Lumiya (L7016). Luke takes Ben home.

### SP2. Character profiles
- **Jacen:** his loyalty to Allana is the last thing he will not sacrifice: «there were some things he would never sacrifice-not even if it meant saving the galaxy.» (L6314). By the end he is turning that on his parents: «There's one law, Jaina, and it applies to everyone- even to Solos.» (L6990).
- **Lumiya:** a patient strategist who knowingly walks into Jacen's trap (S20) and is proud of him for it.
- **Alema:** broken, hateful, and speaking in the Killik "we". She serves «the Balance» (L6392) and blackmails Jacen over Allana's paternity (L6380–6394, L6494).
- **Luke:** grows suspicious but remains fair: «You're very quick to believe the worst about someone you love. That's a problem.» (L6880).
- **Mara:** shifts from defending Jacen to preparing to «end it» (L4228), then softens again in the Epilogue (L6974).
- **Ben:** a zealous GAG loyalist: «That was why the galaxy needed someone like Jacen» (L6118). He tries to arrest Han (L6568).
- **Han and Leia:** «my reputation doesn't matter» (L5622). They choose conscience over Corellia.
- **Tenel Ka:** a modernizing queen. She can't feel Jacen when he closes off (L6952) and finds him «just a little frightening» (L6984).

### SP3. Themes
- **Sacrifice:** «Great strength demands great sacrifice.» (L2736). Jacen's search for what he must sacrifice drives the book, and he ends it firing on his parents.
- **Family vs. duty:** «Lumiya had been right. Jacen had put loyalty to family above his mission.» (L6510).
- **Selflessness as the road to the dark side:** S19.
- **Trust and suspicion:** the Epilogue's mirrored accusations (L6880, L6972).

### SP4. Notable prose passages
- L2632–2646: the «Mine.» vision over Hapes.
- L5194: «But pain was an old friend of Jacen's»
- L5850: Luke «literally boiling his own cells».
- L6524: Jacen sees Allana's trust and orders «Assign our best gunnery team and fire away.»
- L6788: «The hyperdrive finally kicked in, and the stars stretched to lines one more time.»
- L7016: «After all, I've never even met the woman.»

### SP5. Worldbuilding stated in the text
- The *Anakin Solo*: black hull, gravity-well generator, cloaking (L4132), and long-range turbolasers that drain ship power (L6254). Pellaeon shared the tech with Hapan Battle Dragons (L6278).
- Galactic Loyalty Act "Suspicious Silence Provision" (L2132).
- Aurra Sing's history: >75 years earlier she was a Jedi trainee taken by pirates, later captured by Aayla Secura and imprisoned on Oovo IV (L3928–3934).
- Alema's memory cord and her survival on Tenupe (L3612–3632).

### SP6. Timeline
- 40 ABY. Han is «not even seventy yet» (L402) and «already older than Obi-Wan Kenobi had been» (L5256). Allana is about 4. LotF 3 of 9: after *Bloodlines*, before *Exile*. Lumiya's survival and Luke's suspicion carry into *Exile*.

## Character analyses (added 2026-10-17 under the 7+ characters rule)

*Method:* built from the verified feat/statement log above (Sections 2–5). `L####` = line in `2f7702c1…txt` (chapter given). Quotes in «…» are verbatim and were re-checked by script after writing. No wiki material is used. **Before:** 8 short story profiles (SP2), none with feats, fights and scaling. **Added:** 12 analyses.

### 1. Jacen Solo
- **Role/arc:** GAG colonel commanding the *Anakin Solo* and Allana's secret father. His loyalty to Allana is the one thing he won't sacrifice (L6314). By the end he chokes an ally and fires on his parents.
- **Key feats:** Alema says that if he had been behind the door «she would have sensed nothing at all» (Prologue, L138). Luke cannot sense him face to face (Ch16, L4256). He dominates Aurra Sing's mind by surprise: «she simply was not strong enough» (Ch19, L5150). He chokes Espara: «Jacen made a pinching motion with his fingers» (Ch23, L6314).
- **Statements:** Mara: «If you're right-if Jacen has been making a fool of me-he won't be easy to handle. It'll take both of us.» (Ch16, L4220) and «Jacen is too powerful already» (Ch21, L5696). Alema: «Alema could not best Jacen Solo in a straight duel» (L234). Lumiya calls him «a thernbee-sly, ruthless, and deadly. I couldn't be more proud.» (L4578). Luke: «Jacen isn't ready to be a Master . . . and I don't think he ever will be.» (L1008).
- **Notable quotes:** «There's one law, Jaina, and it applies to everyone- even to Solos.» (L6990).
- **Fights/results:** Alema's prologue ambush. He missed her dart and Lumiya saved him (L320–322). Beats Aurra Sing (Ch19, L5146–5236), but he was surprised, held back his lightning (L5206), took knee and knife strikes, and Allana sedated Sing at the end. Pulls free of Tenel Ka's Force grip easily (Ch11, L2926).
- **Scaling:** stealth and mind domination are elite. His saber showing is modest. Mara's statements put him above her alone, and Luke remains above him in power.

### 2. Luke Skywalker
- **Role/arc:** hunts Lumiya, fighting Jar'Kai with a shoto. He loses his cybernetic hand at Roqoo and still wins.
- **Key feats:** holds Ben's hilt motionless two meters away with his eyes closed (Ch3, L942) and «somersaulted three meters up» onto a beam (L844). In the cantina he hurls Alema's grenade back while unable to feel her (Ch21, L5718). He «made an unbelievable parry across his body with his short blade, then spun into a slashing, whirling advance» against Lumiya (L5776). Wounded and «literally boiling his own cells» (L5850), he keeps fighting and drops Lumiya with a Force-guided blaster shot (L5892–5900).
- **Statements:** Ben: «The greatest sword handler in the galaxy» (L810). Alema: «Master Skywalker is not someone to trifle with» (L4594). Lumiya: «being killed is a more likely outcome than I would prefer» (L4638).
- **Notable quotes:** «A Jedi's greatest weapon is his mind.» (L964). «You're very quick to believe the worst about someone you love. That's a problem.» (L6880).
- **Fights/results:** dominates Ben in a spar while holding back (Ch3). With Mara, beats Lumiya and Alema (Ch21). He was maimed only through a 2v1 psychic distraction (L5786–5790).
- **Scaling:** Luke > Lumiya clearly. The top of the book.

### 3. Mara Jade Skywalker
- **Role/arc:** moves from defending Jacen to preparing to «end it» (L4228), then softens again (L6974).
- **Key feats:** detects the booby trap on Lumiya's door with Luke (Ch10, L2388–2400). Beats Alema with a scissor takedown after her throat is pierced (Ch21, L5836–5840), then disarms Alema's dead-man vest (L5846–5848).
- **Statements:** her own POV: «Alema was not much of a challenge» (L5832). Narration: «As overconfidence always does, Mara's proved costly» (L5834). To Luke: «You can't dispute Jacen's Force knowledge.» (L1006).
- **Fights/results:** Mara > Alema, a decisive win.
- **Scaling:** well above Alema. Her own judgment that it would take her and Luke together (L4220) places her below Jacen.

### 4. Lumiya
- **Role/arc:** a Sith Master who walks into Jacen's trap knowingly to clear his path: «If I kill Luke, it creates an opening for Jacen» (L4574). She is shot and her fate is left open (L7014).
- **Key feats:** lightwhip skill against Quarren guards (Prologue, L232). Senses Alema's hidden dart when Jacen does not and saves him (L320–322). Turns a cantina crowd's fear on Luke (Ch21, L5754).
- **Statements:** her own: «I'm as much machine as human, and you know how that limits me.» Vader's limits were «Neither did your grandfather's-except to the Emperor, whose power had no limit» (Ch12, L2716–2720). «I have fought him before, you know.» (L4596). Alema's POV in the cantina: «Lumiya's counter was, of course, masterful.» (L5766).
- **Fights/results:** a Council Master, Tresina Lobi, was «slowly forcing Lumiya back» 1v1 and severed her cybernetic arm (Ch6, L1668–1674). She killed Lobi only 2v1 with Alema (L1696–1700). Luke beats her (Ch21).
- **Scaling:** below a Council-level duelist in a straight fight. She is an ambusher and manipulator, so Luke > Lobi ≳ Lumiya.

### 5. Alema Rar
- **Role/arc:** the crippled last of the Gorog. She serves «the Balance» (L6392) and blackmails Jacen over Allana's paternity.
- **Key feats:** Force-crushes a Nikto: «lifted him off his feet and squeezed until she heard the happy crackle of crushed cartilage» (Prologue, L180). Lomi Plo's «slippery presence», by which she vanishes from memory when out of sight (L224). Lightning drops Lobi (Ch6, L1656).
- **Statements:** self-assessment, she cannot beat Jacen «in a straight duel-not with one half-useless arm and one clumsy half foot» (L234). Prologue: she «knew she lacked the power to kill Jacen with one attack» (L340).
- **Notable quote:** «Even you cannot defeat two of us.» (L1688).
- **Fights/results:** with Lumiya, kills Lobi (2v1). Loses to Mara (Ch21). Pierces Mara's throat during the fight (L5836).
- **Scaling:** a stealth specialist below Mara, Lobi and Jacen. Dangerous only with surprise.

### 6. Tresina Lobi
- **Role/arc:** a Council Master investigating Lumiya, killed in Ch6.
- **Key feats:** counters Alema's Force-leap with an elbow to the knee (L1632). Alema cannot redirect her thrown urn: «Lobi's Force grasp was too secure» (L1666). Takes Lumiya's weapon arm (L1674).
- **Statement:** «Lobi had never been a flashy fighter, but she was powerful and effective-and clearly deserving of her rank.» (L1634).
- **Fights/results:** winning 1v1 against Lumiya, then killed 2v1.
- **Scaling:** the calibration piece. A Council Master ≥ Lumiya 1v1.

### 7. Ben Skywalker
- **Role/arc:** a zealous GAG loyalist at 13. «That was why the galaxy needed someone like Jacen» (L6118). He tries to arrest Han (L6568).
- **Key feats:** knocks Luke off the beam with a Force-shoved wobble ball (Ch3, L866). Hides a lie from Jaina and Zekk (Ch18, L4936). Narration: «not even Jacen could always tell when he was lying» (L4942).
- **Statements:** Ben: «Jacen says I'm stronger in the Force than any of the academy's apprentices.» (L826). This is Jacen's flattery and low reliability.
- **Fights/results:** loses the spar with Luke. Accidentally slashes Zekk when the ship is hit (Ch24, L6712–6722).
- **Scaling:** an apprentice-level fighter, though already good at masking.

### 8. Jaina Solo (with Zekk)
- **Role/arc:** a discharged pilot turned Jedi agent, battle-melded with Zekk.
- **Key feats:** against YVH droids, where narration says «Even Jedi were a poor match for the accuracy and ferocious firepower of YVH battle droids» (Ch13, L3360). She Force-flips past cannons, controls her heartbeat to hide (L3396–3410), and slide-cuts a YVH at the knee (L3416–3418). She lifts the wounded Zekk (L6748).
- **Fights/results:** cripples the droids but takes shrapnel (L3420).
- **Scaling:** a strong Knight. She sits below Mara and Jacen here.

### 9. Leia Organa Solo
- **Role/arc:** a Jedi Knight who chooses conscience over Corellia («my reputation doesn't matter», L5622).
- **Key feats:** senses Jaina and Zekk's meld at range, and Jaina is «astonished by her mother's Force sensitivity» (Ch2, L720–722). Strength from training: «she was just that strong» (Ch5, L1460). At the Hapan palace she kills three assassins and «used the Force to crush two more beneath a flying suit of heavy plexoid armor» (Ch7, L1832). Deflects full-power bolts upward so she kills no one (Ch14, L3776). Flies the *Falcon* through a partial Reverse Slingshot (Ch22, L5988–5994).
- **Scaling:** a competent Knight who handles non-Force assassins well.

### 10. Aurra Sing ("Nashtah")
- **Role/arc:** a prequel-era Jedi hunter, about 80, hired against Tenel Ka's family.
- **Key feats:** clears a Renatyl drug with a Force trance (Ch14, L3756). Head-shot kills (L3768–3792). In the duel she blocks into a blade lock and disarms Jacen while flying from his push (Ch19, L5180–5198).
- **Statements:** «I specialize in Jedi» (L3046). «There is nothing I love more than killing Jedi.» (L1924). After the mind attack: «Don't ever touch me like that again.» (L5164).
- **Fights/results:** loses to Jacen. Allana sedates her (L5228–5236).
- **Scaling:** a minor Force-sensitive and a skilled killer. She gives Jacen a real fight only because he holds back.

### 11. Tenel Ka
- **Role/arc:** a modernizing Hapan Queen Mother. She finds Jacen «just a little frightening» (L6984).
- **Feats:** Force-pulls Jacen's hands off a keyboard, and he «pulled his hands free of her Force grasp» (Ch11, L2926). She cannot feel him when he closes off (L6952).
- **Scaling:** well below Jacen in Force strength.

### 12. Han Solo
- **Role/arc:** a Corellian envoy who turns on Corellia to save Tenel Ka. Jacen fires on the *Falcon* (Ch23).
- **Fights:** with Leia, repels the Hapan palace assassins (Ch7).
- **Scaling:** a non-Force pilot and fighter. He serves as context for the Solo family arc.

<!-- 021-legacy-of-the-force/lotf-4-exile-notes.md (verbatim copy) -->

# Legacy of the Force 4: Exile (novel), feats and scaling notes

**read up to: COMPLETE (Front matter to Ch20, L1–L6812)**

- **Book:** *Star Wars: Legacy of the Force: Exile* by Aaron Allston (2007). Book 4 of 9 in Legacy of the Force (LotF).
- **Continuity:** Legends, 40 ABY. It begins days after *Tempest*: «Several days earlier he had ordered the long-range turbolasers of the Anakin Solo to fire upon the Millennium Falcon» (L505). Ben turns 14 during the book (L4613).
- **Source file:** `[local path]` (6,812 lines; OCR by "Emesen"). A line-identical working copy is at `[local path]`.
- **Method:** I read every line in order, in chunks of about 600–1,100 lines. «…» quotes are exact, and OCR typos are kept or noted. A script checked every «…» against the cited L-numbers (see the footer).
- **Structure:** Dramatis personae (L19–43), then Chapters 1–20 with location headers. There is no prologue or epilogue. Alternating threads: Jacen/Lumiya; Luke/Mara; Han/Leia/Lando and the Wedge/Corran/Booster circle aboard the *Errant Venture*; Ben's solo mission on Ziost; Jag/Jaina/Zekk hunting Alema.
- **Feat vs. statement:** **FEAT** = shown happening. **STATEMENT** = a claim or belief. *(POV)* = narrated belief.
- **Headline:** Exile is a spy and intrigue book with limited saber combat. The key items: Lumiya's long-range possession and phantom feat (Ch10); **Luke vs. Lumiya** at Gilatter VIII (brief, no decision; Lumiya stands down); **Leia vs. Alema** (Errant Venture trap and Gilatter); Ben's Ziost survival story with TK kills; and **two characters saying Lumiya fought Luke "to a standstill" at Roqoo** (L3545, L4815). Lumiya's own POV contradicts that (L865).

---

## 1. Cast who fight or use the Force

| Character | Role in this book | Force user? |
|---|---|---|
| Jacen Solo | GAG colonel; manipulates Luke; tests Ben through Lumiya; nears his Sith naming | Yes |
| Lumiya | Recovers from Luke's 5 blaster shots; long-range puppeteer (Klauskin, Bothans, Commenor); Jacen's teacher | Yes |
| Luke Skywalker | Grand Master with a new cybernetic hand; hunts Lumiya; Gilatter duel | Yes |
| Mara Jade Skywalker | Investigator (Lumiya's quarters, the Brisha lead); StealthX pilot | Yes |
| Ben Skywalker | 13→14; Jacen's secret mission to Ziost; survival, TK kills, takes a Sith ship | Yes |
| Leia Organa Solo | Mind tricks, the reversed-dart feat, fights Alema at Gilatter | Yes |
| Alema Rar | Stows away with Lavint; hunts the Solos; memory-erasing presence | Yes |
| Corran Horn | Rescues Wedge; plucks Alema's dart from the air | Yes |
| Kyp Durron, Kyle Katarn, Cilghal | Council; Kyp gives a TK statement | Yes |
| Jaina Solo, Zekk | Alema task force with Jag Fel | Yes |
| Han, Lando, Wedge, Iella, Myri, Mirax, Booster | Intelligence ring aboard the *Errant Venture* | No |
| Uran Lavint | Smuggler, Jacen's victim, then Alema's host | No |
| Turr Phennir | Confederation Supreme Commander (ex-Imperial) | No |
| Boba Fett, Tenel Ka | **Absent** | — |

---

## 2. Combat and Force feats, in chapter order

1. **Ch1 · L175 · Kyp (FEAT, minor TK):** jokingly moves the *Jade Shadow*: «the Jade Shadow vibrated for a moment, moving under the pressure he exerted with the Force.»
2. **Ch4 · L847–861 · Jacen + Lumiya (FEAT, long-range sensing and projection):** Jacen, levitating and «fully open to the Force», locates Lumiya across the galaxy (L849–851). Lumiya projects a phantom of herself to talk with him (L853–859). Jacen envies the technique (L859).
3. **Ch6 · L1315–1321 · Corran (FEAT, minor):** in disguise, he drops four stories and kicks a sniper unconscious.
4. **Ch6 · L1511–1555 · Leia (FEAT, mind trick):** gets a GA lieutenant to hand over blockade access codes. «He was even more weakminded than I'm used to.» (L1555).
5. **Ch7 · L1575 · Lumiya's injuries (context):** «Not long ago, Luke Skywalker had shot her five times with a blaster. Two of those shots had hit prosthetic limbs». The other three «had found meat», and despite Force healing trances and the Emperor's alterations, «she was far from recovered.» She is about 60 (L1583).
6. **Ch7 · L1799–1823, L1861–1867 · Leia (FEAT):** senses CorSec massing outside, cuts an escape hole in the floor with her saber, then mind-tricks a garbage-loader pilot at range without being seen. «the effort to impose her will on someone at range, without benefit of the target being able to see her eyes or hear her voice, had taken a toll on her.» (L1867).
7. **Ch8 · L2101–2111 · Ben (FEAT, TK):** Force-flattens a training ball to jam the droid and resists a Force-pull on his saber.
8. **Ch9 · L2353–2377 · Jacen deceives Luke (FEAT, concealment):** «Even if Jacen had been lying, he was sure Luke would not have been able to detect it.» (L2355; Jacen POV). «Causing people to think and feel what he wanted them to, even without resorting to the Force, was becoming easier and easier ... even with difficult subjects like the almighty Luke Skywalker.» (L2377).
9. **Ch10 · L2581–2583 · Mara (FEAT, reflexes):** bends backward as a meter-long dart trap fires from Lumiya's closet. Earlier she defuses a bomb and trihexalon trap (L2509–2517).
10. **Ch10 · L2713–2823 · Lumiya (FEAT, key: long-range possession and phantom).** From her asteroid near Bimmiel she finds Klauskin's mind on Coruscant, a mind she «had touched many times and reshaped» (L2717). «The enemy's techniques were nowhere near as effective as hers.» (L2721). She takes over a sleeping Quarren nurse, «Like a spectral parasite» (L2731), then appears as Klauskin's dead wife, opens his door, and frees him (L2761–2813). *Cost:* «That's when the pain and weakness hit hard. She spasmed» (L2817). She wonders, «Would she have died?» (L2823). *Context:* still wounded; galactic range; she calls it an «elaborate Sith technique» (L2725).
11. **Ch10 · L2859–2865, L2899 · Ben (FEAT):** snaps a glow-rod pod free (with strain), floats his saber over the customs wall, and Force-intimidates a passenger: «See what I become.» (L2899).
12. **Ch11 · L3075–3111 · Ben (FEAT):** a mental suggestion that he got into the lift, which leaves him «sweating through his garments» (L3081). He TK-cranks a window, slips, and Force-arrests his fall (L3111).
13. **Ch12 · L3347–3353 · Ben:** punches a mechanic and breaks her jaw. «Jacen would have forgiven me if I'd killed you» (L3353).
14. **Ch12 · L3675–3709 · Klauskin (Lumiya's puppet):** murders Captain Biurk and vents the *Shamunaar*'s crew to blind the Bothawui watch. This is Lumiya's possession paying off.
15. **Ch14 · L3869 · Luke (FEAT, sensing):** Alema is hidden in casino shadow and about to fire, but Luke «stood and looked straight at her». She holds off. Her later dart hits an Ortolan instead of Mara (L3887–3889).
16. **Ch14 · L4033–4085 · Alema vs. Lando (FEAT):** she TK-yanks his blaster and cane (L4033, L4055). His stun cane, rated for «a good-sized Wookiee» (L4063), only holds her briefly: she «was still struggling against the shocks» (L4067). Then she wipes Lando's memory as he flees (L4073–4085).
17. **Ch14 · L4107–4111 · Luke + Mara (FEAT, piloting):** a StealthX torpedo run cripples a Nebulon-B frigate.
18. **Ch15 · L4259–4271 · Ben (FEAT, TK):** lifts the R2 out of the Y-wing (it is hard, and he sways afterward).
19. **Ch17 · L5117–5133, L5187 · Ben vs. a TIE (FEAT, TK):** summons a blaster from a distance he had never managed before (L5117), then floats a stone into the TIE's path. The pilot: «He used Jedi magic to hurl a quarter-ton slab of stone at me.» (L5187). The TIE retreats damaged (L5131–5133).
20. **Ch18 · L5353–5365 · Ben (FEAT):** kills a nek with his saber, then fires a blaster «Aiming through the Force» without looking and wounds two more (L5361–5365).
21. **Ch18 · L5555–5643 · The Errant Venture trap on Alema (FEAT, several):** Corran «plucked the dart from the air» (L5559). Alema throws her saber to cut down a chandelier, and Zekk catches it with the Force (L5573–5577). **Leia:** «Leia raised one hand. Alema felt the blowgun twitch-and the dart within it shot backward, straight into her mouth.» (L5591–5593). Alema cuts through a turbolift floor (L5619–5621). Jag's armor stops her dart and he shoots her, breaking her clavicle (L5639–5641). She TK-hurls Jag into cargo crates (L5643) and escapes in Lavint's ship (L5659). *Context:* a 5-on-1 ambush. Alema is crippled but still escapes.
22. **Ch19 · L6043–6061 · Ben kills a TIE (FEAT, TK):** brings down wall-top stones, «Some weighed a quarter ton or more» (L6055), from more than 100 m onto the TIE, destroying it. «It didn't occur to Ben to be sorry about the being whose life he had just taken.» (L6069).
23. **Ch19 · L6147–6203 · Ben (FEAT):** commands a buried Sith ship to rise («Come out!», L6147), then flies it by Force-intent.
24. **Ch20 · L6319–6323 · Jacen → Luke (FEAT, telepathy):** Jacen projects «It's a trap! Mines!» upward. Luke, in a StealthX hundreds of km away, relays it: «He's visualizing mines.» (L6323). Leia, outside the system, feels it too (L6363).
25. **Ch20 · L6391 · Jacen (FEAT):** «He leapt above the torrent of blaster shots», landing behind the guards, «and two of them were suddenly headless.»
26. **Ch20 · L6529 · Luke + Mara (FEAT):** Luke's blade cuts five or six blaster barrels in one circle, and Mara Force-sweeps half a dozen agents.
27. **Ch20 · L6541–6595 · Luke vs. Lumiya, Gilatter (FEAT, brief duel):** Luke fights with saber plus shoto (L6541), and Lumiya dodges Han's shot (L6543). Luke presses in with the shoto, and Lumiya TK-lifts a severed head into the blow (L6555). Luke TK-whips a tablecloth at her back; she cuts it and attacks, and Luke deflects whip tendrils with both blades (L6559). Luke leaps a sweep (L6563). Then Lumiya **stands down**, offers her hand, and Luke takes it: «And nothing happened.» (L6595). *Result:* no decision. Both are fully healed and in the open; she chose to disengage.
28. **Ch20 · L6545–6617 · Leia vs. Alema (FEAT):** Alema trips Leia with a TK-shoved table (L6545) and «casually caught» Han's bolt (L6551). Leia «swung an almost ceaseless flurry of blows at Alema, but the Twi'lek Jedi continued backing away, fighting a defensive action, never trying to attack. It was unlike her.» (L6601). *Context:* Alema is stalling for her ship to ram the dome, and she has a broken clavicle from Ch18. Leia is on top but there is no decision.
29. **Ch20 · L6639–6649 · Leia + Luke:** Leia Force-shoves Han to the exit and is nearly pulled into space. Luke's grappling line saves her.
30. **Ch20 · L6425–6469 · Ben, Ziost ship (FEAT):** «Ben applied himself through the Force and saw the ball change its arc» into the freighter's open hangar and out through its stern (L6465–6467), crippling the pirate ship.
31. **Ch20 · L6735 · Jacen (FEAT, concealment):** «he did concentrate on diminishing his own presence in the Force» when Lumiya tells him to.

---

## 3. Statements and hype (STATEMENT: not feats)
- **S1 · L165 · Mara on Luke:** «he's dropped from being the most accomplished lightsaber artist in the galaxy to, well, still being the most accomplished, just a little less so for the time being.» *Joking but sincere. Luke is still the best duelist alive, with a slight handicap from the new hand.*
- **S2 · L85 · Jacen to a dream-Leia:** «you're not a match for any Jedi Knight who's been fighting and training constantly throughout his career ... because you haven't.» *Jacen's view of Leia's level, in a dream.*
- **S3 · L589 · Omas:** Jacen «possesses skills and power that not even most confirmed Masters can match.» *A politician's claim. Luke: Jacen «lacks the emotional maturity» (L583). Mara: «It's not a matter of power, either.» (L591).*
- **S4 · L633 · Kyp:** as a teenager he could «reach into the gravity well of a gas giant and pull a spacecraft out of it», and today «I'm no weaker in the Force, and I'm a lot more skilled». *Self-report; his Sun Crusher feat.*
- **S5 · L861–865 · Lumiya (key):** «I am healing. Here I can summon my strength. Your uncle hurt me.» and «When ever we meet, I expect him to hurt me. He will probably do so until I die». *Lumiya admits Luke beats her every time. High reliability: it is her own words, said to her apprentice.*
- **S6 · L875 · Jacen:** «Ben is my apprentice. Though I realized not long ago that I can sacrifice him if I have to.»
- **S7 · L995 · Jag on Alema:** «She can scrub herself from the short-term memory of people». Cilghal later develops a counter-technique (L3761).
- **S8 · L2211 · Jacen (to Ben, part of the setup):** the Amulet of Kalara makes its wearer «invisible in the Force. Even his use of the Force is invisible to other Force-users.» *Part of a test; the amulet is real dark-side jewelry (L4553).*
- **S9 · L3525 · Luke:** Lumiya called herself Dark Lady «after Emperor Palpatine and my father were no longer around to slap her down for presumption.»
- **S10 · L3545 · Luke (POV):** Ben may be targeted by «one of the galaxy's deadliest killers-a woman who had fought Luke to a standstill mere weeks before.» *Luke is generous about Roqoo here. Contradicted by S5 and by the Tempest text, where Luke won, though he was crippled by the 2v1.*
- **S11 · L4815 · Zekk:** «I don't think the three of us are a match for Lumiya. She fought the Grand Master to a standstill. She's Master level.» *Secondhand; the same framing.*
- **S12 · L4711 · Jacen:** «I'm a Jedi. It would be next to impossible for one of my senior officers to deceive me that way.» *Irony: Lumiya used a GAG passcode.*
- **S13 · L5035–5049 · Lumiya (POV):** Ben protecting Kiara is «not Sith thinking»; «He was just too much like his father.» Then «Kill them» (L5039), and «One more reason for him to kill her if he found out about it.» (L5049). *She expects Luke would kill her.*
- **S14 · L5445 · Leia:** «Alema's a Jedi who thinks like an assassin.»
- **S15 · L5651 · Han:** Alema had stopped jamming the holocams: «I bet it costs too much energy or concentration.»
- **S16 · L6565–6581 · Lumiya to Luke:** «Yes, I've tried to kill you-but that was professional. Not personal.»; «We belong to rival schools, Luke. That's all.»; «If I'd wanted to hurt you tonight, I would have killed your nephew instead of letting him flee.» *Manipulative and boastful. Jacen had in fact run off. Luke senses «only peaceful intent» (L6591) and later thinks her gentleness was real (L6709).*
- **S17 · L6755–6759 · Lumiya (key lore):** beyond battle meditation is «the capacity to sense, to coordinate, just by the power of the mind and the will. This is the ability that comes with the assumption of the title of Master of the Sith.» Also: «I learned everything there was to learn about this power ... I can just never wield it.» *She again concedes her own ceiling (cybernetics, L6759), while saying Jacen can reach it. Points to Caedus's battle meditation in later books.*
- **S18 · L6797–6803 · Lumiya:** «Emotion doesn't weaken you, Jacen. It scares you.»; «You're not quite ready.»

---

## 4. Duels and fights
| # | Fight | Where | Result | Context |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| D1 | Wedge/Corran/Myri vs. assassins | Ch6, Coronet | Escape | One assassin carries a lightsaber |
| D2 | Ben vs. TIE fighters (×2) | Ch17, Ch19, Ziost | TIE #1 driven off; TIE #2 destroyed | Ben starving, using TK with rocks |
| D3 | Alema vs. Lando | Ch14, *Errant Venture* | Alema wins and erases his memory | Surprise; stun cane |
| D4 | **Jedi trap vs. Alema** | Ch18, *Errant Venture* | Alema escapes, wounded | Corran, Zekk, Leia, Jaina, Jag |
| D5 | **Luke vs. Lumiya** | Ch20, Gilatter VIII | No decision; Lumiya stands down | Both healthy; short exchange |
| D6 | **Leia vs. Alema** | Ch20, Gilatter VIII | Leia pressing; Alema stalls and escapes | Alema injured and defensive by design |
| D7 | Jacen vs. security | Ch20 | Jacen cuts his way out and leaves | Mook fight |
| D8 | Ben (Sith ship) vs. *Boneyard Rendezvous* | Ch20, Ziost orbit | Freighter crippled | Ancient ship, Force-steered round |

---

## 5. Scaling connections

### A. Luke vs. Lumiya (the series' key data point)
- **Primary sources favor Luke:** Lumiya's own «When ever we meet, I expect him to hurt me» (L865), her healing from his five shots (L1575), and her fear that Luke would kill her (L5049). Tempest Ch21 shows Luke winning (see `lotf-3-tempest-notes.md` §2 #23–29).
- **Secondary framing ("fought Luke to a standstill"):** L3545 (Luke POV) and L4815 (Zekk). Read these as acknowledging that Roqoo was close because of the ambush, not as a parity verdict.
- **Gilatter (D5):** an even but very short exchange, and she disengages. **Verdict: Luke > Lumiya, with high confidence.**

### B. Jacen vs. Lumiya
- Lumiya twice concedes her cybernetic ceiling: L6759 here and Tempest L2716–2720. She says Jacen can learn the Sith Master's coordination power that she cannot wield. Her long-range possession (feat #10) is a major technique feat, but it nearly kills her. **Jacen > Lumiya in potential and raw power; Lumiya > Jacen in technique breadth at this moment.**

### C. Jacen vs. Luke
- Jacen out-deceives Luke repeatedly (feat #8, L3803–3837). Jacen also sends a clear telepathic warning to Luke over a long distance (feat #24). There is no fight. The power gap is unchanged from *Bloodlines* L6653 (Luke ahead). See `caedus-legends-quotes.txt`.

### D. Mara vs. Alema / Leia vs. Alema
- Mara won in *Tempest*. Here Alema dodges Mara entirely; her dart misses (L3887). Leia beats Alema's tools (the reversed dart, L5593) and pressures her in melee (L6601). Alema's survival comes from stealth and memory-erasure, not dueling. **Mara > Alema** and **Leia ≥ Alema** (Leia is pressing; Alema is crippled).

### E. Caedus vs. Vader, Sidious, Krayt
- S17 is lore for Caedus's later fleet-coordination power ("Master of the Sith" ability). Compare Sidious's and Vader's battle meditation in `sidious-legends-quotes.txt` and `vader-legends-quotes.txt`, and see `debates-sidious-plagueis-tenebrous-vs-krayt-caedus-valkorion.md`.

### F. Ben
- At 14, Ben's TK lifts quarter-ton stones and drops a TIE (feat #22). That is a strong apprentice-level feat, done while starving. His Force-steered magnetic round (feat #30) is notable as well.

### Quick ladder (this novel only)
Luke > Jacen (deception/telepathy) > Lumiya (supreme manipulator, physically capped) ≈ Mara > Corran ≈ Kyp > Leia > Alema (stealth/memory specialist, crippled) ≈ Jaina ≈ Zekk > Ben.

---

## 6. Verdict-relevant takeaways
1. **Luke > Lumiya.** Lumiya herself says Luke hurts her every time (L865). "Standstill" lines (L3545, L4815) are secondhand framing of an ambush.
2. **Lumiya is a long-range puppet master** (feat #10). The Klauskin operation broke the Corellia blockade, but her body cannot sustain it.
3. **Jacen is closing on Sith Mastery.** Lumiya says only the «sacrifice of love» and a Sith name remain (L6763), and the book ends with him resolving to kill his parents (L6809–6811).
4. **Alema loses every direct contest** (Leia's reversed dart, Jag's shot, Leia's flurry) but escapes every time.
5. **Luke is still "the most accomplished lightsaber artist"** (L165) in 40 ABY narrative framing.
6. No Boba Fett or Tenel Ka. Kyp and Corran have only minor feats.

---

## Story and prose notes

### SP1. Plot summary, in order
- **Ch1–Ch5 (L47–1210):** Jacen's guilt dream; then «Yes, I was. I was trying to kill them.» (L521). Omas and Niathal push Jacen's Mastery and Luke refuses. Wedge is relieved of command and resigns. Han and Leia reach Lando's station. Lumiya, recovering, tells Jacen to test Ben. Jag Fel arrives with anti-Alema tech. Alema stows away on Lavint's sabotaged ship.
- **Ch6–Ch10 (L1211–2912):** Wedge escapes assassins with Corran's help. Luke funds Jag's Alema task force. Han, Leia, and Lando learn from Teppler that Dur Gejjen ordered the Tenel Ka hit. Lumiya baits the Bothans and Commenor and possesses Klauskin. Ben leaves the Temple on Jacen's secret "Amulet of Kalara" mission. Luke and Mara find Lumiya's bolt-hole.
- **Ch11–Ch14 (L2913–4204):** The amulet is already gone ("Faskus of Ziost"). Ben steals a Y-wing. Luke guesses from a decoded file that Brisha Syo («an anagram for Shira B-Shira Brie», L3533) was Lumiya's daughter. Klauskin vents the *Shamunaar*. The Bothans and Commenor break the Corellian blockade. Alema stalks the *Errant Venture*.
- **Ch15–Ch18 (L4205–5714):** Ben on Ziost with the dying Faskus. He nearly abandons Kiara, then comes back for her. Lumiya orders Ben killed. The Confederation grows. Jacen recruits Lavint. The Jedi trap Alema, who escapes.
- **Ch19–Ch20 (L5715–6812):** Ziost's dark voices tempt Ben, who resists, kills a TIE, and takes an ancient Sith ship. The Gilatter VIII "election" is Turr Phennir's trap. Luke and Lumiya fight briefly before she stands down. Alema rams the station. Jacen abandons his parents. Lumiya says he needs a «sacrifice of love», and Jacen resolves on Han and Leia.

### SP2. Character profiles
- **Jacen:** clinical, punitive (Lavint's hyperdrive, L351), and openly contemptuous of Luke. «It doesn't mean that I'm suddenly a fanatic. It just means that I've grown up.» (L457).
- **Lumiya:** a sympathetic, tired, professional Sith: «I don't hate.» (L6565). She is ruthless with Ben (L5039).
- **Ben:** the heart of the book. He slides toward Jacen's coldness («Our mission is more important than her life.», L4589), breaks («For my fourteenth birthday, I killed a little girl.», L4625), and recovers his compassion. «Perhaps, sometimes, Jacen had been wrong, too.» (L5165).
- **Luke and Mara:** detectives. Mara wants to oppose Jacen «as a former Emperor's Hand should be able to» (L3553).
- **Leia:** Corran says she «wants to find out the truth» (L4369).

### SP3. Themes
- **Exile:** Jag (disowned), Wedge (resigned), the Solos (fugitives), Ben (stranded on Ziost), and the Skywalker-Solo family «taken out of the picture» (Corran, L3447).
- **Humor as a sign of health:** Wes Janson's line about fanatics (L455).
- **Logic vs. feeling:** "neither thinking nor feeling heeded [needed] to be the boss of the other" (L6023, OCR typo), set against Lumiya's «Emotion doesn't weaken you, Jacen. It scares you.» (L6797).

### SP4. Notable prose passages
- L53: «It wasn't exactly guilt that kept Jacen awake night after night.»
- L347: «What's it like to have once been a hero?»
- L551: the joke-spinning Jedi boy «was dead, slain on the same mission that had claimed his brother».
- L4625: «For my fourteenth birthday, I killed a little girl.»
- L6811: «Good-bye, Mom,» he said. «Good-bye, Dad.»

### SP5. Worldbuilding
- Ziost: the Sith homeworld, dark-side-saturated, with whispering voices (L3159, L4217, L5733). The ancient Sith ship is Force-piloted and carries a magnetic accelerator and a laser arm (L6425–6435).
- Errant Venture, Booster's casino Star Destroyer. Turr Phennir, the Confederation Supreme Commander. The Corellian Confederation grows: Bothawui, Commenor, Fondor, Bespin, Adumar (L4197, L4657).
- Brisha Syo as Lumiya's daughter: Luke's hypothesis, from a coded «the Lady's daughter» report (L3523–3535). It is not confirmed in this book, and it reframes *Betrayal*.

### SP6. Timeline
- 40 ABY, weeks after *Tempest*. Han and Leia's escape and the Noghri deaths are recalled (L807). LotF 4 of 9: after *Tempest*, before *Sacrifice* (Karen Traviss), where Jacen kills Mara and becomes Darth Caedus.

---
*Quote check: every «…» was checked by script against `[local path]` at the cited L-number(s). The raw reading log is at `[local path]`.*

---

## Character analyses (added 2026-10-17 under the 7+ characters rule)

*Method:* built from the verified log above (Sections 2–5). `L####` = line in `6b7ad64b…txt` (chapter given). Quotes in «…» are verbatim and were re-checked by script. No wiki material is used. **Before:** 5 short story profiles (SP2), none with feats, fights and scaling. **Added:** 11 analyses.

### 1. Jacen Solo
- **Role/arc:** clinical and openly contemptuous of Luke. He tests Ben through Lumiya and, by the end, is resolving to kill a loved one (L6809–6811).
- **Key feats:** finds Lumiya across the galaxy while levitating (Ch4, L849–851). Deceives Luke: «Even if Jacen had been lying, he was sure Luke would not have been able to detect it.» (Ch9, L2355). Sends a telepathic «It's a trap! Mines!» that Luke picks up in a StealthX hundreds of km away (Ch20, L6319–6323). «He leapt above the torrent of blaster shots» and beheads two guards (L6391).
- **Statements:** Omas: he «possesses skills and power that not even most confirmed Masters can match.» (L589). Luke says he «lacks the emotional maturity» (L583). Lumiya: «You're not quite ready.» (L6803).
- **Notable quotes:** «It doesn't mean that I'm suddenly a fanatic. It just means that I've grown up.» (L457). «Ben is my apprentice. Though I realized not long ago that I can sacrifice him if I have to.» (L875).
- **Fights:** only mooks (Ch20).
- **Scaling:** unchanged from Bloodlines. Luke > Jacen in power, and Jacen is the best deceiver of the era.

### 2. Luke Skywalker
- **Role/arc:** fitted with a new cybernetic hand. He hunts Lumiya and ends up taking her offered hand at Gilatter.
- **Key feats:** looks straight at the hidden Alema in a casino, and she holds her fire (Ch14, L3869). StealthX torpedo run cripples a Nebulon-B (L4107–4111). Cuts five or six blaster barrels in one circle (Ch20, L6529). At Gilatter he deflects lightwhip tendrils with both blades and leaps her sweep (L6559–6563).
- **Statements:** Mara: «he's dropped from being the most accomplished lightsaber artist in the galaxy to, well, still being the most accomplished, just a little less so for the time being.» (L165). Lumiya: «When ever we meet, I expect him to hurt me.» (L865). Luke's own POV calls her a woman who «had fought Luke to a standstill mere weeks before» (L3545). Luke is being generous; the Tempest text shows him winning.
- **Fights/results:** vs. Lumiya at Gilatter, no decision after a short exchange because she stands down (L6595).
- **Scaling:** still the best duelist alive per in-book framing. Luke > Lumiya (high confidence).

### 3. Lumiya
- **Role/arc:** recovering from Luke's five shots (L1575). A tired, professional Sith who says «I don't hate.» (L6565).
- **Key feats:** long-range possession. From near Bimmiel she reshapes and frees Klauskin on Coruscant through a Quarren nurse, «Like a spectral parasite» (Ch10, L2717–2813). It nearly kills her: «Would she have died?» (L2823). She projects a phantom of herself to Jacen (Ch4, L853–859). She TK-lifts a severed head into Luke's blow (Ch20, L6555).
- **Statements:** her own: «I am healing. Here I can summon my strength. Your uncle hurt me.» (L861). On the Sith Master's coordination power: «I learned everything there was to learn about this power ... I can just never wield it.» (L6755–6759). Zekk: «She fought the Grand Master to a standstill. She's Master level.» (L4815, secondhand).
- **Notable quote:** «Yes, I've tried to kill you-but that was professional. Not personal.» (L6565–6581).
- **Scaling:** a top-tier manipulator who is physically capped, so Luke > Lumiya and Jacen > her in potential.

### 4. Mara Jade Skywalker
- **Role/arc:** an investigator who wants to oppose Jacen «as a former Emperor's Hand should be able to» (L3553).
- **Key feats:** defuses a bomb and trihexalon trap, then bends backward under a meter-long dart trap (Ch10, L2509–2517, L2581–2583). Force-sweeps half a dozen agents (Ch20, L6529). Avoids Alema's dart, which hits an Ortolan instead (L3887–3889).
- **Scaling:** Mara > Alema (carried over from Tempest). Roughly Lumiya's level overall.

### 5. Ben Skywalker
- **Role/arc:** the heart of the book. He slides toward Jacen's coldness, then breaks: «For my fourteenth birthday, I killed a little girl.» (L4625). Recovers his compassion: «Perhaps, sometimes, Jacen had been wrong, too.» (L5165).
- **Key feats:** a TK stone into a TIE's path. The pilot: «He used Jedi magic to hurl a quarter-ton slab of stone at me.» (Ch17, L5187). Brings down stones, «Some weighed a quarter ton or more» (L6055), from more than 100 m and destroys a TIE (Ch19). Raises a buried Sith ship with «Come out!» (L6147). Aims a blaster «Aiming through the Force» (Ch18, L5361). Steers a ball «and saw the ball change its arc» through a freighter (Ch20, L6465–6467).
- **Notable quotes:** «See what I become.» (L2899). «Our mission is more important than her life.» (L4589).
- **Scaling:** a strong 14-year-old apprentice. Quarter-ton TK while starving is his best feat so far.

### 6. Leia Organa Solo
- **Role/arc:** a truth-seeker (Corran: she «wants to find out the truth», L4369).
- **Key feats:** mind trick for blockade codes: «He was even more weakminded than I'm used to.» (Ch6, L1555). Mind trick at range without eye contact, at a cost (Ch7, L1867). Turns Alema's dart around: «the dart within it shot backward, straight into her mouth.» (Ch18, L5591–5593).
- **Fights/results:** vs. Alema at Gilatter, Leia «swung an almost ceaseless flurry of blows at Alema, but the Twi'lek Jedi continued backing away» (L6601). No decision, as Alema was stalling.
- **Statement:** Jacen in a dream: she is «not a match for any Jedi Knight who's been fighting and training constantly» (L85).
- **Scaling:** Leia ≥ Alema here (Alema has a broken clavicle).

### 7. Alema Rar
- **Role/arc:** stows away with Lavint and hunts the Solos.
- **Key feats:** TK-yanks Lando's blaster and cane, outlasts a stun cane rated for «a good-sized Wookiee» (L4063), and wipes his memory (Ch14, L4073–4085). Catches Han's bolt «casually» (Ch20, L6551). Escapes a five-on-one trap despite a broken clavicle (Ch18, L5555–5659).
- **Statements:** Jag: «She can scrub herself from the short-term memory of people» (L995). Leia: «Alema's a Jedi who thinks like an assassin.» (L5445).
- **Scaling:** loses every direct contest and escapes every time. Below Leia, Mara and Corran.

### 8. Corran Horn
- **Feats:** drops four stories and kicks a sniper out (Ch6, L1315–1321). «plucked the dart from the air» (Ch18, L5559).
- **Scaling:** a solid Master-level combat Jedi with good reactions. Placed ≈ Kyp.

### 9. Kyp Durron
- **Feats:** jokingly rocks the *Jade Shadow* with TK (Ch1, L175).
- **Statements:** says he could «reach into the gravity well of a gas giant and pull a spacecraft out of it» as a teen and «I'm no weaker in the Force, and I'm a lot more skilled» (L633). Self-report.
- **Scaling:** high raw TK on statements only. No fights here.

### 10. Zekk and Jaina Solo
- **Feats:** Zekk catches Alema's thrown saber with the Force (Ch18, L5573–5577). Jaina is part of the trap team.
- **Statement:** Zekk: «I don't think the three of us are a match for Lumiya.» (L4815).
- **Scaling:** Knight level, below Lumiya by their own estimate.

### 11. Jagged Fel
- **Feats:** his armor stops Alema's dart and he shoots her, breaking her clavicle (Ch18, L5639–5641). She TK-hurls him into crates (L5643).
- **Scaling:** an elite non-Force fighter who can hurt Force users with prep and armor. This is the start of his anti-Jedi role (Fury).

<!-- 021-legacy-of-the-force/lotf-5-sacrifice-notes.md (verbatim copy) -->

# Legacy of the Force 5: Sacrifice (novel, read in full): feats, scaling and story notes

<!-- read up to: chapter twenty-four (L9584), END OF BOOK; restructured into house style -->

- **Book:** *Star Wars: Legacy of the Force: Sacrifice* by Karen Traviss (2007). Fifth of the nine Legacy of the Force novels.
- **Continuity:** Legends. Set in 40 ABY, during the Second Galactic Civil War (Galactic Alliance vs. the Confederation). It comes after *Exile* (LOTF 4) and before *Inferno* (LOTF 6). Jacen Solo becomes Darth Caedus on the last line (L9584).
- **Source file:** `[local path]` (9,585 lines; some mojibake, read with errors='replace').
- **Method:** I read every line in order: Prologue, then Ch1–Ch24. `L####` is the line number in that file. Text inside «…» is copied exactly from the file. A script checked every «quote» against the source (see the end of this file). The raw reading log I wrote while reading is kept as an appendix at the bottom.
- **Chapter heading lines:** Prologue L64, Ch1 L136, Ch2 L623, Ch3 L1252, Ch4 L1881, Ch5 L2698, Ch6 L3291, Ch7 L3826, Ch8 L4397, Ch9 L4767, Ch10 L5094, Ch11 L5523, Ch12 L6164, Ch13 L6309, Ch14 L6662, Ch15 L6957, Ch16 L7412, Ch17 L7645, Ch18 L7868, Ch19 L8081, Ch20 L8264, Ch21 L8447, Ch22 L8580, Ch23 L9075, Ch24 L9400.
- **Feat vs. statement:** Section 2 covers what the narration shows happening. Section 3 covers what characters say or think about power, marked **STATEMENT**. Traviss writes in tight third-person POV, so a lot of the "narration" is one character's opinion; those entries are marked *(POV)*.
- **Headline:** this is the **Mara vs. Jacen** book. It has three real fights: Mara vs. Lumiya (+ the Sith sphere) on Hesperidium in Ch10, Mara vs. Jacen in the Kavan tunnels in Ch20–21, and Luke vs. Lumiya on Terephon in Ch22. On points, Mara is beating Jacen when he wins with an illusion of Ben's face and a poison dart.

---

## 1. Cast who fight or use the Force

| Character | Role in this book | Force user? |
|---|---|---|
| Jacen Solo / Darth Caedus | GAG colonel, then joint Chief of State. Kills Mara. Named Darth Caedus at L9584 | Yes |
| Mara Jade Skywalker | Hunts Lumiya, then Jacen. Killed in Ch21 | Yes |
| Luke Skywalker | Grand Master. Kills Lumiya in Ch22 | Yes |
| Ben Skywalker | 14, GAG lieutenant. Assassinates Gejjen. Learns Force camouflage and teaches it to Mara | Yes |
| Lumiya (Shira Brie) | Dark Lady of the Sith, cyborg. Jacen's teacher | Yes |
| Alema Rar | Mentioned and sensed only. No on-page fight | Yes |
| Jaina Solo | Supporting role only (Starfighter Command, Hapes). No fight | Yes |
| Tenel Ka | Queen Mother of Hapes. Jacen visits her; no fight | Yes |
| Kyp Durron, Corran Horn, Kyle Katarn, Saba Sebatyne, Cilghal | Jedi Council scenes. No fights | Yes |
| Han / Leia | Off-world fugitives. Leia talks with Mara at Drall in Ch15. No fights | Leia yes |
| Boba Fett | Mand'alor. Dying until Jaing and Venku cure him. Minor action in Ch3–4 | No |
| Mirta Gev, Jaing Skirata, Venku (Kad'ika), Goran Beviin | Mandalorian cast | No (Venku hints at "a healing touch", L7041) |
| Sith meditation sphere | Living Sith training ship from Ziost. Acts on its own in fights | (sentient ship) |
| Lekauf, Shevu | GAG soldiers on the Gejjen hit | No |

---

## 2. Combat and Force feats, in chapter order

### Ch1–Ch4 (setup)
1. **Ch1 · L224 · Jacen (POV Ben)**: «Jacen's trick of hiding in the Force had become permanent; Ben found he was impossible to track unless he wanted to be found». *Shows:* Jacen's presence suppression is constant. This sets up the whole book's stealth game.
2. **Ch1 · L258–276 · Jacen**: senses Ben across the Perlemian corridor, then magnifies his own presence to reach him. *Shows:* long-range sensing.
3. **Ch1 · L226, L312 · Ben + Sith sphere**: Ben flies the sphere by thought, and it destroys a pursuing ship in answer to his thoughts. *Shows:* the ship's weapons acting on intent, not a personal feat.
4. **Ch2 · L861 · Lumiya**: senses Alema «like a beacon even at this range». *Shows:* sensing.
5. **Ch2 · L989–991 · Lumiya**: rams a vibroblade into her own thigh and hits metal: «I'm actually more machine than organic». *Shows:* how much of her is cybernetic, which explains her durability later (L5256 «She hit solid durasteel.»).
6. **Ch3 · L1434–1436 · Fett/Mirta**: Fett lands a left hook on a barkeep reaching for a disruptor, and both draw first. *Shows:* baseline unpowered skill.
7. **Ch4 · L2057 · Jacen**: «Force-enhanced hearing» to read a procurement meeting. Minor.
8. **Ch4 · L2545–2573 · Ben**: learns **Force camouflage** from Jacen on his first try. Jacen: «"Now," Jacen said softly, "you see why practice is necessary. But full marks for technique."» (L2565). *Shows:* Ben learns this very fast. The technique decides the book's climax.
9. **Ch4 · L2297 · Fett**: fibercord-loops the gangster Fraig over a balcony. **L2381–2397:** an unseen sniper (Jaing) silently drops the pursuing speeders. *Shows:* Mando skill set.

### Ch5–Ch9 (preparation, the Gejjen hit)
10. **Ch5 · L3188 · Jacen vs. Mara (deception)**: he projects «a veneer of weary good humor in the Force» at Mara, and she does not see through him this time. *Shows:* Jacen fools a former Emperor's Hand.
11. **Ch5 · L3248–3282 · Lumiya**: Force-influences the Senate P&R Council from the gallery so it passes Item 357. «I just redirected a little opposition from the gallery.» (L3282). *Shows:* subtle mass influence on a committee.
12. **Ch5 · L2860 · Jaing Skirata**: ambushes Mirta with no warning. Mirta: «I didn't pick him up on my sensors and I didn't even see him coming at me.» *Shows:* unpowered stealth.
13. **Ch7 · L4140–4168 · Ben (sniper training)**: «It was just like using a lightsaber, really. Letting the Force guide the hand, the eye . . .» (L4140). After a couple of hours he hits the five-centimeter zone 95 percent of the time (L4168), and later 97 percent (L4423). *Shows:* Force-guided marksmanship.
14. **Ch8 · L4529–4543 · Luke vs. Lumiya (sensing)**: Luke senses Lumiya at his apartment, but only because she lets him (L4527). He Force-jams the corridor doors (L4535), but she is already gone and leaves Ben's boots behind. *Shows:* Lumiya's stealth beats Luke's sensing until she chooses to show herself. Luke also gets an impression of Ben from the boots (L4543).
15. **Ch9 · L4953–4955 · Ben**: counts six beings in a sealed room through the Force.
16. **Ch9 · L5079 · Ben**: kills Dur Gejjen with a rifle shot from about 100 m: «Yes, he'd killed Gejjen, a textbook head shot.» *Shows:* assassination skill. (Lekauf then blows himself up as the cover story, L5516.)

### Ch10 — DUEL A: Mara vs. Lumiya (and the Sith sphere), Hesperidium
17. **Ch10 · L5248 · Mara**: «She Force-leapt five meters at Lumiya, slashing down right to left, two-handed.» The stroke clips Lumiya's headdress, and Mara slows the whip tails with a Force push.
18. **Ch10 · L5250 · Lumiya (weapon)**: the lightwhip is «leather strips studded with impervious Mandalorian iron fragments and tendrils of sheer, raw, murderous dark energy». It gouges through a parked ship's durasteel hull.
19. **Ch10 · L5252 · Mara**: a blaster hit on Lumiya's shoulder: «A puff of white vapor burst from Lumiya's shoulder».
20. **Ch10 · L5256–5258 · Mara**: «cannoned into Lumiya's legs with all the power she could muster from the Force. She hit solid durasteel.» She then head-butts the cybernetic jaw.
21. **Ch10 · L5258 · Mara**: the hilt-dagger trick. «she killed the lightsaber blade for a second and held the hilt like a dagger, stabbing it down into Lumiya's chest before flicking the energy back on. Lumiya pulled to the side as the blade punched through flesh.» *Shows:* Mara lands a near-kill blow.
22. **Ch10 · L5268 + Ch14 L6816 + Ch17 L7775 · Sith sphere**: a cable wraps Mara's neck from behind and throws her. Mara: «It wasn't a droid. I got bushwhacked by a living ship, a Sith ship.» (L6816). The sphere later confirms it «stopped her fighting» (L7775). **Context: 2v1.** Lumiya was on a decoy mission (L3910, L3940, L6599). She flees wounded and does not finish Mara (L5302).
23. **Ch10 · L5278 · Mara**: with a concussion and a black eye, she mind-tricks two joggers: «You didn't see a fight at all.»

### Ch11–Ch17
24. **Ch13 · L6577–6659 · Ben vs. Jacen + Lumiya (stealth)**: Ben, camouflaged, walks up and eavesdrops undetected. Jacen «probably relied on sensing people coming and going» (L6597). *Shows:* Ben's camouflage beats Jacen's and Lumiya's sensing at this point.
25. **Ch14 · L6868 · Mara**: learns camouflage from Ben: «After a couple of hours, she could manage a minute or two without needing to grab something solid.»
26. **Ch15 · L7143 · Jacen**: «a little Force influence» on CSF officers. Minor.
27. **Ch16 · L7430 · Mara vs. Jacen (stealth)**: she reaches Jacen in the Senate lobby unsensed: «No, he hadn't felt her presence when it mattered. Thank you, Ben. Nice trick.»
28. **Ch17 · L7699–7705 · Ben**: the Sith sphere calls to him telepathically from far away, and he hears it.
29. **Ch17 · L7839 · Jacen (POV Lumiya)**: when he lowers his guard on Hapes, «His Force presence could be strong enough to drown out everyone else's around him.» Through the sphere, Lumiya feels the toy in his hands and works out that Allana exists (L7857).

### Ch18–Ch19 (pursuit and space fight, Hapes Cluster)
30. **Ch18 · L7964–7970 · Ben**: tails the sphere while shut down in the Force, and maps its position by its silent "voice".
31. **Ch18 · L8072 · Mara vs. Jacen (stealth piloting)**: her StealthX gets onto Jacen's tail undetected: «How did the proximity sensors miss it? How did he miss it?» Jacen did sense «something dark» first (L8046) but took it for Lumiya.
32. **Ch19 · L8103–8105 · Mara = Jacen in a StealthX (narration via Jacen)**: «There was no advantage: same starfighter. No edge: she was as good a pilot.» Jacen loops away (L8117).
33. **Ch19 · L8129–8131 · Lumiya (sphere) vs. Mara**: on Jacen's order («Lumiya, crack her canopy. Weak shields.») the sphere strikes Mara's fighter: «Hull breach, she's venting atmosphere.» **Context: 2v1.** Mara ditches on Kavan.
34. **Ch19 · L8143 · Jacen vs. Lumiya**: «locked all eight proton torpedoes on the Sith sphere. "This is what you taught me to be."» The sphere survives, with no explosion seen (L8147).
35. **Ch19 · L8161–8229 · Ben**: talks the sphere out of a fight («Once I fought. Now I educate and protect apprentices.» L8193) and so captures Lumiya: «Ben had caught a Sith.» (L8229).

### Ch20–Ch21 — DUEL B: Mara vs. Jacen, Kavan storm-drain tunnels (L8358–8545)
36. **Ch20 · L8255 · Mara (prep)**: picks the terrain. «If I get him in a confined space, he can't use all his Force skills, but I can make the most of what I've got.» She hides in the Force, then makes her presence loud to lure him (L8259–8261).
37. **Ch20 · L8376 · Jacen (prep)**: «Jacen loaded four poisoned darts into an adapted blaster and slipped the others into slots on his belt». His loadout is shown at L8026: «blaster, lightsaber, vibroblade, and toxin darts».
38. **Ch20 · L8392 · Mara**: the ambush. «His right knee exploded with blinding pain as Mara cannoned out horizontally, Force-assisted, from a side conduit and caught his leg on the joint with her boots, ripping the tendons.» *Shows:* total surprise. Jacen did not sense her.
39. **Ch20 · L8402, L8426 · Mara (TK)**: «An intense Force shake of the brickwork and arches in front of and behind the metal plate weakened them, and then she stopped them from collapsing by Force pressure.» Then «She brought down both sections of tunnel, before and behind, with a massive exertion in the Force that made her breathless.» *Shows:* she buries Jacen under a tunnel collapse.
40. **Ch20 · L8438–8444 · Mara**: fires her blaster point-blank at the eye of the buried Jacen.
41. **Ch21 · L8471 · Jacen**: «He deflected the energy bolt with one last surge of the Force and sent the rubble erupting off his crushed and bleeding body like a detonation. Bricks hammered the walls and rained fragments, knocking Mara flat like a bomb blast.» *Shows:* while crushed, he deflects a point-blank bolt and throws off the rubble in one burst. It costs him: «The effort froze Jacen for two vital seconds.» (L8473).
42. **Ch21 · L8485 · Mara**: shoto in her left hand, fencing-style, plus the vibroblade: «seared the angle of his cheekbone, and caught him under the tip of chin with the vibroblade».
43. **Ch21 · L8487 · Mara vs. Jacen**: he Force-pushes her into a wall, «but she kept coming at him, one-two, one-two with the shoto and the blade, and he was being driven back, his strength ebbing.»
44. **Ch21 · L8489 · Mara**: «He drew his dart gun and fired one after the other, but Mara scattered all four needles in a blur of blue light.» She dodges the debris he hurls with the Force and Force-leaps onto his back.
45. **Ch21 · L8495 · Mara vs. Jacen (Force exchange)**: «They grappled, Force-pushed, Force-crushed: he threw her back again, trying to Force-jolt her spine and paralyze her for a moment, but somehow she deflected it». She «almost Force-snatched the lightsaber from his hand, but even with his injuries he hung on to it.»
46. **Ch21 · L8501 · Mara**: he falls into a subsidence crack, and «Mara to right herself and come back at him with the shoto and plunge it into the soft tissue just under the end of his collarbone.»
47. **Ch21 · L8503 · Jacen**: «blocked by his dwindling Force power each time» against her shoto hacks while she pins him. He drags her face toward his blade by her hair.
48. **Ch21 · L8507–8511 · Jacen (decisive)**: «Jacen stared into her eyes and instantly created the illusion of Ben's face beneath her. She blinked. It gave him the edge for that fraction of a moment. It was long enough to ram the poison dart into her leg». Mara dies of the toxin (L8513–8545).
49. **Ch21 · L8567, L8577 · Ben, Luke**: both feel her death at a distance. Luke «felt a hand brush his hair».

### Ch22–Ch24
50. **Ch22 · L8676, L8712 · Jacen**: «I'm hastening the healing trance as much as I can.» He meditates for an hour, then flies.
51. **Ch22 · L8998–9000 · Luke**: tractors the Sith sphere, slows it, and forces it down onto a mesa on Terephon.
52. **Ch22 · L9008–9034 · Luke vs. Lumiya, DUEL C**: Luke uses only his lightsaber, no shoto. He gets inside the whip's range, drives her «like a battering ram toward the edge of the mesa» (L9026), and holds a blade lock against the whip handle (L9028). She slips, and he catches her hand: «"I'd never let you fall," Luke said, and pulled her back to safety. As she straightened up, he looked her in the eyes-calm, eerily calm-and swung his lightsaber in a single decapitating arc.» (L9034). **Context:** Lumiya came to die («My work and my life are done, Jacen. I'd really welcome a rest.» L8704) and falsely claimed Mara's killing (L8986).
53. **Ch22 · L9068 · Jacen vs. Ben (sensing)**: «Ben was still shut down, hiding his Force presence, but Jacen had found him.» (Ben suspects the death left a trace in the Force that Jacen followed, L9070.)
54. **Ch23 · L9285–9293 · Caedus (Sith battle-sight)**: reads a live holochart and predicts unseen ship movements: «I can see all this.» This is «a battlefield awareness and judgment that made ordinary battle meditation look like a finger painting» (L9293). *Shows:* this power arrives after Mara's death.

## 3. Statements and hype (STATEMENT: not feats)

| # | Line | Speaker / POV | Statement | Reliability |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| S1 | L3958 | Lumiya, to Jacen | «Luke can still take you in a lightsaber fight.» | **High.** She is his teacher and has fought Luke. It is mid-40 ABY, before Jacen becomes a Sith Lord. |
| S2 | L3960 | Jacen | «I'm already a few steps ahead of him. Trust me.» | Low. Self-serving, and about strategy, not dueling. |
| S3 | L3848 | Lumiya (POV) | «He had full mastery of the Force, but he seemed to enjoy using the limited tricks of ordinary people.» | Medium. Biased in his favor. |
| S4 | L6948 | Jacen (POV) | lightsaber and blaster «still wouldn't be enough when Luke and Mara came after him», so he prepares «poisons and pathogens» | **High as self-assessment.** Jacen does not think he can beat a vengeful Luke and Mara straight up. |
| S5 | L7444 | Mara (POV) | «She couldn't outstrip his Force powers, but the tricks of flesh and blood put her on a more level playing field.» | **High.** Mara herself concedes his greater Force power. |
| S6 | L8255 | Mara (POV) | «If I get him in a confined space, he can't use all his Force skills» | High. It is a tactical read, and the fight bears it out. |
| S7 | L8370–8372 | Jacen (POV) | Mara thought «she could take someone who had complete mastery of the Force» / «She was a superb assassin, but her Force skills were crude compared to his.» | Medium-low. Arrogant, and contradicted by how the fight goes. |
| S8 | L8487 | narration, Jacen POV | «She shouldn't have been able to get near him. He had total mastery, and she was just athletic and fast.» | Mixed. On paper he is stronger, but the scene shows her getting near him again and again. |
| S9 | L8103–8105 | narration, Jacen POV | «No edge: she was as good a pilot.» | High (piloting). |
| S10 | L8541 | dying Mara | «But Luke will crush you» | Prediction and motivated, but foreshadowing (pays off in *Inferno* and later). |
| S11 | L8034 | Jacen (POV) | Luke is «a man who could bring down the Empire, and whose blood was closer to Vader's even than his own, wouldn't be paralyzed by grief.» | Respect and fear of Luke. |
| S12 | L8036 | Jacen (POV) | it would be «far harder to protect her from the vengeance of Luke or Mara» than from the Hapans | Fear statement. |
| S13 | L4609 | narration, Luke POV | «whatever gifts the Force had given him more generously than other Jedi» | High (canon framing: Luke is the most gifted Jedi). |
| S14 | L4619 | Luke (POV) | «if he could take on the Empire without a second thought, he could certainly finish off one of the last pitiful remnants of its rule-Lumiya.» | Confidence. It is borne out in Ch22, though Lumiya was not trying to win. |
| S15 | L1021 | Lumiya (POV) | «Lumiya didn't have the complete Sith ability to see all the pieces in the game, every element in the battle. That was for a full Sith Master.» | Lore. Pays off at L9293. |
| S16 | L9293 | Caedus (POV) | battle awareness «that made ordinary battle meditation look like a finger painting» | Medium-high. Comparative to battle meditation. |
| S17 | L1115 / L1117 | Kyp / Mara | Kyp: «You could take her anytime.» Mara: «But she's not so decrepit, believe me. I won't have an easy time of it.» | Kyp low; Mara's caution is the better read. |
| S18 | L5943 | Luke (POV) | «If anyone could take Lumiya, Mara could» | Medium (spouse). |
| S19 | L1872 / L1876 | Mara | «who better to go after a former Emperor's Hand than another one?» / «I'm much more dangerous now I have a cub to protect.» | Self-assessment. |
| S20 | L2778 | Mara | «I've been that dark. I was trained by a Sith Lord. I can think like them.» | Self-assessment. |
| S21 | L7375 | Leia | Lumiya «could never make him act totally against his will.» | High (mother). Establishes that Jacen acts by his own choice. |
| S22 | L7526 | Lumiya (POV) | «The man could meditate in the middle of a hurricane.» | Medium. |
| S23 | L7839 | Lumiya (POV) | «His Force presence could be strong enough to drown out everyone else's around him.» | Medium-high (raw presence). |
| S24 | L9554 | Caedus (POV) | «Ben would eventually become greater than his Jedi father could ever be.» | Low (speculation and wish). |
| S25 | L4292 | narration | «Beskar was the toughest metal known to science. Even lightsabers had trouble with it.» | Lore. |
| S26 | L2878 | Fett | Jaing: «Master assassin, sniper, general pain in the backside. Don't underestimate him.» | Medium. |
| S27 | L4080 | Lekauf (family lore) | Vader had «Really uncanny accuracy in three dimensions» | Third-hand. |
| S28 | L2537 | Jacen | «She's not up to killing you.» (Lumiya vs. Ben) | Self-serving. |
| S29 | L8410 | Mara (POV) | «her training was in destruction. Jacen's was in deception.» | Framing of their styles. Accurate to how the fight plays out. |
