## 4. Scaling connections

| # | Fight | Where (lines) | Length | Winner | Holding back / help / injuries | What it suggests |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| A | **Mara vs. Lumiya** | Hesperidium, L5242–5316 | short | No result. Lumiya flees wounded (shoulder hit, saber through the chest) | **2v1:** the sphere ambushes Mara with a cable (L5268, L6816). Lumiya was a decoy whose job was to buy time (L3940, L6599). Mara: black eye, rope burn. | Mara ≥ Lumiya one-on-one. She lands every clean blow and is only stopped by the ship. Lumiya was not trying to win. |
| B | **Jacen (+Lumiya) vs. Mara, StealthX dogfight** | Hapes, L8072–8135 | short | Mara's fighter is crippled by the sphere | 2v1. Same fighter type. «she was as good a pilot» (L8105). | Pilot parity. The only reason Mara lands on Kavan is the sphere's strike. |
| C | **Mara vs. Jacen, Kavan tunnels** | L8358–8545 | the book's longest fight: ambush, collapse, then a brawl | **Jacen**, by illusion plus a poison dart | **Mara's prep:** terrain choice, camouflage, ambush, trap. **Jacen's prep:** poison darts. **Jacen's injuries:** knee tendons ripped (L8392); crushed and bleeding (L8471); ankle-to-knee pain after the fall (L8501); shoto stab under the collarbone (L8501); cuts on cheek, chin, and jaw to hairline. **Mara's injuries:** scalp cut, slammed into a wall and a pillar. **She is undamaged when the dart lands.** No help on either side. | Mara out-fights Jacen at close quarters and nearly kills him twice (blaster to the eye, blade at his throat). He survives through a big Force burst (L8471) and wins through a mind trick (L8507). He says himself that she «shouldn't have been able to get near him» (L8487). This is pre-Caedus Jacen (he ascends after this fight, L9293–9299). |
| D | **Luke vs. Lumiya** | Terephon, L8998–9034 | short | **Luke**, by decapitation | Lumiya was on a planned suicide mission (L8696–8704). Luke uses no shoto, by choice (L9008). | Luke handles the lightwhip easily on his own. Not a fair test, because she wanted to die. |
| E | **Ben vs. the sphere/Lumiya (talk)** | L8161–8229 | – | Ben (captures her without combat) | – | Not a power feat. |

### Links to existing research files
- **Caedus vs. Mara:** `mara-jade-quotes.txt` L124–128 summarizes this fight from Wookieepedia. It says the article “does not say Mara defeated or stalemated him”. The novel text is more favorable to Mara than that summary. She lands every major hit, and he wins only through the illusion and dart (L8507–8509). That article also calls Jacen's escape a “Force Wave strong enough to blow away the blaster bolt”, which matches L8471.
- **Caedus vs. Luke:** `caedus-legends-quotes.txt` L379: “not as powerful as his uncle in a direct contest of strength”. L307: “The Jedi judged few but Luke and Mara Jade Skywalker to be on his level.” This book supports both: Lumiya's L3958 assessment, and Jacen's own L6948 belief that he needs poisons against Luke and Mara.
- **Caedus vs. Vader:** Jacen says «I'm proud of my grandfather, but not blind to the weaknesses that brought him down.» (L9576). Fett: «Jacen Solo, the boy who wants to be Vader when he grows up.» (L6682). This book has no power comparison with Vader. `dark-lord-novel-feats.md` covers early Vader.
- **Sidious:** Mara compares Jacen to Palpatine on moral grounds («You're ... as vile as he was.» L8527), not on power. On method, Jacen notes Palpatine's manipulation (L1983), and Lumiya warns «Don't make Palpatine's mistakes.» (L3878). See `sidious-legends-quotes.txt`.
- **Krayt:** no connection in this book. (`krayt-legends-quotes.txt` L248 is about Jacen's later belief concerning Allana.)
- **Previous LOTF books** (other worker's files): `lotf-2-bloodlines-notes.md` (Jacen's illusions vs. Luke, and his masking vs. Lumiya), `lotf-3-tempest-notes.md`, and `lotf-4-exile-notes.md` (Luke and Mara vs. Lumiya and Alema, which comes before this book).

### Quick ladder (this novel only)
- **Close-quarters lethality:** Mara ≥ Jacen (pre-Caedus) on the page. Jacen > Mara in raw Force power (S5, and the explosion at L8471).
- **Lightsaber:** Luke > Jacen (S1; stated, not shown). Luke > Lumiya (shown, though she was not trying to win). Mara ≥ Lumiya (shown, Duel A).
- **Stealth:** Ben's camouflage beats Jacen (L6597). Mara with camouflage beats Jacen (L7430, L8072, L8392). Lumiya's stealth beats Luke (L4527, L8968).
- **Sensing:** Jacen finds a camouflaged Ben (L9068), perhaps by tracking the death.

---

## 5. Plot summary relevant to the fights
1. Lumiya reads the prophecy «He will immortalize his love» (L140–158) as an order to kill what Jacen loves most. Jacen dreads it means his daughter Allana, so he settles on Ben (L1558).
2. Jacen teaches Ben Force camouflage (Ch4). Ben assassinates Gejjen (Ch9). Jacen and Niathal depose Omas with a legal coup (Ch12–13).
3. Lumiya fights Mara on Hesperidium as a decoy (Ch10). Mara plants a transponder in the sphere.
4. Ben confesses the assassination to Mara (Ch11), eavesdrops on Jacen and Lumiya (Ch13), and teaches Mara camouflage (Ch14). Mara decides Jacen must die (L7389), gives him one last chance (Ch16), and then hunts him.
5. Jacen visits Allana on Hapes. Mara follows Lumiya's transponder there. They dogfight, and Lumiya's sphere disables Mara's fighter. Jacen fires on Lumiya and fails to kill her (Ch19).
6. On Kavan, Mara ambushes Jacen and nearly kills him. He uses an illusion of Ben and a poison dart. Mara dies saying "Ben" (Ch20–21).
7. Lumiya takes the blame and lets Luke behead her (Ch22). Ben tells Luke it wasn't Lumiya (L9363).
8. Jacen finds his Sith battle-sight (Ch23) and names himself **Darth Caedus** (L9584).

---

## Story and prose notes

### SP1. Plot summary, in order (by chapter group)
- **Prologue–Ch2:** Mara, at 0300, swears to kill Lumiya (L130). Ben flies home from Ziost in the Sith sphere, which Jacen hands to Lumiya. Fett, dying, declares Mandalore neutral (L488). The prophecy is laid out. Lumiya's dramatic irony: she thinks Jacen «had less feeling for her [Mara] than for his own parents. Ben, then.» (L933).
- **Ch3–Ch5:** Luke regrets not killing Lumiya (L1878). Mara arms herself (shoto, flechette launcher, transponders) and gives Ben her old Imperial vibroblade with a transponder hidden inside. Jacen arranges a "law to change laws" (L1993). Fett and Mirta hunt the clone Skirata and meet Jaing. Jacen orders Ben to kill Gejjen and weeps alone (L2695).
- **Ch6–Ch9:** Niathal turns. Wiretaps catch Omas and Gejjen. Lumiya and Jacen plan the decoy (L3940). Ben trains as a sniper. Mandalore finds a new beskar lode. Ben kills Gejjen at Vulpter.
- **Ch10–Ch13:** Duel A on Hesperidium. Lekauf's suicide cover story (the theme of sacrifice). The Jedi Council meets. Ben confesses to Mara. Omas is arrested at 0001 (L6244), and Jacen becomes joint Chief of State. Ben overhears Jacen and Lumiya, including the Nelani mind-wipe (L6605).
- **Ch14–Ch17:** Venku and Jaing cure Fett. Ben teaches Mara camouflage. Jacen prepares poisons (L6948). Mara and Leia talk at Drall. Mara's ultimatum (Ch16). Lumiya follows Jacen to Hapes and learns about Allana (L7857).
- **Ch18–Ch21:** Mara follows Lumiya to Hapes. Jacen visits Allana. The dogfight. The Kavan duel. Mara dies.
- **Ch22–Ch24:** Jacen hides on Ziost. Lumiya's suicide-by-Skywalker. Luke kills her, then learns from Ben that she was innocent of Mara's death (L9363). Mara left a body as evidence (L9329, L9375). Fett learns Sintas is alive in carbonite (L9494). Jacen decides the prophecy meant killing Ben's love for him (L9540–9542) and becomes «Darth Caedus» (L9584).

### SP2. Character profiles
- **Jacen/Caedus:** consistently fears his own heart. He weeps, and he tests his hand in a candle flame (L3908). He wants order: «Democracy, freedom, and peace. They were all tricks» (L5372). He kills by method, not rage: «He felt no violence or malice at all, but then he never did in combat.» (L8137). «Killing didn't get any easier. He was just getting better at it.» (L8606). He is still capable of love (Allana, L7988–8010) and suppresses it. After killing Mara he feels anticlimax (L8630), then embarrassment rather than guilt (L9562).
- **Mara:** the book is built around her return to her Emperor's Hand self: «She felt as detached and steady as she'd ever been as the Emperor's Hand.» (L8440). She blames herself for missing Jacen's fall (L7478). Her motive is Ben, and her last word is "Ben" (L8545). She chooses to leave a body so her family can find evidence and say goodbye (L9329, L9375).
- **Luke:** passive and too trusting at the start (L8970: «Mara used to say he bent over backward to see the good in everyone.»). After Mara dies he has clarity: «Luke felt like his old self for the first time in years.» (L8940). He executes Lumiya, then is broken by learning she was innocent: «it broke something deep, deep inside him.» (L9397).
- **Ben:** a child soldier with a lieutenant's methods (L9056, crime-scene discipline). He learns camouflage at once, kills Gejjen, grieves Lekauf, and starts the investigation into Mara's death.
- **Lumiya:** loyal to the Sith idea above her own life. «A death in service of a great ideal is a fine thing.» (L3940). She weeps for Jacen (L7861). She dies by her own design.
- **Fett:** dying to recovering. «I'm back.» (L7598). He refuses to waste troops on a private feud. Sintas is alive (L9494).

### SP3. Themes
- **Sacrifice** (the title): Lekauf's self-destruction (L5516), Mara's death, Lumiya's suicide, and Jacen sacrificing Ben's love (L9540).
- **Immortalized love / the tassel prophecy:** misread four times (Ben, Allana, Mara, then «Ben's admiration and devotion»).
- **The banality of tyranny:** «The Galactic Alliance would fall, not with a clash of lightsaber blades ... but with a sheet of flimsi and a nod of heads.» (L3278).
- **Parents and children:** Mara, Ben and Luke. Fett, Ailyn, Mirta and Sintas. Jacen and Allana. Han and Leia's absent grief (L8776).
- **Hindsight about Palpatine:** «Palpatine, Palpatine, Palpatine . . . your lesson in twenty-twenty hindsight.» (L8249).

### SP4. Notable prose passages
- «There was no advantage: same starfighter. No edge: she was as good a pilot. No refuge: they were in open space. It was down to who hated more, and who was more prepared to die to take out the other.» (L8103–8105)
- «It wasn't pretty, and it wouldn't capture the public's imagination like a lightsaber display at the academy, but her training was in destruction. Jacen's was in deception.» (L8410)
- «"Tell my mom I'm sorry I failed her," Jacen whispered. / "She knows," Mara said, and squeezed the trigger.» (L8442–8444)
- «This wasn't a duel: it was a brawl.» (L8491)
- «Ben was the last word she ever spoke.» (L8545)
- «In the cockpit of his StealthX heading for Hapes, Luke Skywalker felt a hand brush his hair, and as he reached out involuntarily to touch it, he knew his world had ended.» (L8577)
- «"I'd never let you fall," Luke said, and pulled her back to safety.» (L9034)
- «He will immortalize his love . . . where immortalize means "dead."» (L9542)
- «"Caedus," he said. "My name is Darth Caedus."» (L9584)

### SP5. Worldbuilding and lore stated in the text
- **The Sith meditation sphere** (from Ziost): a living training ship. «Once I fought. Now I educate and protect apprentices.» (L8193). «Masters may use their ships to fight but not involve apprentices.» (L8183). It senses darkness in Mara and Ben (L7771) and can track Jacen while he is hidden (L7554).
- **Force camouflage:** «You blend with the universe. Think of it as Force camouflage.» (L2569).
- **Sith battle-sight** belongs to full Masters only (L1021, L9293).
- **Beskar:** a new lode north of Enceri (L4278–4292). The **Bes'uliik** fighter: «Point four hyper-drive» (L4789). Mandalorian–Verpine pact (L5589–5613).
- **The Legislative and Regulations Statute Amendment** («I can change a law to let me change laws.» L1993) and **Item 357** (L3248).
- **Psychometry** with the heart-of-fire stone (Kiffar ability; L9149–9175).

### SP6. Timeline placement and connections
- 40 ABY. Follows *Exile* (Lumiya and Alema hunt; Gilatter VIII; Ailyn Vel's death under Jacen's interrogation, L727). Followed by *Inferno*, where Ben investigates Mara's murder, and later Luke vs. Caedus.
- Callbacks: Luke shot down Shira Brie's TIE (L883). Vergere's torture of Jacen (L9536). Kamino and the Null ARCs (Jaing). Geonosis (Fett, L9231).
- Foreshadowing: Mara's last words «Luke will crush you» (L8541). Caedus expects Ben's future hatred (L9554). Lumiya warns «Alema is still prowling» (L8708). The sphere is stolen from Terephon (L9410).

---

## 6. Verdict-relevant takeaways
1. **Caedus vs. Mara:** the only on-page result is a Jacen win, but on conditions that favored Mara (her terrain, her ambush, his injuries). She was winning the physical fight. He was Jacen, not yet the full Sith Lord: his Sith battle-sight only arrives after her death (L9293), which by the book's own logic is a power-up. Both POVs agree that Jacen is the stronger Force user (S5, S7). **Fair 1v1 at full power: Caedus wins most of the time. Mara with prep can kill pre-Caedus Jacen.**
2. **Caedus vs. Luke:** this book only gives statements. Lumiya's «Luke can still take you in a lightsaber fight.» (L3958) and Jacen's own poison planning (L6948) both point to Luke.
3. **Jaina vs. Caedus:** no data here.
4. **Pre-Caedus Jacen's top Force feat in the book:** while crushed and bleeding, he deflects a point-blank bolt and blasts off a tunnel's worth of rubble (L8471).
5. **Stealth matters a great deal at this tier.** Camouflage let Ben, then Mara, get past Jacen's senses several times.

## Appendix: raw reading log (written while reading, in order; quotes verified with the same script)

#### Prologue (L64–135) — Mara POV, first person, 0300 hours Coruscant
- Mara counts her kills; wonders if she should have killed Palpatine (L84). Vows against Lumiya: «She tried to kill my kid. For that, sweetheart, you're going to have to answer to me. I'm coming for you, and soon.» (L130)
- Luke «can do that without warning. Bang-he just snaps alert.» (L110). Luke: «But he's okay. I can still feel him.» (L118)
#### Ch1 (L136–622)
- Epigraph: knotted-tassel prophecy "He will strengthen himself through sacrifice ... He will immortalize his love." (L140–158)
- Ben flies Sith meditation sphere from Ziost, controlled by thought (L174–176); it destroyed a pursuing ship on his thoughts (L226, L312).
- Jacen can't be sensed unless he wants: Ben «Jacen's trick of hiding in the Force had become permanent; Ben found he was impossible to track unless he wanted to be found» (L224) [feat-ish/POV]; Jacen: «I can't be sensed in the Force if I don't want to be» (L352, statement).
- Jacen locates Ben (55 kg boy) across Perlemian corridor through Force, magnifies presence (L258–276) — sensing feat.
- Jacen: «it was now hard to let go and be swept, much harder than harnessing its power» (L268) — characterization of his dark drift.
- Sphere recognizes Lumiya as Sith (L350); tracked Jacen via his "Force disturbance in the Gilatter system" (L356).
- Lumiya on the sphere: «It's ancient, absolutely ancient.» (L344); «The woman who's more machine, and the machine that's more creature.» (L366). Jacen: «What, whether you intended Ben to be killed, or who I have to kill to achieve full Sith Mastery?» (L372). Lumiya: «But all I feel is soon, and close.» (L382)
- Fett at Keldabe Meeting of Clans (L392–620): declares neutrality «Mandalore has no position on the current war» (L488); names Goran Beviin stand-in; Beviin «a complete savage with a beskad» (L552, statement). Fett dying (L402), hunting clone "Skirata" (L566,L584). «His time will come. Leave him to me.» re Jacen (L566). Purple-armored "Graad" (L534) = foreshadow (Kad'ika/Venku?).
#### Ch2 (L623–1251)
- Omas/Niathal/Jacen wash-up of Gilatter VIII. Niathal: «I hear you fought quite remarkably.» (L663, statement). Jacen admits he «lost Boba Fett's daughter during an interrogation» (L727). Omas: «he'll either win the war for us ... or bring us down.» (L803).
- Lumiya in sphere senses Alema «like a beacon even at this range» (L861) — sensing. Lumiya flashback to Luke shooting down her TIE (L883). Sphere: Ben «Not like us» (L899); Alema «Less like us than the one who follows» (L903).
- Lumiya's reasoning about "immortalize his love": «He had to immortalize his love-to kill it, to destroy what he loved most.» (L921); she rates Mara: «Mara? She might have been the last person to stand by Jacen, but he had less feeling for her than for his own parents. Ben, then.» (L933) — dramatic irony (it's Mara he kills).
- Lumiya: «perhaps . . . it's going to be me he kills.» (L935)
- Lumiya threatens Alema: «Get in my way, and I'll kill you.» (L969); Alema «insane, and a Dark Jedi with those problems was a very dangerous complication» (L947). Lumiya rams vibroblade in own thigh: metal (L989); «I'm actually more machine than organic» (L991). Lumiya's limits: «Telepathy was beyond her» (L981); «Lumiya didn't have the complete Sith ability to see all the pieces in the game, every element in the battle. That was for a full Sith Master.» (L1021) — statement (battle-meditation-like ability reserved to full Master). Alema: «Our expertise is surveillance and assassination» (L1031). Alema crippled: one arm limp «paralyzed by Luke Skywalker's lightsaber» (L1055), crippled foot, lost lekku stump.
- Mara/Kyp (L1069–1159): Mara: «I'm going to kill Lumiya.» (L1109). Kyp: «The old cyborg must be running low on lube oil by now. You could take her anytime.» (L1115, statement). Mara: «But she's not so decrepit, believe me. I won't have an easy time of it.» (L1117). Kyp offers backup (L1119). Shadow Council forming (L1125). Mara «mused on ways to get around a lightwhip» (L1143).
- Mara at spaceport: Mara «didn't care how many Centerpoints he could take out» (L1077). Ben: «Oh, Jacen can take him.» re Fett (L1205, statement, low reliability). Ben: «You have to go with the best data you have at the time.» (L1245). Mara: by Ben's age she'd learned «the most silent and efficient ways to kill the Emperor's enemies» (L1171).
#### Ch3 (L1252–)
- Fett & Mirta to Bador, Kuat (Paradise cantina). Mirta Kiffar psychometry partial (L1358). Fett left hook on barkeep drawing disruptor, he and Mirta draw first (L1434–1436) — minor skill feat. Fett «didn't deploy full dampers when Slave /jumped» (sic, OCR) (L1376).
- Fett learns gray-gloved clone "Skirata" killed gang boss Cherit; lead: Fraig at Tekshar Falls Casino, Kuat City (L1454–1484). «If you see him again, tell him little Boba wants to see him about a job.» (L1474)
- Jacen's "voice in his head" (Vergere/Lumiya/conscience) (L1528). «I just can't kill my little girl.» (L1538) → «"It's not them," he said. "It has to be Ben."» (L1558). Lapse: didn't notice caf placed (L1560–1562). «When I rule as a Sith Lord.» (L1582). Lekauf = grandson of a Vader loyalist (L1564).
- Procurement plot begins (Niathal's kit complaint).
- Luke (L1678–1878): touched Lumiya's hand mid-battle (L1686). Luke practices with a **shoto** «the short lightsaber that he felt he needed to deal with Lumiya and her lightwhip» (L1690) — prep. Luke on Palpatine: «the only truly irredeemable being he'd ever known was Palpatine» (L1740). Ben refuses to leave GAG (L1734, L1746).
- Mara gives Ben her Imperial vibroblade: «That," she said, "was the best vibroblade the Empire could buy. It saved me more than once. A lightsaber is great, but a lightsaber and a vibroblade is even better."» (L1796) [this blade matters later?]
- Mara: «Luke, you could have killed her. Cut her in two. Finished the job. But you didn't.» (L1822); «She's not your father, Luke. There's nothing good left in her to redeem.» (L1830). Mara preps: blaster with grenade-launcher muzzle «With a flechette cartridge. Let's see her try a lightwhip on that.» (L1850); takes Luke's shoto (L1864). «I can hunt very well.» (L1864). «And who better to go after a former Emperor's Hand than another one?» (L1872); «I'm much more dangerous now I have a cub to protect.» (L1876) — statement/self-assessment. Luke: «it was the first time in his life that he regretted not killing someone when he'd had the chance.» (L1878)
#### Ch4 (L1881–)
- Niathal memo authorizes Jacen «to use any means necessary» on procurement (L1895). Legal droid HM-3 drafts the Legislative and Regulations Statute Amendment (law to change laws) (L1909–1997). HM-3: «With Admiral Niathal, you are effectively a triumvirate.» (L1975). Jacen: «He'd spent five years learning the most arcane Force techniques in the galaxy, but-again-he didn't have to use a single Force skill to gain power this time.» (L1979); compares to Palpatine's manipulation (L1983). «I can change a law to let me change laws.» (L1993)
- Jacen reassigns purchasing officer Biris Te Gaf to front-line ship *Ocean* (L2057); uses «Force-enhanced hearing» to read the room (L2057) — minor feat. Lekauf: «Your grandfather would have done the same, sir.» (L2067). «I can change the law that lets me change laws. If I use that wisely, I can bypass the Senate when I need to.» (L2097–2099). «The power of simple human reason was as effective as the Force some days.» (L2101)
- Fett/Mirta at Tekshar Falls Casino (L2107–2437): Fett hacks Kuat police DB from HUD (L2153); fibercord loops gangster Fraig over balcony (L2297) — skill; Mirta head-butts/stabs Hamadryas guard (L2301). Fett ageing: «This would have been an easier task a few years ago.» (L2345). Lead: Twi'lek family Himar (L2321).
- Unseen sniper drops pursuing speeders silently (L2381–2397). Gray-armored Mandalorian with Verpine rifle + strill holds Mirta in headlock: «It's little Bob'ika. Last time we met, my brother was shoving your head down the 'freshers to teach you some manners.» (L2437) [= Mird Skirata/clone; actually "Venku"? reveal later].
- Ben back at GAG (L2443–). Jacen hides from Force-sensitives but courts mundane attention (L2473); Ben wants to learn the hiding trick (L2475). Jacen authorizes tapping Senators' comlinks under EMA (L2485). Jacen on leadership (L2513).
- Jacen's presence «vanished for a few seconds and then returned» (L2527). Jacen: «Don't you worry about Lumiya. She's not up to killing you.» (L2537, statement). Jacen teaches Ben **Force camouflage** (L2545–2573): «You blend with the universe. Think of it as Force camouflage.» (L2569). Ben does it first try: «"Now," Jacen said softly, "you see why practice is necessary. But full marks for technique."» (L2565) — Ben feat (learning speed).
- Jacen orders Ben to **assassinate Dur Gejjen** (L2621) for plotting Tenel Ka's death; backup Shevu & Lekauf (L2645). «I can't exactly ask Boba Fett to do this, can I?» (L2635). Ben recalls Mara as «once the Emperor's Hands, the best assassin of her day» (L2653, statement, POV).
- Jacen alone: «I can't do this. I can't kill him.» (L2659–2661); doubts prophecy reading; «So only the Sith deal in absolutes, do they, Obi-Wan? ... You liar.» (L2675); weeps (L2695).
#### Ch5 (L2698–)
- Epigraph: Gejjen plans to get Jacen «out of the way» (L2702).
- Mara in XJ7 hunting Lumiya; calls Leia (Leia offers to be bait, L2742). «I'm going to find her ship and finish what Luke started. They're always vulnerable in transit.» (L2740). Mara: «I've got one advantage you haven't, Leia, and that's darkness. I've been that dark. I was trained by a Sith Lord. I can think like them.» (L2778, statement/self). The vibroblade given to Ben hides **a long-range passive transponder** (L2784); «She had one more transponder left» (L2790) [Chekhov].
- Mara feels Ben vanish from the Force (practicing camouflage) and return, elated (L2800–2822). «Ben had proved capable of astounding feats.» (L2822)
- Gray Mando = **Jaing Skirata**, a Null ARC clone (L2852, L2870); strill Lord Mirdalan (L2898). He ambushed Mirta unseen: «I didn't pick him up on my sensors and I didn't even see him coming at me.» (L2860); Fett: «Jaing tracked Grievous in the war. Master assassin, sniper, general pain in the backside. Don't underestimate him.» (L2878, statement). Ko Sai research destroyed after use (L2978). Fett «A year. Maybe two» to live (L3000). Jaing's condition: «listen to Kad'ika's advice, and build a strong, united, stable state» (L3038). Blood sample taken (L3056). Fett «an armory on legs» (L3056).
- Jaing: Fett's armor «duse. Not even proper beskar» (L3070); strills fly (L3076); Jaing avoids leaving DNA (L3082). Jaing on Fett: «he wins, and we need winners» (L3088).
- Jacen identifies Mara at the gate through the Force (L3148). Lumiya ("Shira") in public gallery via cybernetic comm implant (L3178–3180). Jacen projects «a veneer of weary good humor in the Force» to Mara (L3188) — deception feat vs Mara. Mara confronts him about Lumiya's mole & abandoning his parents (L3198–3228). Jacen: «She was Palpatine's Hand. If anyone on the Jedi Council can see it, she will.» (L3200, POV statement on Mara's perceptiveness). Jacen concludes «She knows ... that proves it has to be Ben.» (L3230–3234).
- Lumiya admits Ziost attack on Ben: «It's my duty to retire the unsuitable.» (L3240); calls Mara «my former colleague» (L3236,L3244) [both Emperor's Hands]. Lumiya Force-influences the P&R Council from the gallery to pass amendment Item 357 (L3248–3282): «I just redirected a little opposition from the gallery.» (L3282). Jacen: «The Galactic Alliance would fall, not with a clash of lightsaber blades ... but with a sheet of flimsi and a nod of heads.» (L3278). «They deserved to be ruled by the Sith. They needed to be.» (L3286–3288)
- Jacen anticipates: «Once I kill Ben Skywalker, once Mara and Luke find out that it's me ... they'll hunt me down. I'll bring down the whole Jedi order on my head.» (L3262)
#### Ch6 (L3291–)
- Niathal aboard *Bounty* vs new Bothan frigate flotilla (19 frigates); 12 destroyed/disabled in ~20 min (L3423–3439). Niathal decides to give Omas an ultimatum, needs Jacen (L3455).
- Ben in GAG monitoring center with Girdun (L3465–). Wiretap: Gejjen–Omas secret talk: «Niathal has to go. Jacen Solo has to go.» (L3507); Omas: Centerpoint «It already is» inoperative (L3515).
- Omas agrees to meet Gejjen on Vulpter (L3535–3537). Jacen deliberately does not Force-influence Niathal (L3577). Jacen: «he was sure he could sense bugs in a room. There were none.» (L3759) (sensing claim, POV). Jacen proposes **regime change**: duumvirate with Niathal (L3763–3777); «Jacen liked two; two was the Sith way.» (L3779). Plan: house arrest Omas, Ben assassinates Gejjen, then Jacen calls Lumiya «I need you to do a job for me.» (L3823). Jacen's shift in language: «Ben has to die. It felt very different from I have to kill him.» (L3657).
- Niathal: «She was his equal in maneuvering, but she didn't have Force powers.» (L3649, Jacen POV).
#### Ch7 (L3826–)
- Epigraph: Mandalore site foreman finds something in rock N of Enceri (→ beskar deposit, L3830).
- **Lumiya–Jacen safe house (L3840–3964)** — key statements:
  - Lumiya POV: «He had full mastery of the Force, but he seemed to enjoy using the limited tricks of ordinary people.» (L3848) — STATEMENT (Lumiya, his teacher; high-ish reliability, but she's biased/hyping).
  - Lumiya: «Even now your powers put you far beyond their reach. Omas can't touch you. Neither can Gejjen.» (L3874) — re mundanes.
  - Jacen: «The Force can't affect the minds of millions.» (L3876). Lumiya: «Don't make Palpatine's mistakes.» (L3878)
  - Jacen holds hand in candle flame as test (L3908, Vergere's pain-training).
  - Jacen asks Lumiya to **distract Mara** (L3910); Brisha story was Lumiya's cover (L3914–3916). Lumiya on why she offered Luke her hand: «killing him would have changed the course of events for all of us» (L3922). «The Skywalkers are too mired in their domesticity to be effective Jedi» (L3928).
  - Lumiya will use a pair of Ben's boots as bait (L3936–3938). Lumiya: «I might win, and anyway-it'll buy you time.» (L3940); «I'm expendable ... A death in service of a great ideal is a fine thing.» (L3940).
  - **Lumiya: «Think laterally," Lumiya said gently. "Luke can still take you in a lightsaber fight."»** (L3958) — KEY STATEMENT (teacher's assessment: Luke > Jacen with a saber at this point). Jacen: «I'm already a few steps ahead of him. Trust me.» (L3960). Jacen: «I have quite an armory» (L3956) [→ poison darts later?].
- Ben: Niathal designated acting Chief of State for a day as Omas goes to Vulpter (L3974–3982). Lekauf now a lieutenant (L4006). Plan: Ben gets close to Gejjen after the Omas meeting (L4030).
- Ben's sniper training (Karpaki Fifty, gel-form droid, L4036–4168). Lekauf on Vader: «Really uncanny accuracy in three dimensions, whether he was flying a ship or using a weapon.» (L4080, statement 3rd hand); Vader cared for burned lieutenant (L4088). Ben uses the Force: «It was just like using a lightsaber, really. Letting the Force guide the hand, the eye . . .» (L4140); first shot hits temple (L4142); **«A couple of hours later, he was hitting the five-centimeter zone 95 percent of the time.»** (L4168) — skill feat; later «I'm scoring ninety-seven percent, sir.» (L4423). Gel-form «made no impression in the Force, which limited Ben's senses» (L4150).
- Mandalore: Medrit Vasur forges test beskad from new **beskar** lode north of Enceri (L4278–4288), exposed by Vong craters. «Beskar was the toughest metal known to science. Even lightsabers had trouble with it.» (L4292) — narration statement. Fett tells Beviin «Two years, tops. Eight, nine months if I carry on like this.» (L4212). Beviin killed Vong with beskad (L4268).
#### Ch8 (L4397–)
- Epigraph: Gejjen's long game (L4401).
- Shevu briefs Ben: hit at Charbi City Spaceport, Vulpter; Corellian-registered ship as false flag; Ben dyes hair brown (L4429–4481). Shevu: «it's amazing that we can pretty well train a sniper in a day. If he's a Jedi, of course.» (L4429). Ben may «nudge a mind» to escape interrogation (L4489–4491). **Ben's parade-best boots are missing** (L4497) → taken by Jacen for Lumiya's bait.
- **Lumiya at the Skywalker apartment (L4511–4619)**: Luke senses her presence as «a cloud of billowing smoke ... it suddenly lit up as if a silent explosion had lifted it in a ball of soaring flame.» (L4529); she reveals herself on purpose (amusement L4527). Luke tries to Force-jam two sets of corridor doors to trap her (L4535) — she's already gone; Ben's boots wedge the door (L4541). Luke feels Ben in the boots (psychometry-like, L4543). Jacen deflects: Lekauf «As his grandfather was to Vader» (L4575). Luke: «So much for what he'd learned about arcane Force techniques during his five-year sabbatical.» (L4577). Luke's self-assessment: «Just because he had whatever gifts the Force had given him more generously than other Jedi» (L4609, narration statement: Luke most gifted); «He was Luke Skywalker. And if he could take on the Empire without a second thought, he could certainly finish off one of the last pitiful remnants of its rule-Lumiya.» (L4619)
- Starfighter Command: Mara & Jaina plot Lumiya/Alema sightings. Mara: Lumiya and Alema «both good at hiding, whether by disguising their presence or erasing the memory of being seen» (L4643). Mara/Jaina spat re Jacen, Jag/Zekk (L4659–4691). Luke: «I'm going after Lumiya. Enough.» Mara overrules: «You have too much history with Lumiya, and you're too stoked up. You have to kill cold.» (L4735). Mara's loadout: «Lightsaber, vibroblades, hold-out blaster, flechette launcher, and the last of my transponders.» (L4721) — PREP. Jaina comes along to hunt Alema (L4723). Mara sees Ben's transponder on **Vulpter** (L4757), hides it from Luke (L4761). Mara: «I'm coming, cyborg. It's time.» (L4765)
#### Ch9 (L4767–)
- MandalMotors **Bes'uliik** (Basilisk) prototype: «Point four hyper-drive» (L4789), stealth hull, beskar laminate planned (L4809). Fett: sell to GA only if «Colonel Jacen Solo comes here in person to negotiate the deal.» (L4837).
- Vulpter, Charbi Spaceport: Ben & Lekauf in false-flag Corellian tourer; Ben counts six beings in sealed room via the Force (L4953–4955) — sensing feat. Strip-cam records Omas–Gejjen talks (L4957–4989).
- Omas–Gejjen: Gejjen offers to kill Jacen and Niathal «permanently»; Omas: «If you do, I don't need to know about it.» (L5001–5003); they mock Girdun as bribable (L5007).
- **Ben kills Gejjen**: rooftop shot with Karpaki from observation deck, ~100 m (scope «a hundred meters closer», L5043): «Yes, he'd killed Gejjen, a textbook head shot.» (L5079). Ben afterward: «I can't feel my face.» (L5081). CSA officer locks down port (L5089).
#### Ch10 (L5094–)
- Mara tracks Lumiya's deliberately magnified wake (L5112) to Hesperidium (moon) (L5154). Mara: Lumiya «seemed to have discovered a rare talent for Force-acting» (benign impression) (L5128). Mara: «Ben or Jacen ...» targets. Mara: Lumiya «The very last of her kind» (L5152). Mara senses Ben vanish (L5136).
- Meditation sphere talks to Mara: «You are . . . very similar.» (L5216); «You know darkness well. Better than the other one that I expected to see return.» (L5226). Mara plants **last transponder** inside the sphere's hatch (L5232).
- **DUEL: Mara vs Lumiya, Hesperidium (L5242–5316)**:
  - Lumiya: «Hello, little housewife . . .» (L5244). Mara: «Amateurs gave speeches; professionals got on with the job.» (L5246)
  - «She Force-leapt five meters at Lumiya, slashing down right to left, two-handed. The stroke-all power, no finesse-clipped the Sith's headdress as she sprang back» (L5248). Mara slows the whip tails with a Force push (L5248).
  - Lightwhip: «leather strips studded with impervious Mandalorian iron fragments and tendrils of sheer, raw, murderous dark energy» (L5250); whip gouges through a ship's durasteel hull (L5250).
  - Mara blaster hit to Lumiya's shoulder: «A puff of white vapor burst from Lumiya's shoulder» (L5252) («Maybe I hit metal», L5254).
  - Mara Force-tackles: «cannoned into Lumiya's legs with all the power she could muster from the Force. She hit solid durasteel.» (L5256); head-butts the cybernetic jaw, cuts own forehead (L5258).
  - **Mara stabs Lumiya through the chest**: «she killed the lightsaber blade for a second and held the hilt like a dagger, stabbing it down into Lumiya's chest before flicking the energy back on. Lumiya pulled to the side as the blade punched through flesh.» (L5258)
  - Unknown machine attacker hits Mara from behind, wire around her neck, throws her (L5268); Mara: «Something like a droid jumped me. A machine, anyway. That's why I didn't sense it.» (L5290). Lumiya flees in sphere (L5274). Lumiya did not finish Mara: «Why didn't she kill me? She had the chance.» (L5302).
  - Outcome: Mara wounded (blood in eyes, right eye swelling, rope burn), Lumiya escapes wounded. Context: Lumiya's mission was only to distract (L3910, L3940); she had a hidden helper (likely the sphere/droid — check later).
  - Mara mind-tricks two joggers: «You didn't see a fight at all.» (L5278–5280) — Force feat (mind trick while concussed).
  - Mara's transponder tracks Lumiya toward Hydian Way node (L5304–5312). Mara heals by trance (L5300, L5316).
- Jacen gets strip-cam recording; HM-3 enacts amendment giving GAG power to detain heads of state (L5342); midnight arrest of Omas planned (L5362). Jacen on democracy: «Democracy, freedom, and peace. They were all tricks» (L5372).
- Vulpter lockdown: Ben: «I can jump anywhere, open any door» (L5414); Shevu: «No Force nonsense» (L5418). **Lekauf takes the rifle**, takes a hostage, claims to be a Corellian patriot who killed Gejjen (L5426–5470) — self-sacrifice decoy.
- **Lekauf's sacrifice**: Lekauf blows himself up in the tourer to provide the cover story (L5516); Shevu: «Has to be done.» / «A good cover story.» (L5496–5500). Ben senses Lekauf «terrified, oddly triumphant, and with a strange sense of peace despite the dread» (L5492). [Book title theme: sacrifice.]
#### Ch11 (L5523–)
- HNE: Gejjen «shot dead ... by a Corellian terrorist» who «blew himself up» (L5527).
- Fett signs a mutual-aid/nonaggression deal with the Verpine of Roche (beskar for tech) (L5589–5613). Fett visits Jango's grave; warns Ghes Orade re Mirta (L5637); Mando mass graves / manda (L5701–5713). Fett: «I put the full fee for Sal-Solo in a trust fund for you.» (L5683) [confirms Fett killed Thrackan for Gejjen].
- Jaing's message: «I SEE YOU DID RIGHT BY MANDALORE. I'LL DO RIGHT BY YOU.» (L5795).
- **Jedi Council emergency meeting (L5813–5929)**: Saba, Corran, Kyp, Kyle Katarn, Cilghal present (eleven members, L5905). Kyle's reads on Mandalorians/Verpine (L5867). Mara arrives with black eye after healing trance: «Look, I caught Lumiya. She's in a worse state than I am, believe me.» (L5895); «Lumiya's mine.» (L5911). To Luke: «I head-butted her ... Metal jaw, nonmetal head.» (L5933); «I think she has a droid with her ... it can pay out a metal cable.» (L5937). Luke: «If anyone could take Lumiya, Mara could» (L5943, POV statement). Luke on why Lumiya/Alema are hard to hunt: «two Jedi with concealment skills could vanish very effectively in an entire galaxy» (L5945). Dinner at eight (L5955) [poignant: last home dinner promise].
- Ben grieves Lekauf (L5995–6043): «And it was Ben's fault. Lekauf had died to protect him.» (L6003). Shevu: «it was supposed to be me who blew the ship if things went wrong» (L6039). Shevu hints Ben shouldn't be around Jacen (L6027–6029). Ben suspects «if Jacen had set him up» (L6045).
- **Mara–Ben tapcaf (L6053–6161)**: Ben confesses: «I did it, Mom.» ... «Folding-stock Karpaki, frangible round.» (L6085–6089); «Jacen.» gave the order (L6105). Mara: «Let's not tell your dad, because he'll rip Jacen's head off» (L6109). Mara: «This is what we made you, isn't it?» (L6129). Mara: «Please, Ben, don't hide from me. Ever.» (L6135). Ben tells her of the Omas–Gejjen meeting (L6151). Mara's fury at Jacen established (L6107, L6153) — motive for her investigation of Jacen.
#### Ch12 (L6164–)
- 0001 hours: Jacen with Sgt Wirut & Trooper Limm arrests Omas at Republica House (L6186–6244): «Sir ... I'm arresting you in the name of the Galactic Alliance for activity likely to compromise the safety of the state.» (L6244). Niathal goes to G'Sil (L6202).
- Omas arrested with dignity: «You obnoxious, power-crazed, ludicrous little jerk ... You had Gejjen killed, too, didn't you?» (L6282). Jacen's hands shake after (L6290). Bloodless coup: GA troops at government buildings (L6298).
#### Ch13 (L6309–)
- Niathal addresses Senate as Acting Chief of State; plays the Omas–Gejjen footage; proposes Jacen as joint Chief of State (L6409–6431). Niathal: «If he gets out of hand . . . well, there's always Fett.» (L6355). Niathal on Mandalorians: «I accept that they're phenomenal soldiers.» (L6373); G'Sil: «But Fett's a smart, smart man.» (L6379). Alliance Intel placed under GAG, Girdun in charge (L6441). Omas's office sealed (L6445–6449).
- Jedi Council next day: Kyp: «Jacen. There, I said it. Jacen, Jacen, Jacen.» (L6507); Corran: not a coup legally (L6501); Kyp: Jacen acts as security officer, not Jedi (L6529); Mara: «"There's rights," said Mara, "and there's right."» (L6535). Mara grinding teeth in sleep, withdrawn (L6491–6493). Luke resolves to see Niathal (L6539).
- **Ben eavesdrops on Jacen & Lumiya (L6577–6659)**: Ben, hidden in the Force, approaches undetected — Jacen «probably relied on sensing people coming and going» (L6597) → Ben's camouflage beats Jacen's senses here (feat). Ben can't sense Lumiya either (L6585, L6593). Jacen to Lumiya: «You cut it too fine ... There's being a decoy, and there's being too clever ... Are you recovered now?» (L6599) — confirms Hesperidium fight was Lumiya as **decoy**, and she was genuinely hurt. Lumiya: «She really thinks I want revenge for some daughter . . .» (L6601). **Lumiya reveals Jacen mind-rubbed Ben about Nelani**: «And mind-rubbing Ben about Nelani isn't?» (L6605). Lumiya: «that's why he can't ever be your apprentice. Get rid of him» (L6611). Ben decides to warn Mara (L6657).
#### Ch14 (L6662–)
- Fett: «Jacen Solo, the boy who wants to be Vader when he grows up.» (L6682). **Venku** (mixed-armor Mandalorian; = Kad'ika, Jaing's nephew-figure) delivers a blue container — the cure — «There'll be someone along shortly to administer it.» (L6714).
- Venku reveals himself: «But you probably know me better as Kad'ika. Interesting to meet you at last . . . Uncle Boba.» (L6744). Cure must be injected into bone marrow (L6718).
- **Mara–Ben tapcaf #2 (L6750–6886)**: Ben has near-eidetic recall (L6766). Mara reclassifies Jacen: «Not a vain, conceited, naive victim of a manipulative Sith: an accomplice.» (L6770). Mara pieces together the sphere = Ben's Ziost ship → Jacen gave it to Lumiya (L6828–6830). Mara concludes the "droid" was the sphere: «It wasn't a droid. I got bushwhacked by a living ship, a Sith ship.» (L6816) — so Hesperidium fight was effectively **2v1 (Lumiya + Sith sphere)**. Mara's transponder shows the sphere «stationary on Coruscant» (L6792).
  - Mara: «She was the Hand again, planning her move.» (L6818). «I want you to behave like a coward for a change.» (L6842)
  - **Ben teaches Mara Force camouflage**: «After a couple of hours, she could manage a minute or two without needing to grab something solid.» (L6868) — Mara skill acquisition (sets up final hunt).
  - Mara: «you're probably going to see a side of me soon that isn't good old Mom ... I love you, and you're my heart» (L6878) — foreshadowing.
- **Jacen alone (L6892–6950)**: writes «HE WILL IMMORTALIZE HIS LOVE.» in steam (L6908); nusito-pup parable (L6920–6922); reaches out to Allana in the Force (L6924–6928); Force-visions of Ben sobbing «It's too high a price.» (L6934). **Prep for Luke & Mara**: «He examined his lightsaber and blaster, and knew that those still wouldn't be enough when Luke and Mara came after him to exact vengeance for Ben. He took out the box of assorted poisons and pathogens that could be delivered by dart or projectile ... He had all the bases covered: chemical, biological, mechanical.» (L6948) — KEY PREP (Jacen's own assessment that saber+blaster insufficient vs Luke & Mara).
- Jacen eyes Niathal as rule-of-two candidate: «It was just as well she wasn't a Force-user.» (L6954).
#### Ch15 (L6957–)
- Fett gets Jaing/Venku's cure via bone-marrow injections in sternum from a vet (L6987–7027): Venku: «he'll start to show signs of recovery in a few days» (L7021). Fett's toughness: crushes Mirta's fingers, no sound (L7005, L7025). Venku/Kad'ika is son of a clone trooper (L7031); «I have a healing touch» (L7041) [Venku is Force-sensitive in other works; hint only].
- Bes'uliik with micronized beskar skin flies (L7073–7083): «If we skin her completely in solid beskar, she's going to be invulnerable, but heavy.» (L7087, statement).
- Jacen at Senate plaza: dissuades CSF officers «with a little Force influence» (L7143) — minor feat; shows protesters the sealed Chief's office for HNE (L7155–7169). «How to take over the state: smile, look reluctant, and keep the traffic flowing.» (L7135). Niathal: «Last time I saw anything that slick and oily...» (L7173). Murkhana senator fears Roche–Mandalore treaty (L7187–7195).
- Jacen proposes sending GA frigate/fighters toward Roche to scout Bes'uliik (L7235–7255); Jacen: Fett «far too smart to waste his troops to fight a personal feud» (L7259). Mandalorians as destabilizing «third element in a universe of pairs» (L7265). «Vader had had the measure of them in his day: Jacen would, as well.» (L7303). Jacen takes 48 hours to visit Hapes (L7297–7301).
- **Mara & Leia at Drall spa (L7311–7409)**: Leia on Jacen: «He's stubborn and he's his own man. She could make the difference between him doing something and hesitating, but she could never make him act totally against his will.» (L7375) — STATEMENT (mother; high reliability; establishes Jacen's agency). **Mara decides: «I'm going to have to stop Jacen. I have to. I'm going to have to kill your son, because that's the only way of stopping him now.»** (L7389). She resolves to confront him once more first (L7407). Mara recalls Palpatine hindsight (L7383).
#### Ch16 (L7412–)
- Bothawui offers Mandalore 20M credits/month for an assault fleet + Bes'uliik exclusivity (L7416).
- **Mara confronts Jacen in Senate lobby (L7426–)**: Mara used Ben's camouflage: «No, he hadn't felt her presence when it mattered. Thank you, Ben. Nice trick.» (L7430) — FEAT (Mara undetected by Jacen). Jacen gives off no Force presence (L7442). **Mara POV statement: «She couldn't outstrip his Force powers, but the tricks of flesh and blood put her on a more level playing field.»** (L7444) — KEY STATEMENT (Mara concedes Jacen is stronger in the Force).
- Mara's ultimatum: «come with me right now to the Temple. We'll get the whole Council together and we'll deprogram you.» (L7462); «Drop this Sith garbage now, or take what's coming.» (L7470). Jacen: «Good night, Aunt Mara» (L7474). Mara: «Jacen was going to die.» (L7482). Mara: «You should have listened to Luke. He was never fooled by all that sophistry» (L7478).
- Lumiya on Jacen: «The perfect balance: strength driven by passion but not confined by sentimentality.» (L7496); «The man could meditate in the middle of a hurricane.» (L7526, Lumiya POV). Lumiya: Ben «fit to be the sacrifice ... too dangerously weak to be his apprentice» (L7510). Jacen leaves 24–48h (to Hapes). Lumiya follows him with the sphere, which can find Jacen when hidden (L7554–7556). [Mara's transponder is still in the sphere → leads Mara/Luke.]
- Fett's cure works: «The cell degeneration's stopped.» (L7620); «another thirty years, maybe more» (L7634). Fett on Jacen: «Better that you rot than die. I can wait.» (L7596). Fett's helmet: «Fett could see through solid walls and eavesdrop kilometers away.» (L7600, tech).
#### Ch17 (L7645–)
- Ben at Shevu's (girlfriend Shula). The Sith sphere calls Ben telepathically from afar (L7699–7705). Ben still thinks Lumiya is twisting Jacen (L7705).
- Ben leaves to hunt the sphere; **left his vibroblade (Mara's transponder) at Shevu's** (L7713) — so Mara's tracker shows him at Shevu's (L7735). Ben signs out an unmarked long-range speeder.
- **Mara leaves to hunt Jacen (L7719–7749)**: packs gray fatigues + weapons; follows Lumiya's transponder to Perlemian; books a **StealthX** (L7735). Kisses sleeping Luke: «Sorry I never spotted it ... But better late than never.» (L7741). Note: «Gone hunting for a few days. Don't be mad at me, farmboy . . .» (L7747).
- Sphere to Lumiya: it «stopped her fighting» (L7775) — confirms the sphere attacked Mara on Hesperidium; it senses darkness in Mara and Ben («The two with my flame», L7771). «You darknesses should never fight.» (L7775).
- Lumiya follows Jacen to **Hapes** (L7793); Jacen's presence explodes with emotion: «His Force presence could be strong enough to drown out everyone else's around him.» (L7839) — Lumiya POV statement on raw presence. Lumiya feels through Jacen's hands a soft toy → **discovers Allana**: «Oh, Jacen, you had a child with Tenel Ka.» (L7857). Lumiya weeps, concludes the sacrifice will be the child (L7859–7865). «Lumiya accepted that her days were numbered.» (L7811)
#### Ch18 (L7868–)
- Roche gives Murkhana 24h ultimatum; GA fighters patrol (L7872).
- Mara in StealthX at Hapes; Lumiya's sphere in asteroids (L7902). Mara: «Yeah, I'm going to kill my sister-in-law's son. On the dark scale of ten, that's a twelve.» (L7900). Social-engineers Hapan Fleet Ops, learns Jacen is at the Fountain Palace (L7928–7948); tells Hapans they may neutralize the sphere (L7946).
- Ben in a GAG shuttle tails the sphere to Hapes, staying shut down in the Force; senses Mara's presence nearby (L7964–7978). Ben maps the sphere's position by its silent "voice" (L7968) — sensing feat.
- **Jacen at Fountain Palace**: gives Allana a stuffed tauntaun she names «Jacen» (L7998–8002). Jacen's loadout: «blaster, lightsaber, vibroblade, and toxin darts in the belt around his waist» (L8026) — PREP (relevant to Mara duel). Jacen on what he'd face if Ben died: Mara «an animal robbed of her young» and «Luke ... a man who could bring down the Empire, and whose blood was closer to Vader's even than his own, wouldn't be paralyzed by grief.» (L8034) — Jacen POV respect for Luke. Allana = «his weak point» (L8036); Jacen fears «the vengeance of Luke or Mara» more than Hapan nobles (L8036).
- Jacen senses «something dark ... something hostile that was trying hard not to be» (L8046); assumes Lumiya; «Lumiya, I can't let you live now» (L8066). **Mara's StealthX sits so close on his tail he can't fire**: «How did the proximity sensors miss it? How did he miss it?» (L8072) — Mara stealth feat vs Jacen. «"Game over, Lumiya," he said ... "You bet it is," said Mara.» (L8076–8078)
#### Ch19 (L8081–) — StealthX dogfight, Hapes Cluster
- Epigraph: Mara logged out StealthX Five-Alpha at 0036, no flight plan (L8085).
- Narration: «There was no advantage: same starfighter. No edge: she was as good a pilot.» (L8103–8105) — STATEMENT (narration via Jacen POV: Mara = Jacen as pilot). «It was down to who hated more, and who was more prepared to die» (L8105).
- Jacen: «Mara seemed to be able to dip in and out of the Force, just like him. Just like Ben.» (L8123); «He should never have taught Ben to do it.» (L8125).
- Lumiya joins: «I have her, Jacen.» (L8111). Jacen orders: «Lumiya, crack her canopy. Weak shields.» (L8129) → sphere rams/strikes; «Hull breach, she's venting atmosphere.» (L8131). So Mara's fighter was disabled by a **2v1 (Jacen + Lumiya/sphere)**. Mara heads for the moon/planet **Kavan** (L8135).
- Jacen in combat: «He felt no violence or malice at all, but then he never did in combat. He just felt an overwhelming desire to win and survive» (L8137).
- **Jacen fires all eight proton torpedoes at Lumiya's sphere**: «"You certainly will," he said, and locked all eight proton torpedoes on the Sith sphere. "This is what you taught me to be."» (L8143); no explosion seen (L8147).
- Ben talks the sphere down: «The rule of ages is that I must not be targeted.» (L8169); «Masters may use their ships to fight but not involve apprentices.» (L8183); «Once I fought. Now I educate and protect apprentices.» (L8193); «I teach apprentices to fight.» (L8207); sphere treats both Ben and Lumiya as apprentices (L8197–8215). Ben realizes the sphere stopped Mara killing Lumiya on Hesperidium as its job (L8223). Lumiya: «He has to survive. We don't.» (L8167).
- Ben + sphere (with Lumiya inside) drop toward **Reboam** (L8227): «Ben had caught a Sith.» (L8229)
- **Kavan, Mara's ditch (L8235–8261)**: Mara chose terrain: «If I get him in a confined space, he can't use all his Force skills, but I can make the most of what I've got.» (L8255) — PREP / Mara's own assessment. «It was getting easier by the minute to disappear into the Force» (L8259); then magnifies presence as a lure (L8259–8261). Mara ignores Luke's Force-reaching so Jacen can't detect (L8253).
#### Ch20 (L8264–)

Mara magnifies presence: «defiant, unafraid, and spoiling for a fight» (L8328); Jaina: «Feels like ... a fight.» (L8334). 18-hour transit to Hapes (L8336). Luke can't feel Ben (L8294).
- **DUEL: Mara vs Jacen, Kavan storm-drain tunnels (L8358–8491+)** — Round 1:
  - Jacen POV: Mara «thinking she was still an A-list assassin and that she could take someone who had complete mastery of the Force.» (L8370); **«She was a superb assassin, but her Force skills were crude compared to his.»** (L8372) — STATEMENT (Jacen POV, arrogant/biased).
  - Jacen loads «four poisoned darts into an adapted blaster» (L8376); enters with lightsaber + blaster (L8382); threatens to smoke her out (L8384).
  - **Mara ambush**: «His right knee exploded with blinding pain as Mara cannoned out horizontally, Force-assisted, from a side conduit and caught his leg on the joint with her boots, ripping the tendons.» (L8392) — Mara undetected by Jacen (she's hidden in the Force); surprise attack.
  - **Mara's tunnel trap**: «An intense Force shake of the brickwork and arches in front of and behind the metal plate weakened them, and then she stopped them from collapsing by Force pressure.» (L8402); «She brought down both sections of tunnel, before and behind, with a massive exertion in the Force that made her breathless.» (L8426) — Mara TK feat; buries Jacen.
  - Mara: «her training was in destruction. Jacen's was in deception.» (L8410). «Trap, immobilize, kill.» (L8408)
  - Jacen buried, only an arm protrudes; reaches out a hand — Mara: «an old, tired Sith stunt» (L8436). Jacen: «Tell my mom I'm sorry I failed her» / «She knows» — Mara fires blaster at his eye point-blank (L8442–8444).
#### Ch21 (L8447–) — Round 2
- **Jacen survives**: «He deflected the energy bolt with one last surge of the Force and sent the rubble erupting off his crushed and bleeding body like a detonation. Bricks hammered the walls and rained fragments, knocking Mara flat like a bomb blast.» (L8471) — Jacen Force feat under burial (point-blank bolt deflection + TK burst). Frozen 2 seconds by the effort (L8473).
  - Jacen realizes: «It was Mara who had to die to fulfill his destiny.» (L8477).
  - Mara attacks with shoto (left-handed, fencing) + vibroblade: sears his cheekbone, cuts under his chin (L8485).
  - **Narration via Jacen POV: «She shouldn't have been able to get near him. He had total mastery, and she was just athletic and fast.»** He Force-pushes her into a wall; «she kept coming at him, one-two, one-two with the shoto and the blade, and he was being driven back, his strength ebbing.» (L8487)
  - **Mara deflects all four poison darts**: «He drew his dart gun and fired one after the other, but Mara scattered all four needles in a blur of blue light.» (L8489). Jacen hurls debris via Force; she leaps «from block to boulder» and Force-leaps onto his back (L8489).
  - Brawl: «This wasn't a duel: it was a brawl.» Vibroblade skates from his jaw to hairline (L8491); his lightsaber «almost useless in such a close-quarters struggle» (L8491). Context: Jacen has a ruptured knee (tendons ripped), crushed and bleeding body.
  - Mara: «Ben . . . I'll see you dead first. . . before . . . you get . . . Ben.» (L8493)
  - **Force exchange**: «They grappled, Force-pushed, Force-crushed: he threw her back again, trying to Force-jolt her spine and paralyze her for a moment, but somehow she deflected it and bricks flew out of the wall ... She almost Force-snatched the lightsaber from his hand, but even with his injuries he hung on to it.» (L8495) — Mara deflects Jacen's Force attack; near-disarm.
  - Jacen: «You can't beat me ... It's not meant to be.» Mara: «Really? ... I say it is.» (L8497–8499)
  - Jacen Force-pushes her into a pillar mid-leap, but stumbles into a subsidence crack — «red-hot pain seared from ankle to knee. His lightsaber went flying.» — Mara **plunges the shoto «into the soft tissue just under the end of his collarbone»** (L8501). Jacen: «Lightsaber wounds hurt a lot more than he ever imagined. Jacen screamed.» (L8503)
  - Jacen recalls lightsaber; Mara pins him, vibroblade «a hand span from his throat»; he drags her face toward his blade by her hair; «hacking at him with the shoto but blocked by his dwindling Force power each time» (L8503).
  - **Decisive trick**: «Jacen stared into her eyes and instantly created the illusion of Ben's face beneath her. She blinked. It gave him the edge for that fraction of a moment. It was long enough to ram the poison dart into her leg» (L8507–8509). Dart 10 cm, punched through plastoid cone (L8511).
  - Jacen: «I'm sorry, Mara. Had to be you. Thought it was Ben.» (L8515); «Don't fight it. No healing trance. Just let go . . .» (L8519). Mara: «You're ... as vile as he was.» / «Palpatine.» (L8527–8531). **Mara's last words: «You think . . . you've won ... But Luke will crush you . . . and I refuse ... to let you . . . destroy the future . . . for my Ben.»** (L8541); «Ben was the last word she ever spoke.» (L8545). STATEMENT (dying Mara: Luke will crush Jacen; motivated, but foreshadowing).
  - **Duel verdict**: Mara WON the fight on points (ambush knee, tunnel collapse, shoto through shoulder, cuts, deflected all four darts, pinned him); Jacen won via illusion + poison while losing. Jacen injured: ripped knee tendons, crushed under rubble, ankle-to-knee injury from fall, lightsaber wound under collarbone, facial cuts. Mara: scalp cut, slammed into wall/pillar. Mara had the prep advantage (terrain choice, stealth, ambush); Jacen had prep (poison darts, dart gun). Author framing: «She shouldn't have been able to get near him.» (L8487) — the narration grants Jacen superior Force power but shows Mara's skill/ferocity overcoming it until the trick.
- Lumiya feels the Force shift; «The new Dark Lord needs me.» (L8557). Ben hears «Ben, Ben, Ben» — a farewell (L8567). **Luke**: «felt a hand brush his hair ... he knew his world had ended.» (L8577)
#### Ch22 (L8580–)
- Jacen on Ziost, injured: «Killing didn't get any easier. He was just getting better at it.» (L8606). Doubts the prophecy: Mara wasn't what he loved most; no transformation felt (L8622–8630). «if Mara hadn't been trying to kill him to begin with» (L8626). Lumiya: «Your new existence has started, Dark Lord.» (L8636). Jacen's shoulder pain (L8638); «I'm hastening the healing trance» (L8676). Mara left a body (L8680–8686). Alema still prowling (L8708).
- Fountain Palace: Luke numb; Tenel Ka has all guard searching; Jaina: Jacen «a total stranger» (L8764). Jaina: «It's Lumiya.» Luke: «We don't know that.» (L8788–8790). Tenel Ka: «Jacen was here earlier.» (L8748).
- Jaina: «If I'm supposed to be the Sword of the Jedi, then it's time I took it seriously» (L8798) — vows to make Lumiya pay. Hapan logs show Mara arrived after Jacen (L8806). Luke: «She's-she was my wife. I insist that I handle this myself.» (L8818).
- Mandalore: 4th Bes'uliik; Mandalore resurgent, diaspora returning (L8840–8870). Fett: «It all ran better under Vader.» (L8910). Mirta vows to slit Jacen's throat (L8912). Fett sends Han beskar crushgaunts «With deepest sympathy» (L8922–8926).
- Corran takes over the Council (L8938). Luke: «Luke felt like his old self for the first time in years. He felt clarity.» (L8940). Corran: «It's a trap, Luke.» (L8956).
- **DUEL: Luke vs Lumiya, Terephon mesa (L8976–9036)**:
  - Luke tractors the sphere and forces her to land (L8998–9000) — sphere makes no move against Luke (L9002).
  - Lumiya unveils, draws lightwhip; Lumiya: «I had to make sure she died» (L8986) — a deliberate false confession; «Nothing personal ... Just doing what I swore an oath to the Emperor to do.» (L9018).
  - «he was flooded with a new confidence that he could take her with just the lightsaber» — no shoto this time (L9008).
  - Lightwhip «sending forks of dark energy crackling into the ground at his feet» (L9010); Luke closes inside whip range, drives her «like a battering ram toward the edge of the mesa» (L9024–9026); blade lock vs whip handle used as staff (L9028); she slips off the crumbling edge, whip falls; **Luke catches her hand: «"I'd never let you fall," Luke said, and pulled her back to safety. As she straightened up, he looked her in the eyes-calm, eerily calm-and swung his lightsaber in a single decapitating arc.»** (L9032–9034). **Luke kills Lumiya.** Context: Lumiya was on a deliberate suicide mission (L8696–8704) — she likely did not fight all-out; Luke enraged/grieving but controlled.
  - Luke on Lumiya's stealth: «He wondered how many times she'd passed by him unnoticed and undetected» (L8968).
- Ben with Mara's body in the Kavan tunnels (L9042–); GAG training: don't tamper with crime scene (L9054).
- **Jacen finds Ben despite Ben hiding in the Force**: «Ben was still shut down, hiding his Force presence, but Jacen had found him.» (L9068) — Ben notes it as evidence (L9070–9072). Jacen fakes surprise: «We'll get whoever did this. I swear we will.» (L9066). Ben «couldn't feel the person in the Force» (L9060).
#### Ch23 (L9075–)
- Omas pressing for public trial (L9079).
- Keldabe: old Mando «Gotab» (with Venku) reads Mirta's heart-of-fire stone — psychometry (L9093–9185); Ailyn's mother Sintas died hunting «Resada? Rezoda?» on **Phaeda** (L9171–9175). [Outside knowledge, not stated in text: Gotab = ex-Jedi Bardan Jusik — do not cite as feat.] Fett: «I stopped being happy on Geonosis and I never bothered trying again.» (L9231).
- **Jacen's Sith battle-sight awakens** on GA warship *Ocean*: reads the holochart and predicts enemy movements — «I can see all this.» (L9285); **«Lumiya had promised him a battlefield awareness and judgment that made ordinary battle meditation look like a finger painting-to sense and coordinate by the power of his mind and will alone, a power that only came to fruition in the Master of the Sith.»** (L9293); «It was Mara's sacrifice after all, I accept that now.» (L9295); «He was a Sith Lord.» (L9299). FEAT/STATEMENT — Mara's death is the trigger for Caedus's Sith ascension. Officer: «Colonel Solo is rarely mistaken.» (L9287). Jacen learns Luke killed Lumiya: «Lumiya had managed to commit suicide-by-Skywalker.» (L9253).
- Luke with Mara's body (Jedi Council shuttle): she left a body; Cilghal: «It was poison, one I've never seen before. But don't doubt that she also wanted you to be able to say good-bye.» (L9329). Luke believes Lumiya did it (L9335).
- Ben tells Luke: «Dad, it wasn't Lumiya.» (L9363) — Ben was beside Lumiya at the moment Mara died (L9369). Luke: «I killed the wrong-» / Ben: «Sith.» (L9379–9381). «She'd fooled him one last time ... and it broke something deep, deep inside him.» (L9397). Ben: Mom stayed (left a body) «so we could find evidence» (L9375). Ben: «You did the galaxy a necessary service.» (L9375).

- Sith sphere stolen from Tu'ana City, Terephon (L9410) [Alema? — per Lumiya's hint L8708; not confirmed here].
- Fett leads Bes'uliik strike on Murkhana; Mirta finds a carbonite slab in gangster Rezodar's Phaeda estate: **«Sintas was alive.»** (L9494).

Jacen: «Ben would eventually become greater than his Jedi father could ever be.» (L9554) — Jacen POV, speculative. Vergere's torture as origin (L9536).
