## Character analyses (added 2026-10-17 under the 7+ characters rule)

*Method:* built from the verified log above (Sections 2–4 and Appendix). `L####` = line in `06b223d3…txt` (chapter given). Quotes in «…» are verbatim and were re-checked by script. No wiki material is used except where marked. **Before:** 6 short story profiles (SP2), none with feats, fights and scaling. **Added:** 10 analyses.

### 1. Jacen Solo / Darth Caedus
- **Role/arc:** goes from GAG colonel to joint Chief of State. He kills Mara, gains Sith battle-sight, and names himself Darth Caedus (L9584).
- **Key feats:** permanent presence-hiding, so that Ben «found he was impossible to track unless he wanted to be found» (Ch1, L224). Fools Mara with «a veneer of weary good humor in the Force» (Ch5, L3188). Buried and point-blank under fire, «He deflected the energy bolt with one last surge of the Force and sent the rubble erupting off his crushed and bleeding body like a detonation.» (Ch21, L8471). Wins with an illusion of Ben's face and a poison dart (L8507–8511). Battle-sight «that made ordinary battle meditation look like a finger painting» (Ch23, L9293).
- **Statements:** Lumiya: «Luke can still take you in a lightsaber fight.» (L3958). Jacen's own fear: blade and blaster «still wouldn't be enough when Luke and Mara came after him» (L6948). Mara: «She couldn't outstrip his Force powers» (L7444). Fett: «Jacen Solo, the boy who wants to be Vader when he grows up.» (L6682).
- **Notable quotes:** «Killing didn't get any easier. He was just getting better at it.» (L8606). «I'm proud of my grandfather, but not blind to the weaknesses that brought him down.» (L9576).
- **Fights/results:** beats Mara (Kavan), but only after a knee ripped open, a tunnel collapse and a shoto under the collarbone. His own POV admits «She shouldn't have been able to get near him.» (L8487). Fires eight torpedoes at Lumiya's sphere, which survives (L8143).
- **Scaling:** Jacen > Mara in raw Force, but Mara ≥ him in close-quarters killing. Luke > Jacen with a saber (stated). His power jumps after Mara's death, which feeds into Inferno.

### 2. Mara Jade Skywalker
- **Role/arc:** returns to her Emperor's Hand self to protect Ben: «She felt as detached and steady as she'd ever been as the Emperor's Hand.» (L8440). Killed in Ch21. Her last word is "Ben" (L8545).
- **Key feats:** Force-leaps five meters at Lumiya (Ch10, L5248). Turns her saber off and stabs the hilt into Lumiya's chest before reigniting it (L5258). Gets her StealthX on Jacen's tail unseen (Ch18, L8072). Ambushes Jacen and rips his knee tendons (Ch20, L8392). Brings down both tunnel sections «with a massive exertion in the Force» (L8426). «Mara scattered all four needles in a blur of blue light» (L8489). Deflects his spine-jolt (L8495).
- **Statements:** Mara: «I'm much more dangerous now I have a cub to protect.» (L1876). Narration: «No edge: she was as good a pilot.» (L8105). Her own POV: «her training was in destruction. Jacen's was in deception.» (L8410). Luke's POV: «If anyone could take Lumiya, Mara could» (L5943).
- **Notable quote:** «But Luke will crush you» (L8541).
- **Fights/results:** vs. Lumiya, no result, as the sphere ambushed her 2v1 (L5268). Lost the dogfight to Jacen plus the sphere, 2v1 (L8129–8131). Lost to Jacen (Kavan), narrowly, after nearly killing him twice.
- **Scaling:** Mara ≥ Lumiya 1v1. Mara < Jacen, narrowly and only through a trick. She is the closest anyone comes to killing pre-Caedus Jacen.

### 3. Luke Skywalker
- **Role/arc:** passive and over-trusting until Mara's death. Then «Luke felt like his old self for the first time in years.» (L8940). He executes Lumiya and is broken to learn she was innocent (L9397).
- **Key feats:** tractors the Sith sphere down onto a mesa (Ch22, L8998–9000). Beats Lumiya with a single saber and no shoto (L9008–9034): «"I'd never let you fall," Luke said, and pulled her back to safety.» Then the decapitation (L9034). Feels Mara's death from a distance (L8577).
- **Statements:** narration: «whatever gifts the Force had given him more generously than other Jedi» (L4609). Jacen's POV calls Luke «a man who could bring down the Empire» (L8034).
- **Fights/results:** beats Lumiya, though she came to die (L8704). Lumiya's stealth beats his sensing until she lets him find her (Ch8, L4527).
- **Scaling:** still the top Jedi. Luke > Jacen with a saber (L3958).

### 4. Ben Skywalker
- **Role/arc:** a 14-year-old GAG lieutenant. Assassinates Gejjen, learns Force camouflage and teaches it to Mara, and starts investigating her death.
- **Key feats:** learns camouflage on the first try. Jacen: «But full marks for technique.» (L2565). Force-guided sniping at 97 percent (L4423). Kills Gejjen: «a textbook head shot» (L5079). Camouflaged, he eavesdrops on Jacen and Lumiya undetected (Ch13, L6577–6659). Talks the sphere out of fighting: «Ben had caught a Sith.» (L8229).
- **Scaling:** his stealth beats Jacen's sensing at this point, though Jacen later finds him (L9068). Caedus thinks Ben «would eventually become greater than his Jedi father could ever be.» (L9554), which is speculation.

### 5. Lumiya
- **Role/arc:** puts the Sith idea above her own life: «A death in service of a great ideal is a fine thing.» (L3940). She claims Mara's killing and dies by design (L8986).
- **Key feats:** stabs her own thigh and hits metal: «I'm actually more machine than organic» (L989–991). Influences a Senate council from the gallery: «I just redirected a little opposition from the gallery.» (L3282). Senses Alema «like a beacon even at this range» (L861).
- **Statements:** «Lumiya didn't have the complete Sith ability to see all the pieces in the game ... That was for a full Sith Master.» (L1021).
- **Fights/results:** vs. Mara, wounded and fleeing (Ch10). Beheaded by Luke (Ch22, by choice).
- **Scaling:** below Mara 1v1 and well below Luke. Her strength is influence.

### 6. Sith meditation sphere
- **Role:** a living Sith training ship and Lumiya's ally.
- **Feats:** wraps a cable around Mara's neck from behind; Mara: «I got bushwhacked by a living ship, a Sith ship.» (L6816). Cracks Mara's canopy on Jacen's order (L8129–8131). Survives eight proton torpedoes (L8143–8147).
- **Notable quote:** «Once I fought. Now I educate and protect apprentices.» (L8193).
- **Scaling:** the "second" in two 2v1 results. Count its help whenever you cite Mara's losses.

### 7. Boba Fett
- **Role/arc:** goes from dying to cured by Jaing and Venku: «I'm back.» (L7598).
- **Feats:** a left hook on a barkeep reaching for a disruptor (Ch3, L1434–1436). Loops the gangster Fraig over a balcony on fibercord (Ch4, L2297).
- **Scaling:** non-Force. Matters for Revelation (Mando anti-Jedi gear).

### 8. Jaing Skirata
- **Feats:** ambushes Mirta unseen: «I didn't pick him up on my sensors and I didn't even see him coming at me.» (L2860). Silently drops the pursuing speeders (L2381–2397).
- **Statement:** «Master assassin, sniper, general pain in the backside. Don't underestimate him.» (L2878).
- **Scaling:** elite non-Force commando.

### 9. Mirta Gev
- **Feats:** draws first with Fett in the bar fight (L1434–1436). Jaing ambushes her (L2860).
- **Scaling:** a capable non-Force fighter, below Jaing.

### 10. Leia Organa Solo
- **Role:** a fugitive who talks with Mara at Drall (Ch15).
- **Statement:** «could never make him act totally against his will.» (L7375). This confirms Jacen's agency.
- **Scaling:** no combat here. Included for her statement about Jacen.


---

<!-- 021-legacy-of-the-force/lotf-6-inferno-notes.md (verbatim copy) -->

# Legacy of the Force 6: Inferno (novel, read in full): feats, scaling and story notes

<!-- read up to: chapter twenty-one + back-matter interview (L5525), END OF BOOK; restructured into house style -->

- **Book:** *Star Wars: Legacy of the Force: Inferno* by Troy Denning (2007). Sixth of the nine Legacy of the Force novels.
- **Continuity:** Legends. Set in 40 ABY, during the Second Galactic Civil War, about a week after Mara's murder (L95). It comes after *Sacrifice* (LOTF 5) and before *Fury* (LOTF 7). Jacen is already Darth Caedus throughout; the dramatis personae lists him as «Jacen Solo; Sith Lord (male human)» (L33).
- **Source file:** `[local path]` (5,526 lines; OCR noise such as "It Caedus" for "If Caedus" is kept verbatim and marked sic).
- **Method:** I read every line in order: front matter, Prologue, Ch1–Ch21, then the back-matter "Round-Robin Interview" with Allston, Traviss and Denning. `L####` is the line number in that file. Text inside «…» is copied exactly from the file and was checked by script (see the end of this file). The raw reading log is kept as an appendix.
- **Chapter heading lines:** Prologue L59, Ch1 L183, Ch2 L423, Ch3 L563, Ch4 L931, Ch5 L1333, Ch6 L1531, Ch7 L1703, Ch8 L1869, Ch9 L2131, Ch10 L2347, Ch11 L2479, Ch12 L2779, Ch13 L3007, Ch14 L3167, Ch15 L3421, Ch16 L3645, Ch17 L4065, Ch18 L4329, Ch19 L4615, Ch20 L4947, Ch21 L5281; interview L5381.
- **Feat vs. statement:** Section 2 covers what the narration shows happening. Section 3 covers what characters say or think, marked **STATEMENT**. Denning writes in rotating tight third person, so a lot of "narration" is a character's view; those are marked *(POV)*.
- **Headline:** this is the **Luke vs. Caedus** book. Luke humiliates Caedus with raw telekinesis in Ch11, hides from him completely (Ch8, Ch17, Ch19), and then beats him in a no-quarter duel in the Embrace-of-Pain chamber (Ch19). Caedus's own POV concedes: «Luke had beaten him.» (L4961). Also: Ben's assassination attempt on Caedus, Caedus burns Kashyyyk, Jaina "kills" Luke (he faked it), Tenel Ka turns on Caedus, Leia vs. Tahiri, and the One Sith on Korriban.

---

## 1. Cast who fight or use the Force

| Character | Role in this book | Force user? |
|---|---|---|
| Jacen Solo / Darth Caedus | GAG colonel, de facto ruler. Frames Omas, burns Kashyyyk, tortures Ben. Loses to Luke in Ch19 | Yes |
| Luke Skywalker | Grand Master. Breaks the Jedi from Caedus. Fakes his death and infiltrates the *Anakin Solo* | Yes |
| Ben Skywalker | 14. Fakes loyalty to Caedus to kill him. Stabs him with a vibrodagger in Ch19 | Yes |
| Jaina Solo | Retakes the Ossus academy; flies with Luke at Kashyyyk; "shoots down" Luke | Yes |
| Zekk, Jag Fel | Ossus rescue. Zekk maims Serpa | Zekk yes |
| Leia Organa Solo | Fights GAG in the Temple (Ch2), Wookiee councillors (Ch12), Tahiri (Ch18) | Yes |
| Han Solo | Support; turns Tenel Ka with Leia | No |
| Tahiri Veila | Caedus's spy and flow-walk partner. Duels Leia | Yes |
| Tenel Ka | Queen Mother. Gives Caedus the Home Fleet, then fires on him | Yes |
| Alema Rar | Steals the Sith sphere, visits Korriban, helps Caedus escape | Yes |
| "White Eyes", Morto, the raspy-voiced woman | The One Sith cloister on Korriban (~30 Sith) | Yes |
| Saba, Kyp, Corran, Kenth, Cilghal, Tesar, Lowbacca | Jedi Council and StealthX wing | Yes |
| Cal Omas | Former Chief of State; impales himself on Ben's saber | No |
| Salle Serpa | GAG major who holds the Ossus academy hostage | No |
| Kam Solusar, Tionne | Academy masters. Both shot down by Serpa's snipers (Kam «motionless», L3345); *Fury* L683 says both survived | Yes |
| Sith meditation sphere ("Ship") | Alema's ship; amplifies battle meditation | (sentient ship) |

---

## 2. Combat and Force feats, in chapter order

### Prologue–Ch5
1. **Prologue · L73–L159 · Caedus**: flow-walks Tahiri into the past at Myrkr: «I've been flow-walking for years» (L77). *Shows:* time-perception power. Lore: «Any change they made in the past would be corrected by the Force» (L73).
2. **Ch1 · L187–211 · Tenel Ka**: detects the camouflaged Jacen: «a void so subtle she recognized it only by the surrounding stillness.» (L187). *Shows:* Tenel Ka's sensing beats his camouflage, helped by intimacy.
3. **Ch2 · L479–543 · Leia (+Han) vs. ~12 GAG troopers**: Force-hurls the lieutenant into his squad (L491), sweeps shock nets away (L509–511), slashes off a recruit's arms at the elbows (L525), deflects bolts, and steers a grenade (L541–543). Her mind trick is broken by a trained corporal: «I'm not weak-minded, you know.» (L485). *Shows:* Leia as a competent combat Knight; GAG has anti-mind-trick training.
4. **Ch3 · L721, L771 · Mara (posthumous)**: her body dematerializes at the funeral. *Shows:* she became one with the Force.
5. **Ch4 · L1159–1165 · Jaina**: «batted half a dozen stun bolts back at the men who had fired them» (L1159) and Force-hurls a trooper «across two control stations» (L1165).
6. **Ch5 · L1381–1411 · Alema**: mind-wipes guards (L1381); overpowers the Sith sphere's will: «She pushed harder against its resolve, this time forsaking subtlety for sheer power.» (L1399). *Shows:* Alema's raw strength against a sentient Sith artifact.
7. **Ch5 · L1449–1471 · Alema**: gets through Lumiya's trap gauntlet, including a damage-reflecting Force illusion (L1471).

### Ch6–Ch9 (Balmorra, Omas)
8. **Ch8 · L1917–1925 · Caedus**: precognition. He pushes «his Force-awareness forward into the next few seconds» (L1917) and calls the enemy flight's destruction.
9. **Ch8 · L1945, L1981 · Caedus (limits)**: his battle meditation fails: «The fleet reflected back only confusion and fear» (L1945); «a humbling reminder that battle meditation was not infallible» (L1981).
10. **Ch8 · L2007–2009 · Caedus**: reacts to a sudden mass of Jedi presences with a «reverse flip over the seat back», landing «three meters inside his day cabin, his lightsaber in hand».
11. **Ch8 · L2061–2127 · Luke**: appears in Caedus's day cabin, speaks, and vanishes. Caedus searches «the entire Anakin Solo. He felt no sign of his uncle's presence anywhere.» (L2117). *Shows:* Luke's concealment or projection beats Caedus's senses on his own flagship.
12. **Ch9 · L2139–2163 · Ben vs. Guardian droid**: his saber cuts only halfway through a laminanium arm (L2153); Force-guided second strike through the same cut (L2155); he Force-throws the droid off (L2163) and Force-holds Omas mid-step (L2227–2229).
13. **Ch9 · L2317–2343 · Omas / Ben**: Omas impales himself on Ben's saber to make Ben's cover real; Ben throws back stun grenades and leads CorSec out before the death-trap blows.

### Ch11 — Luke humiliates Caedus (day cabin, Kuat)
14. **Ch11 · L2559 · Luke**: «Luke had not gestured, had not flinched, had not even shifted his gaze; he had simply grabbed Caedus in the Force and hurled him five meters into his chair.» *Shows:* effortless TK on a Sith Lord.
15. **Ch11 · L2563–2569 · Luke**: Caedus «found himself struggling against an invisible weight» (L2563). «While it was true that Luke had taken him by surprise, it was equally true that he had done so with no visible effort-and that he was continuing to hold him with no apparent exertion.» (L2569). *Shows:* Luke's TK hold beats Caedus's active resistance. The book flags the surprise caveat itself.
16. **Ch11 · L2613–2619 · Luke**: keeps Caedus pinned while walking to the door, with only part of his attention.
17. **Ch11 · L2631–2635 · Luke**: crushes the chair pedestal by tapping a finger: «the pedestal collapsed with a low loud crump, depositing Caedus on the floor with his feet sticking out in front of him like a child.»

### Ch12–Ch14
18. **Ch12 · L2865–2869, L2953–2967 · Leia**: Force-assisted heel-kick on Tojjelnoot; brawls Tarfang (comic).
19. **Ch14 · L3197–3219 · Jaina**: through coma gas, Force-hurls the gasser and kills an officer in the dark with a Force-guided thrown spoon.
20. **Ch14 · L3325–3343 · Jaina**: silently kills two sniper teams («crossed the roof in two Force bounds», L3341).
21. **Ch14 · L3409–3415 · Zekk**: severs Serpa's weapon arm, then strikes so fast that «even Jaina did not see the attack» (L3415).

### Ch15 — Ben's assassination attempt (*Anakin Solo* bridge, over Kashyyyk)
22. **Ch15 · L3623 · Caedus**: in a regretful meld with Lowbacca he senses «the weight of a shadow bomb in Lowbacca's Force grasp».
23. **Ch15 · L3627 · Ben vs. Caedus**: Ben's surprise strike only grazes him: «a bolt of danger sense was sizzling down his spine and whirled away from the holodisplay, then felt the heat of a lightsaber brush across his ribs as Ben's blade snapped to life and buried itself in the torso of Commander Twizzl.» *Shows:* Caedus's danger sense saves him from a 14-year-old's well-hidden attack, barely. «It might have been Lowbacca's shadow bomb or Caedus's own swift reflexes that saved his life.» (L3631).
24. **Ch15 · L3633 · Caedus**: «With no time to snatch his own weapon off his belt, Caedus lashed out, loosing a bolt of Force lightning that sent Ben tumbling across the floor in a smoking, convuls-ing heap.» (sic, OCR hyphen). He then summons Ben's saber.

### Ch17 — Kashyyyk space battle
25. **Ch17 · L4227 · Luke (pilot)**: «slipping away from turbolaser strikes half a second before they blossomed, ducking cannon bolts as though he had a telepathic connection to the gunner's mind». Jaina's view: «he hadn't revealed half of what he could do. Maybe not even a quarter.»
26. **Ch17 · L4261 · Luke and Jaina**: both draw their presences in to hide from Caedus, who is «looking for the Jedi combat-meld» (L4253).
27. **Ch17 · L4285–4315 · Caedus in a StealthX vs. Luke + Jaina**: Caedus was waiting for them (L4291). «Her brother seemed to anticipate every shot before she took it.» (L4303). Jaina still lands hits (L4301) and takes off one of his wing cannons (L4315). Caedus goads her through the Force (L4307–4309).
28. **Ch17 · L4317–4325 · Luke (fake death)**: Luke's damaged fighter skids into Jaina's fire and explodes. «Luke Skywalker was dead. / And Jaina had shot him down.» Revealed later as a fake (L4739–4741, L5301).

### Ch18 — Leia vs. Tahiri (Royal Hangar, Hapes)
29. **Ch18 · L4461 · Leia and Tahiri**: both bat stun bolts back at the marines who fire on them.
30. **Ch18 · L4475 · Leia**: «beating down her guard, slipping in knee and elbow strikes with a speed and ferocity that only a Barabel-trained fighter could achieve.» Tahiri lands a back-kick only when Han's shot distracts Leia (L4481). *Shows:* Leia > Tahiri on this showing.

### Ch19 — DUEL: Luke vs. Caedus (Embrace-of-Pain chamber, *Anakin Solo*)
31. **Ch19 · L4719–4735 · Caedus**: cuts Ben off from the Force and restores it with a touch. «How long could the Embrace have held you, had I let you keep the Force?» (L4725).
32. **Ch19 · L4739–4741 · Luke**: hides from everyone except Ben: «Ben was the only one whom his dad was allowing to sense him through the Force. Not even Jacen had that kind of control.» Caedus: «I don't feel any Jedi presence at all» (L4785) while Luke is on his deck.
33. **Ch19 · L4813–4815 · Luke opens**: kidney stab from surprise; Caedus partly parries. «But Jacen thrived on pain, fed on it to make himself stronger and faster.» (L4815). He answers with a rib-crunching roundhouse.
34. **Ch19 · L4821 · Luke**: «landing an elbow smash to the temple that dropped Jacen to his knees. He brought his own knee up under Jacen's chin, hearing teeth crack-and relishing it.»
35. **Ch19 · L4825–4827 · Caedus**: feints lightning, then Force-hurls a thorn bed that pins Luke to the wall. «The lightning never came.»
36. **Ch19 · L4831 · Luke**: «Luke landed a snap-kick to the stomach that lifted Jacen a meter off the deck».
37. **Ch19 · L4835 · Luke (bind)**: «beating his nephew's guard down so far that Jacen's emerald blade bit into his own shoulder.» Caedus pops Luke's knee; Luke's sweep scorches Caedus's skull (L4837).
38. **Ch19 · L4842–4843 · Luke**: finger-strike to the eye, feint, then a Force wave: «Jacen went flying».
39. **Ch19 · L4865–4875 · Caedus**: drops the ceiling light panel on Luke (breaks his nose), then flips over him and runs: «Jacen was running.» (L4875).
40. **Ch19 · L4877 · Luke**: drags him back through the Force; «their blades colliding faster than the eye could follow ... He landed an elbow and felt a bone in Jacen's face shatter.»
41. **Ch19 · L4885–4893 · Caedus garrote / Luke escape**: Caedus baits Luke and garrotes him with a toxic tendril: «Should have let me go when you had the chance, "Jacen snarled. "Now you're done.» Luke Force-hurls them both into the wall, and «The garrote went completely slack.» (L4893).
42. **Ch19 · L4895–4901 · Ben**: stabs Caedus between the shoulder blades with the vibrodagger. Caedus: «a hand-sized rectangle of scorched skull showed through his scalp, and still he was stretching a hand toward his lightsaber.» (L4901). Luke spares him to keep Ben from the dark side.
43. **Ch19 · L4921 · Luke**: sabotaged all four long-range turbolaser batteries; they blew during the fight.

### Ch20 — aftermath and escape
44. **Ch20 · L5007–5011 · Caedus (durability)**: «you have burn damage to your kidney and a perforation in your lung», plus brain swelling (L5007). He uses the Force to hold himself upright and keeps command.
45. **Ch20 · L5261 · Caedus**: presses on Admiral Atoko's mind across the fleet until «his resistance quickly yielded to obedience».
46. **Ch20 · L5271–5277 · Caedus (+Alema and the sphere)**: builds on the indecision Alema planted in the Bothan commanders. They advance instead of falling back, and the Fifth Fleet escapes. *Shows:* fleet-scale battle meditation while badly hurt, with Sith-sphere help.
47. **Ch20 · L5193 · Sith sphere**: can «Control, not command» an enemy fleet that has no meditation spheres of its own.

### Korriban — Alema and the One Sith (Ch16, Ch20)
48. **Ch16 · L3797–3805 · Alema**: Force-chokes a Bothan and crushes a human's jaw.
49. **Ch16 · L3875–3877 · Morto (One Sith)**: grabs Alema so fast «that Alema barely realized his hand had moved» and pins her: «his strength so obvious and great that Alema didn't even try to fight». She still poisons him with a dart and a bacteria trick.
50. **Ch16 · L4039, L4057 · White Eyes**: silences Alema with a raised hand and hurls Morto into a pillar with a look.

---

## 3. Statements and hype (STATEMENT: not feats)

| # | Line | Speaker / POV | Statement | Reliability |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| S1 | L4961 | Caedus (POV), after Ch19 | «Luke had beaten him. Luke had just kept coming despite his injuries. He had inflicted more damage on Caedus than he had suffered himself, and he had even escaped the garrote before Ben struck. In fact, it was probably that attack that had saved Caedus's life.» | **Very high.** The loser's own assessment, with no reason to flatter Luke. |
| S2 | L5067 | Caedus (POV) | «his uncle had nearly killed him» | Very high. Same reason. |
| S3 | L4963 | Caedus (POV) | «when Luke came after him next time, Caedus would be ready» | Low–medium. Wounded pride. It does mark that he was caught unprepared. |
| S4 | L2569 | Caedus (POV) | Luke surprised him, but «with no visible effort ... with no apparent exertion» | **High.** Concedes the surprise caveat and the gap in raw power together. |
| S5 | L2571, L2585–2587 | Caedus (POV) | only «Luke Skywalker's much-strained sense of decency» stands between him and death; had it been Mara's death Luke had just learned of, «Caedus knew he would already be dead.» | High as Caedus's belief. |
| S6 | L713 | Caedus (POV) | «the galaxy was littered with the body parts of those who had underestimated the strength of Luke Skywalker.» | High. Respect from an enemy. |
| S7 | L2011 | Caedus (POV) | «so could the Masters-as Mara had demonstrated when she nearly killed him on Kavan.» | **High.** Caedus admits Mara nearly killed him (supports reading the Sacrifice duel as close). |
| S8 | L829 | Luke (POV) | Jacen's darkness is the deepest «perhaps since the days of Darth Vader and the Emperor» | High on darkness; says nothing about power. |
| S9 | L4741 | narration (Ben POV) | «Not even Jacen had that kind of control.» (Luke's selective presence-hiding) | High. Narration frames it as a Luke-only skill. |
| S10 | L4747 | Caedus | «Even I'm not good enough to project emotions into other Force-users» | Medium. Shows how far ahead Luke's trick is. |
| S11 | L4639 | Caedus to Ben | «it will take decades to develop the power to confront me openly. And I hope you understand the futility of trying to take me by surprise.» | Medium. Ben's surprise attack had just grazed him. |
| S12 | L4227 | Jaina (POV) | Luke «hadn't revealed half of what he could do. Maybe not even a quarter.» | Medium-high. A hype statement from a top pilot. |
| S13 | L3327 | Jaina (POV) | «Had Jaina been as skilled as her uncle, there might have been a way to incapacitate the pair without killing them.» | High. Luke > Jaina in control. |
| S14 | L4303 | Jaina (POV) | «Her brother seemed to anticipate every shot before she took it.» | High. It matches the dogfight. |
| S15 | L1681 | Caedus (POV) | he «was fairly certain Fett would not come out at the worse end of that fight» (Ben vs. Fett) | Medium. Self-serving manipulation context. |
| S16 | L1353 | Alema (POV) | the sphere is «a sort of Force-augmented control vessel, designed to amplify a commander's battle meditation abilities» | High (lore). |
| S17 | L3923 | White Eyes | «You were taught the old ways ... We are only one Sith now.» | High (lore): the One Sith exist in 40 ABY. |
| S18 | L4025–4029 | White Eyes / raspy woman | «Our Master met Vergere while he was a captive of the Yuuzhan Vong. She liked his vision of One Sith.» Lumiya and Vergere «decided to create Jacen». | Medium-high. In-universe Sith claim about Caedus's origin. |
| S19 | L4051–4053 | Alema / One Sith reaction | «Luke Skywalker will soon discover who killed his wife. Do you really want him to track us here?», which produces «misgivings and concern» | High as reaction. The One Sith fear Luke. |
| S20 | L5295, L5301 | Babo; Luke | «Your son is a master tactician»; Luke: Caedus «may be using ancient battle meditation techniques to confuse his opponents.» | High. |
| S21 | L4815 | narration | «Jacen thrived on pain, fed on it to make himself stronger and faster.» | High (narration). |
| S22 | L5391–5397 | Karen Traviss (author) | «I'm afraid it was me who suggested that Jacen had to kill someone he loved.» The SS puppy test is «a typical Sith test» | **Author intent.** Mara's death is a Sith-sacrifice beat, not a power showcase. |
| S23 | L5395 | Troy Denning (author) | «Nobody killed Mara just for the heck of it.» | Author intent. |
| S24 | L5449 | Traviss | «Jacen is actually just a very smart guy with an excessively high opinion of himself.» | Author intent (character). |

---

## 4. Scaling connections

| # | Fight | Where (lines) | Length | Winner | Holding back / help / injuries | What it suggests |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| A | **Luke vs. Caedus, TK confrontation** | Ch11, L2559–2639 | short | **Luke** (no blades) | Luke had surprise (L2569). Caedus had prepared weapons and traps at his desk (L2535) but never reached them. | Luke's raw TK is far above Caedus's. |
| B | **Ben vs. Caedus (assassination)** | Ch15, L3627–3641 | instant | **Caedus** | Full surprise. Lowbacca's shadow bomb hit at the same moment (L3631). Caedus's ribs were burned. | Caedus's danger sense and lightning beat a hidden apprentice, barely. |
| C | **Caedus vs. Luke + Jaina, StealthX** | Ch17, L4273–4319 | short | No result. Luke fakes his death | Caedus was waiting in ambush. Luke was flying a damaged fighter on purpose. | Caedus ≥ Jaina as a pilot here; his precognition beats her aim. |
| D | **Leia vs. Tahiri** | Ch18, L4451–4491 | short | Leia ahead; stopped by Han and Tenel Ka | Leia was enraged. Han distracted her once. | Leia > Tahiri. |
| E | **Luke vs. Caedus, Embrace-of-Pain chamber** | Ch19, L4809–4943 | the book's main duel: several exchanges plus a Force-pulled re-engagement | **Luke** (Caedus's own POV, L4961) | Luke had surprise; Caedus believed him dead. Luke was in a battle rage. **Luke's injuries:** re-cracked Lumiya rib wound, a knee that "popped", broken nose, toxic garrote wound; later «two black eyes, and half a dozen casts» (L5301). **Caedus's injuries:** kidney stab, shoulder bitten by his own blade, scorched skull, eye injury, shattered facial bone, then Ben's vibrodagger near the heart; perforated lung and brain swelling (L5007). Ben's stab ended it, but Caedus says Luke had already escaped the garrote. | Luke wins a lightsaber-and-Force duel against Darth Caedus. Caedus's best moves are tricks (thorn bed, light panel, garrote), not straight dueling. |
| F | **Caedus vs. combined fleets (battle meditation)** | Ch20, L5257–5277 | – | Caedus escapes | Alema and the sphere helped (L5271). Caedus was badly injured. | Caedus's battle meditation is a genuine strategic weapon. |

### Links to existing research files
- **Caedus vs. Luke:** `caedus-legends-quotes.txt` L379, “not as powerful as his uncle in a direct contest of strength”, is borne out by Fights A and E. L307, “few but Luke and Mara Jade Skywalker to be on his level”, fits. `lotf-5-sacrifice-notes.md` S1 (Lumiya: “Luke can still take you in a lightsaber fight.”, Sacrifice L3958) is now shown on the page.
- **Caedus vs. Mara:** S7 (L2011) is Caedus admitting Mara nearly killed him. That supports `lotf-5-sacrifice-notes.md` Duel C and corrects the more neutral summary in `mara-jade-quotes.txt` L124–128.
- **Jaina vs. Caedus:** `jaina-solo-quotes.txt` L17, L30, L34 (her later duel). This book has only the dogfight (Fight C), where Caedus anticipates her every shot. The real test is in *Invincible*.
- **Krayt:** the One Sith appear (L3923, L4025). "Our Master" who met Vergere while held by the Vong is Darth Krayt in wider Legends lore, but he is not named in this book. See `krayt-legends-quotes.txt` L248 (Caedus and Allana).
- **Vader / Sidious:** Luke compares Jacen's darkness to Vader and the Emperor (L829). Alema: «all it took to bring Palpatine down was a farmboy and a self-absorbed Princess» (L3975). No power comparison. See `dark-lord-novel-feats.md`.
- **Previous LOTF books** (other worker's files): `lotf-2-bloodlines-notes.md`, `lotf-3-tempest-notes.md`, `lotf-4-exile-notes.md`. Luke's flagship "visit" in Ch8 echoes earlier projection and illusion tricks.

### Quick ladder (this novel only)
- **Raw Force / TK:** Luke >> Caedus (Fight A, S4).
- **Duel:** Luke > Caedus (Fight E, S1), with surprise and rage caveats.
- **Concealment:** Luke > Caedus (Ch8, Ch19; S9). Caedus > most Jedi; Tenel Ka can sense him when she knows him well (L187–211).
- **Piloting / precognition:** Caedus ≥ Jaina (Fight C). Luke's flying is the best in the book (L4227).
- **Battle meditation:** Caedus is improving (it fails at Balmorra, works at Kuat and Kashyyyk).
- **Second tier:** Leia > Tahiri (Fight D). Zekk and Jaina outclass unpowered GAG troops. The One Sith Morto > Alema in a TK grip (L3877).

---

## 5. Plot summary relevant to the fights
- **Ch1–Ch5:** Caedus corrupts Tahiri by flow-walking; Tenel Ka gives him the Home Fleet on condition of peace with Luke. At Mara's funeral her body vanishes; GAG tries to arrest the Solos. Serpa seizes the Ossus academy. Alema takes the Sith sphere and finds Lumiya's datachip pointing to more Sith.
- **Ch6–Ch10:** Balmorra. Caedus's battle meditation falters; Luke "visits" his cabin and vanishes. Caedus doctors a recording to aim Ben at Omas. Omas dies on Ben's blade by choice.
- **Ch11–Ch14:** Luke pins and humiliates Caedus, then the Jedi desert at Kuat. Leia wins over Kashyyyk. Ben returns to Caedus to kill him and betrays Luke's Kashyyyk plan to win back trust. Jaina, Zekk and Jag retake Ossus; Kam Solusar and Tionne are shot down (they survive, per *Fury* L683).
- **Ch15–Ch18:** Caedus burns Kashyyyk. Ben strikes and fails, is lightning-blasted, and goes into the Embrace of Pain. Alema meets the One Sith on Korriban. Luke "dies" to Jaina's fire. Leia fights Tahiri; Tenel Ka turns.
- **Ch19–Ch21:** Luke attacks Caedus in the torture chamber and wins; Ben stabs Caedus; Luke spares him. Tenel Ka demands Caedus's surrender and fires on him. Caedus and Alema push the Bothans into a mistake, and the Fifth escapes. The Jedi declare «As long as Jacen controls the Alliance, there is no Alliance.» (L5325).

---

## Story and prose notes

### SP1. Plot summary, in order
See Section 5. Structurally, the book alternates three hunts: the Jedi hunting Caedus (Luke, Ben), Jaina hunting Alema, and Alema hunting for Sith help for Jacen. They meet at Kashyyyk.

### SP2. Character profiles
- **Caedus:** Denning's Caedus is a self-sacrificing zealot who still loves. «This was what it meant to be Sith: to use friends without hesitation, to sacrifice family for destiny, to live with a stained soul.» (L179). He powers his meditation «through his love of them all» (L5269). He tests Ben by presenting his back (L3471, L3519). He takes Tenel Ka's betrayal as «one more offering to his Sith destiny» (L5153).
- **Luke:** goes from grief-paralysis to decisive action. He uses deception (fake reconciliation, fake death) where *Sacrifice* had him trusting. He still refuses vengeance: «I did that with Lumiya, and all it did was make me weak.» (L4909).
- **Ben:** plays double agent and trades lives to keep his cover (L4691), which makes him «more like Jacen than even Jacen knew» (L4695). Luke pulls him back from the kill (L4933–4943).
- **Jaina:** commando and ace. She decides Jacen «become another Emperor, and he simply had to be stopped» (L4175). She ends Ch17 believing she killed Luke.
- **Leia / Han:** «Jacen Solo is dead. My son wouldn't do this.» (L3663). Leia mourns her living son as she mourned Anakin (L3669). She nearly kills Tahiri in rage (L4479).
- **Tenel Ka:** loyal to Jacen until Kashyyyk, then decisive: «I am a Hapan queen ... You won't deceive me with equivocation» (L5121).
- **Alema:** "Balance" logic. She believes she killed Mara by a chain of reasoning (L3953) and acts as Jacen's unwanted guardian angel.
- **Tahiri:** guilt-ridden collaborator: «Anakin would want me to support Jacen.» (L4435).

### SP3. Themes
- **Inferno / scorched earth:** Kashyyyk's forests burned for strategy: «Worlds will tremble at the thought of deserting us.» (L3555).
- **Parents losing a child who is still alive:** Han and Leia declare Jacen dead (L3663–3671, L4359).
- **Sacrifice as justification:** Caedus's «I'm willing to carry that burden» (L4697) against Omas's real self-sacrifice (L2317–2321).
- **Deception against a deceiver:** Luke's fake reconciliation and fake death, and Ben's double-agent play, turn Caedus's methods back on him.
- **The Sith as an institution:** the One Sith's «We are only one Sith now» (L3923) against Caedus's lone-Sith project.

### SP4. Notable prose passages
- «the galaxy was littered with the body parts of those who had underestimated the strength of Luke Skywalker.» (L713)
- «Luke had not gestured, had not flinched, had not even shifted his gaze; he had simply grabbed Caedus in the Force and hurled him five meters into his chair.» (L2559)
- «Because you really don't want to see me angry.» (L2633)
- «Luke Skywalker was dead.» / «And Jaina had shot him down.» (L4323–4325)
- «Luke didn't give Jacen a chance to surrender. He just sprang.» (L4809)
- «Jacen was running.» (L4875)
- «Luke had beaten him.» (L4961)
- «As long as Jacen controls the Alliance, there is no Alliance.» (L5325)
- «Han, what have we done?» (L5375)

### SP5. Worldbuilding and lore stated in the text
- **Flow-walking** corrections: «Any change they made in the past would be corrected by the Force» (L73); flow-walkers become visible when they react (L113, L135); short forward range (L1877).
- **Sith meditation sphere:** amplifies battle meditation and conceals the user (L1353); it can «Control, not command» fleets that have no spheres of their own (L5193).
- **The One Sith on Korriban:** about 30 Sith (L3925), a cloister at the Valley of the Dark Lords (L3835–3843). Lomi Plo was one of them (L4005–4007). Vergere is called a Sith (L3983–3987). The Holocron of Darth Vectivus is their "gift" (L5175–5177).
- **Embrace of Pain:** Vong torture device; Caedus can cut a captive off from the Force (L4725).
- **Lumiya's traps:** a Force illusion that reflects damage back on the attacker (L1471).
- **GAG anti-mind-trick training** (L485, L1291).

### SP6. Timeline placement and connections
- 40 ABY, about a week after Mara's death (L95). It follows *Sacrifice* and leads into *Fury* (Alema and the One Sith, Luke and Ben's promise to come for Jacen «when the time is right», L4937).
- Callbacks: Anakin and Tahiri on Myrkr (Prologue); Kyp and Carida (L4175); Onimi (L5135).
- **Author intent (back-matter interview):** Mara's death came from Traviss's "kill someone you love" Sith test (L5391–5397). Denning seeded Jacen's fall in Dark Nest (L5437). Denning: «the only person responsible for what Jacen has become is Jacen himself» (L5447).

---

## 6. Verdict-relevant takeaways
1. **Caedus vs. Luke:** the strongest evidence in the series so far for Luke. Luke out-muscles Caedus in raw TK with no visible effort (Fight A), out-hides him (S9), and beats him in a real duel (Fight E). Caedus says so himself (S1). Caveats: surprise both times, Luke's rage, and Caedus not yet at his peak (see *Invincible*). **Luke > Caedus, high confidence for this point in the timeline.**
2. **Caedus vs. Mara:** Caedus's own admission that «Mara had demonstrated when she nearly killed him on Kavan» (L2011) supports the view that the *Sacrifice* duel was close and that prepared Mara is a real threat.
3. **Jaina vs. Caedus:** only the dogfight. Caedus anticipates her shots (S14) but she still damages him (L4301, L4315). Not a duel data point.
4. **Caedus's best tools are tricks and endurance:** pain-feeding (S21), feints (thorn bed, garrote), lightning, and the willingness to run. When cornered by a stronger opponent he relies on cunning.
5. **Caedus's battle meditation is a strategic multiplier** (Fight F) and lets him escape a four-fleet trap while badly wounded.

- Caedus senses the whole battle: Corellians, Bothans hampered, Commenorians/Hutts relegated (L2485). Tahiri informs Caedus — she's his spy in the Jedi (L2505–2517); they've flow-walked twice more to Anakin (L2511).

Luke had not gestured, had not flinched, had not even shifted his gaze; he had simply grabbed Caedus in the Force and hurled him five meters into his chair.» (L2559). Caedus can't rise: «he found himself struggling against an invisible weight. He felt as if he were accelerating to lightspeed with a faulty inertial compensator.» (L2563); can't even reach the chair controls (L2565). **«While it was true that Luke had taken him by surprise, it was equally true that he had done so with no visible effort-and that he was continuing to hold him with no apparent exertion.»** (L2569) — KEY FEAT (Luke > Caedus in raw TK/Force strength; context: surprise, Caedus not resisting at first? he tries to rise and fails). Head held motionless by the Force (L2585).
- Caedus POV: «all that stood between him and a quick death was Luke Skywalker's much-strained sense of decency» (L2571); «Had it been Mara's death instead of Omas's that Luke had just heard about, Caedus knew he would already be dead.» (L2585–2587) — STATEMENTS (Caedus believes Luke could kill him at this moment).
- Luke: «If she were here-if she had known what you were using Ben for-there'd be pieces of you scattered along the entire length of the Hydian Way.» (L2567) — Luke statement re Mara vs Jacen (ironic). Luke deduces Caedus pointed Ben «like a missile» (L2595).
- Caedus retains personal control of the Hapan Home Fleet (L2601).
- Caedus remains Force-pinned while Luke walks to the door: «Caedus knew he would be freed as soon as Luke turned his concentration to something other than Force-pinning him-but that might take minutes» (L2613); tries to rise again and can't (L2619) — Luke holds him without attention/at distance.
- **Luke crushes the chair pedestal by tapping a finger** (L2631–2635): «The pedestal gave a loud whumpf, and the seat dropped a quarter meter» ... «the pedestal collapsed with a low loud crump, depositing Caedus on the floor with his feet sticking out in front of him like a child.» Luke: «Because you really don't want to see me angry.» (L2633). Luke releases: «the weight vanished from Caedus's chest» (L2639). Caedus: «Avenging his humiliation could wait» (L2639).
- Luke: «"There is no Alliance." Luke turned to leave. "It died with Cal Omas."» (L2661). Luke's hand brushes his saber; Caedus anticipates «the fight he had been anticipating since Mara's death-anticipating, dreading, and wanting» (L2665); Luke declines because of the hostages (L2667).
- SD-XX: Caedus talks in his sleep about Mara and «your daughter» (L2685–2695).
- Caedus later can't turn his damaged meditation chair even with the Force: «No matter how hard he exerted himself, it would not budge.» (L2733) [ambiguous: mechanical damage vs lingering Luke hold].
- Caedus's battle meditation at Kuat: «nudging a task force commander forward here, warning off a Star Destroyer captain there» (L2739) — works this time.
- **The Jedi desert at Kuat**: StealthXs fly through the Hapan fleet and out of the system (L2753–2755). Bwua'tu won't press without them; Caedus calls off the attack to protect Tenel Ka and Allana (L2769–2775): «The Jedi have betrayed us.»
### Ch12 (L2779–) — Kashyyyk Rock Council
- Leia Force-whisper (L2811); Waroo lets them out of jail; Han plans to grab the speaker's tyrossum jaw (L2821).
- **Leia vs Tojjelnoot (Wookiee council leader)**: Force-springs onto the rock; handspring heel-kick to his stomach with «just enough power» of Force (L2865–2869) — Saba's training («Saba had drilled into Leia the folly of taking unnecessary chances», L2867). Gently Force-lifts him up (L2881).
- Leia's speech: Jacen imprisoned «over a million Coruscanti residents» (L2929); True Victory Party members dead (L2933); Jacen fired on the Falcon with Jaina and Ben aboard (L2939).
- **Leia vs Tarfang**: roundhouse kick launches him off the rock (L2953–2955); Tarfang spits blood and teeth, head-butts, chokes, slams her skull on stone (L2963–2966); Force-elbow drives him off (L2967) — comedic brawl; Leia hurt.
- Luke arrives with Saba and Council Masters (L2973–2977). **Luke to Rock Council: «What you probably don't know is that Jacen Solo arranged it ... the Jedi Council has voted to begin active opposition to his continued leadership»** (L3003) — Jedi openly break with Caedus. Saba: «this is not a good day for anyone, Jedi Solo-especially not for you.» (L2995).
### Ch13 (L3007–) — Ben returns to Caedus on *Anakin Solo* (Kuat)
- Ben POV: Jacen as «a stain on the galaxy, a shadow spreading fire across the stars» (L3011). Ben must «keep his thoughts pure, to actually believe in the dark dream again. Jacen would see through anything less» (L3011) — Ben's infiltration plan to kill Caedus.
- Caedus probes Ben with «a faculty other than sight» (L3017). Ben hides his intent; «He could probably have hidden an outright lie from Jacen» (L3041) — Ben POV statement on his concealment. Ben avoids an apparent bait (Caedus turning his back «a little too deliberate», L3075). Ben: «if I had told Dad that I thought you killed Mom ... One of you would be dead now.» (L3061).
- Caedus: «if you can't handle an old man like Omas, how are you going to eliminate two Jedi Masters and Jaina?» (L3129). Ben betrays Luke's Kashyyyk plan to regain trust (L3137–3141). Caedus punches the wall (L3147); orders Fifth Fleet + *Anakin Solo* to Kashyyyk «to hunt down a band of traitors and deserters» (L3159–3163). Battle of Kuat «raging for over a week» (L3153).
### Ch14 (L3167–) — Ossus, Jaina
- Ben's warning reaches Jaina in her dreams: «All of you, be ready.» (L3175) — Ben long-range Force message (from Kuat to Ossus). Zekk hiding in the forest (L3179). Jaina fights coma gas by anchoring with Force-awareness (L3185–3195).
- **Jaina through coma gas**: Force-hurls the gasser into walls (L3197); Force-guided thrown sharpened spoon kills an officer in the dark (L3211–3219); mercy-hypo (L3221–3225).
- Serpa's ritual: children arranged in circle (L3233–3249). **Serpa shoots Tionne** (knee, then later arm/leg amputated by bolts) (L3275, L3349); Tionne «Force-slap» too slow (L3275). Serpa's snipers kill Jedi Knights Vis'l & Loli (L3297); **Kam Solusar, Ozlo, Jerga shot down** by sniper fire when drawn out (L3345, L3355; «K. Solusar down» — not stated dead; *Fury* L683 has Kam and Tionne recovering). Alfi and Hedda down (L3355).
- **Jaina's commando feats**: Force-snapped branch distraction; silently kills a sniper team by neck-snap + «Forceenhanced knife-hand strike» (sic, OCR) (L3325–3329): «Had Jaina been as skilled as her uncle, there might have been a way to incapacitate the pair without killing them.» (L3327, POV statement Luke > Jaina in control). Force-leap halfway up a roof (L3337); «crossed the roof in two Force bounds» and kills second sniper team (L3341–3343). Jag: «"You're a Jedi, "Jag replied. "How can anyone sneak up on you?"» (L3283).
- Jaina rolls off the roof firing «trusting her aim to the Force» (L3383–3386); «Jaina really missed her lightsaber.» (L3387). **Students revolt** using the Force to hurl troopers and disarm them (L3393–3397); some students killed (L3397). **Zekk severs Serpa's weapon arm, then strikes so fast «even Jaina did not see the attack»** (L3409–3415) — Zekk speed (Serpa an unpowered human). «"From now on, "Zekk said, "we'll be asking the questions."» (L3417).
### Ch15 (L3421–) — *Anakin Solo* over Kashyyyk
- Caedus: «That was the burden and the fate of Darth Caedus, to deliver justice wherever it was deserved» (L3427). «Jacen Solo was gone.» (L3441); insists on "Colonel".
- **Caedus repeatedly presents his back to Ben, inviting an attack** as a Sith apprentice test: «The mere attempt would be the catalyst that moved Ben to the next stage.» (L3471); «if Ben did not strike soon, he would have to be eliminated as unworthy» (L3519). Caedus knows Ben suspects him (L3483). Ben hides his presence (L3469, L3521).
- **Caedus orders the long-range batteries to burn Kashyyyk's wroshyr forests** (L3515–3525): «Worlds will tremble at the thought of deserting us.» (L3555) — strategy: force Confederation to defend Kashyyyk and draw them from Coruscant; terror. Caedus «understood Shyrii-wook since I was five» (sic, OCR) (L3493).
- Ben suggests targeting cities to stay in character (L3587). Wookie Owools «riding the stripe» (using the Star Destroyer's own turbolaser strikes for sensor cover) — Lowbacca leads (L3603–3615).
- Caedus opens a regretful battle-meld to Lowbacca: «for a moment, Caedus became Jacen once again» (L3617–3625); senses the shadow bomb in Lowie's Force grasp (L3623).
- **BEN vs CAEDUS (assassination attempt), flight deck (L3627–3641)**: «a bolt of danger sense was sizzling down his spine and whirled away ... then felt the heat of a lightsaber brush across his ribs as Ben's blade snapped to life and buried itself in the torso of Commander Twizzl.» (L3627) — Ben's surprise strike grazes/burns Caedus's ribs (scorched flesh, L3629). Lowbacca's shadow bomb hits simultaneously; «It might have been Lowbacca's shadow bomb or Caedus's own swift reflexes that saved his life.» (L3631). Caedus: «How terribly he had underestimated his young cousin!» (L3631).
  - **Caedus Force lightning**: «With no time to snatch his own weapon off his belt, Caedus lashed out, loosing a bolt of Force lightning that sent Ben tumbling across the floor in a smoking, convuls-ing heap. He summoned the boy's lightsaber to hand» (L3633) — FEAT (Caedus's first on-page lightning in these notes).
  - Caedus: «Not bad ... Artfully done, even.» (L3637); «Ben, we're just getting started.» (L3641). Caedus Force-amplifies his voice over alarms (L3637).
### Ch16 (L3645–) — Kashyyyk hangar, Rwookrrorro burning
- Han: «Jacen Solo is dead. My son wouldn't do this.» (L3663); Leia accepts it (L3669–3671). Leia: «Jacen Solo saved the galaxy.» (L3661). Luke: Han and Leia «not in a good frame of mind to fight» (L3703). Leia's plan (unspecified) (L3739).
- Alema reaches **Korriban** via the navigation string (L3753–3759) — dilapidated spaceport.
- Alema Force-chokes a Bothan, Force-crushes a human's jaw (L3797–3805); Rak'k (Barabel) points her to «The Valley of the Dark Lordz» (L3835) and a cloister at the valley mouth (L3843).
- **Korriban cloister — the "One Sith"**: Togorian **Morto** grabs Alema, pins her with the Force «his strength so obvious and great that Alema didn't even try to fight» (L3877) — STATEMENT (Alema POV: Morto > Alema in TK). Alema's poisoned dart behind his knee; tricks him into rubbing in Tenupe flesh-eating bacteria (L3893–3913).
- ~30 cloaked Sith on balconies (L3917, L3925); leader **"White Eyes"** (pale white eyes, L3919). **«You were taught the old ways ... We are only one Sith now.»** (L3923) — the One Sith (cf. Krayt's order in Legacy era; Krayt not named here). Datachip torn from her hand by TK (L3929).
- Alema claims she killed Mara (by her twisted logic) and the Sith detect no lie (L3945–3955).
- **White Eyes: «Lumiya's plan-not ours. Hers and Vergere's.»** (L3983); raspy woman: «You didn't know that?» re Vergere being a Sith (L3985–3987) — STATEMENT (One Sith claim that Vergere was a Sith). White Eyes: «Jacen Solo is not our problem. Nor do we want him to be.» (L3991). Alema: «all it took to bring Palpatine down was a farmboy and a self-absorbed Princess» (L3975).
- One Sith lore: message was meant to invite Lumiya to join «our organization»; Lomi Plo and her apprentice were One Sith, ambushed by the Vong (L4005–4007); **«Our Master met Vergere while he was a captive of the Yuuzhan Vong. She liked his vision of One Sith.»** (L4025); Lumiya convinced Vergere the Master's plan «was too slow» (L4027); «So they decided to create Jacen» (L4029) — STATEMENT (One Sith account of Caedus's origins; links to Krayt — "our Master" captive of the Vong = Krayt per Legacy lore, not named here).
- White Eyes Force-silences Alema with a gesture (L4039, L4045); hurls Morto into a pillar with a look (L4057). Alema's leverage: «Luke Skywalker will soon discover who killed his wife. Do you really want him to track us here?» (L4051) — the One Sith fear Luke (L4053). White Eyes: «We may have something for Jacen Solo after all.» (L4061).
### Ch17 (L4065–) — Kashyyyk, Jaina joins Luke
- Students got their lightsabers back; Serpa's battalion subdued (L4083). Luke can't feel Ben (L4095). Jaina tells Luke about Alema at Roqoo Depot; Luke suspects Alema of Mara's murder (L4119–4135) and delegates hunting her to Jaina: «After my mistake with Lumiya? ... It's better for someone else to handle this.» (L4139). Luke's plan: diversion strike on the Fifth Fleet, then a run at the *Anakin Solo* (L4151). Jaina flies with Luke. Tahiri «seems to have disappeared» (L4155).
- Luke draws his presence in, won't meld (L4169). Jaina: Jacen «become another Emperor, and he simply had to be stopped» (L4175). **A Bothan fleet arrives to defend Kashyyyk** (L4179–4193) — Caedus's strategy working. StealthX wing splits into six squadrons (L4213).
- *Vulnerator* anticipates the StealthXs; three Night Blades killed in the opening salvo (L4217–4237). **Luke's flying**: «weaving through the storm almost effortlessly, slipping away from turbolaser strikes half a second before they blossomed, ducking cannon bolts as though he had a telepathic connection to the gunner's mind ... if his flying was any example, he hadn't revealed half of what he could do. Maybe not even a quarter.» (L4227) — FEAT + Jaina POV STATEMENT (Luke's hidden depth).
- Jaina realizes «Luke was hiding from Jacen» — Jacen had been «looking for the Jedi combat-meld» (L4251–4253). Luke and Jaina draw in their presences (L4261). Luke's wild maneuvers: «it was the Force instead of bolts and welds holding his craft together» (L4269).
- **CAEDUS (in a StealthX) ambushes Luke & Jaina** at the *Anakin Solo* (L4273–4299): blows down Jaina's rear shields and an engine (L4273, L4281); hems Luke below the midline with fire (L4285). «He had to have been waiting for Luke to come after him. "He knew the whole plan!"» (L4291).
- **Jaina vs Caedus dogfight**: «He anticipated and slipped in the opposite direction, and one of her bolts lit up Luke's rear shields.» (L4297); «Her brother seemed to anticipate every shot before she took it.» (L4303) — FEAT (Caedus precognitive evasion). Caedus mocks her in the Force: «She had no business flying here against real pilots» (L4307); «a dark chuckle» goading her (L4309). Jaina stitches bolts across his upper shields (L4301); Caedus takes «a wing hit that sent a laser cannon spinning away» (L4315).
- **Luke's StealthX "destroyed" by Jaina's fire** (L4317–4325): Luke's voice «Do it! ... Lock'em down!» (L4311); his damaged engine flames and skids into her line of fire. «Luke Skywalker was dead. And Jaina had shot him down.» (L4323–4325). [Wookieepedia: Luke faked his death to infiltrate the *Anakin Solo* — see luke-legends-quotes.txt L108, L157; check later chapters.]
### Ch18 (L4329–) — Han & Leia at the Fountain Palace, Hapes
- Leia senses Luke's "death": «I felt this surprise, then.... this anguish. And Luke was just gone.» (L4375). Han and Leia decided Jacen is dead to them (L4359).
- Tahiri arrives to detain the Solos: «He's only doing what's necessary to save the Alliance.» (L4431); «Anakin would want me to support Jacen.» (L4435).
- **LEIA vs TAHIRI duel, Royal Hangar (L4451–4491)**: both bat stun bolts back at the marines (L4461) — FEAT (both). Leia «a jaw-cracking elbow under Tahiri's chin» (L4455); thrust kick bends Tahiri over a strut (L4463); Tahiri Force-hurls a blaster rifle into Leia's back (L4465); Leia's pirouette at Tahiri's neck (L4467–4471); Leia «beating down her guard, slipping in knee and elbow strikes with a speed and ferocity that only a Barabel-trained fighter could achieve.» (L4475) — FEAT (Leia dominant). Han: Leia «was determined to make Tahiri pay» (L4479). Tahiri lands a spinning back-kick only when Leia is distracted by Han's shot (L4481). Leia nearly throws Han by instinct (L4483). Result: Leia ahead on points; stopped by Han and Tenel Ka.
- **Tenel Ka** intervenes with lightsaber (L4495); seizes Tahiri's StealthX and sends her to Jacen as a "prisoner of war" (L4547–4551). Tenel Ka: Hapes provides «nearly a fifth of the Alliance's combat capacity» (L4527). She lists Jacen's betrayals (L4597): «He took my last fleet» and is ready to switch sides: «I think the time has come for someone to do the same to him» (L4603). Uses the Force to reduce eye puffiness (L4607).
- Tenel Ka: «We both know that if you aren't for Jacen, you're against him.» (L4611).
### Ch19 (L4615–) — Ben in the Embrace of Pain aboard the *Anakin Solo*
- Ben clings to his hate: «Colonel Jacen Solo, who is the murderer of my mother» (L4621). Jacen: «Hang on to your hate. It will help you endure ... I couldn't hate, and it nearly destroyed me.» (L4635).
- **Caedus STATEMENT**: «it will take decades to develop the power to confront me openly. And I hope you understand the futility of trying to take me by surprise.» (L4639) — self-assessment (Ben's surprise attack did graze him).
- Jacen shares the Embrace's pain: «Everything the Embrace of Pain does to you, I feel.» (L4653) (cf. Vergere). Jacen's "one life for billions" argument (L4693–4697): «And I didn't-but I would have. That's the difference between us. I'm willing to carry that burden.» (L4697).
- **Caedus severs Ben from the Force** (L4719–4729): «How long could the Embrace have held you, had I let you keep the Force?» (L4725) — FEAT; restores it by a touch (L4735).
- **LUKE HIDES FROM CAEDUS (fake death confirmed)**: Ben senses Luke «a small, tight presence a deck or two above» (L4739); «Ben was the only one whom his dad was allowing to sense him through the Force. Not even Jacen had that kind of control.» (L4741) — FEAT + narrator STATEMENT (Luke's Force concealment/selective projection > Caedus). Caedus: «Even I'm not good enough to project emotions into other Force-users» (L4747); «I know because I felt him die» (L4751) — Caedus fooled. Luke sabotages a long-range turbolaser (cooling coils, L4773–4777). Caedus searches and finds nothing: «I don't feel any Jedi presence at all» (L4785) — Luke undetected at close range (cf. Ch8 cabin, L2061–2127).
- Luke POV: Jacen using the Embrace on Ben (L4799–4807). **«Luke didn't give Jacen a chance to surrender. He just sprang.»** (L4809). Jacen spins, igniting his saber «in the same motion» (L4811). → DUEL: LUKE vs CAEDUS (Anakin Solo).

#### ★ DUEL: LUKE vs CAEDUS — Embrace-of-Pain chamber, *Anakin Solo* (L4809–4943) ★
Context: Luke attacks from surprise (Caedus distracted taunting Ben). Luke is in a battle rage (grief over Mara + finding Ben tortured). Luke's ribs carry «the barely healed scar from his first fight with Lumiya» (L4817). No-quarter fight in the dark; ends with Ben's vibrodagger in Caedus's back.
- L4813–4815: «Luke was attacking low, striking for the kidney» ... «The tip sank a few centimeters, drawing a pained hiss as it touched a kidney» — Caedus reacts in time to limit it. **«But Jacen thrived on pain, fed on it to make himself stronger and faster.»** (L4815) — narrator STATEMENT. Caedus «landed a rib-crunching roundhouse» (L4815).
- L4821: Luke blocks a high slash, «landing an elbow smash to the temple that dropped Jacen to his knees. He brought his own knee up under Jacen's chin, hearing teeth crack-and relishing it.» — FEAT (Luke).
- L4823–4827: Caedus Force-slides away; Luke anticipates lightning and raises a Force shield, but «The lightning never came. Instead, Luke was blindsided by something heavy and spiky» — Caedus TK-hurls a thorn bed, pinning Luke to the wall (L4827) — FEAT (Caedus feint/TK).
- L4831: Luke slashes free; «Luke landed a snap-kick to the stomach that lifted Jacen a meter off the deck» — FEAT (Luke).
- L4833: Caedus ducks the neck slash, «driving a Force-enhanced punch into Luke's ribs».
- **L4835: «Luke struck again with his lightsaber, using both hands and putting all his strength into the attack, beating his nephew's guard down so far that Jacen's emerald blade bit into his own shoulder.»** — FEAT (Luke overpowers Caedus in a bind). Caedus kicks Luke's knee: «Something popped» (L4835). Luke sweeps on the way down; «Jacen screamed, and the smell of scorched bone and singed hair filled the air» (L4837) — Luke's blade scorches Caedus's skull (cf. L4901 «a hand-sized rectangle of scorched skull»).
- L4839–4843: finger-strike to Jacen's eye; feint to the blind side; **Force wave sends Jacen flying** into the tendril rack (L4842–4843) — FEAT (Luke).
- L4845–4847: Luke closes to finish; Ben: «"Wait!" Ben cried from behind him. "Let me do it!"» (L4849); Luke falls on the injured knee (L4851).
- L4865–4871: Caedus TK-drops the ceiling illumination panel on Luke — «He heard something crunch in his nose» (L4869). Luke Force-hurls it off.
- **L4875: «Jacen was running.»** — Caedus disengages toward the door (Force flip over Luke, L4873).
- L4877: Luke Force-drags him back: «their blades colliding faster than the eye could follow ... He landed an elbow and felt a bone in Jacen's face shatter.» — FEAT (Luke).
- L4881–4887: Luke beats down his guard «too quickly» — a trap; **Caedus garrotes Luke with a toxic tendril** from behind: «Should have let me go when you had the chance ... Now you're done.» (L4887) — FEAT (Caedus guile). Elbow to Jacen's ribs «was like hitting a permacrete wall» (L4889).
- **L4889–4893: Luke escapes the garrote himself** — Force-hurls them both into the wall, Jacen's skull hits first; double-armed elbow under the chin; «The garrote went completely slack.» (L4893).
- L4895–4901: Ben (freed with the vibrodagger) stabs Caedus between the shoulder blades. Caedus's state: «His face was inflamed and misshapen, his clothes were smoking and tattered, a hand-sized rectangle of scorched skull showed through his scalp, and still he was stretching a hand toward his lightsaber.» (L4901).
- Luke refuses to let Ben kill him; spares Caedus to save Ben from the dark side (L4909, L4933–4941); Luke takes Caedus's lightsaber (L4943). Luke: «I did that with Lumiya, and all it did was make me weak.» (L4909). Luke sabotaged all four long-range batteries (L4921) and they blew during the fight.
- **Caedus's own verdict (Ch20, L4961) — key STATEMENT (high reliability, loser's POV): «Luke had beaten him. Luke had just kept coming despite his injuries. He had inflicted more damage on Caedus than he had suffered himself, and he had even escaped the garrote before Ben struck. In fact, it was probably that attack that had saved Caedus's life. Nothing else could have shocked Luke out of his battle rage-only the sight of Ben slipping so far to the dark side.»** Caedus: «when Luke came after him next time, Caedus would be ready» (L4963) — hedge: he was unprepared.
- RESULT: Luke wins decisively (Caedus's own POV). Caveats: Luke had surprise; Caedus had just been sabotaged and believed Luke dead; Luke was enraged (amplifying him, cf. Lumiya fight); Luke also injured (ribs, knee popped, nose broken, garrote toxin). Caedus fought pre-"full Sith" but already Darth Caedus.
### Ch20 (L4947–) — infirmary
- Caedus: «only a few broken bones and some damaged organs» (L4951); «Caedus was feeding on the pain, burning it like fuel» (L4979); Qilqu: «The colonel has an extraordinary constitution» (L4975). Caedus sacrificed «a couple of thousand crew members to draw the Confederation away from the Battle of Kuat» (L4955) — his strategy worked; Fifth Fleet now surrounded (L4989); Niathal withheld Bwua'tu/Darklighter (L4993–4995). Atoko prepares to scuttle (L4997).
- **Caedus's injury list after Luke+Ben** (MD droid): «Even if the swelling in your brain doesn't destroy your balance, you have burn damage to your kidney and a perforation in your lung. You're simply too weak.» (L5007). Caedus: «I'm a master of the Force, EmDee ... I'm never weak.» (L5009); uses the Force to hold himself upright (L5011) — FEAT (durability).
- **L5067 Caedus POV STATEMENT: «his uncle had nearly killed him, and his cousin had planted a vibrodagger so near his heart that the handle had twitched in time to his pulse.»** — corroborates L4961.
- Caedus considers having the Fifth Fleet (70,000+ crew) fight to the death (L5019–5035); planned to escape in a StealthX (L5031). Mistakes the arriving Hapans for allies (L5057–5069); «The fight with Luke had drained him» (L5091); isolation (L5091–5093).
- **Tenel Ka turns on Caedus**: demands surrender (L5103); «I am a Hapan queen ... You won't deceive me with equivocation» (L5121); «it is going to break my heart to open fire on you» (L5129). She draws her aura in so he cannot touch it (L5131), then opens her heart to show the decision is hers (L5149–5151). Caedus: «Tenel Ka had deserted him, their love just one more offering to his Sith destiny.» (L5153). Han: «pretend you died in that fight with Onimi» (L5135). Caedus to his parents: «I wouldn't think of dying before I make you pay for this-both of you.» (L5141). Hapans open fire (L5163).
- Alema trained 3 days by the One Sith in the meditation sphere; their gift to Jacen: the **Holocron of Darth Vectivus** (L5173–5177) — Alema mocks it. Ship: Vectivus was «a middle manager in a galactic mining conglomerate» (L5177).
- Ship (Sith meditation sphere) can **«Control, not command»** an enemy fleet because «they have no meditation spheres of their own» (L5193) — Alema + sphere influence the Bothan fleet.
- Caedus's battle meditation, badly injured: senses Alema's pull, her power «greatly magnified, far too ancient and somehow even darker than before» (L5241). Orders the Fifth to charge the Bothans head-on (L5249). **Presses down on Admiral Atoko's presence until «his resistance quickly yielded to obedience»** (L5261) — FEAT (mind-pressure on a fleet commander at range). Brushes calm onto the fleet's officers (L5257–5259).
- Caedus draws on the Force «through his love of them all» (L5269) — notable framing: love as his power source, even now. **Presses the Bothan commanders' minds** to build on Alema's induced indecision; they advance instead of falling back, letting the Fifth escape (L5271–5277) — FEAT (fleet-scale battle meditation, aided by Alema/sphere). He blacks out into a vision of peace (L5277).
### Ch21 (L5281–5377) — *Dragon Queen*
- Babo (Bothan): «Your son is a master tactician» (L5295). **Luke: «There may also have been some Force pressure involved ... The colonel may be using ancient battle meditation techniques to confuse his opponents.»** (L5301). Luke's injuries: «a bruised face, two black eyes, and half a dozen casts and bandages» (L5301) — Luke took real damage in the duel. Luke had **faked his death** (L5301 «if he ever faked his death again»).
- Tenel Ka: «Colonel Solo is not the Alliance» (L5309). Luke: **«As long as Jacen controls the Alliance, there is no Alliance.»** (L5325). Jedi will not interfere with Bothan assassins (L5365). Leia: «Han, what have we done?» Han: «What we had to.» (L5375–5377).
### Back-matter: ROUND-ROBIN INTERVIEW (L5381–) — Allston, Traviss, Denning (AUTHOR INTENT)
- Mara's death: Traviss «I'm afraid it was me who suggested that Jacen had to kill someone he loved.» (L5391); Denning: «the storyline demanded a crisis that would shock Ben to the core ... Nobody killed Mara just for the heck of it.» (L5395). Traviss's SS-puppy test analogy: «a typical Sith test-to be so loyal to the Sith ideal that you'd obey orders and kill someone you loved» (L5397) — author intent: Mara's death = Jacen's Sith "sacrifice" test, not a power showcase.
- Allston on danger: «putting characters in danger and then never killing any of them ... robs a series of any tension. Oh, dear, Luke is in danger again, hohum.» (L5415).
- Jacen's fall: Denning «the kernel of the idea occurred to me while I was writing that trilogy» (Dark Nest) (L5437); «the only person responsible for what Jacen has become is Jacen himself» (L5447). Traviss: «Jacen is actually just a very smart guy with an excessively high opinion of himself ... he edges toward the bad stuff a slice at a time» (L5449).
- Traviss on Jedi: «These people are superweapons» (L5483); «a small religious schismatic war within a tiny unelected elite» (L5475). Denning: «even Jacen was responsible for ending the Yuuzhan Vong war» (L5485). Lumiya was originally a role called «the wizard» (L5501). Denning hint: «grand space battles and classic lightsaber duels» (L5513).

---
**Quote verification:** `[local path]` result: checked 418 fragments; bad 0. Fragments were split on " ... ", " / " and [brackets]. Quotes taken from other research files or other novels use “ ” and were not checked against this novel.

---

## Character analyses (added 2026-10-17 under the 7+ characters rule)

*Method:* built from the verified log above (Sections 2–4 and the raw chapter log). `L####` = line in `5c501d6d…txt` (chapter given). Quotes in «…» are verbatim and were re-checked by script. "Our Master" = Krayt is a **[wiki/wider-Legends inference]**, not stated in the book. **Before:** 8 short story profiles (SP2), none with feats, fights and scaling. **Added:** 12 analyses.

### 1. Darth Caedus (Jacen Solo)
- **Role/arc:** de facto ruler. Frames Omas, burns Kashyyyk, tortures Ben, and loses to Luke. «This was what it meant to be Sith: to use friends without hesitation, to sacrifice family for destiny, to live with a stained soul.» (L179).
- **Key feats:** flow-walks Tahiri to Myrkr: «I've been flow-walking for years» (Prologue, L77). Short-range precognition (Ch8, L1917). His danger sense survives Ben's surprise strike, and then «loosing a bolt of Force lightning that sent Ben tumbling across the floor» (Ch15, L3627–3633). Cuts Ben off from the Force in the Embrace of Pain (Ch19, L4719–4725). Fleet-scale battle meditation while his lung is perforated (Ch20, L5007, L5261–5277).
- **Limits:** «a humbling reminder that battle meditation was not infallible» (L1981). «Even I'm not good enough to project emotions into other Force-users» (L4747).
- **Statements:** his own POV after Ch19: «Luke had beaten him. Luke had just kept coming despite his injuries.» (L4961); «his uncle had nearly killed him» (L5067). On Mara: «so could the Masters-as Mara had demonstrated when she nearly killed him on Kavan.» (L2011). Traviss: «Jacen is actually just a very smart guy with an excessively high opinion of himself.» (L5449, author interview).
- **Fights/results:** loses to Luke twice (Ch11 TK, Ch19 duel). Beats Ben (Ch15). Draws the dogfight with Luke and Jaina (Ch17).
- **Scaling:** Luke > Caedus in TK and in a duel. Caedus's best tools are tricks and battle meditation.

### 2. Luke Skywalker
- **Role/arc:** goes from grief to decisive deception (a fake reconciliation, then a fake death). Refuses vengeance: «I did that with Lumiya, and all it did was make me weak.» (L4909).
- **Key feats:** pins Caedus without effort: «he had simply grabbed Caedus in the Force and hurled him five meters into his chair.» (Ch11, L2559). Holds him «with no apparent exertion» (L2569), then collapses the chair pedestal with a finger tap (L2631–2635). Hides from all but Ben: «Not even Jacen had that kind of control.» (Ch19, L4741). In the duel he lands «an elbow smash to the temple that dropped Jacen to his knees» (L4821) and «a snap-kick to the stomach that lifted Jacen a meter off the deck» (L4831), and escapes the garrote (L4893). Flying: «ducking cannon bolts as though he had a telepathic connection to the gunner's mind» (Ch17, L4227).
- **Statements:** Caedus's POV: «the galaxy was littered with the body parts of those who had underestimated the strength of Luke Skywalker.» (L713). Jaina's POV: he «hadn't revealed half of what he could do. Maybe not even a quarter.» (L4227).
- **Fights/results:** beats Caedus (Ch19). Injuries include a broken nose, a popped knee and a garrote wound. He had surprise and was in a battle rage.
- **Scaling:** the decisive Luke > Caedus data point (high confidence).

### 3. Ben Skywalker
- **Role/arc:** a double agent «more like Jacen than even Jacen knew» (L4695). Luke pulls him back from the kill.
- **Key feats:** a Force-guided second strike through the cut in a Guardian droid's laminanium arm (Ch9, L2155). Surprise saber strike that only grazes Caedus (L3627). Vibrodagger between Caedus's shoulder blades (Ch19, L4895–4901).
- **Statements:** Caedus to Ben: «it will take decades to develop the power to confront me openly.» (L4639).
- **Scaling:** far below Caedus head-on. Dangerous through stealth.

### 4. Jaina Solo
- **Role/arc:** commando and ace. She decides Jacen will «become another Emperor, and he simply had to be stopped» (L4175).
- **Key feats:** «batted half a dozen stun bolts back at the men who had fired them» (Ch4, L1159). Through coma gas, kills an officer with a Force-guided thrown spoon (Ch14, L3197–3219). Takes out two sniper teams, «crossed the roof in two Force bounds» (L3341). Shoots a wing cannon off Caedus's StealthX (L4315).
- **Statements:** her own POV: «Her brother seemed to anticipate every shot before she took it.» (L4303). «Had Jaina been as skilled as her uncle, there might have been a way to incapacitate the pair without killing them.» (L3327).
- **Scaling:** Caedus ≥ Jaina in a fighter here. Luke > Jaina in control.

### 5. Leia Organa Solo
- **Role/arc:** mourns her living son: «Jacen Solo is dead. My son wouldn't do this.» (L3663).
- **Key feats:** against about 12 GAG troopers she slashes off a recruit's arms and steers a grenade (Ch2, L525, L541–543). Her mind trick fails on a trained corporal: «I'm not weak-minded, you know.» (L485). Against Tahiri, «beating down her guard, slipping in knee and elbow strikes with a speed and ferocity that only a Barabel-trained fighter could achieve.» (Ch18, L4475).
- **Scaling:** Leia > Tahiri.

### 6. Tahiri Veila
- **Role/arc:** a guilt-ridden collaborator and Caedus's flow-walk partner: «Anakin would want me to support Jacen.» (L4435).
- **Feats:** bats back marine stun bolts (L4461). Lands a back-kick only while Han distracts Leia (L4481).
- **Scaling:** below Leia in this book. She rises later (Ben vs. Tahiri in Invincible).

### 7. Tenel Ka
- **Role/arc:** Jacen's loyal backer until Kashyyyk, then turns her fleet on him: «I am a Hapan queen ... You won't deceive me with equivocation» (L5121).
- **Feat:** senses his camouflage, «a void so subtle she recognized it only by the surrounding stillness.» (Ch1, L187).
- **Scaling:** below him in power. Intimacy lets her beat his stealth.

### 8. Zekk
- **Feat:** cuts off Serpa's weapon arm with a strike so fast that «even Jaina did not see the attack» (Ch14, L3415).
- **Scaling:** Knight level, with very fast saber work.

### 9. Alema Rar
- **Role/arc:** "Balance" logic and Jacen's unwanted guardian angel.
- **Key feats:** overpowers the Sith sphere's will, «forsaking subtlety for sheer power» (Ch5, L1399). Gets through Lumiya's trap gauntlet, including a damage-reflecting illusion (L1449–1471). Helps Caedus's battle meditation with the sphere (L5271).
- **Fights/results:** Morto pins her (below), but she poisons him.
- **Scaling:** below the One Sith rank-and-file in TK, but resourceful.

### 10. Morto (One Sith)
- **Feats:** grabs Alema so fast «that Alema barely realized his hand had moved», with «his strength so obvious and great that Alema didn't even try to fight» (Ch16, L3875–3877).
- **Fight/result:** Alema's dart and bacteria trick get him.
- **Scaling:** a One Sith rank-and-filer > Alema in raw power.

### 11. "White Eyes" (One Sith leader at the Korriban cloister)
- **Feats:** silences Alema with a raised hand and hurls Morto into a pillar with a look (L4039, L4057).
- **Statements:** «You were taught the old ways ... We are only one Sith now.» (L3923). «Our Master met Vergere while he was a captive of the Yuuzhan Vong. She liked his vision of One Sith.» (L4025). The cloister reacts with «misgivings and concern» at the thought of Luke tracking them (L4051–4053).
- **Scaling:** the first on-page One Sith, and they fear Luke. "Our Master" is Krayt only in wider Legends **[wiki inference]**.

### 12. Cal Omas
- **Role:** a former Chief of State who impales himself on Ben's saber to make Ben's cover real (Ch9, L2317–2343).
- **Scaling:** none. Included as a key cast member of the Ben plot.

<!-- 021-legacy-of-the-force/lotf-7-fury-notes.md (verbatim copy) -->

# Legacy of the Force 7: Fury (novel, read in full): feats, scaling and story notes

<!-- read up to: chapter thirty-eight (L7067), END OF BOOK; restructured into house style -->

- **Book:** *Star Wars: Legacy of the Force: Fury* by Aaron Allston (2007). Seventh of the nine Legacy of the Force novels.
- **Continuity:** Legends. Set in 40–41 ABY, during the Second Galactic Civil War, a few weeks after *Inferno*. Caedus is still recovering from his fight with Luke at the start (L343–351) and is only «fully healed for the first time since his fight with Luke» by Ch31 (L5573). It comes after *Inferno* (LOTF 6) and before *Revelation* (LOTF 8). The dramatis personae lists him as «Jacen Solo / Darth Caedus; Sith, joint Chief of State of the Galactic Alliance (human male)» (L51).
- **Source file:** `[local path]` (7,068 lines). OCR noise such as «strike at]acen» (L837) and «Chaptaer 33» (L5929) is kept verbatim and marked sic where quoted.
- **Method:** I read every line in order: front matter, then Ch1–Ch38. There is no back-matter interview in this edition. `L####` is the line number in that file. Text inside «…» is copied exactly from the file and was checked by script (see the end of this file). The raw reading log is kept as an appendix.
- **Chapter heading lines:** Ch1 L83, Ch2 L313, Ch3 L501, Ch4 L675, Ch5 L847, Ch6 L999, Ch7 L1101, Ch8 L1211, Ch9 L1325, Ch10 L1465, Ch11 L1655, Ch12 L1935, Ch13 L2189, Ch14 L2337, Ch15 L2521, Ch16 L2715, Ch17 L2963, Ch18 L3113, Ch19 L3275, Ch20 L3439, Ch21 L3591, Ch22 L3733, Ch23 L3979, Ch24 (heading lost in OCR; it starts around L4339), Ch25 L4347, Ch26 L4471, Ch27 L4635, Ch28 L4923, Ch29 L5177, Ch30 L5385, Ch31 L5565, Ch32 L5763, Ch33 L5929 (sic «Chaptaer»), Ch34 L6179, Ch35 L6343, Ch36 L6583, Ch37 L6759, Ch38 L6979.
- **Feat vs. statement:** Section 2 covers what the narration shows happening. Section 3 covers what characters say or think, marked **STATEMENT**. Allston writes in rotating tight third person, so a lot of "narration" is a character's view; those are marked *(POV)*. **Allston's framing of Caedus is kinder than Denning's.** In Caedus's POV the Luke duel was even (L345), and in Luke's POV «Jacen was Luke's match» (L6729). Weigh that against *Inferno*, where Caedus's own POV says Luke beat him.
- **Headline:** this is the **Alema Rar endgame and Allana rescue** book. Caedus kidnaps Allana (Ch5–6). He beats a four-Jedi ambush led by Kyle Katarn and beheads Mithric (Ch10–11). Luke hunts him in space but breaks off on sensing Allana (Ch18–19). **Jag Fel kills Alema Rar** using anti-Jedi tactics (Ch27). Luke, Saba and Ben storm the *Anakin Solo*'s bridge while Han, Leia and Iella take Allana back (Ch35–37). Centerpoint destroys itself (Ch37). Caedus Force-chokes Lt. Tebut (Ch37). Jaina leaves to find «her teacher» (L7067), Boba Fett.

---

## 1. Cast who fight or use the Force

| Character | Role in this book | Force user? |
|---|---|---|
| Jacen Solo / Darth Caedus | Joint Chief of State. Kidnaps Allana, survives the Katarn ambush, loses Allana and Centerpoint, kills Tebut | Yes |
| Luke Skywalker | Grand Master. Grieving and passive until Ben shakes him out of it (Ch28). Leads the bridge assault | Yes |
| Ben Skywalker | 14. Suspects Jacen killed Mara (L1803). Told to build his lightsaber (L5165). YVH-grenade tactics on the bridge | Yes |
| Jaina Solo | Top Knight. Trains with Jag, hunts Alema, leaves for Fett at the end | Yes |
| Jagged Fel | Non-Force-user in beskar and cortosis. **Kills Alema** | No |
| Zekk | Frees the Sith sphere by touching the dark side, and is nearly consumed | Yes |
| Alema Rar | Projects Force "phantoms" from Lumiya's asteroid using a dark-side well. Killed by Jag | Yes |

| Wedge, Syal, Tycho, Iella; Seyah; Koyan, Teppler, Delpin, Phennir, Vibro | Non-Force cast: Rakehell Squadron, Centerpoint sabotage, Corellian politics | No |
