## 2. Combat and Force feats, in chapter order

### Ch1–Ch4
1. **Ch1 · L153, L277–285 · Alema (phantom)**: appears on the *Falcon* undetected. Her ruined arm is whole («Her left arm was useless, had been ruined years earlier-but now it was fine.», L153). She shrugs off Leia's push: «Alema merely rocked back on her heels and looked faintly amused.» (L285). *Shows:* the phantom ignores physics; it is a projection, not a body.
2. **Ch1 · L291–309 · Leia vs Alema phantom**: Leia defends «purely defensively» (L303); the phantom's «strength seemed to» fade (L305) and it vanishes.
3. **Ch4 · L693, L711, L717 · Luke (Masters' sparring)**: pulls a tree branch onto Kyp (L693), disarms Cilghal by catching her hilt (L711), and against a swarm of Force-thrown bolts «he calculated which objects had a chance of hitting him and incinerated only them with his blades» (L717). *Shows:* casual Master-tier control, even while depressed.

### Ch5–Ch6 — Allana kidnapped (Hapes)
4. **Ch5 · L871–875 · Caedus**: finds Allana as «a bright, joyous flare in the Force» and sees through her eyes. He uses a stun hold-out because «stun bolts were far better than lethal attacks, lightsabers, or anything that manifested strongly in the Force» (L897). *Shows:* stealth infiltration of the Queen Mother's palace.
5. **Ch6 · L1003–1061 · Caedus**: kills three palace staff (L1003, L1013), cuts through a floor, blows up the diplomatic shuttle and escapes in a StealthX with Allana. Tenel Ka arrives too late (L1009–1039).

### Ch10–Ch11 — DUEL: Caedus vs Katarn, Valin, Mithric, Hu'lya (Senate plaza, L1545–1745)
6. **L1557 · Caedus**: «Contemptuous, he flicked his hand toward the darkness and felt his own power turn the cylinders around.» (He turns back the rolling grenades.)
7. **L1573–1577 · Caedus**: blocks Katarn, Force-hurls Hu'lya into Mithric, and kicks Valin off his feet. «Two seconds had passed since the attack began.» (L1577)
8. **L1641–1651 · Caedus**: feints a push at Katarn and instead yanks a GAG speeder into Katarn's back, driving him onto his blade: «Katarn looked down to see Caedus's lightsaber buried to its hilt in his chest.» (L1649). Katarn survives. Cilghal on the wound: «The attack severed two ribs, penetrated his left lung, and exited through his left shoulder blade. A few centimeters off, and it would have gone clean through his heart.» (L2397).
9. **L1719–1723 · Caedus**: catches a bolt and Mithric's blade in one motion, deflects the bolt into Mithric's chest, then «Mithric's head fell from his shoulders.» *Caveat:* Caedus «wasn't yet recovered from his duel with Luke» (L1715). He is grazed by a bolt (L1685), and Mithric «was starting to beat down his parries» (L1713).

### Ch12 — Jaina vs Jag (training)
10. **L2035, L2065–2081 · Jaina / Jag**: «Almost faster than the eye could follow, Jaina lunged.» (L2035). She wins two bouts, then Jag takes her blade on beskar, crushes the practice hilt and wins (L2065–2075). Jaina Force-throws him into a tree (L2079–2081). *Shows:* Jaina's speed; the anti-Jedi lesson is set up.

### Ch13–Ch17
11. **Ch13 · L2207 · Caedus**: «his Sith battle meditation ability helped his forces inflict heavy losses in both theaters of operation».
12. **Ch15 · L2543 · Leia**: «She glanced at the button, and it clicked down without Caedus's help.» (TK in front of Caedus).
13. **Ch15 · L2573–2575 · ysalamir**: hides Jaina, Han, Zekk and Jag from Caedus aboard his own ship.
14. **Ch16 · L2847–2871 · Alema (lore feat)**: projects herself across the galaxy from the asteroid, drawing on «the immense pool of dark power waiting hundreds of meters below her», and anchors on the sleeping Allana aboard the *Anakin Solo*.

### Ch18–Ch19 — SPACE FIGHT: Luke + Red Sword Flight vs Caedus in the TIE Blur (L3187–3377)
15. **L3215 · Luke**: «he swung his snubfighter's nose a trifle to starboard, located his target by feel, and fired» and hits the stealth prototype first time.
16. **L3241, L3251 · Caedus**: «made himself smaller in the Force, reducing his presence to nothingness», then loses it to rage: «So great it was that he could no longer contain his presence in the Force-his anger flowed through him, through Allana, through his pursuers, through everything in tune with him or the Force.» Luke: «It was so strong it felt like a kick in the gut, and he wondered for an instant if Jacen had perfected some new Force attack.» (L3279).
17. **L3283 · Caedus (piloting)**: survives Luke, Kyp and Corran by «brilliant evasive flying». Luke breaks off on sensing Allana: «There was, in fact, a little girl in the cockpit with Jacen.» (L3343).

### Ch23–Ch27 — Alema's asteroid
18. **L4205–4207, L4253–4255 · Alema**: mynock phantoms tied to real people: «Burn that and you burn yourself.» Leia, Han, Jaina, Zekk and Jag are all targeted at once. The cost: «already panting, tired by the effort of maintaining so many phantoms at once» (L4341).
19. **L4355, L4363 · Sith sphere**: fires from the dark and cuts the track twice.
20. **L4405–4409 · Zekk**: frees Ship from Alema by opening himself to the dark side: «Into his words he poured his own power of will, and felt it joined, strengthened by the power of this dark place.» The cost: «it was strong, so very strong, and it laughed at his pathetic efforts.» (L4423).
21. **L4451–4455 · Jaina**: reads through the phantom "Jag" and dodges Alema's hidden blade: «Predictable, Alema, predictable.»
22. **L4479–4485, L4535 · Leia vs Alema phantom (2)**: Alema uses «all fourth-form technique» (L4479). Leia rides a Force link to Han's piloting so Alema stumbles (L4483). Alema's «sledgehammerlike blows had grown weaker» (L4535).
23. **L4695 · Alema**: «Energy-invisible, reeking of the dark side-slammed into Jaina, forcing her back. The track she held on to bent several meters above her head». She then climbs the track «using only her feet and the Force» (L4699).
24. **L4741–4749 · Alema**: tears a 4 m section of track loose and clubs Jag with it twice. The beskar holds.
25. **L4767–4803 · Jag kills Alema**: she Force-pulls his blaster mid-draw. It is rigged and explodes: «Her right arm was gone from just below the elbow.» (L4785). Then «He felt the crack of Alema's vertebrae under his hand as they shattered.» (L4803). Her own analysis of the weakness: «We cannot feel droid intent» (L4773).

### Ch28 — Luke recovers
26. **L5021 · Luke (sensing)**: feels the outpost «as though it were a skin», down to the moon's core. After Ben's intervention he takes up his duty again (L5133–5143).

### Ch31–Ch37 — Centerpoint operation and the *Anakin Solo* bridge
27. **L5723 · Caedus (battle meditation)**: «from them, probabilities and eventualities began streaming, glimpses of possible futures»; held through a hyperspace jump (L5725). He nudges a Sullustan commander (L5771).
28. **L6003–6005 · Luke, Ben, Saba, Leia**: «Moving so fast that they blurred»; six guards drop in an instant.
29. **L6215 · Saba**: Force-flips the cut plug onto a grenade and holds it down. Ben: «He was fast, but not as fast as Saba Sebatyne.»
30. **L6237, L6265 · Saba vs YVH**: «Saba, her sword work brilliant, was parrying full-autofire streams of blaster cannon fire.» She then rolls under and beheads a laminanium droid (L6265).
31. **L6237, L6261, L6269 · Luke vs YVH**: herded by cannon fire at first (L6237). Then he detonates a droid's minirocket in its port (L6261) and Force-topples and stabs another (L6269).
32. **L6255–6257 · Ben**: wrenches a YVH's cannon arm through the Force to shoot another droid in half.
33. **L6621–6627 · Ben**: plants plundered grenades on four YVHs, blowing two «into and through the bulkhead armor» (L6625).
34. **L6629–6633 · Caedus vs Saba**: «He gestured, exerting himself telekinetically, and Saba Sebatyne slipped laterally into the starboard pit, almost atop the doomed droid there.» The blast slams her into the wall. Caedus: «One down.» *Context:* Saba was busy deflecting cannon fire.
35. **L6711 · Saba (durability)**: «Impossibly, Saba stood», charred, and keeps deflecting.
36. **L6723–6725 · Luke**: feints at Jacen and hurls a YVH through the weakened viewport. Caedus staggers in the decompression.
37. **L6645–6665 · Leia + Han vs YVH**: Han shoots into the cannon barrel (L6655). Leia ducks the swing and drives her blade up into the skull (L6663–6665).
38. **L6849 · Caedus**: «With the Force lending him speed», he chases the rescuers but is too late.
39. **L6957–6969 · Caedus**: Force-lifts and chokes Tebut, flings Nevil across the bridge with his free hand (L6961), and breaks her neck: «The sound of Tebut's neck breaking was startlingly loud». *Shows:* Vader-style Force choke; he is losing control.

---

## 3. Statements and hype (STATEMENT: not feats)

| # | Line | Speaker / POV | Statement | Reliability |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| S1 | L345 | Caedus (POV) on the *Inferno* duel | «with the advantage being held first by Luke, then by Caedus ... Caedus felt a swelling of pride» | **Low–medium.** Self-flattering. It contradicts Denning's *Inferno* L4961, also Caedus's POV, which concedes Luke beat him. Allston's framing. |
| S2 | L347–351 | Caedus (POV) | Ben's vibroblade struck when «Caedus had lost a position of advantage-Luke had slipped free of the poison-injecting torture vines»; injuries listed | High as fact. It matches *Inferno*. |
| S3 | L441 | Luke (POV) | Jacen «now commanded Master-level abilities in the Force» | High. |
| S4 | L783 | Corran | Luke is «the most dangerous man in the galaxy»; Jacen «the second-most dangerous man» | Medium–high. Partly a pep-talk act (L787), but it fits the book. |
| S5 | L795 | Luke | a single «negligent attack» from Jacen would «distract a Jedi Master, hurt a Jedi Knight... . and kill you» | High. Master-tier framing. |
| S6 | L1569–1571 | Caedus (POV) | «Separately or collectively, these Jedi Knights were no match for him.» / «Katarn, though, was a threat.» | High. The duel bears it out. |
| S7 | L1717 | Caedus (POV) | «But he was still the best lightsaber swordsman around-excepting possibly Luke, perhaps the best there ever had been.» | Medium. Self-assessment, but even he excepts Luke. |
| S8 | L1957 | Zekk | «You've outstripped me with the lightsaber ... I doubt any Jedi Knight can stand against you. You need to practice against a Master.» | High. Jaina is the top Knight, below Master. |
| S9 | L2013 | Jaina | «you can't begin to simulate Jacen's abilities.» | Medium. |
| S10 | L2115 | Jag | «you need to be thinking like someone who hunts Jedi. Like me.» | Thematic. Sets up *Invincible*. |
| S11 | L3655 | Syal | «Everyone's afraid of him. Everyone. Nobody talks about him.» | High (reputation). |
| S12 | L3377 | Caedus | «Antilles, you just sent Luke Skywalker into retreat.» | **Low.** Spin; Luke withdrew because of Allana (L3343–3351). |
| S13 | L4317 | Jaina (POV) | phantoms «seemed to be contained, confined to the limits of the bodies they simulated. Some could wield lightsabers» | High (lore). |
| S14 | L3877 | One Sith | Alema's technique «appears in every way to match the lost technique of Darth Vectivus» | High (lore). |
| S15 | L5573 | Caedus (POV) | «Caedus himself felt healthy again, fully healed for the first time since his fight with Luke.» | **High.** Luke's damage lasted weeks. |
| S16 | L5993–5995 | Caedus (POV) | «Jacen could defeat his mother or Ben without trouble; Saba, with difficulty. Saba plus Luke would be impossible odds. One of the Masters had to fall if Caedus was to survive this day.» | **High.** His own threat assessment, made in private. He does not claim he can beat Luke and Saba together, and says nothing about Luke alone. |
| S17 | L6067 | Caedus (POV) | «Luke had found him easily enough-Caedus wasn't hiding.» | High. |
| S18 | L6729 | Luke (POV) | «Jacen was Luke's match, which meant Ben had to cope with three combat droids.» | **Medium–high.** Luke's sober estimate of a fresh, healed Caedus. Allston's framing; this is the strongest pro-Caedus line on Luke in LOTF 5–7. |
| S19 | L1803 | Ben (POV) | «He suspected-he knew, deep down-that it had been Jacen, not Alema Rar, who had killed his mother.» | High (true per *Sacrifice*). |
| S20 | L4991, L5001 | Luke (POV) | Luke believes Alema killed Mara: «Mara's murderer has met justice» | **False in-universe.** Dramatic irony. |
| S21 | L5075–5077 | Luke / Ben | «Other Jedi can do what I do.» / «No, they can't. They can't be Luke Skywalker.» | Character, not power. |
| S22 | L7039 | Jaina | «I'm his twin. I have as much power as he does. ... potentially. But he's had training I haven't.» | **High** as self-assessment: equal potential, behind in training. |
| S23 | L7041 | Jag | «I think Alema was just about my match. Jacen. ... he's far more dangerous.» | High. |
| S24 | L4773 | Alema | «We cannot feel droid intent» | High (lore and weakness). |

The Jedi used gas and dazzle grenades. Katarn was downed by a speeder trick, not swordplay. | Caedus beats a Master plus three Knights while hurt. Strong Master tier. |

### Links to existing research files
- **Caedus vs. Luke:** `lotf-6-inferno-notes.md` S1 and Fight E (Luke beat Caedus; Caedus's POV “Luke had beaten him”). *Fury* S1 (L345) is Allston's softer retelling; S15 (L5573) confirms the injuries lasted weeks. Against that, S18 (L6729) and S16 show that a healed Caedus is near Luke. `caedus-legends-quotes.txt` L379 (“not as powerful as his uncle in a direct contest of strength”) and L307 still hold; S7 (L1717) has Caedus himself except Luke. `luke-legends-quotes.txt` L108, L157.
- **Caedus vs. Mara:** no new fight. Ben's suspicion (S19) and Luke's false belief (S20) keep *Sacrifice* Duel C (`lotf-5-sacrifice-notes.md`) and *Inferno* S7 (Mara “nearly killed him”) as the evidence. `mara-jade-quotes.txt` L124–128.
- **Jaina vs. Caedus:** S8 (top Knight), S22 (equal potential, less training) and Jag's lesson (S10, Fight D) set up *Invincible*. `jaina-solo-quotes.txt` L17, L30, L34. The final line, «Toward her teacher.» (L7067), points to Fett.
- **Kyle Katarn:** `kyle-katarn-quotes.txt`. Fight A is a data point *against* Katarn: he is run through by Caedus in seconds, though by a trick.
- **Krayt / One Sith:** Dician's cell and the Vectivus lore (S14); the sphere flees toward Ziost (L4617–4619). `krayt-legends-quotes.txt` L248 (Caedus and Allana).
- **Vader / Sidious:** the Tebut Force choke (feat 39) is a deliberate Vader echo; Syal compares fear of Caedus to Palpatine (L3665). See `dark-lord-novel-feats.md`, `vader-*` and `sidious-*` files.
- **Previous LOTF books:** `lotf-5-sacrifice-notes.md`, `lotf-6-inferno-notes.md`; the other worker's `lotf-2-bloodlines-notes.md`, `lotf-3-tempest-notes.md`, `lotf-4-exile-notes.md`. Alema's phantom technique matches Lumiya's evil "Mara" phantom (L589–593).

### Quick ladder (this novel only)
- **Top:** Luke ≈ Caedus by Allston's framing (S18), Luke > Caedus by series context (*Inferno*). Both are above all other Jedi.
- **Masters:** Saba (Caedus: beatable only «with difficulty», S16) ≥ Katarn (lost to Caedus in Fight A) ≈ Kyp and Corran.
- **Knights:** Jaina (top, S8) > Zekk and Valin > Mithric and Hu'lya.
- **Alema:** dangerous at range through phantoms; drained in person; killed by Jag.
- **Non-Force:** Jag with prep can kill a weakened dark Jedi (Fight D).

---

## 5. Plot summary relevant to the fights
- **Ch1–Ch4:** Alema's phantom attacks the Solos on Kashyyyk. Caedus heals from the Luke duel. Luke is grieving and passive. The Masters plan a strike, and Katarn leads it.
- **Ch5–Ch9:** Caedus kidnaps Allana from Hapes and forces the Hapan fleet back into the war. GA forces drop asteroids on Commenor.
- **Ch10–Ch14:** Katarn's ambush fails as an attack but plants a tracer. Mithric is killed. Jag teaches Jaina to think like a Jedi-hunter. Corellia offers talks.
- **Ch15–Ch20:** The Solos infiltrate the *Anakin Solo* and steal Lumiya's asteroid coordinates. The talks are a Centerpoint trap. Caedus flees in the Blur with Allana, and Luke breaks off. Centerpoint destroys both task forces.
- **Ch21–Ch28:** The One Sith send Dician. The hunters reach the asteroid. Zekk frees Ship and is touched by the dark side. Jag kills Alema. The One Sith nuke the asteroid. Ben pulls Luke out of his grief.
- **Ch29–Ch38:** The Jedi plan the Allana rescue and Centerpoint's destruction. Infiltration by Tycho's shuttle. Bridge fight. Allana is rescued. Koyan is shot by a GA pilot. Vibro fires Centerpoint at itself because of Seyah's data swap. Caedus kills Tebut. Leia realizes Allana is her granddaughter. Jaina leaves to find her teacher.

---

## Story and prose notes

### SP1. Plot summary, in order
See Section 5 and the raw log. The A-plot (Allana) and B-plot (Alema) converge on the theme of *who can be rescued*: Allana and Luke can, Alema and Jacen cannot.

### SP2. Character profiles
- **Caedus:** a father first in this book. His tenderness with Allana (L3843) and «I love you, Allana.» (L1773) sit beside murders (L1003, L6969). Allston shows his self-image as the best swordsman (L1717) and his rage leaking out (L3251, L6965). The arc runs from «In a day, he would own Centerpoint.» (L5575) to kneeling on the deck (L6859).
- **Luke:** depressed and death-seeking («You want to be dead. At peace. With her. Dead.», L5097). He steps back into duty in Ch28. He keeps his «without hate» vow (L1881, L3283, L6469).
- **Ben:** maturing. His trials are counted as done (L5169), he insists his motive is «Not revenge» (L5353–5355), and he finds the tactical solutions on the bridge.
- **Jaina:** hardening into the "Sword of the Jedi" (L1199–1203). Jag teaches her that she is «training yourself to lose» (L2097). She ends by choosing a "last resort" teacher.
- **Jag:** the Jedi-hunter. Killing Alema ends his clan duty and his last tie to it (L5421).
- **Alema:** the tragic end of the Dark Nest. «Remember.» (L4793).
- **Zekk:** his Shadow Academy past resurfaces (L4383) and is a vulnerability.

### SP3. Themes
- **Fury / anger as weakness:** Caedus's rage breaks his concealment (L3251) and leads him to murder (L6965–6971). The Jedi win by *not* hating (L3283, L6469).
- **Attachment:** Ben's definition (L5107–5109); Caedus's attachment to Allana is what defeats him on the bridge (L6733).
- **Let the enemy do the work:** the operation's catchphrase (L5707, L5887) mirrors Jag's anti-Jedi lessons and Seyah's self-targeting trick.
- **Fathers and children:** Luke/Ben, Caedus/Allana, Wedge/Syal, Han/Leia/Allana.

### SP4. Notable prose passages
- «Mere days before, he had waged the most ferocious, most terrible lightsaber duel of his life.» (L343)
- «Separately or collectively, these Jedi Knights were no match for him.» (L1569)
- «And Luke. ... did not hate.» (L3283)
- «He felt the crack of Alema's vertebrae under his hand as they shattered.» (L4803)
- «You want to be dead. At peace. With her. Dead.» (L5097)
- «Jacen was Luke's match, which meant Ben had to cope with three combat droids.» (L6729)
- «His legs failed him and he sank to the deck plates» (L6859)
- «Toward her teacher.» (L7067)

### SP5. Worldbuilding and lore stated in the text
- **Force phantoms (Vectivus technique):** projected from a dark-side well and anchored on a sleeping mind (L2847–2871). They are confined to the simulated body (L4317), invisible to sonar (L3137), tied to a real person's presence (L4205–4207), and exhausting to maintain (L2853, L4341).
- **Sith meditation sphere:** obeys «the right dark sider» (L4397). Flees to Ziost (L4619).
- **Beskar and cortosis:** Fett's crushgaunts and breastplate (L1133, L1141); a cortosis-laced flight suit (L3771).
- **Centerpoint:** «A round-tipped cylinder 350 kilometers long» (L5741), imprinted on the *Anakin Solo* (L3463–3467). It destroyed itself through redefined target coordinates (L6375–6385, L6889).
- **Ysalamir:** blocks Caedus's senses (L2573–2575).

### SP6. Timeline placement and connections
- About 40–41 ABY, weeks after *Inferno*. It sets up *Revelation*: Jaina goes to Fett, Ben suspects Jacen, Allana is safe on Hapes, and the One Sith head toward Ziost.
- **Author intent:** there is no interview in this edition. Allston's narrative choices (S1, S7, S18) deliberately rebuild Caedus as Luke's near-equal after Denning's *Inferno*. Readers should treat these as one author's framing.

---

## 6. Verdict-relevant takeaways
1. **Caedus vs. Luke:** *Fury* pulls back from *Inferno*'s decisive Luke win. Caedus's POV calls the duel even (S1), and Luke's POV calls a fresh Caedus «Luke's match» (S18). But Caedus himself still excepts Luke (S7), Corran ranks Luke first (S4), the injuries took weeks to heal (S15), and Luke out-senses him in the Blur fight. **Luke > Caedus still holds, at slightly reduced confidence.**
2. **Caedus vs. Mara:** no new fight. Ben's suspicion keeps Mara's death central. Mara evidence remains *Sacrifice* Duel C plus *Inferno* S7.
3. **Jaina vs. Caedus:** at end of *Fury*, Jaina is the top Knight (S8) with equal potential but less training (S22). Jag's Jedi-hunter lessons (S10) and the Alema kill (Fight D) are the blueprint for how she beats Caedus in *Invincible*. Caedus would beat this Jaina, but she leaves to close the gap.
4. **Caedus's peak range:** he beats a Master plus three Knights while hurt (Fight A) and takes Saba out of a fight with one opportunistic shove (feat 34). He fears Luke and Saba together (S16). This is a high Master / Sith Lord tier just below Luke.
5. **Alema's death** is a prep-and-tech kill on a drained opponent. It is not evidence that Jag ≥ a healthy Alema.

## Raw chapter log
### Front matter
- Dramatis personae: «Jacen Solo / Darth Caedus; Sith, joint Chief of State of the Galactic Alliance (human male)» (L51); «Alema Rar; Jedi Knight (Twi'lek female)» (L39). Author: Aaron Allston.
### Ch1 (L83–309) — *Millennium Falcon* above Kashyyyk
- Han & Leia making firebreaks (L129–135). **Alema appears aboard undetected**, Force-pulls Han's blaster: «Her left arm was useless, had been ruined years earlier-but now it was fine.» (L153). [Alema's restored body = an illusion projected via the Sith sphere/One Sith training — check later.]
- Han kills inertial compensator + gravity and stands the *Falcon* on its tail (L163–167).
- Alema walks on the ceiling as if gravity didn't matter (L217, L271); «We are simply ageless and eternal now, as we have always deserved to be.» (L277). Leia's Force push: «Alema merely rocked back on her heels and looked faintly amused.» (L285).
- **Leia vs Alema (L291–309)**: Alema's first swing «curiously clumsy and unpracticed» (L291); Leia blocks a strike «as suitably swift and ferocious as any Jedi's» (L297); Leia fights «purely defensively», blocks two limb strikes and dodges a third (L303); Alema's «strength seemed to» fade (L305); «We will meet later.» Alema vanishes (L309). Leia blocks Waroo's bowcaster bolt — senses «something wrong» (L297). Leia's Force sense detects Waroo twice (L211–213) — hint Alema is disguising as Waroo / illusion.
### Ch2 (L313–) — Caedus on Coruscant; Luke on Endor
- Hapan withdrawal removes «at least twenty percent of our naval strength» (L327).
- **Allston's Caedus POV on the Luke duel (contrasts Denning)**: «Mere days before, he had waged the most ferocious, most terrible lightsaber duel of his life.» (L343); «It had been brutal, with the advantage being held first by Luke, then by Caedus, in what he knew had been brilliant demonstrations of lightsaber technique, of raw power within the Force, of subtle Jedi and Sith skills. For all his pain, Caedus felt a swelling of pride-not just that he had survived that duel, but that he had waged it so well.» (L345) — STATEMENT (Caedus POV, self-flattering; cf. Inferno L4961 “Luke had beaten him”).
  - «At the end, Caedus had lost a position of advantage-Luke had slipped free of the poison-injecting torture vines with which Caedus had been strangling him-when Ben had driven a vibroblade deep into Caedus's back, punching clean through a shoulder blade, nearly reaching his heart.» (L347). «Caedus should have been killed immediately.» (L349). Injuries: «including the vibroblade puncture, a lightsaber-scored kidney, and a fierce scalp wound» (L351).
- Caedus: «I have an operation in motion» to force the Hapans back (L365) [→ kidnap Allana?]. Orders Seyah's arrest/execution (L389). Isolation: «Everyone he had loved now hated him ... except Allana.» (L423); «He would cut down anyone who stood between him and his child. / Anyone.» (L423–425).
- **Luke POV (Endor)**: Jacen «now commanded Master-level abilities in the Force, though he had not been, and never would be, elevated to the rank of Jedi Master.» (L441) — STATEMENT (Luke; Caedus = Master-tier). Luke agonizes over sparing Jacen: «And Luke would be responsible.» (L453). Yoda lore: electrical shocks prevent a Jedi from channeling the Force (L435).
- Luke passive; turns his comlink off without noticing (L469); «I don't know. Why don't you arrange that?» re next action vs Jacen (L489). Memory of Jacen «bloodied and battered almost beyond recognition, crawling away from him» (L495).
### Ch3 (L501–671) — Kashyyyk, Maitell Base
- Jaina repaired the *Falcon*. **Alema left no fingerprints, threads or skin cells** (L557); Leia: «I couldn't even feel her in the Force. Only Lumpy. I mean, Waroo.» (L573). Jaina's theory: Alema is projecting a **phantom**, like Brisha Syo's evil Mara phantom that Ben fought (L589–593), via the Sith sphere (L597). Jag: Brisha Syo was likely «a fiction. An agent of, or an alternate identity of, Lumiya herself.» (L657). Jag: «She's going to use this technique again. And she'll get better with practice.» (L613). Han's plan: reconstruct the asteroid's location from Caedus's shuttle telemetry aboard the *Anakin Solo* (L615–623).
### Ch4 (L675–) — Endor, Jedi Masters' sparring free-for-all
- Masters present: Luke, Kyle Katarn, Cilghal, Kyp, Corran, Saba, Octa Ramis (L683). Kam and Tionne «both still recovering from their near-fatal wounds» (L683) [Allston: Kam alive; contrast Inferno L3345–3355 where Kam appeared killed — continuity note].
- Kyp Force-hurls a wheel at Luke (L685); «This friendly exercise would be horribly dangerous to anyone but a Jedi Master» (L691). **Luke Force-yanks a tree branch down onto Kyp** (L693) — «Tactical superiority is never fair.» (L697).
- Masters' free-for-all (L701–717): Saba dual-wields; Corran's saber «adjusted to its second setting, its blade now three meters in length» (L701); Octa Ramis Force-hurls stones; Luke inverted back-flip block on Cilghal (L701), bends backward and **disarms Cilghal by catching her hilt** (L711), then fights Kyp two-bladed (L715). **Luke vs Force-propelled bolt swarm**: «he calculated which objects had a chance of hitting him and incinerated only them with his blades» (L717) — FEAT (casual, sparring). Cilghal Force-pulls Kyle's saber (L713).
- Kyp's capture-or-neutralize mission plan vs Jacen at the Senate Building via the undercity (L705–721). Kyp: «Horn, you can't throw anybody any distance. With the Force, anyway.» (L735) — Corran's TK weakness.
- **Seha** (Jacen-sponsored apprentice, Temple spy for him) confesses: she helped Ben leave the Temple at Jacen's order (L765–769). Seha: «to torture Kam Solusar and Tionne and kill all those others» (L781).
- **Corran STATEMENT**: «you've left the most dangerous man in the galaxy disappointed in you» (gesturing at Luke) ... «you've volunteered to go on a mission to capture the second-most dangerous man» (L783) — Corran ranks Luke #1, Jacen #2 (partly simulated emotion, L787).
- **Luke STATEMENT**: «Jacen is ... extraordinarily dangerous. If he sees you, he might devote only a single, negligent attack to you. Such an attack would distract a Jedi Master, hurt a Jedi Knight... . and kill you.» (L795).
- Luke feels the Force silent on the bigger picture since Mara's death (L815–819); refuses to lead: «If I lead this mission, I will strike at]acen with hatred.» (L837, sic OCR). Kyle Katarn leads (L839). Luke «had neither heard nor felt him coming» (L823) — Luke's senses dulled by grief.
### Ch5 (L847–) — Caedus infiltrates Hapes disguised as a shuttle engineer
- Disguise: synthskin, whiskers, eye patch (L853–857). Opening himself to the Force «tended to make him easier to detect by Jedi» (L869). Finds Allana «a bright, joyous flare in the Force» (L871); remote viewing through her eyes (L873–875) — FEAT.
- Caedus would kill Tenel Ka if confronted: «If he did, he would probably have to kill her.» (L885). Uses a stun hold-out blaster to avoid Force signature: «stun bolts were far better than lethal attacks, lightsabers, or anything that manifested strongly in the Force.» (L897). Brought a decoy child, "Tika", dressed as Allana (L901–909).
- Tenel Ka senses «a short, distinct pulse in the Force» (L977): «There is a Force-user in the palace.» (L979); the decoy at the modeling table (L981–983). Runs with Force speed (L995).
### Ch6 (L999–) — Allana kidnapped
- Caedus's trail: guardswoman throat slit, courtier neck broken, cook killed (L1003, L1013); cut a meter hole in the floor (L1005). Tenel Ka drops 10 m twice with Force cushioning (L1009, L1015).
- Caedus in Tahiri's StealthX with Allana in his lap (L1029–1033). Tenel Ka's Force-broadcast warning to guards (L1039). **Caedus detonates a shaped charge in the diplomatic shuttle** with a tight smile (L1043–1045); blows a hole in the hangar wall and escapes to orbit (L1061). Tenel Ka knocked out. Diplomats were unwitting diversion (L1067).
- Caedus's ultimatum: all Hapan forces under his command «or your daughter will die» (L1083); «you changed me into someone capable of doing exactly what I have promised» (L1085); eyes «lighter in color than usual» (L1085) [Sith eyes]. Isolder: «a man whose humanity has been utterly extracted from him» (L1095).
### Ch7 (L1101–1207) — Fett's gift
- **Fett sends Han beskar crushgaunts and breastplate** «With deepest sympathy» (L1133). Han: beskar «Legend says armor made from the stuff can take a lightsaber hit and survive» (L1141). Jag takes the armor for the hunt on Alema (L1177–1187).
- Jag on **Jaina as "Sword of the Jedi"**: «She thinks she's going to have to face her brother. And that one of them's not going to come out of it alive.» (L1199); «if you sharpen a sword all the time, even when it's not dull, by the time you need it there's no metal left.» (L1203).
### Ch8 (L1211–) — Allana in the GAG building; Katarn's strike team in the undercity
- Allana doesn't hear «the special words» from Jacen and grows suspicious (L1249, L1263); Jacen's eyes «so bright and intent they almost frightened her» (L1221).
- Strike team: Kyle Katarn (former stormtrooper, L1273), Valin Horn, Thann Mithric (Falleen), Kolir Hu'lya (Bothan), Seha guide (L1273–1275). Katarn takes the position closest to Caedus's approach (L1319).
### Ch9 (L1325–1461) — Commenor asteroid strike
- K-wing pilot Oldathan detects a **cloaked nickel-iron asteroid** «thirty, forty kilometers across» (L1413); the GA force shatters it into dozens of chunks aimed at Commenor (L1443–1445). War-crime drift: rumors of POW mistreatment (L1359–1363). (No Force feats.)
### Ch10 (L1465–) — Katarn ambush on Caedus outside the Senate Building
- HoloNet: Third Fleet dropped asteroids on Commenor population centers; commander acted on his own initiative (L1487–1491). Jedi avoid Force comms because «Exchanges of images or feelings through the Force might be detected by Jacen Solo» (L1481) — Caedus's sensing reputation.

#### ★ DUEL: CAEDUS vs KYLE KATARN + VALIN HORN + THANN MITHRIC + KOLIR HU'LYA — Senate plaza (L1545–1745) ★
Context: Jedi ambush with dazzle grenades and gas; Caedus escorting a crate with Allana inside (YVH-908 carries it, L1527, L1549). Caedus «wasn't yet recovered from his duel with Luke» (L1715). GAG troopers blinded at first, later firing. A YVH droid: Jedi Knight vs YVH «about even» (L1515).
- L1551–1557: Caedus senses falling and rolling grenade cylinders; «Contemptuous, he flicked his hand toward the darkness and felt his own power turn the cylinders around.» (L1557) — FEAT (TK vs multiple Force-propelled objects). Dazzle grenades still blind his troops (L1559–1561).
- **L1569 Caedus POV STATEMENT: «Separately or collectively, these Jedi Knights were no match for him.» / L1571 «Katarn, though, was a threat.»**
- L1573–1577 (first two seconds): blocks Katarn by muscle memory; Force-hurls Hu'lya into Mithric; steps back from Katarn's spin; side kick to Valin's chin knocking him off his feet. «Two seconds had passed since the attack began.» (L1577) — FEAT.
- L1609: «Caedus rolled out of Katarn's kick to his head, catching a scrape along his cheek»; his leg cut at Katarn is deflected by Kolir: «His strength batted her weapon away». Caedus POV: «They're coordinating. Good for them. Bad for me.» (L1611).
- L1619–1623: makes Valin flinch into Mithric; hurls Hu'lya at Katarn; «he negligently swatted it away with his own blade» (Katarn's thrown saber, L1623). L1635: TK-yanks Hu'lya into the path of Mithric's and Valin's Force attack.
- **L1641–1651: Caedus feints a Force push at Katarn, but «exerted himself against the oncom-ing GAG speeder, yanking it down and toward all of them.»** The speeder hits Katarn's back, hurling him onto Caedus's blade: «Katarn looked down to see Caedus's lightsaber buried to its hilt in his chest.» (L1649). «Caedus felt a rush of satisfaction, enjoyment» (L1649) — FEAT (Caedus kills/downs a Jedi Master via TK trick, ~15–20 s in). [Katarn survives: «"You're alive!"» L1693.]
- Seha plants a tracking patch on Caedus's cloak hem (L1591–1599, L1671) — Katarn's real objective. Seha Force-drags Katarn's body away (L1675–1689).
- L1685: Valin deflects a trooper's bolt into Caedus: «The bolt grazed Jacen's right leg, sending him to his knee.» L1711: «Caedus hadn't felt the blaster bolt coming. His concentration was slipping.» L1713: Mithric «was starting to beat down his parries. His strength was slipping.» L1715: «He wasn't yet recovered from his duel with Luke.»
- **L1717 Caedus POV STATEMENT: «But he was still the best lightsaber swordsman around-excepting possibly Luke, perhaps the best there ever had been.»** (self-assessment; note the Luke exception).
- L1719–1723: times a blaster bolt with Mithric's blow, catches both in one move, deflects the bolt into Mithric's chest, then «a single, precise lateral blow. / Mithric's head fell from his shoulders.» — FEAT.
- Valin catches three bolts without looking (L1725). Caedus: «You're a fraction of the man your father is.» Valin: «Funny. That's what I was going to say to you.» (L1733–1735).
- RESULT: Caedus wins (Katarn down, Mithric killed) while wounded; the Jedi achieve their hidden aim (tracker). Caveat: Katarn defeated via a speeder trick, not swordplay; Caedus still recovering from Luke.
- Caedus to Allana: «I love you, Allana.» (L1773); she still waits for «the special words» (L1771).
- Endor: **Ben (narration, his POV)**: «He suspected-he knew, deep down-that it had been Jacen, not Alema Rar, who had killed his mother.» (L1803). Ben on Vader's isolation = what Jacen did to him (L1819–1823). **Luke: «Ben, I would kill Jacen.»** (L1861) — choosing Valin over Jacen; admits special treatment of Ben (L1865). Luke's condition: «you take your shot only if you can do it without hate» (L1881).
- Kyp: «The mission group reports partial success» (L1905); Katarn badly injured, Mithric dead; **the ambush was designed to fail; the tracer was the real goal** (L1913–1921). Luke: «I was certain Kyle would be able to escape-or die before being broken.» (L1921). Luke calls in Wedge, Booster, Karrde (L1927); Kyp: «Welcome back.» (L1929).
### Ch12 (L1935–) — Jaina's training, Maitell Base
- **Zekk STATEMENT: «You've outstripped me with the lightsaber ... I doubt any Jedi Knight can stand against you. You need to practice against a Master.»** (L1957) — Jaina top-Knight tier.
- Jaina Force-pulls Zekk's saber, somersaults over him using his pull, kicks his knee (L1963–1965); Zekk: «Jaina, you're playing too rough. You're a danger to yourself and others.» (L1979).
- Jag arrives in Vader-like GAG armor (the beskar gift) to spar (L1987–1999). Jaina: «in hand-to-hand combat, you're nowhere near my equal. And you can't begin to simulate Jacen's abilities.» (L2013); «He's studied Alema Rar for years. He knows what she's capable of. I'm worse.» (L2019).
- **Jaina vs Jag (training sabers; Jag in beskar crushgaunts and breastplate, L2023–2121)**: Jaina wins bouts 1–2 easily («Almost faster than the eye could follow, Jaina lunged.» L2035). Bout 3: Jag takes the blade on his beskar forearm, **crushes Jaina's practice saber hilt** (L2065–2069), and wins with a neck strike (L2075). Jaina Force-throws Jag five meters into a tree in anger (L2079–2081). Jag: «you're training yourself to lose. To die.» (L2097); «You've been sharpening yourself into an amazing sword, but you've forgotten what it means to be a Jedi.» (L2107); «you need to be thinking like someone who hunts Jedi. Like me.» (L2115) — **this is the seed of Jaina's later Mandalorian training and her anti-Jedi tactics vs Caedus** (Invincible). Zekk: Jag is «more Force-blind than a rock, you handle a lightsaber like a drunken Hutt» (L2165).
### Ch13 (L2189–) — war montage
- Caedus keeps Allana secretly with him guarded by YVH-908 (L2197). **«his Sith battle meditation ability helped his forces inflict heavy losses in both theaters of operation»** (Kuat, Corellian outskirts) (L2207) — FEAT (summary).
- Commenor retaliates with affliceria bioweapon on Coruscant (L2209–2213). Caedus now despises Anakin (L2205). Considers **erasing Allana's memories of Tenel Ka** (L2219). Tahiri can't find the Jedi (L2203).
- Lando's wife Tendra is pregnant (L2263).
- Caedus thinks of the *Anakin Solo* as home; plans a bigger flagship (L2315–2319). Captain Nevil (Quarren) commands it (L2325).
### Ch14 (L2337–) — Corellian negotiation offer; Katarn's wound; Wedge
- Koyan offers secret talks with Caedus at a midpoint (L2341–2349). Caedus suspects a trap but accepts; his caution is about Allana (L2371).
- **Cilghal on Katarn's wound from Caedus: «The attack severed two ribs, penetrated his left lung, and exited through his left shoulder blade. A few centimeters off, and it would have gone clean through his heart.»** (L2397) — confirms the Senate-plaza result.
- Valin's report flags the YVH «programmed to get its cargo out of harm's way rather than help its master defend against four Jedi» (L2403) — clue about Allana.
- Wedge arrives; intel on the Corellian meeting (L2429–2449); Luke sends a StealthX to shadow the *Anakin Solo* (L2451). Wedge: the Jedi are «setting up a third government here» (L2457–2461).
- Caedus is building a **red-bladed lightsaber** to announce himself as «Lord of the Sith» (L2477).
- Leia boards the *Anakin Solo* from Lando's yacht to "talk" (L2489–2515); Caedus senses only her aboard (L2509–2511) and plans to imprison her and Han.
### Ch15 (L2521–) — infiltration via the *Love Commander*
- **Leia Force-presses Caedus's door button down against his intent**: «She glanced at the button, and it clicked down without Caedus's help.» (L2543) — minor FEAT (Leia acts in front of him). Jacen on legitimacy; Leia: «Clearly, you got your intelligence and your political acumen from your grandfather.» He: «There we are in agreement.» (L2547).
- **An ysalamir hides Jaina, Han, Zekk and Jag from Caedus's senses** (L2573–2575) — lore: «making everything inside its border un-detectable by Force-sensitives outside its range».
- Holocam loop package by Iella Antilles (L2601–2603). Jaina's kick «breaking ribs despite his chest armor» (L2609). Zekk in GAG armor heads to the tractor beams with «very specialized holocomm gear» (L2629). Jaina into Caedus's shuttle (L2627) for the Lumiya-asteroid data.
- Negotiation site: Corellian ships are old (Victory-class *Valorum*, L2659–2665); Caedus's Second Fleet ambush force under Admiral Limpan on *Blue Diver*, jumps in if holocomm contact breaks for 15 s (L2689). Caedus: «I've already attained the highest rank you ever did, and I'm not done yet.» (L2669).
### Ch16 (L2715–) — the Centerpoint trap
- Leia detects the Corellian transmission has no bridge noise — the *Valorum* is automated/skeleton crew (L2755–2759, L2789). Caedus issues a 10-minute ultimatum (L2797).
- Corellians at **Centerpoint Station** prepare to fire on the *Anakin Solo* (L2811–2835, L2953–2957); «Anakin Solo imprinting lockout bypasses are holding» (L2953). Admiral Delpin authorized to fire at will (L2957).
- **Alema's Force projection — lore**: she sits in Lumiya's asteroid habitat (system MZX32905 near Bimmiel) «in her old body, the crippled one» (L2847); draws on «the immense pool of dark power waiting hundreds of meters below her, in the asteroid proper» (L2855); projects herself across the galaxy; «Her last projection to Kashyyyk had tired her immensely. She had slept for days afterward.» (L2853). She needs an anchor presence: too-bright presences are «too strong for her to merge with» (L2863). **She anchors on a sleeping child — Allana — aboard the *Anakin Solo*** (L2865–2871) and goes hunting for Han. Alema plans to exterminate the Korriban Sith (L2851). Allana: «I dreamed there was a lady here.» (L2923).
- Caedus finds the guard knocked out, Leia gone (L2889); doors open for her, holocams subverted, vent command fails (L2931–2935) — the Solos have hacked his ship. Caedus fears a bomb (his own Hapes trick) and takes Allana out (L2939–2943).
### Ch17 (L2963–3109) — Caedus flees in the TIE "Blur" with Allana
- Caedus takes the prototype TIE Reconnaissance Fighter "Blur" (L2987–2995), Allana in his lap. «He wouldn't need to employ his Sith battle meditation technique to turn this into a gruesome victory» (L3057). Han's old warning about overconfidence (L3077). **Allana feels pilots dying through the Force** — «Somebody went away.» (L3083–3089); Caedus: «Inured as he was to death in combat» (L3089).
- The Solos escape in the *Love Commander* hugging the Star Destroyer's hull (L3105).
### Ch18 (L3113–) — Alema's projection aboard the yacht; Luke's Red Sword Flight
- **Alema projection vs Zekk, Leia, Jag**: lunges «with speed surpassing that which her Jedi training should have allowed her» (L3127). Leia fights her only defensively, like «a stage performer» (L3133). **Jag's sensor helmet**: on infrared Alema is «a homogeneous color» (L3135); **«And Alema was nowhere on that image.»** (sonar, L3137) — the projection is detectable by tech. Leia: «If you just want some more sword training, Alema, come back to the Order. Luke will whistle you up a youngling to practice against.» (L3157). The projection ripples like a sail and vanishes (L3161–3163). Lesson: the projection can only hurt those who fight back/accept it [check later].
- Red Sword Flight: Luke, Kyp, Corran, Tyria Tainer, Twool (sensor StealthX tracking Caedus's tracer), Sanola Ti (L3177–3185).

#### ★ SPACE FIGHT: LUKE (+ Kyp, Corran, three Knights) vs CAEDUS in the TIE Blur, with Allana aboard (L3187–3377) ★
- Luke locates Jacen via Twool's tracer sensors; initiates the battle-meld at the moment of firing: «not bothering to work with targeting computers, blips, and brackets, he swung his snubfighter's nose a trifle to starboard, located his target by feel, and fired» (L3215) — FEAT (Luke Force-aimed first-shot hit on a stealth fighter).
- Caedus feels the meld, flees; Blur hit from behind (L3223–3229). Caedus «made himself smaller in the Force, reducing his presence to nothingness» (L3241); the Jedi still track him — he believes via Allana's presence (L3247–3249).
- **Caedus's rage overwhelms his concealment**: «So great it was that he could no longer contain his presence in the Force-his anger flowed through him, through Allana, through his pursuers, through everything in tune with him or the Force.» (L3251). Leia far away is hit: «Redness and heat swamped her» (L3261); «I've never felt him like this.» (L3265).
- **Luke POV: «It was so strong it felt like a kick in the gut, and he wondered for an instant if Jacen had perfected some new Force attack.»** (L3279) — but reads it as «frustration, helplessness, even fear ... like a man in his last seconds of life» (L3281). **«And Luke. ... did not hate.»** (L3283) — Luke keeps his promise (cf. L1881). Caedus survives by «brilliant evasive flying» (L3283) — FEAT (Caedus pilot); Kyp and Corran herd him (L3301–3303). Caedus: «To comfort her now would be to die.» (L3307); «lost in his rage» (L3311).
- **Luke breaks off when he senses Allana**: «There was, in fact, a little girl in the cockpit with Jacen.» (L3343); «Kill Jacen. ... kill an innocent.» (L3347). He withdraws the Jedi (L3349–3351). Syal Antilles's Aleph had grazed him (L3345).
- Caedus: «Antilles, you just sent Luke Skywalker into retreat.» (L3377) — Caedus's spin; real cause = Allana. RESULT: no result; Luke withdraws by choice.
- **Centerpoint fires** (L3379–3393): a kilometers-wide twisting beam erases both task forces (GA and Corellian); Allana faints from the death-shock (L3391). Luke feels it (L3401). Leia compares it to Alderaan (L3419). The Corellians sacrificed their own old ships as bait (L3317).
### Ch20 (L3439–3587) — aftermath of the Centerpoint shot
- Centerpoint overloaded; it was imprinted on Anakin Solo (the droid), so «only he could fire it» — bypassing it «punished us» (L3463–3467). Days to weeks before another shot (L3477).
- Caedus: the attempt «was a tribute» (L3515); he will make supporters of Corellia «pay» because they nearly killed Allana (L3517). Can't truly feel sympathy for Nevil's dead son: «Turl Nevil was a nobody» (L3533). Blue Diver and Admiral Limpan lost (L3549). Caedus's cover story to Niathal (L3555). **Plan: seize Centerpoint and «point it at anyone we choose»** (L3575); «You and I, we are that will.» (L3581).
### Ch21 (L3591–) — Syal and Tycho
- Syal lost her fiancé Tiom Rordan at Centerpoint (L3621). On Caedus: «Everyone's afraid of him. Everyone. Nobody talks about him.» (L3655) — Tycho compares Palpatine (L3665). Tycho (Niathal's analyst) orders Syal to contact Wedge (L3673–3681) — Niathal's side moving against Caedus.
- Phennir (Confederation) vs Koyan over unilateral use of Centerpoint (L3703–3725); *Anakin Solo* survived because it had left the engagement zone (L3711).
### Ch22 (L3733–) — Jag's anti-Alema kit; Allana; One Sith; Tenel Ka on Endor
- **Jag's Jedi-hunter kit**: beskar helmet/breastplate/crushgaunts, thruster pack, custom oversized blaster with a secret feature (L3763–3769), flight suit «Laced with cortosis alloy ... a graze from a lightsaber could result in minor or no damage instead of an amputation» (L3771); «It doesn't make me a Jedi. ... but we don't need another Jedi. We need something she can't predict.» (L3779). Karrde's data gives probable coordinates of Brisha Syo's (Lumiya's) habitat (L3785).
- **Caedus tells Allana he is her father** (L3839–3847): «for the first time in months, perhaps years, there was nothing of Sith training to his thoughts ... There was only what he felt.» (L3843). Allana: «Daddy.» (L3845). Allana had threatened him with her injector pen (L3805, L3827).
- **One Sith conclave on Korriban** (L3855–3893): Dician (tattooed, poses as a Confederation intel operative, L3875); white-eyed leader. **Lore: Alema's phantom projection «appears in every way to match the lost technique of Darth Vectivus»** (L3877); «Vectivus's history is clear. He was a fraud» (L3879). They gave Alema an «ersatz Sith Holocron» that tracked her (L3865). Orders: take the warship *Poison Moon*, destroy the dark-side energy source powering the technique, capture or kill Alema (L3891).
- Tenel Ka on Endor with Luke (L3903–3931).
- Tenel Ka tells Luke of Allana's kidnapping; she can feel Allana moving with the *Anakin Solo* (L3963). Luke realizes the girl in the Blur was Allana (L3965). **The Jedi Order offers to rescue Allana** (L3973–3975); Luke: «Rescuing Allana could end this war» (L3973). Tenel Ka: «I don't see visions of the future anymore.» (L3955).
### Ch23 (L3979–) — Lumiya's asteroid near Bimmiel
- Han/Leia in the *Falcon*, Jag in X-wing, Jaina & Zekk in StealthXs locate the asteroid (L3985–3995). Alema senses being hunted: «this was the first time she had ever been hunted in a place where she made the rules-all the rules.» (L4023). The dark power «like a racing thruster engine ... tended to drown out all lesser noises» (L4017).
- Dician's frigate *Poison Moon* approaches stealthily with asteroid-buster bombs (L4031–4053).
- Jag flies into the habitat with his thruster pack through vacuum (L4077–4087); finds two improvised bombs (L4109). Zekk traces the Sith ship's trail down into the asteroid (L4115–4117). «Let's end this.» (L4125).
- Dician (One Sith) plans to kill Han and Leia: «Leia Organa Solo, Jedi and traitor to the noble Sith name of Skywalker» (L4143). Bridge drill: «Perfect.» (L4145–4149).
- **Alema's phantoms are linked to real people**: a mynock on the *Falcon*'s viewport is a phantom «And it's you» (Han) — «Burn that and you burn yourself.» (L4205–4207); a flock of 20+ mynock phantoms in the caverns, the lead one is Jag (L4253–4255). Lore/statement: phantoms «seemed to be contained, confined to the limits of the bodies they simulated. Some could wield lightsabers» (L4317). Alema also brushes the *Poison Moon* (L4231). *Poison Moon* attacks the *Falcon* and launches raiding shuttles (L4285–4289).
- Booby-trap protocol droids with rail clubs; Zekk's ribs cracked; Zekk Force-throws the droid away (L4303–4313).
- Jag blocks mynock tails on his beskar (L4325) and is towed away (L4331).
- (Ch24 heading not found in OCR; the section from about L4339 is Alema's POV.) Alema sits at the bottom near **Darth Vectivus's private cavern and mansion** (L4339); «already panting, tired by the effort of maintaining so many phantoms at once» (L4341).
### Ch25 (L4347–) — the Sith sphere attacks
- Zekk Force-pushes Jaina out of the path of the sphere's shot (L4353–4355); **the Sith meditation sphere (Alema's ship) fires from the dark** and severs the track twice (L4355, L4363). Zekk reads its self-image through the Force (L4357). Zekk stays to distract it: «By dying, probably.» (L4371). He taunts the ship with contempt through the Force (L4377); it grasps at him (L4379).
- **Zekk frees the Sith sphere from Alema by opening himself to the dark side** (Shadow Academy techniques, L4383–4387): «Into his words he poured his own power of will, and felt it joined, strengthened by the power of this dark place.» (L4405); erodes Alema's «older orders» (L4407–4409) — FEAT (Zekk out-wills Alema's hold on Ship with dark-side help; cf. Alema overpowering Ship in Inferno L1399–1411). Ship flees (L4409). **Zekk then falls under the dark side**: plans to kill Alema, then Jag, then bind Jaina through their link (L4413–4417); «it was strong, so very strong, and it laughed at his pathetic efforts.» (L4423).
- Jaina spots the phantom-"Jag" by Force-sensing (Jag's own "assumption" lesson, L4443–4447): the phantom becomes Alema and slashes with a blue-black lightsaber, only cutting her robe (L4451–4453). «Predictable, Alema, predictable.» (L4455). Jaina: «I'm prepared to die. Are you?» (L4467).
### Ch26 (L4471–) — Leia vs phantom Alema; Ship flees; Poison Moon
- **Leia vs Alema phantom on the *Falcon***: Alema «all fourth-form technique» (Form IV, Ataru, L4479); Leia defends economically in the doorway (L4481), uses a Force link to Han to anticipate his maneuvers so Alema is thrown off balance (L4483) — FEAT; after «a dozen failed blows» Alema's smile fades (L4485); Alema tires, «sledgehammerlike blows had grown weaker» (L4535). Leia: «Across forty years now, the same speech.» (L4489).
- The dark energy is «Eating Zekk» (L4503); Jaina loses his presence (L4507). Ship leaves; Alema vanishes from both fights (L4525, L4537). Jag hits a wall; left leg cracked, blacks out (L4565).
- Alema back in her crippled body, «already tired. More than tired.» (L4581); hides «small in the Force» (L4583) and rides the railcar up.
- Dician lets the Falcon go and chases the empty sphere; it heads for **Ziost** (L4617–4623); Dician triggers asteroid-buster bombs (L4627). An explosion vents the asteroid's atmosphere (L4657).
### Ch27 (L4635–) — Jaina vs Alema on the track
- Jaina cuts a gap in the rails to wreck the railcar (L4667–4669); Alema thrown out, catches a cross-tie (L4671). Jaina: «how are you going to climb the track and swing a lightsaber with only one working arm?» (L4677).

#### ★ DUEL: ALEMA vs JAINA, then JAG (asteroid caverns, low gravity, near-vacuum) (L4681–4803) ★
- Alema refuses surrender: «With the universe still out of balance? With the wicked not punished? We think not.» (L4683).
- **Alema's dark-side Force blow on Jaina**: «Energy-invisible, reeking of the dark side-slammed into Jaina, forcing her back. The track she held on to bent several meters above her head» (L4695); «The blow drove the wind from her lungs» (L4697) — FEAT (Alema; Jaina caught off guard). Alema leaps 20 m past her and climbs the track «using only her feet and the Force» (L4699).
- Jag's blaster bolt from above knocks Alema 50 m down (L4701). Jag sends Jaina up to save Zekk (L4715–4725): «Who dies first? Go on.» (L4719).
- Alema Force-tosses Jag's explosive pouch off the track before he can detonate it (L4737). **Alema tears a 4 m section of track free and swings it like a club at Jag** twice (L4741–4749); the beskar plate holds; Jag's leg «probably already broken at the thigh» (L4747) — FEAT (Alema TK).
- Alema Force-pulls Jag's blaster mid-draw without looking (L4767). **Jag's trick blaster has a distance-triggered explosive** (the secret feature from L3763) and explodes in her hand: «Her right arm was gone from just below the elbow.» (L4785). **Jag snaps Alema's neck with his crushgaunt**: «He felt the crack of Alema's vertebrae under his hand as they shattered.» (L4803). Her last word: «Remember.» (L4793).
- RESULT: **Alema Rar killed by Jag Fel**, a non-Force-user using anti-Jedi tactics (the theme he taught Jaina in Ch12). Alema was badly drained by maintaining phantoms and by losing Ship (L4581). Alema on her own weakness: «Droids firing lasers-now, that would have been intelligent and dangerous to us.» / «We cannot feel droid intent» (L4771–4773).
- Zekk dark-side-sick: «It's in me. The evil of this place.» (L4823); Jaina carries him up. A space mine (fission) rolls down the track (L4843–4849). The habitat is blown loose; Jaina uses a shadow bomb to blow open the hangar wall (L4889, L4909). The asteroid is destroyed by fission bombs (L4929–4931). Jag survives and reaches his X-wing (L4941–4945).
### Ch28 (L4923–) — Endor report
- Leia: «Luke, Alema Rar is dead.» (L4965).
- Luke believes Mara's killer has met justice (L5001); senses Ben's doubt (L5005). Luke on Alema: «unlike Jacen, Alema Rar had insanity to blame» (L4991).
- **Luke's death-wish and recovery (L5019–5173)**: Luke's sensing — he feels the outpost «as though it were a skin» down to the moon's core (L5021); but «the Force still offered him no guidance» (L5027). Luke: «Other Jedi can do what I do.» Ben: «No, they can't. They can't be Luke Skywalker.» (L5075–5077). Luke plans to groom «the next Grand Master» (L5079). **Ben: «You want to be dead. At peace. With her. Dead.»** (L5097); attachment defined (L5107–5109). Luke steps out from under his grief and takes up duty again (L5133–5143) — turning point (Luke "back"). **Luke tells Ben to build his lightsaber** — trials counted as passed (L5165–5169).
### Ch29 (L5177–) — Endor; Tycho, Syal, Seyah
- Tycho and Syal arrive; Syal reunites with Wedge (L5209). Tycho brings Dr. Seyah (gravitic physicist, Centerpoint expert) for safekeeping (L5223–5243).
- Planning session under the Death Star hull (L5253–5381): Katarn up and «moving well» (L5253). Luke refuses Niathal's request to return the Order to the GA under Jacen (L5271); «We retain trust in Admiral Niathal.» (L5273). **Plan**: rescue Allana; **destroy Centerpoint** when Jacen moves to capture it (L5277–5291). Zekk had planted a full-power holocomm beacon on the *Anakin Solo* (L5305–5307). Infiltrate via Tycho's shuttle. **Luke leads a Jedi unit to assault Jacen** — «to take him out if possible, and to distract him from the rescue operation in any case» (L5345); Han & Leia lead the Allana rescue; Ben insists on coming as a diversion, «Not revenge? / Yes, Grand Master.» (L5351–5355). Syal named GA envoy to the Jedi (L5371).
### Ch30 (L5385–) — Jag recovering; Teppler's treason
- Jag's injuries: neck, left elbow fracture, multiple breaks in left thigh, internal injuries (L5393). Killing Alema severed his last clan tie (L5421). Jaina: «Yes. I am.» (his friend) (L5453).
- Teppler secretly contacts Phennir (L5483–5495).
- Phennir urges Teppler to remove Koyan (L5533–5537); Teppler considers a joint ruler arrangement with Admiral Delpin (L5553–5561).
### Ch31 (L5565–) — Caedus healed; mission launch
- Caedus POV: Allana accepted him «instantly, with boundless affection-as her father» (L5573); **«Caedus himself felt healthy again, fully healed for the first time since his fight with Luke.»** (L5573) — STATEMENT (narrator via Caedus POV, reliable): the Luke duel's injuries lasted weeks. Overconfidence: «In a day, he would own Centerpoint.» (L5575).
- **Lt. Tebut finds the tracer patch on Caedus's cloak** (L5591–5603) — Caedus never sensed it (a mundane device evades his Force senses; compare Alema L4773 on droid intent).
- Errant Venture staging (L5621–5671). Jag flies the *Falcon* (L5629–5633). Assault team on the *Reveille*: Luke, Leia, Han, Ben, Saba, Iella, R2 (L5659–5665). Rakehell Squadron under Wedge: Sanola Ti, Tycho, Syal, Corran, Tyria Tainer, Jaina, Zekk, Wes Janson and others (L5681–5691). Kyp's Jedi disguised as GAG troops on the *Broadside*, with Seyah (L5701–5707).
- **Caedus's Sith battle meditation** precog: «from them, probabilities and eventualities began streaming, glimpses of possible futures» (L5723); held through a hyperspace jump (L5725) — FEAT (Caedus, fleet-scale).
- Centerpoint: «A round-tipped cylinder 350 kilometers long» (L5741).
### Ch32 (L5763–) — the attack on Centerpoint; the infiltration
- Caedus's battle meditation: «He lent a touch of urgency to the Panther Star's commander, subtly pushing the Sullustan» (L5771); he senses the Rakehells only through unaligned «streamers of possibility» (L5775) and misreads them as cowards.
- Teppler sounds out Delpin (L5785–5797). Centerpoint is operational and may now be able to eliminate «an entire planet or star»; Koyan is aboard (L5811).
- Rakehells chase the *Reveille* into the *Anakin Solo* (L5827–5909). **Tycho does his duty and warns the ship** (L5915–5919); a nervous guard shoots him in the chest (L5925). Smoke pours from the *Reveille* (L5921). (OCR heading «Chaptaer 33» at L5929.)
### Ch33 (L5929–) — Plan Bastion
- Teppler tells Phennir: «I suspect that Koyan will not remain ... obstinate much longer.» (L5953).
- Caedus senses «Luke. Ben. Saba Sebatyne. / His mother.» (L5973–5975); his battle meditation collapses (L5977). Plan Bastion: eight YVH droids on the bridge (L5991).
- **Caedus's self-assessment (STATEMENT, Caedus POV, fairly reliable but self-serving)**: «Caedus didn't think these measures would stop the Jedi.» (L5993); **«Jacen could defeat his mother or Ben without trouble; Saba, with difficulty. Saba plus Luke would be impossible odds. One of the Masters had to fall if Caedus was to survive this day.»** (L5995) — he ranks Saba near his level and Luke + Saba as far beyond him.
- The Jedi break out of the smoke: «Moving so fast that they blurred» (L6003); six security troops down in an instant (L6005).
- Caedus watches Luke, Ben and Saba cut through hardpoints (L6065). He notes that «Luke had found him easily enough-Caedus wasn't hiding» and that Leia's presence slid past him (L6067). Leia is in fact hiding/on the rescue.
- Koyan considers destroying Coruscant (L6083); the tech Vibro urges it (L6089). Teppler lures Koyan to a shuttle and gets joint firing authority transferred to himself and Delpin (L6113–6131).
- Rogue Squadron (Alliance) attacks the Rakehells; **Syal kills a Rogue to protect Wedge** (L6159–6169).
### Ch34 (L6179–) — Centerpoint boarding; YVH droids
- The *Broadside* fake GAG team (Kyp, Seyah) boards Centerpoint under fire (L6183–6201).
- Ben cuts through a blast door; **Saba telekinetically flips the cut plug over a grenade and holds it down** (L6215–6217). Ben's verdict: «He was fast, but not as fast as Saba Sebatyne.» (L6215) — FEAT (Saba reflex + TK).
- **YVH droids with blaster cannons**: «Saba, her sword work brilliant, was parrying full-autofire streams of blaster cannon fire. Luke, more mobile, was avoiding the fire» but «was being herded back toward the blast doors» (L6237) — Luke is pressed by four YVHs; not a top-tier showing. Ben deflects at an angle rather than blocking (L6241–6245). **Ben Force-wrenches a YVH's cannon arm to shoot another droid** (L6255–6257) — FEAT (Ben TK vs a heavy laminanium droid). **Luke makes a droid's minirocket explode in its chest port** (L6261) — FEAT (Luke TK precision).
- **Saba vs YVH**: she walks up the cannon stream, catches its lightning arc on her blade, rolls under and beheads it through laminanium (L6265) — FEAT (Saba). **Luke Force-topples his YVH and stabs it** (L6269). Saba: «Good tacticz» (L6271).
- Koyan flees to a shuttle that turns out to be Alliance-crewed; a Chev pilot shoots him (L6301–6309): «Kork. Forgot to set it to stun.» (L6309).
- Wedge kills Rogue Leader Lensi (L6329). The Rakehells lose Twool (Six), and Cheriss (Eight) is «dead or extravehicular» (L6329).
### Ch35 (L6343–) — Seyah's sabotage; the bridge standoff
- Seyah's three «master plan»s (L6353–6399): the spin reversal (fails, L6569); **redefining every star's coordinates as Hollowtown's centre** so the weapon targets itself (L6375–6385); overloading Glowpoint.
- Leia, Han and Iella crawl through the ducts; Leia tracks Allana's «bright, pure presence» and avoids touching Jacen (L6421–6427).
- **Bridge confrontation**: Caedus «was fresh. He had eight YVH droids.» (L6453). Caedus: «Uncle Luke. Ben. Master Sabatyne. Care for some caf?» (L6461). Luke: «Care to surrender?» (L6465). Caedus's taunt about Ben draws «not one flicker of anger from either of them» (L6469). «All right. Kill.» (L6471).
- The Commenor reinforcements arrive (L6497). **Vibro**, a secret widower who hates Coruscant, targets Coruscant and powers the weapon (L6517–6539). Kyp and Seyah hear the power-up and abandon the third plan (L6565–6575).
### Ch36 (L6583–) — bridge fight; the Allana rescue
- Leia finds **Caedus's unfinished lightsaber** in the workshop: «someone was building a lightsaber. It was almost done» (L6593). This is the red saber.

#### ★ BRIDGE FIGHT: CAEDUS + 8 YVH droids vs LUKE, SABA, BEN (L6619–6755) ★
- Caedus «waited, patient» while the Jedi absorb fire (L6619). **Ben plants plundered YVH grenades on droids** (L6621–6627); Luke and Saba kick two droids into the pits (L6627).
- **Caedus Force-shoves Saba into the blast pit**: «He gestured, exerting himself telekinetically, and Saba Sebatyne slipped laterally into the starboard pit, almost atop the doomed droid there.» (L6629). The blast slams her into the wall «five meters above the floor» and she slides down «flaming» (L6631). Caedus: «One down.» (L6633) — FEAT (Caedus TK on a Master, in a chaotic moment while she is busy with fire).
- Saba stands again, charred, and deflects bolts (L6711) — durability FEAT. Luke orders her out (L6717–6721).
- Luke's feint: Caedus guards against an attack while Luke **hurls a YVH droid through the weakened viewport** (L6723–6725).
- **STATEMENT (Luke POV)**: «Three YVH droids were left. And Jacen. Against Luke and Ben. Jacen was Luke's match, which meant Ben had to cope with three combat droids.» (L6729). Allston has Luke rate Caedus as his equal (contrast Denning's Inferno; see Fury L1717).
- Caedus senses Allana's joy and abandons the fight (L6731–6735). Luke covers Ben's exit as «the nearly impenetrable blaster shield» (L6745); Ben welds the doors (L6753).
- **Leia + Han vs a YVH** (L6643–6665): Leia deflects «the first three or five thousand bolts» (Han's hyperbole, L6645); Han shoots into its barrel (L6655); Leia ducks a swing and drives her blade up into its skull (L6663–6665) — FEAT (Leia/Han).
- **Allana rescued** (L6679–6703); Tenel Ka's holo with the code phrase «bantha excess glow rod» (L6689).
### Ch37 (L6759–) — Caedus loses Allana
- Caedus chases them; Jag flies the *Falcon* to the air lock (L6785–6809); Katarn: «Two-Injured-Men-and-a-Droid Shuttle Service» (L6831). Wedge: «That boy can fly» / «another Jagged Antilles» (L6811).
- Caedus «With the Force lending him speed» (L6849) arrives too late. «Do not fire on the Millennium Falcon. Anyone who fires on her dies.» (L6853). The *Falcon* jumps; «His legs failed him and he sank to the deck plates» (L6857–6859).
- **Centerpoint destroyed**: Vibro fires on «Coruscant», but Seyah's data swap makes the station target itself (L6883–6893): «a perfect sphere of light perhaps five hundred kilometers in diameter» (L6889). Katarn: «A cessation in the Force.» (L6899). The *Anakin Solo* is unharmed (L6895).
- **Caedus kills Tebut**: he Force-lifts and chokes her (L6957–6959), throws Captain Nevil across the bridge with his free hand (L6961), and «The sound of Tebut's neck breaking was startlingly loud» (L6969). «His outrage turned everything he saw a reddish hue» (L6965) — characterization (red vision/rage; Vader-echo Force choke). Caedus is dazed and grieving: «Tell Admiral Niathal there's been a problem.» (L6931).
### Ch38 (L6979–7067) — Gyndine; Endor
- **Leia recognizes Allana as Jacen's daughter** (L6993); Allana: «He's my daddy.» (L7003); «I'm your grandmother.» (L7007); Han: «I'm your grandfather.» (L7011).
- **Jaina's self-assessment (STATEMENT, Jaina, reliable re: potential)**: «I'm his twin. I have as much power as he does. ... potentially. But he's had training I haven't. I need to counter it with training he hasn't had.» (L7039). **Jag (STATEMENT)**: «I think Alema was just about my match. Jacen. ... he's far more dangerous.» (L7041).
- Final line: Jaina leaves «Toward an act of last resort. / Toward her teacher.» (L7065–7067), setting up her training with Boba Fett in *Revelation*.

## Character analyses (added 2026-10-17 under the 7+ characters rule)

*Method:* built from the verified log above (Sections 2–4 and the raw chapter log). `L####` = line in `369da51a…txt` (chapter given). Quotes in «…» are verbatim and were re-checked by script. No wiki material is used. **Before:** 7 short story profiles (SP2), none with feats, fights and scaling. **Added:** 13 analyses.

### 1. Darth Caedus
- **Role/arc:** a father first. He kidnaps Allana, survives the Katarn ambush, loses Allana and Centerpoint, and kills Tebut. «I love you, Allana.» (L1773).
- **Key feats:** turns back rolling grenades with a contemptuous flick (Ch10, L1557). Downs three Jedi: «Two seconds had passed since the attack began.» (L1577). Yanks a speeder into Katarn's back so «Katarn looked down to see Caedus's lightsaber buried to its hilt in his chest.» (L1649). Deflects a bolt into Mithric, then «Mithric's head fell from his shoulders.» (L1719–1723). Rage leaks through the Force; Luke: «It was so strong it felt like a kick in the gut» (Ch19, L3279). TK-slips Saba into a grenade pit (Ch36, L6629–6633). Chokes Tebut: «The sound of Tebut's neck breaking was startlingly loud» (L6957–6969).
- **Statements:** Corran calls him «the second-most dangerous man» after Luke (L783). His own POV: «But he was still the best lightsaber swordsman around-excepting possibly Luke» (L1717). His threat assessment: «Jacen could defeat his mother or Ben without trouble; Saba, with difficulty. Saba plus Luke would be impossible odds.» (L5993–5995). Luke's POV: «Jacen was Luke's match» (L6729). Healing: «fully healed for the first time since his fight with Luke.» (L5573).
- **Fights/results:** beats Katarn, Valin, Mithric and Hu'lya while still hurt (L1715). Escapes Luke's flight in the Blur (Luke breaks off for Allana). The bridge fight has no result.
- **Scaling:** strong Master tier, above every Jedi except Luke. Allston frames a healed Caedus as Luke's match, which is the strongest pro-Caedus line in LotF 5–7.

### 2. Luke Skywalker
- **Role/arc:** depressed and death-seeking («You want to be dead. At peace. With her. Dead.», L5097). Ben shakes him back into duty in Ch28.
- **Key feats:** in the Masters' free-for-all «he calculated which objects had a chance of hitting him and incinerated only them with his blades» (Ch4, L717). Hits the stealth Blur first time «by feel» (L3215). Detonates a YVH's minirocket in its port (L6261) and hurls a droid through the viewport (L6723–6725).
- **Statements:** Corran: «the most dangerous man in the galaxy» (L783). Luke on Caedus: a single «negligent attack» would «distract a Jedi Master, hurt a Jedi Knight... . and kill you» (L795). Ben: «No, they can't. They can't be Luke Skywalker.» (L5077).
- **Scaling:** at the top; ≈ Caedus here, and > Caedus on Inferno.

### 3. Ben Skywalker
- **Role/arc:** maturing. His trials count as done (L5169). «Not revenge» (L5353–5355). He suspects the truth: «it had been Jacen, not Alema Rar, who had killed his mother.» (L1803).
- **Key feats:** wrenches a YVH's cannon arm to shoot another droid in half (L6255–6257). Grenades four YVHs, blowing two «into and through the bulkhead armor» (L6625).
- **Scaling:** Caedus says he could beat Ben «without trouble» (L5993). Ben is a tactical problem-solver.

### 4. Jaina Solo
- **Role/arc:** hardening into the Sword of the Jedi. Jag tells her she is «training yourself to lose» (L2097). She ends by going to Fett.
- **Key feats:** «Almost faster than the eye could follow, Jaina lunged.» (L2035). Reads through Alema's phantom "Jag": «Predictable, Alema, predictable.» (L4451–4455).
- **Statements:** Zekk: «You've outstripped me with the lightsaber ... I doubt any Jedi Knight can stand against you.» (L1957). Her own: «I'm his twin. I have as much power as he does. ... potentially. But he's had training I haven't.» (L7039).
- **Fights/results:** beats Jag twice in training, then loses when beskar takes her blade (L2065–2075).
- **Scaling:** the top Knight, below Master tier. This sets up Invincible.

### 5. Jagged Fel
- **Role/arc:** a Jedi-hunter in beskar and cortosis who ends his clan duty by killing Alema.
- **Key feats:** survives two hits from a 4 m section of track on beskar (L4741–4749). Booby-trapped blaster: «Her right arm was gone from just below the elbow.» (L4785). «He felt the crack of Alema's vertebrae under his hand as they shattered.» (L4803).
- **Statements:** «you need to be thinking like someone who hunts Jedi. Like me.» (L2115). «I think Alema was just about my match. Jacen. ... he's far more dangerous.» (L7041).
- **Scaling:** with prep he kills a drained dark Jedi. The template for Jaina's plan.

### 6. Alema Rar
- **Role/arc:** the tragic end of the Dark Nest. «Remember.» (L4793).
- **Key feats:** a phantom with her arm restored that shrugs off Leia's push: «Alema merely rocked back on her heels and looked faintly amused.» (L285). Projects across the galaxy from a dark-side well (Ch16, L2847–2871). Holds several mynock phantoms tied to real people (L4205–4207), until «tired by the effort of maintaining so many phantoms at once» (L4341). Bends a track with an invisible blow (L4695).
- **Statements:** the One Sith say her technique «appears in every way to match the lost technique of Darth Vectivus» (L3877). Her weakness: «We cannot feel droid intent» (L4773).
- **Fights/results:** Leia holds her phantom three times. Jag kills her.
- **Scaling:** dangerous at range through phantoms, weak in person.

### 7. Leia Organa Solo
- **Feats:** holds off Alema's phantom, which loses strength (L291–309). Rides a Force link to Han's piloting (L4483); Alema's «sledgehammerlike blows had grown weaker» (L4535). TK in front of Caedus: «it clicked down without Caedus's help» (L2543). Kills a YVH with an upward thrust into its skull (L6663–6665).
- **Scaling:** Caedus says she is beatable «without trouble» (L5993). Leia ≥ the phantom Alema.

### 8. Saba Sebatyne
- **Feats:** «Saba, her sword work brilliant, was parrying full-autofire streams of blaster cannon fire.» (L6237). Beheads a laminanium droid (L6265). «Impossibly, Saba stood» after the grenade blast (L6711). Ben: «He was fast, but not as fast as Saba Sebatyne.» (L6215).
- **Scaling:** Caedus can beat her only «with difficulty» (L5993). A top Master.

### 9. Kyle Katarn
- **Feats/fight:** leads the plaza ambush. Run through by the speeder trick. Cilghal: «A few centimeters off, and it would have gone clean through his heart.» (L2397).
- **Statement:** Caedus: «Katarn, though, was a threat.» (L1571).
- **Scaling:** a Master who loses to Caedus in seconds, though to a trick.

### 10. Thann Mithric
- **Feats:** Mithric «was starting to beat down his parries» (L1713).
- **Fight/result:** killed by Caedus (L1723).
- **Scaling:** a strong Knight who briefly pressed a wounded Caedus.

### 11. Zekk
- **Feats:** frees the Sith sphere by drawing on the dark side: «Into his words he poured his own power of will, and felt it joined, strengthened by the power of this dark place.» (L4405–4409). The cost: «it was strong, so very strong, and it laughed at his pathetic efforts.» (L4423).
- **Scaling:** Knight level, with a vulnerability to the dark side.

### 12. Valin Horn and Kolir Hu'lya
- **Fight:** Caedus throws Hu'lya into Mithric and kicks Valin off his feet within two seconds (L1573–1577).
- **Statement:** «Separately or collectively, these Jedi Knights were no match for him.» (L1569).
- **Scaling:** Knights far below Caedus.

### 13. Han Solo
- **Feat:** shoots a YVH in its cannon barrel (L6655).
- **Scaling:** non-Force support.
