<!-- 021-legacy-of-the-force/lotf-8-revelation-notes.md (verbatim copy) -->

# Legacy of the Force 8: Revelation (novel, read in full): feats, scaling and story notes

<!-- read up to: L8485, END OF BOOK; COMPLETE -->

- **Book:** *Star Wars: Legacy of the Force: Revelation* by Karen Traviss (2008). Eighth of the nine Legacy of the Force novels.
- **Continuity:** Legends, 41 ABY, «TWELVE WEEKS AFTER THE DEATH OF MARA JADE SKYWALKER» (L75, L123), days after *Fury* («TWO DAYS AFTER THE RETURN OF THE ANAKIN SOLO», L337). Comes after *Fury* (LotF 7) and before *Invincible* (LotF 9). Dramatis personae: «Jacen Solo; joint Chief of State, Galactic Alliance, and Sith Lord (male human)» (L45).
- **Source file:** `[local path]` (8,485 lines; working copy `[local path]`). OCR noise kept verbatim (e.g. «Tahiti» for Tahiri, L359; Ch11 heading garbled at L4743; Ch17 misprinted «Chapter 16» at L6885).
- **Method (be precise about depth):** I went through every line in order, front matter to L8485. Because of length, most narrative paragraphs were read through a per-paragraph view (first 150–230 characters of each paragraph, with the file line number); **every combat, Force-use, power-statement, and Caedus/Jaina/Luke/Ben/Fett/Tahiri passage that matters for verdicts was then pulled and read in full**, and all «…» quotes below were copied from full lines. Politics-only passages (Moff Council, Niathal–Pellaeon talks, Fett family scenes) were read at the paragraph-opening level, so fine detail inside long paragraphs there could be missed. `L####` is the line number in the source file. There is no back-matter interview in this edition.
- **Chapter heading lines:** Prologue L71, Ch1 L113, Ch2 L411, Ch3 L821, Ch4 L1271, Ch5 L1727, Ch6 L2139, Ch7 L2611, Ch8 L3209, Ch9 L3659, Ch10 L4203, Ch11 L4743 (garbled), Ch12 L5215, Ch13 L5659, Ch14 L6041, Ch15 L6323, Ch16 L6671, Ch17 L6885 (printed «Chapter 16»), Ch18 L7111, Ch19 L7531, Ch20 L8015.
- **Feat vs. statement:** Section 2 is what the narration shows; Section 3 is what characters say or think (**STATEMENT**); *(POV)* marks narration filtered through a character. **Traviss's framing:** Caedus is shown as powerful but brittle, increasingly self-deceiving, and repeatedly beaten by *non-Force* means (mines destroyed undetected, Mandalorian beskar, a kneecapping). Luke is shown restraining himself, not lacking power. Treat Caedus-POV self-assessments as unreliable when they flatter him (see S6).
- **Headline:** the **Mandalorian training / Mara-evidence** book. Jaina goes to Boba Fett and is trained in anti-Jedi thinking (Fett, Beviin, Mirta, ex-Jedi Bardan Jusik). Ben and Shevu prove Jacen killed Mara (her hair in his StealthX; his recorded confession). **Luke beats Caedus in a StealthX duel at Fondor**, ripping his fighter apart with the Force, but lets him escape rather than kill civilians. Caedus brings down Fondor's planetary shield by mass mind-influence and bombs Oridin City. Niathal relieves him; the GA splits. **Tahiri murders Pellaeon** and becomes Caedus's apprentice. Daala returns. On *Bloodfin*, Mirta and Jaina gut Tahiri, Jaina breaks Caedus's Force choke, and a Mandalorian kneecaps Caedus. Jaina resolves to kill her brother «out of love».

---

## 1. Cast who fight or use the Force

| Character | Role in this book | Force user? |
|---|---|---|
| Jacen Solo / Darth Caedus | Chief of State. Grooms Tahiri, loses the Luke space duel, drops Fondor's shield by mind-influence, bombs Oridin, splits the fleet, is kneecapped on *Bloodfin* | Yes |
| Luke Skywalker | Grand Master. Phantom-fleet illusion, wrecks Caedus's StealthX, hides the Fondorian fleet, sees Mara's ghost at the end | Yes |
| Ben Skywalker | 15ish. Investigator with Shevu; finds Mara's hair; plans the Jedi evacuation; sees Mara's ghost | Yes |
| Jaina Solo | Trains with Fett on Mandalore; fights Tahiri; breaks Caedus's choke; decides she must kill him | Yes |
| Tahiri Veila | GA lieutenant, Caedus's new apprentice. Shoots Pellaeon; nearly bleeds out | Yes |
| Boba Fett | Mand'alor; trains Jaina; Daala's contractor; leaves Jacen «for Jaina» | No |
| Goran Beviin, Mirta Gev, Baltan Carid, Medrit Vasur, Novoc Vevut | Mandalorian trainers/commandos. Carid kneecaps Caedus; Mirta guts Tahiri | No |
| Gotab (Bardan Jusik), Venku (Kad'ika) | Force-sensitive Mandalorians: an ex-Jedi Clone Wars general (healer) and his adopted son with two lightsabers | Yes |
| Lon Shevu | GAG captain, CSF cop; gets Caedus's confession on record | No |
| Cha Niathal, Gilad Pellaeon, Natasi Daala | Admirals: Niathal leaks to Luke and breaks with Jacen; Pellaeon killed; Daala's Maw fleet routs Caedus | No |
| Leia, Han, Zekk, Jag | Minor here (evacuation, reactions to the evidence) | Leia, Zekk yes |

---

## 2. Combat and Force feats, in chapter order

### Ch3–Ch8 — Coruscant, Kavan, Mandalore
1. **Ch3 · L871–L887 · Ben**: Force-overloads security cams, Force-wipes a lock, deflects Shevu's surprise shot. Minor utility feats.
2. **Ch5 · L1841–1843 · Mara (Force ghost)**: appears to Ben and tugs her hair: «Love you too, Ben.» It points him to the forensic clue. *(Lore/aftermath, not combat.)*
3. **Ch7 · L2909–2913 · Fett vs Jaina (barn, training)**: Fett sucker-punches her; she slashes «up across Fett's chest in a split sec-ond» by reflex; beskar leaves only «a scorch mark ... from belly to chin». *Shows:* beskar stops a real lightsaber slash; Jaina's reflexes; Fett is «a blank slate in the Force» to her (L2867). Training; she «still held back» (L2953).
4. **Ch8 · L3383 · Jaina (concealment)**: Caedus «couldn't feel Jaina» when he searches for her. *Shows:* her Force-hiding works on him at galactic range.
5. **Ch8 · L3499 · Ben**: Force-plucks the forensics droid out of Jacen's StealthX unseen.
6. **Ch9 · L3763–3771, L3849 · Beviin vs Jaina (beskad training)**: she is «taken down in a saber fight of any kind» for the first time in years and only scores by Force-pushing and pinning him; Beviin's berserker mode with a stick — «complete lack of all emotion except a single... word ... end» — hammers her down until she throws him off with the Force. *Shows:* Mandalorian total-war mindset; a lesson, not a power scale.

### Ch9–Ch12 — Fondor
7. **Ch9 · L4119–4197 · Caedus (failure)**: five stealth minelayers destroyed before laying mines; his Force danger sense is «next to useless on a battlefield» (L4125). His eyes turn yellow (L4197).
8. **Ch11 · L5089, L5093 · Caedus (battle awareness)**: «a living grid of interconnected reactions»; drawback: blurs inner images and real sight.
9. **Ch11 · L5127–5187 · Luke (illusion)**: a phantom fleet «in a complete 360- degree ring» (L5127), seen by Caedus alone (L5249); he broadcasts warnings to the whole fleet; nothing is there. Caedus later calls it «a brilliant Fallanassi illusion» (L5677).
10. **Ch12 · L5281–5287 · Luke (concealment + flying)**: twice wingtip-rams Caedus undetected: «Caedus hadn't felt anyone. Luke-he could always sense Luke.»
11. **Ch12 · L5333–5335 · Luke vs Caedus (TK)**: Luke tears at the StealthX's drives through the Force; **«Caedus tried to block Luke in the Force and suddenly got an idea of just how much power Luke could muster.»** His seat shears off; «Something cracked in his chest.» Cannons ripped off (L5311–5313).
12. **Ch12 · L5339–5363 · Caedus (escape)**: aims at a civilian replenishment tunnel so Luke balks (L5351, L5359); crash-lands holding the wreck «as much by the Force as by its controls» (L5363).
13. **Ch13 · L5849 · Jedi (collective concealment)**: the Fondorian fleet hidden inside the yards from Caedus: «putting all their effort into blocking his senses».
14. **Ch13 · L5857, L5939–5951 · Caedus (battle meditation, aggressive)**: makes his commanders «fighting mad»; feedback «syphoned back into him as a changed and magnified thing» (L5981).
15. **Ch13 · L6001–6023 · Caedus (mass mind-influence) — biggest Caedus feat of the book**: channels the fleet's rage into the minds of Fondor's planetary-shield operators: «Open up. Let us in.» → «"Shields down!"»; concussion missiles airburst over Oridin City. Cost: drained for hours (L6343, L7081).
16. **Ch15–16 · L6437–6447 · Caedus (misread)**: assumes Daala's real fleet is another Luke illusion; he no longer trusts his senses.

### Ch17–Ch18 — *Bloodfin*
17. **L6291 · Tahiri**: shoots the unarmed 92-year-old Pellaeon in the chest. Non-Force.
18. **L7015–7023 · Jaina + Mirta vs Tahiri (2v1)**: Tahiri deflects Mirta's fire, but her lightsaber «simply failed to slice through» Mirta's beskar (L7019); Jaina fights in Beviin's «blind violence» mode (L7021); Mirta's vibroblade cuts Tahiri's femoral artery (L7023).
19. **L7027 · Caedus (Force choke) vs Jaina (counter)**: Caedus chokes Mirta from beyond a hatch to cover Tahiri; **Jaina breaks it**: «She saw it like a black chain and visualized the links flying apart». Context: Caedus exhausted (L7081), blind, splitting attention.
20. **L7135–7143 · Caedus vs Carid**: Caedus projects Ailyn Vel's face (his Mara trick, L7135); Carid isn't fooled and shoots him through the knee; Caedus Force-punches the hatch and escapes with Tahiri.

### Ch20
21. **L7931–7977 · Gotab (healing)**: restores Sintas's sight over sessions; healing «Drains you, healing.» (L7841).
22. **L8439–8445 · Venku**: wields two blue lightsabers sparring with Jaina (no result given).

## 3. Statements and hype (STATEMENT: not feats)

| # | Line | Speaker / POV | Statement | Reliability |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| S1 | L97 | Jaina (journal) | «I'm his equal, and I'm the Sword of the Jedi. But I just don't have his... training.» / «He's way too cocky not to overestimate himself.» | High as self-assessment; repeats *Fury* L7039. |
| S2 | L2475 | Jaina to Fett | «Jacen and I are matched in terms of Force strengths. But he's picked up training in Force tech-niques I don't even understand» | High. Third statement of equal power / unequal training. |
| S3 | L1059 | Luke to Niathal | Jacen has no special powers: «He's just very strong, and he has an ability to use a battle meditation technique that gives him a remarkable awareness of the battlefield.» | High. Luke's sober, slightly dismissive rating. |
| S4 | L5299 | Luke to Caedus | «If I wanted to kill you, I could have done it several times over by now.» | **High** — matches feats 10–11. |
| S5 | L5335 | Narration (Caedus POV) | «suddenly got an idea of just how much power Luke could muster» | **High** (concession from the loser's POV). |
| S6 | L5683–5687 | Caedus (POV) | the illusion and the StealthX attack were «the best Luke ... could do»; «Luke's best shot wasn't good enough.» | **Low** for power (rationalization; Luke was capturing, not killing). High as characterization: Caedus believes Luke won't kill him — the blind spot *Invincible* exploits via Jaina. |
| S7 | L5233 | Caedus (POV) | «explored more arcane Force techniques than any member of the Jedi Council. He did not fall prey to tricks.» | Low (immediately falsified by feat 9). Medium for breadth of technique. |
| S8 | L5993 | Caedus to Nevil | «I'm learn-ing to use combat powers way beyond those of the Jedi» | Medium — the shield feat (15) backs the *breadth* claim. |
| S9 | L5003, L7083 | Caedus (POV) | «I'm not omnipotent. I have to understand my limits.» / «No, I am not omnipotent.» | High. Self-aware limits. |

Caedus: cracked chest, fighter gutted. He escapes by threatening civilians. | **Luke > Caedus** in illusion, concealment and raw TK (S5). Strongest LotF-8 data point on the Luke–Caedus gap. |

### Links to existing research files

- **Caedus vs Mara:** S10–S13 confirm `lotf-5-sacrifice-notes.md` Duel C — she nearly killed him; poison was his last resort. `mara-jade-quotes.txt`.
- **Jaina vs Caedus:** S1, S2, S19, S20, feats 4 and 19. Leads into `lotf-9-invincible-notes.md` (final duel). `jaina-solo-quotes.txt`.
- **Mandalorians:** beskar vs lightsaber (feats 3, 18); Fett's Jedi-hunting record; Gotab = Bardan Jusik (Clone Wars, Traviss's *Republic Commando* line).
- **Previous LotF books:** `lotf-2-bloodlines-notes.md` … `lotf-7-fury-notes.md`.

### Quick ladder (this novel only)
- **Top:** Luke > Caedus (Fight A; S4–S5). Caedus's mass-influence (Fight B) is unmatched in breadth.
- **Caedus vs others:** beatable by collective Jedi concealment, by prep/tech (mines, beskar), and when drained.
- **Knights:** Jaina (top; concealment beats Caedus; broke a drained Caedus's choke) > Tahiri.
- **Non-Force:** Mandalorians in beskar are a real counter to lightsaber users (feats 3, 18, 20).

## 5. Plot summary relevant to the fights
- **Prologue–Ch3:** Jaina decides to go to Fett. Ben suspects Jacen and goes to Coruscant to Shevu. Niathal, shaken by Tebut's murder, becomes Luke's informant. Caedus sends Tahiri to court Pellaeon. Fett recovers Sintas in carbonite.
- **Ch4–Ch8:** Caedus tests Tahiri at Fondor. Jaina reaches Keldabe; Fett agrees to train her. Ben and Shevu go to Kavan; Mara's ghost points to her hair; the CSF droid finds it in Jacen's StealthX. Jacen admits to Niathal he is a Sith. Pellaeon recalls Daala.
- **Ch9–Ch12:** Beviin's training. The Fondor minelayers die (Niathal's leak). Ben's DNA match; Bith witnesses. Daala hires Fett. Luke's phantom fleet humiliates Caedus; Luke wrecks his StealthX.
- **Ch13–Ch16:** Second Battle of Fondor. Jedi hide the Fondorian fleet; Caedus drives his commanders mad with battle-rage, drops the planetary shield by mind-influence and bombs Oridin. Fondor surrenders to Niathal; Caedus refuses; she relieves him. Tahiri kills Pellaeon. Daala's Maw fleet routs Caedus; the GA splits; Niathal forms a government in exile.
- **Ch17–Ch18:** Mandalorians storm *Bloodfin*. Mirta and Jaina cut down Tahiri; Jaina breaks Caedus's choke; Carid kneecaps Caedus, who escapes with Tahiri. Caedus confesses the Mara killing to Shevu on record.
- **Ch19–Ch20:** The family hears the evidence. Fett and Gotab push Jaina to accept that she must kill Jacen. Sintas regains sight and memory. Mirta marries. Mara's ghost appears to Luke.

---

## Story and prose notes

### SP1. Plot summary, in order
See Section 5 and the raw log. Two converging investigations: Ben's (proof of Mara's murder) and Jaina's (how to beat Jacen). The "revelation" is plural: Jacen's guilt, Gotab's secret, Sintas's memory, and Jaina's acceptance.

### SP2. Character arcs
- **Caedus/Jacen:** dissociating. He forgets Tebut is dead (L1417), rebrands his sacrifice as Allana (S21), stops feeling Jaina and declares Jacen «Gone» (S22), sees phantom fleets, spews his confession to the one honest man he trusts. Power grows in breadth (Fight B) while judgment and control decay (yellow eyes L4197, L5859; holozine rage L7409–7421). He still values loyalty (rescues Tahiri personally, L6415, L7089).
- **Luke:** older, grieving, but capable and controlled. Beats Caedus in Fight A and refuses to kill civilians to finish him (L5359). Weeps with pride at Ben (L4271). Ends comforted by Mara's ghost (L8479).
- **Ben:** cop, not avenger: «he didn't mean killing him» (L851); «I'm not the jury or the judge» (L4263). Luke calls him «a moral compass» (L4351). Sees Mara's ghost first.
- **Jaina:** from «I'll bring him in» (L779) to «this is as far as it goes. I have to stop him now» (L8373). Learns to «Be someone else» (L2983), to commit without restraint, and to separate love from approval (L5559–5561).
- **Mara's aftermath:** her ghost (L1843, L8479), her hair (L3461, L4447), her threat (L4207), and Caedus's own confession (S11) re-establish her as the one who nearly won.
- **Boba Fett:** father/grandfather arc (Sintas, Mirta, Ailyn); principled refusal to kill Jacen himself (L7681); professional respect for Jaina (L7359).
- **Mandalorians:** Beviin (heart), Carid (violence), Mirta (redeemed hatred), Gotab/Venku (secret Force-users). Traviss's thesis: armor, culture and attitude beat Force power.
- **Tahiri:** blooded by Pellaeon's murder, named apprentice (L6413), nearly killed by Mirta.
- **Niathal / Pellaeon / Daala:** Niathal's guilt over the leaked minelayers (L4635–4655); Pellaeon dies on principle (L6281–6289); Daala returns as kingmaker.

### SP3. Themes
- **Family vs justice:** «Most murders happen within families» (L857); Gotab: «I'd kill him, out of love.» (L8351).
- **Attitude over power:** Fett: «It's not about their powers. It's about their attitude.» (L6577); S19.
- **Self-deception of power:** Fett: «they have to tell themselves lies to explain how they got there» (L7667).
- **The cost of restraint:** Luke's mercy lets Caedus escape and bomb Oridin; Gotab calls the Jedi's restraint moral cowardice (L8339).

### SP4. Notable prose passages
- «My brother died in the Yuuzhan Vong War. / Not Anakin: Jacen.» (L79–81)
- «He was utterly alone now, rejected even by his daughter, Allana. / That.... that was my sacrifice.» (L1537–1539)
- «Jacen Solo. Gone, now; not concealed. Gone, and never coming back.» (L3387)
- «Caedus tried to block Luke in the Force and suddenly got an idea of just how much power Luke could muster.» (L5335)
- «Doesn't suit you, dar'jetii» (L7141)
- «"I'd kill him, out of love."» (L8351)
- «Oh.... sweetheart.... you found me. You found me. Stay awhile.» (L8479)

### SP5. Worldbuilding and lore stated in the text
- **Beskar** stops lightsabers (L2913, L7019); Imperials couldn't work it without Mando smiths (L7329). Beskar restraints offered for Jedi (L7315).
- **Fallanassi-type illusion** targeted at one mind (L5249, L5677).
- **Battle meditation feedback loop** (L5981, L6001).
- **Sith eyes** turn yellow under strain (L4197, L5859).
- **Kiffar psychometry** via heart-of-fire gems (L2669, L8037).
- **Gotab = Bardan Jusik**, Clone Wars Jedi general who left the Order (L7873, L8323).

### SP6. Timeline placement and connections
- 41 ABY, twelve-plus weeks after Mara's death. Sets up *Invincible*: Jaina trained and resolved; the Jedi in the Transitory Mists; Tahiri found the old Endor base (L8019); Niathal's government in exile; Daala allied with Fett; Caedus kneecapped with a cracked chest.
- **Author intent:** Traviss consistently frames Jedi/Sith Force power as overrated against preparation and will (S19; L6577; L8339). She gives Luke the clear win in Fight A but makes his restraint the reason Caedus survives.

---

## 6. Verdict-relevant takeaways
1. **Caedus vs Luke:** *Revelation* restores a clear Luke edge after Allston's *Fury*. Luke out-illusions, out-hides and out-muscles Caedus in TK (feats 9–11, S5), and says he could have killed him «several times over» (S4). Caveat: it is a fighter duel, not a saber duel, and Luke had a wingmate. **Luke > Caedus, confidence back to medium-high.**
2. **Caedus vs Mara:** Caedus's recorded confession (S11) and private thought (S10) confirm she had him injured and the poison was a last resort. Fett: she «nearly succeeded» (S12). Keeps *Sacrifice* Duel C as "Mara nearly won".
3. **Jaina vs Caedus:** equal power, less training (S1, S2), but her Force-hiding beats his senses (feat 4), she breaks a drained Caedus's choke (feat 19), she is forewarned about his illusion trick (S20), and she adopts Mandalorian commitment (feats 3, 6, 18). Caedus's weaknesses are mapped: arrogance (S6), sensory overload (S23), reliance on illusion (feat 20).
4. **Caedus's peak range:** breadth is now enormous (planetary-shield mind-influence, fleet battle meditation), but he is repeatedly beaten by Luke, by collective Jedi concealment, and by non-Force prep, and is drained by his big feats. **High Sith Lord / Master tier, below Luke.**
5. **Tahiri** is a dangerous Knight but well below Jaina (Fight D).

Source: [local path] (8,485 lines; working copy [local path]). L#### = line in that file.
### Front matter
- Dramatis personae: «Jacen Solo; joint Chief of State, Galactic Alliance, and Sith Lord (male human)» (L45); «Jaina Solo; Jedi Knight (female human)» (L47); «Boba Fett; Mandalorian bounty hunter, Mand'alor (male human)» (L31); «Tahiri Veila; Galactic Alliance lieutenant, Jedi Knight (female human)» (L65).
### Prologue (L71–109) — Jaina's first-person journal, Endor, twelve weeks after Mara's death
- «I think the core of Jacen probably died in the Embrace of Pain» (L85). STATEMENT (Jaina, thematic).
- «Mandalorian iron-beskar-is pretty well nearly lightsaber-proof.» (L89).
- **STATEMENT (Jaina, self-assessment):** «If anyone can stop Jacen, then, it's me. I'm his equal, and I'm the Sword of the Jedi. But I just don't have his... training.» (L97); «He's way too cocky not to overestimate himself.» (L97); «if being a Sith made Jacen invincible, he'd have taken over the galaxy by now.» (L99). Reliability: high as her view; parity of power, gap in training (repeats Fury L7039).
### Ch1 (L113–407) — Endor; Bastion; Coruscant
- Ben's case against Jacen for Mara: «Only a really skilled Jedi could ever take down Mom. Look at all the stuff Jacen can do.» (L221) — STATEMENT (Ben; Mara-scaling: only top tier could beat her). Ben recorded the scene (L151, L239). Motive/means/opportunity argued with Leia and Jaina (L219–235); they doubt him (L255).
- Pellaeon refuses Jacen's treaty letter (L305–321); fantasizes Mara would have «lost patience with him eventually, and then he'd have had a good thrashing» (L327) — STATEMENT (Pellaeon, non-Force, low weight).
- Caedus and Tahiri: Tahiri now his aide, calls him "Jacen" (L367–369); sent to woo Pellaeon (L373–389). Caedus: «I don't guess» (L397). Fett's Bes'uliik fighters sold to Corellia: Caedus reads it as Fett's «much longer and more destructive game» of revenge (L397). He will «steer clear of Mandalore for the time being» (L399). Next target Fondor (L401–407).
### Ch2 (L411–817) — Mandalore; Fleet HQ; Endor
- Fett at Shysa's memorial; Mirta calls him Ba'buir (L435). Sintas Vel found in carbonite (L487–489). Fett confesses: «I killed him, ... I killed Fenn Shysa.» (L553).
- Niathal: Caedus «had snapped Lieutenant Tebut's neck without even touching her» (L571); «Jacen was too strong for her to confront and depose alone.» (L603). She plans to be Luke's spy; to Nevil: «When you fire on a target like Jacen Solo, you daren't miss» (L619).
- Caedus to Niathal on Tebut: «Tebut was careless.» (L667); Niathal notes he «looked more different by the day, a little older and less luminously youthful» (L659) — NARRATION (Niathal POV; physical strain of the dark side).
- Jaina asks Han how to contact Fett (L743–811). «Jacen's not invincible, Dad. Nobody is. But when I go after him, it'll have to be with skills he doesn't have. Like Fett's.» (L801) — STATEMENT (title echo). Her goal: «I'll bring him in» (L779), not kill. Han: «Fett doesn't have all the moves, or he'd have killed Jacen by now» (L799).
### Ch3 (L821–) — Coruscant; N'Zoth; Phaeda
- Ben on Coruscant, Force-hidden: «He shut himself down in the Force» (L837). Force-overloads cams (L871), Force-wipes a lock (L873). Deflects Shevu's surprise shot (L887) — minor FEAT.
- Shevu: Jacen «snapped Lieutenant Tebut's neck without even touching her, and threw Captain Nevil across the deck» (L927); calls him «A psychopath» (L933). Shevu married Shula secretly and sent her away; building a file on Jacen (L961–965). Ben's goal: a case, «he didn't mean killing him» (L851).
- **Luke to Niathal (STATEMENT, important for tiering):** asked if Jacen has different powers: «Not really. He's just very strong, and he has an ability to use a battle meditation technique that gives him a remarkable awareness of the battlefield.» (L1059). Luke «looked older ... with noticeable folds in his cheeks» (L1027). Luke trusts Shevu by «Force certainty» (L1075). Luke's gymnastic StealthX exit (L1085).
- Phaeda: Sintas Vel's carbonite slab recovered (L1101–1171). Fett: «I shot her ... And it was for her own good.» (L1153); «Shysa was a mercy killing.» (L1165).
- Sintas thawed at the Beviin-Vasur farm: blind, amnesiac (L1227–1267). Fett: «It's not her who needs to forget, ... It's me.» (L1267).
### Ch4 (L1271–1723) — Bastion; *Anakin Solo* off Fondor; Keldabe
- Moff Council: Pellaeon reveals the Bilbringi/Borleias offer (L1353); expects Jacen to «overstretch himself» (L1379). Pellaeon's sources report the Tebut killing (L1395–1399).
- **Caedus forgets Tebut is dead** twice (L1417); reads his unease as «a price, then, not a failing» (L1425). NARRATION (Caedus POV; erosion of self).

Take, take, take.» (L1501); he: «I told you that you could fire. ... The decision was yours.» (L1523). Grooming Tahiri as Ben's replacement apprentice; she must be «blooded» like Ben with Gejjen (L1447).
- Caedus redefines his Sith sacrifice: «He was utterly alone now, rejected even by his daughter, Allana. / That.... that was my sacrifice.» (L1537–1539) — NARRATION (Caedus POV; key arc beat: the sacrifice was not Mara).
- Caedus gives Shevu 72 hours' leave; reassured «that he hadn't turned into a monster» (L1571–1575). Caedus senses Shevu's «wariness and.... distaste in the Force» (L1547) but misreads it.

In the Oyu'baat she senses something «that couldn't be true» (L1717): **Force-sensitive Mandalorians Venku (Kad'ika) and Gotab** (L2039–2091) — lore: Venku hides being Force-sensitive. Gotab draws a blaster: «Hello, Jedi» (L1723).

- **Ben and Shevu at Kavan** (L1741–1909). Forensics: lightsaber vaporization traces (L1789); Jacen «could hide in the Force, and even cloak Lumiya's presence right under the Jedi Council's nose» (L1793) — STATEMENT (Ben POV; Caedus concealment hype). Jacen's StealthX was signed out at the time (L1809).

Except I almost liked Vader.» (L2487); «Alive's always more complicated.» (L2499). Fett agrees: «Okay, I'll do it, ... And it's going to cost.» (L2543).

### Ch7 (L2611–3205) — farm; barn training; Bastion

- **TRAINING: Fett vs Jaina (barn, L2843–2989).** Fett holds a lightsaber «like a hammer» (L2845), quizzes her on Jacen's powers (TK, mind-wipes, invisibility: «We... can't sense him in the Force...» / «...and he can make himself invisible, sometimes.», L2883–2887), then sucker-punches her: «the wind was knocked out of her as he landed a punch in her gut. Her lightsaber was back on and slashing up across Fett's chest in a split sec-ond» (L2909); beskar holds: «a scorch mark across Fett from belly to chin» (L2913). FEAT (beskar vs lightsaber; Jaina's reflex). Fett is «a blank slate in the Force» to her (L2867). Lesson: «Winning isn't about being better. It's finding your opponent's weakness and ex-ploiting it.» (L2961); «And he knows you. Be someone else.» (L2983). Context: training, Jaina held back (L2953).
- Pellaeon meets Tahiri (L3043–3107); Moffs vote to join the Fondor operation while planning to grab Fondor themselves (L3137–3149). Pellaeon drums a coded Irmenu message (Darakaer legend) to an old friend — insurance against Jacen (L3171–3205) [→ Daala].
### Ch8 (L3209–3655) — Coruscant; GA hangar; Fleet HQ
- Tahiri resents being Caedus's searcher; Caedus thinks Luke «yesterday's man» (L3255). Caedus flow-walk/listening trance hears the fleet gossip that he killed Mara and reacts with fury: «She came after me. She was trying to kill me. If I hadn't killed her, I'd be the one getting a state funeral now.» (L3281) — NARRATION (Caedus POV; confirms the Mara killing and that he felt she could have killed him). He uses «the flow-walking bait» on Tahiri (Anakin) (L3295).
- «My back, ... is never turned, no matter where I am.» (L3307).
- **Caedus cannot feel Jaina in the Force** (L3383): «He couldn't feel Jaina ... Perhaps Ben had taught Jaina how to shut down in the Force» — FEAT/NARRATION (Jaina's concealment works on Caedus at range; also Caedus's separation: «Jacen Solo. Gone, now; not concealed.», L3387; «Darth Caedus had no twin sister», L3395).
- **Mara's hair found in Jacen's StealthX** by the CSF droid: «HAIR, FEMALE, 2.9 CMS. FOLLICULAR TAG PRESENT. TRACES OF BLOOD ON HAIR SHAFT.» (L3461). Ben Force-plucks the droid out (L3499). Circumstantial, Shevu warns (L3509–3531).
- Niathal leaks the Fondor mine-shell plan to Luke (L3625–3645): Jacen sails with ~150,000 troops; Remnant task force of twenty Star Destroyers (L3603).
### Ch9 (L3659–) — Bralsin; beskad training
- **TRAINING: Beviin vs Jaina with beskad (durasteel training blades, beskar plates, L3743–3863).** Beviin downs her repeatedly: «It was the first time she had been taken down in a saber fight of any kind for years.» (L3763); she only lands two blows by Force-pushing him and pins him (L3771). Beviin's «total war» berserker mode with a stick: «Instantly she felt him change in the Force into complete lack of all emotion except a single... word, yes, almost a word: end.» (L3849); she throws him off with the Force, «scared for both of them». Lessons: commit, visualize, no pulling blows (L3837–3861). FEAT (non-Force fighting style beats Jedi saber habits in training; context: unfamiliar weapon, Jaina restraining Force use).
- Beviin and Fett fought beside a Jedi Master (Kubariet) in the Vong war (L3789, L2801).
- Beviin afterwards: «You still held back, anyway, ... If I'd had Force powers, I'd have used them, too.» (L3895). Jaina reads the berserker switch as «the dark side» but notes «Beviin stopped. Jacen hasn't.» (L3891–3891). Venku is a «political agitator» behind Mandalore-First (L3921); Jaina deduces Gotab is a Force healer (L3959–3963). Theme: «Be a different Jaina.» (L3969).
- **Daala arrives** on Pellaeon's new *Bloodfin* (L4099); agrees to field «a full fleet at Fondor with one standard hour's lead time» (L4531). Niathal and Pellaeon tacitly agree to remove Jacen if he blunders (L4057–4067).
- **Fondor (0500):** the five stealth minelayers were destroyed before laying mines (L4119–4181) [the Niathal→Luke leak]. Caedus did not sense it: Force danger sense «next to useless on a battlefield» (L4125); «Your fault, Lumiya. You nagged me to stop relying on my mundane senses.» (L4137) — NARRATION (Caedus POV; limit of his Force senses). **Sith eyes:** «Caedus's eyes were yellow.» (L4197).
### Ch10 (L4203–) — Endor; *Bloodfin*; Fleet HQ; MandalMotors
- Epigraph: Bith Senator heard Mara threaten Jacen, «leave Ben out of it» (L4207).
- **Mara's hair DNA: «POSITIVE MATCH.»** (L4447). Ben keeps the hair; Bith witnesses confirm Mara's threats (L4459–4469): «Part of him was proud that his mother had faced down a Sith Lord in combat.» (L4473). Luke to Ben: «you're a moral compass» (L4351); Luke has not seen Mara's ghost: «The Force gives us what we need.» (L4313). Ben is left behind to plan the Jedi evacuation to the Transitory Mists (L4329–4345). Mara's locket (L4477).
- Niathal takes responsibility for the dead minelayer crews (L4635–4655); Caedus: «I trust my Force awareness.» / «To make an example of Fondor.» (L4685–4689). Niathal: «He's gone too far. He has to go.» (L4701).
- [Ch11 begins at L4743, garbled OCR heading «' ¤_Łň 11»] Fett and the Tra'kad (zero-power ambush ship) (L4779–4811). **Daala hires Fett** as standby at Fondor (L4833–4889). Sintas remembers only post-revival events (L4925). Mirta insists on going «For my mother» (L4973); Fett invites Jaina along as «good training» (L4981).
### Ch11 cont. (L4995–5211) — Fondor
- Caedus: «I'm not omnipotent. I have to understand my limits.» (L5003). Plans to remove Niathal: «Endex, Admiral.» (L5015). Rationalizes Tebut (L5021–5027).
- Caedus's **Sith battle awareness**: «a living grid of interconnected reactions that tilted, panned and zoomed like a holochart» (L5089); drawback: «the harder it was sometimes to separate the images in his inner eye from what he could physically see» (L5093).
- **Phantom fleet:** in his StealthX, Caedus sees «ships popping into existence all around him in a complete 360- degree ring» (L5127) and broadcasts warnings; nothing is there (Niathal's torpedo test, L5167–5171). Bridge crews: «he's lost it» (L5175). FEAT (Luke's targeted illusion, see Ch12).
### Ch12 (L5215–) — DUEL (space): LUKE (+ a second Jedi with grappling StealthX) vs CAEDUS in StealthXs, Fondor assembly area (L5227–5389)
- Caedus POV: «he had explored more arcane Force techniques than any member of the Jedi Council. He did not fall prey to tricks.» (L5233) — undercut by events. The illusion was «designed to disturb him, and him alone» (L5249).
- Luke wingtip-rams him twice undetected: «Caedus hadn't felt anyone. Luke-he could always sense Luke.» (L5281). FEAT (Luke Force-concealment beats Caedus's senses).
- Luke: «If I wanted to kill you, I could have done it several times over by now.» (L5299) — STATEMENT (Luke; consistent with the action).
- **Luke tears Caedus's fighter apart with the Force:** «He had a sense of someone rummag-ing furiously in the drives ... He's ripping the thing apart...» (L5333); **«Caedus tried to block Luke in the Force and suddenly got an idea of just how much power Luke could muster.»** Seat shears off; «Something cracked in his chest.» (L5335) — FEAT (Luke > Caedus in raw TK contest; Caedus injured). A second Jedi StealthX with grappling arms tries to capture him (L5335–5337).
- Caedus escapes by threatening a civilian replenishment tunnel (Luke balks: «Don't...», L5351; «he felt Luke's moment of horror», L5359) and crash-lands holding the fighter «as much by the Force as by its controls» (L5363). RESULT: Luke wins the exchange (Caedus disarmed, fighter wrecked, ribs cracked); Caedus escapes via hostage tactic. Context: Luke trying to capture, not kill.
- Caedus spins it to the crew: «I swerved to avoid a Jedi» (L5379); «At least I know who generated the phantom fleet» (L5385).
- Niathal: Jacen is «a man who has powers like Luke Skywalker, but no rules, no moral limits»; «Luke could even deceive Jacen into fighting a fleet that wasn't there.» (L5413) — STATEMENT (Niathal; equates their powers loosely).
- Niathal gives Fondor's President Vadde a deadline (L5447–5465).
- Jaina on the Tra'kad with Mirta's commandos: «I still love my brother, but there's nothing left to like about him» (L5559). Fett gives Jaina a Bes'uliik; Mirta: «Easy to be seduced by it, isn't it?» (L5643) — Jaina feels her own «ease ... toward darkness» (L5645).
### Ch13 (L5659–6037) — *Bloodfin* conference; Second Battle of Fondor
- **Caedus's read of the Luke fight (NARRATION, Caedus POV, self-serving):** «He'd been duped with a brilliant Fallanassi illusion and had his StealthX almost ripped from under him.» (L5677); «The illusion, however masterful, was the best that Luke and his entourage could do.» (L5683); «they didn't have what it took to kill him, militarily or emotion-ally.» (L5685); «Luke was the greatest Jedi Master, and he'd just exposed the absolute limits of his powers ... Luke's best shot wasn't good enough.» (L5687). Reliability: LOW for power (rationalization; Luke was restraining himself), but HIGH that Luke's *will* to kill is the real limit — exactly the thesis Invincible pays off.
- Caedus orders Tahiri: «If Pellaeon interferes with my plan in any way, then you stop him.» (L5785); «Some deaths... some sacrifices are necessary» (L5789).
- **Jedi conceal the Fondorian fleet from Caedus** inside the orbital yards: «Jedi. That's where they were, putting all their effort into blocking his senses» (L5849). FEAT (collective Jedi concealment beats his battle awareness).
- **Battle meditation, aggressive form:** «imbued them all with a little more aggression, a little less willingness to play by the rules of engagement» (L5857); commanders go «fighting mad» and profane (Niathal, L5939–5951). Back-pressure: «the passion for the battle that he was channeling into them gained power and momentum, and syphoned back into him as a changed and magnified thing» (L5981).
- Caedus to Nevil: «I'm learn-ing to use combat powers way beyond those of the Jedi, and I wasn't fully in control of them then. I am now.» (L5993) — STATEMENT (self-claim).
- **FEAT: Caedus channels the fleet's rage into the minds of Fondor's planetary-shield operators** and makes them drop the shield: «Open up. Let us in.» (L6015); «"Shields down!"» (L6021); ten concussion missiles airburst over Oridin City (L5993, L6023); he feels «a moment of pure animal terror» from the victims (L6023). Mass mind-influence at planetary range via battle-meditation feedback. Cost: exhausted afterwards (L6343). Sith yellow eyes again (L5859).
### Ch14 (L6041–6319) — surrender; Pellaeon's murder
- Nevil abandons ship (escape pod, L6051). Niathal accepts Fondor's surrender (L6099–6105); Caedus: «I refuse to stand down» (L6125) / «I don't recognize your authority.» (L6133). Niathal relieves him; split fleet; Pellaeon orders the Imperials to honor the ceasefire (L6149).
- Pellaeon on Caedus: «Can't he snap her head off with a thought ... bring-ing down a planetary shield singlehanded» (L6209–6213); Tahiri: «Colonel Solo has... exceptional powers ... I don't.» (L6211).
- **Tahiri shoots Pellaeon** in his cabin when he refuses to order the Moffs to support Caedus: «The blaster bolt hit him square in the chest» (L6291). Pellaeon: «So that's Jacen's new Sith Order» (L6295); Tahiri offers to save him, refused: «Go... rot somewhere else... villip» (L6301). Moff Quille watches and leaves him to die (L6297–6307). Pellaeon drums the Darakaer signal to Daala as he dies (L6313–6317).
### Ch15 (L6323–6667) — Daala's Maw Irregular Fleet; Bloodfin boarding
- Caedus names Tahiri his apprentice: «Yes.... my lord.» (L6413); «it's easier to kill a powerful enemy than it is an apparently weak one» (L6407). He promises to come for her (L6415).
- **Daala's fleet arrives** (*Chimaera* restored, L6481); Caedus first assumes another illusion (L6437–6447). Daala: «Good afternoon, you insignificant little man.» (L6501). MCPS (metal-crystal phase shifter) weapons shatter GA ships (L6635–6645).
- Mandalorian boarding of the mutinied *Bloodfin* to remove the Moffs (Daala's contract) (L6511–6597). Jaina: «I can't locate Jacen in the Force, but I can find her [Tahiri], so she'll be his transponder.» (L6561).
### Ch16 (L6671–6881) — Caedus withdraws; goes for Tahiri
- Caedus losing «more than thirty vessels» withdraws ~two-thirds of the GA fleet loyal to him (L6711–6733); Niathal forms a government in exile at Nallastia (L6773–6811). Luke to Niathal: the Jedi hid the Fondorian fleet to protect civilians in the yards (L6793).
- Caedus flies a med-evac speeder alone to *Bloodfin* to rescue Tahiri (L6727, L6849). He senses Jaina; she «troubled him most» (L6855); «She would never feel him now that he was hidden in the Force» (L6857) — NARRATION (Caedus can hide from Jaina).
### Ch17 (heading misprinted «Chapter 16», L6885–) — *Bloodfin*
- Mandos «de-Moff» the citadel (L6915–6969). Tahiri flees into the ducts toward the med sprinter; Jaina senses «someone who can vanish in the Force» (L6947); Mirta goes into the trunking after them against Fett's order (L6949–6951).
- **FIGHT: Jaina + Mirta vs Tahiri (Bloodfin ducts, L6995–7027).** Tahiri deflects Mirta's fire; Mirta charges; **Tahiri's lightsaber fails on beskar**: «the blade of energy simply failed to slice through Mirta's body: My lightsaber doesn't work.» (L7019). Jaina goes Beviin-mode: «ac-cessing that blind violence that Beviin had shown her ... it shut down every warning about the dark side.» (L7021). Mirta's vibroblade opens Tahiri's femoral artery (L7023). RESULT: Tahiri defeated (2v1, plus beskar), escapes through the deckhead.
- **Caedus Force-chokes Mirta through a hatch to cover Tahiri; Jaina breaks the choke:** «he was throt-tling Mirta to let Tahiri escape ... Jaina put every scrap of strength she had into breaking Jacen's invisible Force choke hold on Mirta. She saw it like a black chain and visualized the links flying apart» (L7027). FEAT (Jaina counters Caedus's TK at range; context: Caedus exhausted from the Fondor shield feat, L7081, and multitasking blind).
- **Docking tube (L7051–7157):** Caedus, exhausted («No, I am not omnipotent.», L7083), can't seal the tube, stop the bleeding and fight at once. Carid grabs his ankle; Caedus projects **Ailyn Vel's face**, «a trick that had bought him time with Mara» (L7135); Carid: «Doesn't suit you, dar'jetii» and **shoots him in the knee** (L7141–7143). Caedus Force-punches the hatch open and escapes with Tahiri (L7143). RESULT: Caedus escapes wounded (kneecapped) — a non-Force Mandalorian beat his illusion trick. Fett had ordered: «I said he was Jaina's» (L7169).
### Ch18 (L7111–7527) — aftermath; Coruscant
- Fett to Jaina: «You're not ready to do what needs doing. We'll make you ready.» (L7177). «did you know your brother can change his appearance in a fight, to look like someone else?» (L7247) — intel for Invincible. Fett: «Everyone can see how we win, but it's another thing to do it.» (L7243).
- Daala: «As long as you've got Jedi, you'll get Sith» (L7287); proposes «a third way ... No Jedi Council» (L7297) — sets up her later Chief-of-State politics. Beskar restraints for Jedi (L7315); beskar «effective ... against Force-users» (L7323).
- Caedus stung by a holozine exposé (L7403–7421): «Mara, Lieutenant Tebut-I didn't kill them for personal reasons.» (L7417).
- **Caedus's confession to Shevu (recorded):** «She vowed to kill me in front of witnesses ... she ambushed me in her StealthX, and we ended up on Kavan ... We brawled, actually brawled-she brought down the ceiling and she was like a madwoman. Complete blind rage. I had lightsaber, blaster, and crush in-juries, and the only way I could stop her was to use the poison darts I keep as a last line of defense.» (L7501) — STATEMENT (Caedus, private-ish; HIGH reliability for Mara scaling: she had him hurt and the poison was his last resort; he omits Lumiya, L7503). «It was part of my path to Sith ascendancy.» (L7509). «Technically, it's my lord» (L7523).
### Ch19 (L7531–8011) — Endor; Bralsin; farm; Oyu'baat
- Ben plays the evidence and Shevu's recording to Luke, Han, Leia and (by comlink) Jaina (L7547–7627). Ben: Jacen's danger sense «had proved pretty useless to him in the end» (L7555).
- **Fett on the recording (STATEMENT):** «A sane man.» (L7667); **«That's a barve who nearly got his backside handed to him by Skywalker's wife. He still looks scared when he remembers it. Because she went at him like a maniac. Just like Beviin showed you.»** (L7669); «She just went to do some necessary pest control. She nearly succeeded.» (L7675). Why Fett leaves Jacen to Jaina: «you clear up your own mess» (L7681). Fett on beheading (L7711).
- Fett tells Sintas of Ailyn's death (L7775–7795). **Gotab = Bardan Jusik**, ex-Jedi general of the Clone Wars (L7873, L8323); heals Sintas's sight (L7931–7977). Sintas remembers: Fett was exiled for killing her rapist, his superior officer (L8011).
### Ch20 (L8015–8485) — Keldabe; Bralsin; Transitory Mists
- Tahiri (epigraph) has found the abandoned Endor base (L8019).
- Fett and Sintas reconcile without reuniting (blue heart-of-fire, L8035–8129). Mirta's wedding. **Mirta to Jaina: don't execute your brother, «just remember me»** (L8173); «I'm Fett's granddaughter in every sense» (L8183). Fett and Daala ("Natasi") (L8231–8293).
- **Gotab's counsel (key to Jaina's arc):** the Jedi «could have stopped him, any of you, if you'd united against him. One Sith can't stand against hundreds of Jedi.» (L8339) — STATEMENT (Gotab; reliability medium: a former Jedi's judgment, consistent with the Fondor events). «I'd kill him, out of love.» (L8351). «You're going to kill your brother because he's a power-hungry, murdering dictator, no one else in your Jedi circle has the moral courage to do it, and you stand the best chance of stopping him.» (L8395). Jaina: «It's not justice, and it's not punishment ... this is as far as it goes. I have to stop him now.» (L8373).
- Jaina spars Venku («Kad'ika-Little Saber», L8427), who uses two blue lightsabers (L8439) — training continues (L8445).
- Final scene: Mara's ghost comes to Luke: «Oh.... sweetheart.... you found me.» (L8479). END OF BOOK (L8485).

## Character analyses (added 2026-10-17 under the 7+ characters rule)

*Method:* built from the verified log above (Sections 2–4 and SP2–SP4). `L####` = line in `9b11086c…txt` (chapter given). Quotes in «…» are verbatim and were re-checked by script. "Gotab = Bardan Jusik" is stated in the text (L7873, L8323). His Clone Wars backstory is **[wiki/other-book knowledge]**. **Before:** 9 story-arc profiles (SP2), none with feats, fights and scaling in the same entry. **Added:** 13 analyses.

### 1. Darth Caedus
- **Role/arc:** dissociating. «Jacen Solo. Gone, now; not concealed. Gone, and never coming back.» (L3387). His power grows in breadth while his judgment decays (yellow eyes, L4197).
- **Key feats:** battle awareness as «a living grid of interconnected reactions» (Ch11, L5089). Drives commanders «fighting mad» (L5951), with the feedback «syphoned back into him as a changed and magnified thing» (L5981). Mass mind-influence on Fondor's shield operators ends in «"Shields down!"» (Ch13, L6001–6023), which drains him for hours. Chokes Mirta through a hatch (L7027). Crash-lands his StealthX holding the wreck «as much by the Force as by its controls» (L5363).
- **Statements:** Luke: «He's just very strong, and he has an ability to use a battle meditation technique» (L1059). Caedus: «I'm not omnipotent. I have to understand my limits.» (L5003). His recorded confession about Mara: «the only way I could stop her was to use the poison darts I keep as a last line of defense.» (L7501). Fett: «She nearly succeeded.» (L7675).
- **Fights/results:** loses to Luke in fighters (Ch12). Jaina breaks his choke (L7027). Carid shoots him through the knee after seeing through the Ailyn Vel illusion (L7135–7143).
- **Scaling:** high Sith Lord tier, below Luke. Beatable by collective concealment, preparation, and when drained.

### 2. Luke Skywalker
- **Role/arc:** grieving but controlled. Refuses to fire through civilians to finish Caedus (L5359). Ends comforted by Mara's ghost (L8479).
- **Key feats:** a phantom fleet «in a complete 360- degree ring» seen only by Caedus (Ch11, L5127). Twice wingtip-rams Caedus unsensed: «Luke-he could always sense Luke.» (L5281–5287). The TK contest: «Caedus tried to block Luke in the Force and suddenly got an idea of just how much power Luke could muster.» (L5335).
- **Statements:** to Caedus: «If I wanted to kill you, I could have done it several times over by now.» (L5299). Caedus later calls the illusion «a brilliant Fallanassi illusion» (L5677).
- **Scaling:** Luke > Caedus in illusion, concealment and TK (medium-high).

### 3. Jaina Solo
- **Role/arc:** goes from «I'll bring him in» (L779) to «this is as far as it goes. I have to stop him now» (L8373). Learns from Fett: «And he knows you. Be someone else.» (L2983).
- **Key feats:** a reflex slash «up across Fett's chest» that beskar stops (Ch7, L2909–2913). Caedus «couldn't feel Jaina» (L3383). Breaks his choke: «She saw it like a black chain and visualized the links flying apart» (L7027). Fights Tahiri in Beviin's «blind violence» mode (L7021).
- **Statements:** her journal: «I'm his equal, and I'm the Sword of the Jedi. But I just don't have his... training.» (L97).
- **Scaling:** equal to Caedus in potential and behind him in training. Her concealment beats his senses. This is the setup for Invincible.

### 4. Ben Skywalker
- **Role/arc:** a cop, not an avenger: «I'm not the jury or the judge» (L4263). Luke calls him «a moral compass» (L4351).
- **Feats:** Force-overloads cameras and deflects Shevu's surprise shot (Ch3, L871–887). Force-plucks the forensics droid out of Jacen's StealthX (L3499).
- **Statement:** «Only a really skilled Jedi could ever take down Mom.» (L221).
- **Scaling:** a skilled young Knight-in-training. The value of this book is investigation, not combat.

### 5. Tahiri Veila
- **Role/arc:** blooded by Pellaeon's murder (L6291) and named apprentice (L6413).
- **Fights/results:** deflects Mirta's fire, but her lightsaber «simply failed to slice through» beskar (L7019). Mirta cuts her femoral artery (L7023).
- **Statement:** «Colonel Solo has... exceptional powers» (L6211).
- **Scaling:** Tahiri < Jaina (2v1 here, but clearly outmatched).

### 6. Boba Fett
- **Role/arc:** trains Jaina, contracts with Daala, and refuses to kill Jacen himself (L7681).
- **Feats:** sucker-punches Jaina, and her slash leaves only «a scorch mark» on his beskar (L2909–2913). He is «a blank slate in the Force» to her (L2867).
- **Statements/quotes:** «Winning isn't about being better. It's finding your opponent's weakness and ex-ploiting it.» (L2961). «It's not about their powers. It's about their attitude.» (L6577). On Caedus: «a barve who nearly got his backside handed to him by Skywalker's wife.» (L7669).
- **Scaling:** non-Force, but the architect of the anti-Caedus method.

### 7. Goran Beviin
- **Feats:** takes Jaina down «in a saber fight of any kind» for the first time in years, in berserker mode (Ch9, L3763–3771). She escapes only with the Force.
- **Scaling:** a lesson, not a power scale. It shows the Mandalorian total-war mindset.

### 8. Mirta Gev
- **Feats:** her beskar turns Tahiri's blade, and she cuts Tahiri's femoral artery (L7019–7023). Caedus chokes her and Jaina frees her (L7027).
- **Scaling:** a beskar-armored non-Force fighter who can kill a dark Jedi 2v1.

### 9. Baltan Carid
- **Feats:** sees through Caedus's illusion and kneecaps him (L7135–7143).
- **Quote:** «Doesn't suit you, dar'jetii» (L7141).
- **Scaling:** proof that the illusion trick fails on a prepared professional.

### 10. Gotab (Bardan Jusik)
- **Feats:** restores Sintas's sight over several sessions. «Drains you, healing.» (L7841).
- **Statements:** «One Sith can't stand against hundreds of Jedi.» (L8339). «I'd kill him, out of love.» (L8351).
- **Scaling:** a healer, not a combatant.

### 11. Venku (Kad'ika)
- **Feats:** spars with Jaina using two blue lightsabers, with no result given (L8439–8445).
- **Scaling:** unknown and untested.

### 12. Mara Jade Skywalker (posthumous)
- **Role:** her ghost tugs Ben's hair, «Love you too, Ben.» (L1841–1843). To Luke: «Oh.... sweetheart.... you found me.» (L8479).
- **Statements:** Caedus's POV: (on Force-hiding) «Mara had managed it for critical moments and nearly killed him» (L5281). His confession (L7501) and Fett (L7675) confirm it.
- **Scaling:** confirms that she nearly beat pre-Caedus Jacen.

### 13. Cha Niathal
- **Role:** an admiral who leaks to Luke and breaks with Jacen.
- **Statements:** Jacen is «a man who has powers like Luke Skywalker, but no rules» (L5413). «Not knowing how powerful he actually was-that troubled her.» (L2303–2305).
- **Scaling:** an outside non-Force view.

<!-- 021-legacy-of-the-force/lotf-9-invincible-notes.md (verbatim copy) -->

# Legacy of the Force 9: Invincible (novel, read in full): feats, scaling and story notes

<!-- read up to: L5971, END OF BOOK; COMPLETE -->

- **Book:** *Star Wars: Legacy of the Force: Invincible* by Troy Denning (2008). Ninth and final Legacy of the Force novel.
- **Continuity:** Legends, 41 ABY, «three weeks after the battle of Fondor» (L115). Comes after *Revelation* (LotF 8); leads into the *Fate of the Jedi* era (Daala as Chief of State, L5870). Not to be confused with `invincible-notes.md`, which covers the unrelated *Invincible* comic.
- **Source file:** `[local path]` (5,971 lines; working copy `[local path]`). OCR noise kept verbatim (colons for ellipses, e.g. «Okay, Dad:Caedus must die»).
- **Method (be precise about depth):** I went through every non-blank line in order, front matter to L5971. Most narrative paragraphs were read through a per-paragraph view (first ~105 characters, with the file line number). **Every fight and Force-power passage was then pulled and read in full**: Nickel One (Ch8–9), Ben vs Tahiri (Ch10, Ch19), shatterpoint (Ch14), the Throat battle (Ch15), the final duel (Ch18). So were every Caedus/Jaina/Luke power statement and the epilogue. All «…» quotes were copied from full lines. Politics and banter passages were read at the paragraph-opening level only. `L####` is the line number in the source file.
- **Chapter heading lines:** Prologue L65, Ch1 L107, Ch2 L549, Ch3 L871, Ch4 L1119, Ch5 L1405, Ch6 L1611, Ch7 L1885, Ch8 L2029, Ch9 L2351 (printed «9:»), Ch10 L2640, Ch11 L2992, Ch12 L3272, Ch13 L3576, Ch14 L3834, Ch15 L4086, Ch16 L4364, Ch17 L4644, Ch18 L4934, Ch19 L5190 («19:»), Ch20 L5520 («20:»), Epilogue L5798.
- **Feat vs. statement:** Section 2 is what the narration shows; Section 3 is what characters say or think (**STATEMENT**); *(POV)* marks narration filtered through a character. **Denning's framing:** the book is built to let Jaina win as the «Sword of the Jedi» (L799, L5632–5640). It does so by preparation (Mandalorian training, shatterpoint), by Caedus's arrogance (S9, S10), and by catching him wounded and distracted. Then it reframes his death as a half-redemption: he stops fighting to warn Tenel Ka (L5182–5184). It is **not** framed as Jaina out-powering him; she says the opposite (S6).
- **Headline:** the **Jaina-kills-Caedus** book. At Nickel One, Jaina, boosted by Luke flow-walking or projecting into her, takes Caedus's arm with a Mandalorian beskad. Caedus tracks the Jedi base by a Nightsister blood trail on her skin. Luke teaches Jaina shatterpoint, and she crumbles beskar with it. Caedus kills Isolder. In the *Anakin Solo*'s biodisposal pit, Jaina, alone this time, guts Caedus at the door, survives lightning, incinerator and saber, cuts his Achilles tendon, and runs him through as he stops to warn Tenel Ka. Ben spares a broken Tahiri. Luke hands the Remnant to Jag; Daala becomes Chief of State; Allana survives as "Amelia".

---

## 1. Cast who fight or use the Force

| Character | Role in this book | Force user? |
|---|---|---|
| Darth Caedus / Jacen Solo | Sith Lord, GA dictator. Loses his arm at Nickel One; kills Roegr, Vatok, Isolder; dies to Jaina | Yes |
| Jaina Solo | The Sword. Assassin on Nickel One; learns shatterpoint; kills Caedus | Yes |
| Luke Skywalker | Grand Master. Jams Caedus's foresight, possesses or boosts Jaina at Nickel One, bombs the *Anakin Solo* bridge, shears a blast door; won't kill Caedus himself | Yes |
| Ben Skywalker | Captured and tortured; escapes; beats and spares Tahiri | Yes |
| Tahiri Veila | Sith apprentice. Kills Shevu; disillusioned by Caedus; surrenders to Ben | Yes |
| Leia, Saba, Kyp, Corran, Kenth, Cilghal, Tesar, Lowbacca, Zekk | Masters and Knights; Zekk goes missing | Yes |
| Tenel Ka, Allana | Hapan Queen Mother and daughter; survive the nanokiller | Tenel Ka yes; Allana sensitive |
| Boba Fett, Mirta Gev, Vatok, Roegr | Mandalorians. Roegr and Vatok are killed by Caedus; Mirta paralyzed (later healing) | No |
| Han, Jag Fel, Isolder, Shevu, Daala, Moffs (Lecersen) | Non-Force | No |

## 2. Combat and Force feats, in chapter order

### Ch1–Ch7
1. **Ch1 · L159–163, L335–347 · Jaina**: Force-clears a Verpine swarm; Force-blows back a nanokiller cloud.
2. **Ch1 · L481 · Jaina vs Fett (reflex)**: her lightsaber «landed but did not slice» on his beskar neck guard.
3. **Ch3 · L1099–1115 · Tahiri vs Ben**: ambush; she burns his back, he Force-pulls her down, GAG captures him.
4. **Ch5 · L1469–1513 · Luke (foresight jamming)**: alters what Caedus sees in his visions and hides Jaina from them.
5. **Ch6–7 · L1801–1853, L1913–1929 · Jaina**: dropsuit kills of Starhunters; a twelve-man gun crew in seconds.

### Ch8–9 — Nickel One
6. **L2289 · Caedus**: Force-throws Mirta into a ceiling corner (paralyzed).
7. **L2305–2309 · Caedus (shatterpoint)**: a pommel tap crumbles beskar: «her brother had just destroyed a piece with a tap. Had he mastered the shatterpoint?»
8. **L2315–2347 · Caedus vs Mandalorians + sniper Jaina**: kills Roegr; a mag-pellet hits his shoulder (L2317); he fires Force lightning through the aperture (L2323). One-armed, he deflects for the Moffs, out-fights Vatok, and keeps Vatok between himself and Jaina's line of fire (L2341–2343), then kills him.
9. **L2469 · Luke**: body «just wasn't there» in the blastboat during the fight (flow-walking or projecting).
10. **L2503–2545 · "Luke"/Jaina vs Caedus (Caedus POV)**: a kick lands before his «Aing-Tii fighting-sight» warns him (L2503). A «pure relentless ferocity» combination (L2517); a Force blast «through the seats» (L2525); Caedus's darts dodged (L2533). He buries the attacker under a corpse and throws a grenade (L2535–2545).
11. **L2552–2576 · Jaina (empowered)**: «had never been so filled with the Force» (L2552). Returns the grenade (L2556); **cuts off Caedus's arm with Vatok's beskad** after he trades shoulder for throat (L2564–2566); cuts his thigh (L2570). **Caedus's point-blank Force lightning** throws her into a wall and breaks her ribs (L2572–2574). The surge then drains: «her Force energies returned to their normal level» (L2598).
12. **L2586 · Caedus**: rises after losing the arm: «That would have been impressive-if it weren't so kriffing scary.»

### Ch10–Ch14
13. **L2900–2918 · Tahiri**: Force-lightning torture kills Shevu.
14. **L2944–2952 · Ben**: Force-throws his captors and escapes.
15. **L3550 · Ben**: Force-shoves SD-XX into space.
16. **L3682–3688 · Caedus (blood trail)**: locates the Jedi base by the Nightsister blood trail through Jaina's stains.
17. **L3846–3858 · Jaina**: beats Zekk, Lowbacca, Tesar and Jag at once with electrostaffs (training, three days post-surgery).
18. **L3896–3906 · Jaina (shatterpoint)**: crumbles homogoni, plastoid and **raw beskar** with fingertips.

### Ch15–Ch17
19. **L4310 · Caedus (sensing)**: senses Jaina and Zekk in the Throat through the blood trail/combat-meld; Zekk's StealthX is destroyed (L4330).
20. **L4344–4354 · Luke**: Bes'uliik bombing run on the bridge; Caedus survives by sealing a hatch (L4382).
21. **L4766 · Jaina**: Force-hurls a guard through a door. **L4920 · Caedus**: Force-snaps Isolder's neck.

### Ch18 — the final duel (Jaina alone)
22. **L5100–5102 · Jaina**: ambush stab through Caedus's gut; he Force-blasts her into a wall anyway.
23. **L5106–5110 · both**: his lightning absorbed by her blade; her Force-hurl throws him into the pit.
24. **L5112–5116 · Caedus (melee edge)**: impossible to sense, «anticipating her every move»; smashes her cheekbone.
25. **L5122–5132 · Jaina**: droid-pull, stomp onto the conveyor; escapes a Force neck-twist; chin kick.
26. **L5150–5152 · Caedus (TK)**: drags her toward the incinerator; «it took all her Force strength to pull herself aside the half meter».
27. **L5154–5160 · Jaina (light-side surge)**: draws on «love of what her brother had been» → «a roaring maelstrom of power». Shuts the door with the Force; blocks his lightning.
28. **L5164–5172 · exchange**: his kick breaks her forearm; his blade cuts her abdomen; her blade cuts his Achilles tendon.
29. **L5180–5186 · Jaina (kill)**: a high slash to his armless side; he does not defend, reaching out to warn Tenel Ka; blade to the heart.

### Ch19–Ch20
30. **L5412–5430 · Ben vs Tahiri**: falling duel down a shaft; Ben has a clean kill and parries instead. Tahiri surrenders.
31. **L5680 · Luke**: Force-opens a locked blast door, shearing thirty-centimeter locking pins.

| S2 | L743 | Luke | «I can't kill Caedus.» (meaning he must not; Saba: it would taint him, L769) | High (moral, not power). |

| S10 | L3688 | Caedus POV | «fighting Luke one moment, Jaina the next, and then they had both been there-not just illusions» | High. The Nickel One fight was Jaina + Luke, not Jaina alone. |
