## 5. Plot summary relevant to the fights
- **Ch1–Ch2:** The Remnant takes Nickel One. Fett sends Jaina off. The Jedi council approves killing Caedus; Luke won't do it himself (his foresight says he would win and fall); Jaina gets the job.
- **Ch3–Ch5:** Caedus courts the Moffs. Tahiri captures Ben in Monument Plaza (a trap Luke foresaw and allowed). Luke reveals he is jamming Caedus's foresight.
- **Ch6–Ch9:** The Jedi and Mandalorians hit Nickel One. Mirta's team assaults the Moff forum; Caedus kills them. Jaina snipes him. Luke projects or flow-walks into the fight through Jaina; she takes his arm and leg. Jaina is extracted.
- **Ch10–Ch14:** Tahiri kills Shevu; Ben escapes and is retrieved by the Zel sisters. The *Anakin Solo* captures Isolder. Caedus interrogates Mirta and fixes on killing Luke. Jaina learns shatterpoint. The blood trail is discovered. Tenel Ka's fleet joins the Jedi.
- **Ch15–Ch17:** Battle in the Throat: Zekk lost; Luke bombs the bridge; Jaina boards. Caedus breaks Tahiri's illusions about Anakin and stops the nanokiller to protect Allana. Jaina finds Mirta. Caedus kills Isolder.
- **Ch18:** The Moffs have taken Isolder's blood for a royal-specific nanokiller. Jaina ambushes Caedus in the biodisposal pit and kills him as he tries to warn Tenel Ka.
- **Ch19–Epilogue:** Ben stops Tahiri's warhead and she surrenders. The Jedi take the *Anakin Solo*; Luke installs Jag over the Moffs. Daala becomes Chief of State. Allana lives in hiding as Amelia Solo.

---

## Story and prose notes

### SP1. Plot summary, in order
See Section 5 and the raw log. Denning structures the book in mirrored halves: the boosted assassination attempt (Nickel One) and the solo one (biodisposal pit). The "A LONG TIME AGO" twin flashbacks (L67, L2982, L5504) frame the twin bond being lost and finally felt again (L5564).

### SP2. Character arcs
- **Caedus/Jacen:** tries to recommit to «To serve.» (L887), but kills Isolder, tortures Mirta, and uses Tahiri's grief («That was kind of the point», L4632). His love for Allana survives to the end: he vetoes the nanokiller (L4596) and dies warning Tenel Ka (L5184). Jaina's verdict: «he became Jacen again for a second» (L5914); the galaxy he wanted is born under Daala (L5904).
- **Jaina:** from «I still love him ... But that won't make me hesitate» (L795) to killing him, and then mourning him with the twin bond restored (L5564). She wins by doing it «her own way» (L5004), drawing strength from love, not anger (L5154).
- **Luke:** foresight strategist. He jams Caedus's visions, sacrifices Ben's freedom and Jaina's anonymity, and refuses to be the killer because «I win» would make him the next darkness (L1505, L769). Political endgame: Jag over the Moffs.
- **Ben:** survives torture, keeps his cop's ethic (spares the guards, L2952; spares Tahiri, L5428), offers to lie for her (L5484).
- **Tahiri:** her Sith motive (Anakin) is exposed as perception only (L4446–4476); she kills Shevu by accident (L2920–2928), then surrenders: «I'm done with all Solos» (L5466).
- **Mara's aftermath:** present only as motive (Luke's vengeance, L731, L2519).
- **Boba Fett / Mandalorians:** Fett stays outside the duel; Roegr and Vatok die; Mirta paralyzed; Fett and Mirta exiled from Mandalore by the nanokiller (L5848). Fett's lessons pay off in Jaina's beskad cut and commitment.
- **Han and Leia:** «Caedus must die» (L709) to grief; they adopt Allana (L5930–5938).

### SP3. Themes
- **Sacrifice and its cost:** Luke won't kill Caedus because victory would corrupt him; Jaina kills Caedus at the cost of her twin.
- **Love as the light-side engine:** L5154 is the thesis (contrast with Caedus feeding on pain, L5134).
- **Hubris and foresight:** Caedus «I've seen it» (L4404) vs Luke's jamming (L1469).
- **Jacen's vision fulfilled by others:** Daala's peace (L5902–5904).

### SP4. Notable prose passages
- «"It's worse," Luke said. "I win."» (L1505)
- «That would have been impressive-if it weren't so kriffing scary.» (L2586)
- «Luke Skywalker couldn't kill me. What does she expect to do?» (L4400)
- «not through her anger or pain, as a Sith might, but through her love of what her brother had been» (L5154)
- «Then the blade reached Caedus's heart, and he dropped at her feet, and Jaina felt nothing at all.» (L5186)
- «Hello, Jaina ... They tell me we're going to be sisters now.» (L5938)

### SP5. Worldbuilding and lore stated in the text
- **Shatterpoint** (L2307–2309, L3884–3906): perceiving the Force-bonds of matter; Mace Windu named; Luke has mastered it and teaches Jaina.
- **Nightsister blood trail** (L3688, L4020): Sith blood marks a target so it can be tracked.
- **Aing-Tii fighting-sight** (L2503): Caedus's precognitive combat technique.
- **Flow-walking** (L4446–4476): Caedus says it changes perception, not the past.
- **Tailored nanokillers** (L4554–4568, L5848).

### SP6. Timeline placement and connections
- 41 ABY, ending LotF. Leads to *Fate of the Jedi* (Daala's government, Jedi distrust, Allana as Amelia Solo, Tahiri's trial; «THE STORY CONTINUES», L5944).
- **Author intent:** Denning chooses Jaina to win (Sword of the Jedi), Luke to remain the stronger but morally restrained Master (S1), and Caedus to die half-redeemed. He goes out of his way to show Caedus as frightening even crippled (L2586, L5102–5104, L5134), so the win reads as earned but not a power upset.

---

## 6. Verdict-relevant takeaways
1. **Jaina vs Caedus:** Jaina wins the fight that happened (Fight C), alone, by killing him. But the conditions strongly favor her: he was one-armed, gut-stabbed at once, distracted by the Allana crisis, and at the end he chose not to defend. She calls him «magnitudes stronger» in the Force (S6). **Verdict: Jaina beats Invincible-era Caedus in the conditions Denning gives (high confidence for the in-book result). Jaina vs a healthy, focused Caedus is still a Caedus favorite on raw power, but a closer one given her prep, beskad/shatterpoint, and his tendency to underrate her (medium).**
2. **Caedus vs Luke:** S1 (Luke foresees winning), S3–S5 (Caedus fears him one-armed, «barely» survived Inferno), plus *Revelation* Fight A. **Luke > Caedus, medium-high → high.**
3. **Caedus's final tier:** in breadth, Caedus is a top Master/Sith Lord: shatterpoint, Aing-Tii fighting-sight, blood trail, fleet battle meditation and the mass mind-influence of *Revelation*. Feats 7, 8, 12, 24 and 26 show he stayed dangerous while crippled. He remains below Luke and was killed by a well-prepared Knight under heavy disadvantage. **Final tier: high Master / Sith Lord, below Luke.**
4. **Nickel One is not clean Jaina-solo evidence** (S10, L2598). Weight it as Jaina+Luke.
5. **Ben > Tahiri** (Fight D), and Tahiri is out of the Sith role.
## Appendix: raw reading log (chunked, saved as read)

### Front matter / Prologue (L1–L105)
- Dramatis personae: «Darth Caedus (formerly Jacen Solo); Sith Lord (human male)» (L33); «Tahiri Veila; Sith apprentice» (L53). Each chapter opens with a joke by «Jacen Solo, age 14» (L111 etc.); Ch3's is tagged «(shortly before he cut off Tenel Ka's arm in sparring practice)» (L875).
- Prologue (present tense, flashback): the young twins escape a cell; the twin bond means «neither Jaina nor Jacen is ever truly alone» (L73).
### Ch1 (L107–545) — Nickel One (Roche), Verpine hive
- «three weeks after the battle of Fondor» (L115). Jaina has trained with Fett «a little more than a standard month» (L121). «None of that meant she was good enough to bring down a Sith Lord.» (L129).
- Jaina Force-clears the Verpine swarm (L159–163) and Force-blows back a nanokiller cloud (L335–347). She takes out a plasma-gun sniper team (L461–479). Her blade «landed but did not slice» on Fett's beskar neck guard when he sneaks up on her (L481). Fett: «You've killed more Jedi than I have.» — Fett: «Not like your brother. Not anyone that powerful.» (L227–229). Fett: «Either way, I get my revenge.» … «Your brother kills you.» (L535–539).
### Ch2 (L549–867) — Shedu Maad, Jedi war council
- Han: «Okay, Dad:Caedus must die.» (Jaina, L709). Luke: «I'm not going to need support because I can't kill Caedus.» (L743); his futures where he goes after Caedus: Saba says killing Caedus would taint him (L761–769). Jaina volunteers: «He'll expect me to fight like a Jedi» (L787); «I still love him ... But that won't make me hesitate-not even for a nanosecond.» (L795). The Masters had anticipated her request (L833–847). Sword of the Jedi vision invoked (L799).
### Ch3 (L871–1115) — Caedus and the Moffs; Ben captured
- Caedus resolves to stop being self-absorbed and «To serve.» (L887). Force-lifts a stormtrooper sergeant (L905). Moff: «We are all aware that you are very powerful in the Force, Darth Caedus» (L969).
- Monument Plaza: Tahiri ambushes Ben; her blade burns his lower back (L1099); he Force-pulls her down (L1103) but is captured by GAG troops (L1115).
### Ch4 (L1119–1401) — Jaina and Leia fail to rescue Ben (coma gas, chase).
### Ch5 (L1405–1607) — Luke's strategy
- Luke had foreseen Ben's capture and let it happen: «It would have changed the outcome.» (L1453). **Luke is "jamming" Caedus's foresight**: «When I meditate on the future, I'm focusing so hard on Caedus» (L1469); he keeps Jaina hidden from Caedus's visions (L1513). **Luke on fighting Caedus himself: «"It's worse," Luke said. "I win."»** (L1505) — STATEMENT (Luke's foresight: he would win but fall to darkness). Plan: Jaina dropsuit insertion at Nickel One.
### Ch6 (L1611–1881) — Nickel One assault
- Luke coerces Fett into escort duty (L1671–1685). Luke to Jaina: «Be wary of yourself, of your own emotions.» (L1757). Jaina's dropsuit: kills Starhunters with a mini-missile and her lightsaber (L1801–1853).
### Ch7–Ch9 (L1885–2636) — Strategic Planning Forum
- Jaina kills a twelve-man gun crew (L1913–1929). Mirta's strike team shows Caedus «alone, deep in meditation, and vulnerable» (L2009). Vatok and Jaina, «You were my favorite» (L2141).
- **FIGHT: Caedus vs Mandalorian strike team (Mirta, Roegr, Vatok + one), with Jaina sniping with a QuietSnipe without using the Force (L2281–2347).** Caedus Force-throws Mirta into a ceiling corner; she «did not move again» (L2289; she survives later). **Shatterpoint-like feat:** a pommel tap crumbles Roegr's beskar breastplate: «her brother had just destroyed a piece with a tap. Had he mastered the shatterpoint?» (L2305–2309) — FEAT + Jaina POV interpretation (narration: Mace Windu «had been the last», L2309). He kills Roegr (L2315). **Jaina's mag-pellet hits Caedus in the shoulder** (L2317); he answers with Force lightning through the aperture (L2323). One-armed, he deflects for the Moffs and kills Vatok (L2327–2347), always anticipating Jaina's aim (L2343).
- **Luke "sort of gone":** Luke's body sits motionless in the blastboat, «he just wasn't there» (L2465–2469).
- **FIGHT: Caedus vs "Luke" in the projection booth (Caedus POV, L2499–2545):** a «brown-robed blur» kicks him in the ribs before his «Aing-Tii fighting-sight» warns him (L2503). He sees Luke: «the one swordsman in the galaxy whom Caedus did not dare fight one-armed» (L2515). Luke's attack is «pure relentless ferocity» (L2517). Caedus thinks: «The last time he had fought Luke ... barely managed to survive.» (L2519). A Force blast drives him «through the seats behind him» (L2525); his darts miss (L2533); he hurls a corpse onto "Luke", who lies still (L2535–2539). Caedus notes Luke's «new fighting style ... conservative, brutal, and ruthless» (L2543) and throws a grenade at the body (L2545).
- **Reveal: the fighter was Jaina, possessed or empowered by Luke** (Luke's body is empty; Caedus «sensed his uncle's presence far above», L2513). Jaina: «had never been so filled with the Force ... She could drive her fist through a durasteel wall, or catch a blaster bolt between her fingers» (L2552). She Force-returns the grenade (L2556). **She takes Caedus's arm off with Vatok's beskad**: he leans to take the blow on his wounded shoulder instead of his throat (L2564–2566); she then cuts his thigh (L2570–2576). **Caedus answers with point-blank Force lightning**; she hits a wall with broken ribs (L2572–2574). Afterwards «an abrupt draining as her Force energies returned to their normal level» (L2598). Jaina POV: «the fact that her brother remained standing at all proved how much greater his Force powers were than her own» (L2592) — STATEMENT (Jaina, wounded). Caedus shouts «Skywalker is the dangerous one.» (L2600); he sees Luke still in a corner (L2608–2612). RESULT: **Caedus loses an arm and is carried to the infirmary; Jaina, Luke-boosted, is injured and withdraws** (L2624–2636). Context: Caedus already pellet-shot and fighting one-armed.
### Ch10 (L2640–2988) — Ben in GAG detention
- Ben was tortured by the droid SD-XX ("Double-Ex") and never gave up the base (L2650–2658). Tahiri tries to seduce the coordinates out of him (L2664–2752): «You're too nice to be a Sith ... They enjoy torture.» — «I'm learning, Ben.» (L2748–2750). **Tahiri kills Shevu** with Force lightning while torturing him (L2900–2918); she is horrified (L2920–2928). Ben Force-throws his captors, Tahiri is shocked unconscious by the stun circuit (L2944–2948), and he escapes down the body chute with Shevu's corpse, sparing the guards (L2952–2974).
- Flashback: young Jaina and Jacen beneath the Yavin academy (L2982–2988).
### Ch11 (L2992–3268) — the blastboat convalescence bay
- Jaina's neck is stained by Caedus's blood, which won't wash off (L3002, L3032). **Jaina: «After I cut off his arm, Caedus seemed surprised that it was me.»** (L3092); «he stopped attacking» (L3096); he redirected the troopers' fire (L3100). She wonders if Jacen is still in there (L3104); Han: «He isn't.» (L3106). Fett blames her for Mirta («I thought I had trained someone to take him down», L3230); Jaina: «this is war, not an assassination» (L3232); Fett: «Die proud.» (L3238). Luke has «seen something decisive» (L3252) and predicts Caedus won't return to Coruscant (L3260).
### Ch12 (L3272–3572) — Ben on Roqoo-area station; the Zel sisters
- Hapan agents Taryn and Trista Zel collect Ben (L3338–3372). Ben Force-shoves Double-Ex off the hull (L3550). The *Anakin Solo* (cloaked) captures Prince Isolder's *Beam Racer* (L3554–3572).
### Ch13 (L3576–3830) — Caedus interrogates Mirta
- «Little more than a standard week after losing his arm» Caedus has a prosthetic kneecap (the Revelation kneecapping) but **no replacement arm**, only a fused stump; he refused months in bacta to reattach it (L3588–3592). Mirta survived but is paralyzed (L3594–3604). He finds the Jedi base by «a Nightsister blood trail» through Jaina (L3682–3688). **Caedus POV: «He had been fighting Luke one moment, Jaina the next, and then they had both been there-not just illusions of them, but presences real enough to bat blaster bolts back»** (L3688). Mirta: «There was only one Jedi ... Your sister, Jaina.» (L3774); Caedus disbelieves it (L3776–3788). «It had been Luke's eyes into which he had been looking when his arm was taken» (L3810). Visions: Luke driving a crimson lightsaber through his heart, Luke on his throne (L3810). Decision: «killing Luke Skywalker is the key to winning this war» (L3830). (Tahiri threatens Mirta with a DNA "sample" from her family, L3750–3756.)
### Ch14 (L3834–...) — Shedu Maad training
- **Jaina beats four sparring partners at once** (Zekk, Lowbacca, Tesar, Jag) with electrostaffs, using Beviin's «art of losing herself to the fight» (L3846–3858). Luke has her practice **shatterpoint**: «Shatterpoint is a Force technique ... not a physical one» (L3884).
- Shatterpoint: Luke has mastered it himself and tells Jaina «if her twin brother was one of the few capable of learning it, so was she» (L3892). **Jaina shatters a homogoni slab, a plastoid breastplate, and a raw beskar disk with her fingertips** (L3896–3906): «the disk crumbled, just as Roegr's breastplate had» (L3906). FEAT (training; matches Caedus's Ch8 feat).
- Ben returns with the Zel sisters (L3914). Tenel Ka and Allana arrive with the Hapan Home Fleet; Isolder knew the base location (L3982–4006). **The stain is a Nightsister «blood trail»** (L4020). Luke: «I knew Caedus would be coming ... For me.» (L4024). Saba: «He knowz you are the Sword ... Does one win a battle by breaking the Sword, or the warrior who wieldz it?» (L4028). Jaina's last hope dies: «Caedus had known exactly what he was doing when he ordered those stormtroopers to redirect their fire» (L4038). Allana is afraid of «Jedi Jacen» (L4074).
### Ch15 (L4086–4360) — Battle of Shedu Maad / the Throat
- Jaina and Zekk act as bait in StealthXs; Caedus senses them through the blood trail and the combat-meld (L4310). **Zekk vanishes** from the Force (L4330; status unknown at this point). Luke repeatedly goes «gone» (flow-walking or projecting) during the battle (L4198, L4250). Luke flies a Bes'uliik bombing run at the *Anakin Solo*'s bridge (L4344–4354). Jaina boards the *Anakin Solo* with suit thrusters, hiding her presence: «Get ready, Caedus.» (L4360).
### Ch16 (L4364–4640) — Caedus on the *Anakin Solo*
- Caedus survived Luke's bombing by sealing a hatch, «his intestines had filled with ice» on seeing Luke (L4382). Vision of a grown Allana on his throne (L4386). **Caedus: «Luke Skywalker couldn't kill me. What does she expect to do?»** / «Practically dead already ... My sister is nothing to be concerned with. We've won. I've seen it.» (L4400–4404) — STATEMENT (Caedus; arrogance = Mirta's "he underestimates you", L4834). He can't feel Jaina: «She's hiding in the Force ... It's more like a smell» (L4498). He admits to Tahiri that the Baanu Rass flow-walk only changed her perception, not the past (L4446–4476); this breaks their bond («That was kind of the point», L4632). He vetoes the Moffs' nanokiller attack on the Dragon Queen (to save Allana, L4596). The Moffs have already air-dispersed a Fett-tailored nanokiller over Mandalore (L4560–4568). He sends Tahiri in a StealthX to find the real Jedi base (L4624).
### Ch17 (L4644–4930) — the prison hold
- Jaina, disguised with captured GAG captain "CeeCee", finds Mirta paralyzed (L4792). **Mirta: «he underestimates you.»** (L4834); **Jaina: «He's magnitudes stronger than me in the Force. All I've got on him is five weeks of Mandalorian commando training.»** (L4836) — STATEMENT (Jaina, self-deprecating; HIGH for raw power after Nickel One). Mirta: «He's convinced you couldn't have taken his arm-at least not alone. He thinks Luke was with us.» (L4838). Jaina: «Maybe Luke was there.» (L4840) — the Nickel One power surge stays ambiguous. Mirta takes a blaster and shoots herself (or the guards) after Jaina leaves (L4850; see Ch18+).
- Caedus refuses the nanokiller to protect Allana (L4866–4872). He frees Isolder; Isolder refuses and spits at him; Caedus Force-grips him: «death is certainly an option» (L4910–4918).
- **Caedus kills Isolder** by snapping his neck with the Force (L4920) and takes the body to the incinerator himself.
### Ch18 (L4934–5186) — the Biodisposal Pit: **Jaina vs Caedus, the final duel**
- Jaina decides **not** to call on Luke this time: Caedus «would be ready for Luke's strength ... If Jaina expected to win this fight, she would have to fight in a different way-her own way.» (L5004) — so the final fight is Jaina alone.
- Caedus finds Moff needle marks on Isolder's body: the Moffs are making a nanokiller for Tenel Ka and Allana (L5070–5072). A missile boat has already launched (L5080–5084).
- **Opening:** Jaina ambushes him at the pit door; her lightsaber goes through his gut («a thumb-sized scorch hole just below his ribs. But he was still standing», L5100–5102). His Force blast breaks her half-healed ribs and back synthmesh (L5100–5102). He chains Force lightning into her blade (L5106–5108); she Force-hurls him back into the pit (L5108).
- **Saber and melee phase:** «it was impossible to sense Caedus in the Force. He fought to keep his advantage ... anticipating her every move-and making her pay for each step with a painful kick or elbow strike» (L5112). A pommel strike smashes her cheekbone (L5116). She Force-pulls the pit droid into him and stomps him onto a conveyor belt (L5122–5128). He tries a Force neck-twist; she spins with it and kicks him under the chin (L5128–5132).
- **Caedus pleads:** «Jaina, listen to me ... I'm trying to save Tenel Ka and Allana.» (L5140) — Jaina: «Sure you are ... Just like you saved Isolder.» / «So die already.» (L5142–5146). He Force-pulls her toward the fusion incinerator; she burns her back (L5150–5152).
- **Jaina's resolution moment (author's framing):** «Jaina opened herself fully to the Force, drawing it in through the power of her emotions-not through her anger or pain, as a Sith might, but through her love of what her brother had been» (L5154); «filling her with a roaring maelstrom of power» (L5156). She Force-shuts the door on him (L5158), catches his lightning on her blade (L5160); his snap-kick breaks her forearm (L5164); his blade opens her abdomen while **hers cuts his Achilles tendon** (L5168–5172). «He had one arm and one good leg, and they were not even on the same side of the body.» (L5174).
- **Kill:** he staggers, «his eyes filled with rage and exhaustion and despair» (L5178); Jaina goes for his armless side (L5180). **Caedus does not counter**: «whatever he had in mind, it was not vengeance ... his lightsaber was still hanging at his side» (L5182). He reaches through the Force to warn Tenel Ka (L5184). «Then the blade reached Caedus's heart, and he dropped at her feet, and Jaina felt nothing at all.» (L5186). RESULT: **Jaina kills Caedus.** Context: Caedus fought the whole duel one-armed («lacking a second hand», L5086; the stump, L3590), gut-stabbed in the first second, and syringes in his face; Jaina had half-healed ribs, a broken forearm, a cut abdomen and burns. At the end he stopped fighting to save his daughter.
### Ch19 (L5190–5516) — Shedu Maad ground battle; **Ben vs Tahiri**
- Nanokiller attack aboard the Dragon Queen; royals feared dead (L5228). A Hapan general's treachery (L5272). Tahiri leads stormtroopers with a baradium warhead to the Jedi hangar (L5350–5358). **Ben vs Tahiri down a sinkhole shaft** (L5412–5430): she kicks him into the wall; «she'd had him, but she had been too slow again» (L5420); Ben could «take her legs off at the knees» but parries without countering (L5428); «she really didn't want to kill him» (L5430). Tahiri gives up: «I'm not coming back to the Order, Ben.» / «I'm done with all Solos.» (L5460–5466), then surrenders to the Hapans (L5486–5496). RESULT: Ben wins; Tahiri's will is broken more than her skill.
- Flashback: Jacen comforts Jaina after Ithor (L5504–5516).
### Ch20 (L5520–5794) — boarding the *Anakin Solo*; Moffs
- Han believed Allana was killed and nearly fires a baradium missile (L5544); Leia: «It's Jacen ... Jaina got him.» (L5556). Jaina holds Jacen's head, feeling their twin bond again (L5564). The Sword of the Jedi pronouncement repeated (L5632–5640).
- **Luke Force-opens a sealed blast door, shearing thirty-centimeter locking pins** (L5680): «Now you know who to call if you need a mountain moved.» (L5684). Luke gives the Moffs to Jag Fel as head of the Remnant (L5696–5734); Han lets the Moffs live in exchange for an Allana memorial (L5742–5776). Tenel Ka is alive (L5780).
### Epilogue (L5798–5940)
- Jaina regrets not believing him: «had she only been willing to look for the little goodness that remained in him» (L5808); «I think he became Jacen again for a second before I:before I killed him.» (L5914). Fett and Mirta can never return to Mandalore (nanokiller, L5844–5848). Daala becomes «NEW ALLIANCE CHIEF OF STATE» (L5870) and promises a galaxy «where we won't need Jedi to sort out our differences» (L5902). Jaina: «Jacen had not failed. He had sacrificed everything» (L5904). **Allana is alive**, adopted by Han and Leia as "Amelia": «Hello, Jaina ... They tell me we're going to be sisters now.» (L5938). (Zekk still missing, L5828.) END OF BOOK (L5971).

## Character analyses (added 2026-10-17 under the 7+ characters rule)

*Method:* built from the verified log above (Sections 2–4 and SP2). `L####` = line in `223050c0…txt` (chapter given). Quotes in «…» are verbatim and were re-checked by script. No wiki material is used. **Before:** 8 story-arc profiles (SP2), none with feats, fights and scaling in the same entry. **Added:** 12 analyses.

### 1. Darth Caedus
- **Role/arc:** a GA dictator who tries to recommit to «To serve.» (L887), then kills Roegr, Vatok and Isolder. He vetoes the nanokiller to save Allana (L4596) and dies warning Tenel Ka (L5184).
- **Key feats:** «her brother had just destroyed a piece with a tap. Had he mastered the shatterpoint?» (Nickel One, L2305–2309). Kills Roegr and Vatok one-armed, using Vatok as cover from Jaina's sniping (L2315–2347). Rises after losing his arm: «That would have been impressive-if it weren't so kriffing scary.» (L2586). Tracks the Jedi base by Jaina's blood trail (L3682–3688). Snaps Isolder's neck with the Force (L4920). In the final duel he is «anticipating her every move» (L5112–5116), and against his TK pull «it took all her Force strength to pull herself aside the half meter» (L5150–5152).
- **Statements:** his POV: Luke is «the one swordsman in the galaxy whom Caedus did not dare fight one-armed» (L2515). On Inferno: he «barely managed to survive» (L2519). Seeing Luke's Bes'uliik, «his intestines had filled with ice» (L4382). Jaina: «He's magnitudes stronger than me in the Force.» (L4836). Fett: «Not like your brother. Not anyone that powerful.» (L229).
- **Notable quote:** «You need to get out of my way. I'm trying to save Tenel Ka and Allana.» (L5140).
- **Fights/results:** beats four Mandalorian commandos. Loses an arm to Jaina, boosted by Luke (Nickel One). Killed by Jaina alone (Ch18) while one-armed and gut-stabbed in the first second, and he stops defending at the end (L5182–5184).
- **Scaling:** below Luke, above Jaina in raw power. Peak LotF Caedus is a top-tier Sith but not at the Sidious/Vader peak (see verdicts.md).

### 2. Jaina Solo
- **Role/arc:** goes from «I still love him ... But that won't make me hesitate» (L795) to killing him «her own way» (L5004) with strength drawn from love (L5154).
- **Key feats:** her saber «landed but did not slice» on Fett's beskar (Ch1, L481). Kills a twelve-man gun crew in seconds (Ch6–7). At Nickel One she «had never been so filled with the Force» (L2552) and cuts off Caedus's arm with a beskad (L2564–2566). Beats Zekk, Lowbacca, Tesar and Jag at once in training three days after surgery (L3846–3858). Shatterpoint crumbles raw beskar (L3896–3906). In the final duel she draws «a roaring maelstrom of power» (L5154–5160), cuts his Achilles tendon (L5164–5172) and kills him (L5180–5186).
- **Statements:** her POV: «the fact that her brother remained standing at all proved how much greater his Force powers were than her own» (L2592). Mirta: «He's convinced you couldn't have taken his arm-at least not alone.» (L4838).
- **Notable quote:** «I think he became Jacen again for a second before I:before I killed him.» (L5914).
- **Scaling:** Jaina beats a crippled, distracted Caedus. That does not make her stronger than peak Caedus.

### 3. Luke Skywalker
- **Role/arc:** a foresight strategist who will not be the killer.
- **Key feats:** jams and edits Caedus's visions (Ch5, L1469–1513). His body «just wasn't there» during Nickel One (L2469), meaning he was flow-walking or projecting through Jaina. Bes'uliik bombing run on the bridge (L4344–4354). Shears a blast door's thirty-centimeter locking pins (L5680). Han: «Now you know who to call if you need a mountain moved.» (L5684).
- **Statements:** on fighting Caedus himself: «"It's worse," Luke said. "I win."» (L1505). Caedus's POV: «fighting Luke one moment, Jaina the next, and then they had both been there-not just illusions» (L3688).
- **Scaling:** Luke > Caedus. The strongest statement in the series, and Caedus agrees.

### 4. Ben Skywalker
- **Role/arc:** captured and tortured, but keeps his cop's ethic and spares Tahiri (L5428).
- **Feats:** Force-throws his captors and escapes (L2944–2952). Force-shoves SD-XX into space (L3550). Beats Tahiri in a falling duel down a shaft and parries instead of taking a clean kill (L5412–5430).
- **Fights:** Tahiri ambushes and captures him (Ch3, L1099–1115). He later beats her (Ch19).
- **Scaling:** Ben ≥ Tahiri.

### 5. Tahiri Veila
- **Role/arc:** her Sith motive is exposed as perception only (L4446–4476). Kills Shevu (L2900–2918). «I'm done with all Solos» (L5466).
- **Feats:** burns Ben's back in the ambush (L1099–1115). Kills Shevu with Force lightning (L2900–2918).
- **Scaling:** below Ben by the end. She didn't want to win (L5430).

### 6. Boba Fett
- **Role:** stays out of the duel. His lessons pay off in Jaina's beskad cut.
- **Statement:** on Jedi he has killed: «Not like your brother. Not anyone that powerful.» (L229).
- **Scaling:** non-Force. The anti-Jedi method rather than raw power.

### 7. Mirta Gev
- **Fight/result:** Caedus Force-throws her into a ceiling corner and she is paralyzed (L2289).
- **Scaling:** a non-Force fighter outclassed by Caedus.

### 8. Vatok and Roegr (Mandalorian commandos)
- **Fight/result:** Caedus kills both, even one-armed. He out-fights Vatok and kills him (L2341–2343), and his shatterpoint tap crumbles beskar (L2305–2309).
- **Scaling:** beskar is no longer a hard counter once Caedus has shatterpoint.

### 9. Lon Shevu
- **Role:** the honest GAG captain. Tahiri's lightning torture kills him (L2900–2918).
- **Scaling:** non-Force.

### 10. Tenel Ka
- **Role:** Hapan Queen Mother whose fleet joins the Jedi. Caedus dies trying to warn her (L5184).
- **Scaling:** no combat here.

### 11. Saba Sebatyne
- **Statement:** «He knowz you are the Sword ... Does one win a battle by breaking the Sword, or the warrior who wieldz it?» (L4028). Caedus targets Luke instead of Jaina, which is his blind spot.
- **Scaling:** no combat here.

### 12. Zekk
- **Fight/result:** Caedus senses him through the combat-meld and his StealthX is destroyed. He goes missing (L4310–4330).
- **Scaling:** Caedus's sensing beats Knight-level concealment when there is a blood trail or meld to follow.
